### [1] [Topologically Relevant Landmark Selection on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00019)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出基于Hodge Laplacian的地标选择方法，通过调和参与度打分选取拓扑相关地标，从而在子集上更准确地恢复原始点云的持久同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久同调（PH）是拓扑数据分析的重要工具，但对大规模点云的计算代价过高。常见近似方法是在代表点（地标）子集上计算PH，然而现有地标选择方法主要解决离群点污染等问题，而非旨在恢复完整数据集的拓扑特征。

Method: 基于Hodge Laplacian为每个点赋予一个拓扑相关性分数，该分数由其在调和结构中的参与度（harmonic participation）决定；随后选取分数高的点作为地标。

Result: 在合成数据集与真实数据集上，该方法比几何基线方法以及基于局部PH的方法更准确地恢复原始点云的PH。

Conclusion: 通过量化每个点对全局同调结构的参与度，所提方法能够选出拓扑相关的地标，实现对原始点云持久同调的高效且更精确的近似。

Abstract: Persistent Homology (PH) is an important tool in Topological Data Analysis for point clouds, but can be prohibitively expensive for large datasets. A practical approximation is to compute PH on a smaller subset of representative points, known as landmarks. Existing landmark selection methods mainly address challenges such as outlier contamination rather than restoring the topological features of the full dataset. To select topologically relevant landmarks, we propose a landmark selection method based on Hodge Laplacian. It assigns each point a topological relevance score based on its harmonic participation and selects points with high scores as landmarks. By quantifying each point's participation in global homological structures, our method restores the PH of the original point cloud more accurately than geometric baselines and a method built upon local PH on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [2] [Minimum Spanning Trees for Square Crop Plots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00807)
*Mingyang Gong,Adiesha Liyanage,Braeden Sopp,Muzhou Chen,Binhai Zhu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 研究平面轴对齐单位正方形集合在"crop plot距离"下的最小生成树问题：证明该距离不满足三角不等式，MST计算为NP-hard，并给出因子4的近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受农田中访问作物地块的启发——每个地块只能由给定的入口/出口点对（分三种类型，各含四种情形）进入，由此引出对此类平面轴对齐单位正方形集合的MST问题研究。

Method: 针对定义的crop plot距离（该距离即使整体为几何setup也不满足三角不等式，因此需额外处理）分析其MST问题，进行NP-hard性证明并设计近似算法。

Result: (1) 证明在crop plot距离下计算单位正方形集合P的MST是NP-hard的；(2) 给出MST的因子4近似算法。

Conclusion: crop plot距离下的MST问题计算困难（NP-hard），但可通过4-近似算法有效求解。

Abstract: Motivated by accessing crop plots in a field, where each crop plot can only be visited by a given pair of entry/exit points (we have three different types, each having four cases), we study the corresponding Minimum Spanning Tree (MST) problem of such a planar set of axis-aligned unit squares. It turns out that this crop plot distance does not satisfy triangle inequality, even though the whole setup of the problem is geometric. Hence additional care must be taken. The main results of this paper are as follows: (1) we prove that computing the MST of a set $P$ of unit squares under the crop plot distance is NP-hard, (2) the MST of $P$ can be approximated with a factor-4 approximation.

</details>


### [3] [Optimal Coresets for Hyperbolic Farthest-Point Queries via Ideal-Boundary Envelopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01130)
*Eunku Park*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲空间中最远点查询的coreset，证明对任意固定维数$D\ge2$，最优最坏情况coreset大小为$\Theta(\varepsilon^{-(D-1)/2})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最远点查询需要快速回答距离任意查询点最远的输入点，而双曲空间缺乏类似欧氏空间的coreset理论。本文旨在刻画双曲空间中最远点查询的最优coreset规模下界与上界。

Method: 核心几何工具是将双曲查询精确归约为理想边界上的上包络：在双曲面模型中每个输入点诱导一个正边界得分函数，其对数给出沿测地射线的渐近距离偏移；定义理想边界包络为逐点最大值，并证明所有查询上的上确界加性损失等于输入包络与coreset包络之间的最大对数间隙。上界通过将最小包围球圆心移至原点并归一化空间坐标，得到有界欧氏点集，其边界包络有正下界，再用标准欧氏核同时逼近所有方向得分最大值；下界通过固定半径双曲球面上的球面填充、对跖查询及双曲余弦定理使每个输入点不可替代。

Result: 对每个固定$D\ge2$，最优最坏情况coreset规模为$\Theta(\varepsilon^{-(D-1)/2})$，上下界匹配；且下界对加性误差保证与比例保留保证各自单独成立。

Conclusion: 双曲空间最远点查询的coreset复杂度为$\Theta(\varepsilon^{-(D-1)/2})$，其本质由理想边界包络决定，归约到欧氏核技术可同时处理两类近似保证。

Abstract: We study coresets for farthest-point queries in hyperbolic space. Given a nonempty finite set $P \subset \mathbb{H}^D$ and $0<\varepsilon \le 1$, we seek a coreset $P_{\varepsilon} \subseteq P$ whose farthest distance from every query point underestimates that of $P$ by at most an additive $\varepsilon$ and retains at least a $1-\varepsilon$ fraction of it. For every fixed $D \ge 2$, we prove that the optimal worst-case coreset size is $Θ\bigl(\varepsilon^{-(D-1)/2}\bigr)$.
  Our main geometric ingredient is an exact reduction from hyperbolic queries to an upper envelope on the ideal boundary. In the hyperboloid model, each input point induces a positive boundary-score function whose logarithm gives its asymptotic distance offset along geodesic rays. We define the \emph{ideal-boundary envelope} as the pointwise maximum of these functions and prove that the supremum additive loss over all queries equals the maximum logarithmic gap between the input and coreset envelopes.
  For the upper bound, we move the minimum-enclosing-ball center to the origin and normalize the spatial coordinates, obtaining a bounded Euclidean point set whose boundary envelope is bounded away from zero. A standard Euclidean kernel then approximates all directional score maxima simultaneously, and the structure theorem yields both guarantees. For the lower bound, a spherical packing on a fixed-radius hyperbolic sphere, together with antipodal queries and the hyperbolic cosine law, makes every input point indispensable, matching the upper bound even for either guarantee separately.

</details>


### [4] [Hyperbolic Sphericity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01336)
*Thomas Bläsius,Lennart Großkreutz,Jean-Pierre von der Heydt*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲球面性概念，研究其与欧氏球面性及球半径的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧氏球面性已有研究，双曲空间中图的球交表示尚属空白，且双曲球面性在机器学习图嵌入中有应用价值。

Method: 通过构造性证明分析双曲球面性与欧氏球面性的关系，分半径可变与固定两种情形，并研究球面性随半径变化的规律。

Result: 半径依赖图时双曲球面性不超过欧氏球面性；半径固定时最多大1；增大半径可大幅降低球面性但增幅至多1，并构造出球面性随半径振荡的图。

Conclusion: 双曲球面性显著依赖于球半径，为图嵌入提供理论支持。

Abstract: The sphericity of a graph is the minimum dimension d such that the graph has an intersection representation of d-dimensional balls of equal radius. While sphericity has been studied in Euclidean space, we initiate the study of hyperbolic sphericity. The hyperbolic sphericity of a graph can be significantly smaller than its Euclidean counterpart, but, contrary to the Euclidean setting, depends strongly on the radius of the balls.
  We show that, if the radius of the balls can be chosen depending on the graph, the hyperbolic sphericity is upper bounded by the Euclidean sphericity. This extends a previous result for 2-dimensional hyperbolic space, i.e., uniform disk graphs, to arbitrary dimensions. Moreover, our proof is significantly simpler. If we fix the radius, i.e., do not make it dependent on the graph, we show that hyperbolic sphericity can be larger than Euclidean sphericity, but by at most 1. Additionally, we study how hyperbolic sphericity changes with the ball radius. We show that choosing a larger radius can substantially decrease the sphericity while increasing it by at most 1. We also provide a construction of a graph where the sphericity oscillates between different values as the radius increases.
  Besides being theoretically interesting, we note that these results are relevant for graph embeddings in machine learning, where one is interested in low-dimensional numeric representations of symbolic data like graphs.

</details>

### [5] [Three New Likely Spider Millisecond Pulsar Binaries and Optical Kinematic Tracers of Intrabinary Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00455)
*Jay Strader,Rebecca Kyer,Laura Chomiuk,Elias Aydi,Peter Craig,Thomas M. Do,Isabella Molina,Teresa Panurach,Subhroja Roy,Kirill V. Sokolovsky,Samuel J. Swihart,Ryan Urquhart*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对未关联Fermi伽马射线源的多波段跟踪观测，发现三个候选蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星，其中两个为强红背候选体，并首次证明H-alpha发射可来自环绕脉冲星的双星内激波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未关联的Fermi-LAT伽马射线源中可能隐藏着蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星（红背/黑寡妇），需要通过多波段跟踪观测寻找并确认其光学/ X射线对应体，以扩充此类系统的样本并理解其物理特性。

Method: 对未关联Fermi-LAT伽马射线源开展多波段跟踪观测：利用SOAR 4.1米望远镜进行光学光谱观测，利用Swift获取X射线数据；分析光学源的光谱双星特征、轨道周期、质量函数及H-alpha发射线的径向速度相位。

Result: 发现三个候选蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星。两个强红背候选体位于4FGL J1020.4-5314和4FGL J1408.7-3747定位椭圆中心附近，为光谱双星，轨道周期0.15-0.3天，质量函数0.6-0.9 Msun。4FGL J1020.4-5314在两年内所有光谱中均有明亮、极宽的H-alpha发射（FWHM 2100 km/s），其径向速度相位与伴星吸收线速度存在偏移，与发射源位于主星附近（脉冲星周围双星内激波）的预期信号一致。第三个候选体与4FGL J1429.8-0739的关联不确定，但具有30.5小时轨道周期、潜在亚巨星伴星和高X射线光度等异常特性。

Conclusion: 首次证明（对任何蜘蛛系统而言）H-alpha发射可来自环绕脉冲星的双星内激波，为在部分红背系统中利用光学发射线作为双星内激波的动力学示踪提供了可能；第三个候选体虽关联不确定，但其异常特性值得进一步研究。

Abstract: We report the discovery of three candidate spider millisecond pulsars via multi-wavelength follow-up of unassociated Fermi Large Area Telescope gamma-ray sources. All have optical spectroscopy from the SOAR 4.1-m telescope and X-ray data from Swift. Two optical/X-ray sources are exceptionally strong redback candidates, sitting close to the centres of the localization ellipses of the gamma-ray sources 4FGL J1020.4-5314 and 4FGL J1408.7-3747. We show that both optical sources are spectroscopic binaries with orbital periods (0.15-0.3 d) and mass functions (0.6-0.9 Msun) typical for redbacks. 4FGL J1020.4-5314 shows bright, very broad (full width at half maximum 2100 km/s) H-alpha emission in all spectra obtained over two years. Remarkably, the H-alpha radial velocities are offset in phase from the absorption-line velocities of the secondary, and match the signal expected for an emission source near the primary. This demonstrates, for the first time for any spider, that the H-alpha emission can be associated with an intrabinary shock that wraps around the pulsar, opening up the possibility of using optical emission lines as kinematic tracers of the intrabinary shock in some redbacks. A third candidate spider is less secure in its association with the gamma-ray source 4FGL J1429.8-0739, but has unusual properties, including a 30.5-hour orbital period, a potential subgiant secondary, and a high X-ray luminosity, making it worthy of further investigation.

</details>


### [6] [XClass: An Automated Multiwavelength Machine-Learning Pipeline for Classification of Extragalactic X-ray Sources. I. Pipeline Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00459)
*Blagoy Rangelov,Nicholas Moore,Haley Dutton,Erika Marentes*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出 XClass——一个端到端机器学习流水线，将河外 X 射线点源分类为 AGN、LMXB、HMXB、CV、低质量/高质量前场星及超新星遗迹共七类，通过 SED 平移解决测光异质性问题，在 11,374 源基线集上取得 99.6% 准确率与 0.90 平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chandra 在近邻星系探测到的大多数 X 射线源缺乏天体物理分类。核心难点在于测光异质性：训练源多为银河系源，测光来自 PanSTARRS 与 2MASS 等宽场巡天；而河外目标需依赖 HST 成像，且滤光片系统与前者不重叠，特征空间不一致导致无法直接迁移。

Method: 采用 SED 平移：对每个训练源拟合类别适配的光谱模型，将最佳拟合模型通过 HST 滤光片曲线卷积，生成共同特征空间中的合成星等。分类器为不对称两阶段随机森林：第一阶段区分宽类别（AGN、X 射线双星、SNR、恒星），第二阶段利用包含第一阶段概率的增强特征向量将 X 射线双星细分为 LMXB 与 HMXB。训练集由十个银河系星表与河外 SNR 星表构成，并与 Chandra Source Catalog v2.1 交叉匹配。特征包括 X 射线硬度比、SED 平移后的 HST 颜色及 X 射线与光学流量比。训练集限定为至少有一个光学星等的源，以避免插补伪影。

Result: 在由此得到的 11,374 源光学基线上，流水线达到 99.6% 准确率、0.90 平衡准确率，且校准优良（ECE = 0.002），表明概率输出可靠。

Conclusion: XClass 具有模块化与可推广性，适用于任意 HST 滤光片配置，并将在姊妹论文中应用于 M31 与 M33。

Abstract: Most X-ray sources detected in nearby galaxies by the Chandra X-ray Observatory lack astrophysical classifications. We present XClass (X-ray Classifier for extragalactic sources), an end-to-end machine-learning pipeline that classifies extragalactic X-ray point sources into seven classes: AGN, LMXBs, HMXBs, CVs, low-mass and high-mass foreground stars, and supernova remnants. The key challenge is photometric heterogeneity: training sources are predominantly Galactic with photometry from wide-field surveys (PanSTARRS and 2MASS), while extragalactic targets require Hubble Space Telescope (HST) imaging in a disjoint filter system. We address this through a spectral energy distribution (SED) translation that fits class-appropriate spectral models to each training source and convolves the best-fit model through HST filter curves, producing synthetic magnitudes in a common feature space. The classifier uses an asymmetric two-stage Random Forest: Stage 1 separates broad categories (AGN, X-ray binaries, SNRs, stars) and Stage 2 resolves X-ray binaries into LMXBs and HMXBs using an augmented feature vector that includes Stage 1 probabilities. The training set is assembled from ten Galactic catalogs and extragalactic SNR catalogs, cross-matched with the Chandra Source Catalog v2.1. Features include X-ray hardness ratios, SED-translated HST colors, and X-ray-to-optical flux ratios. We restrict the training set to sources with at least one optical magnitude, avoiding imputation artifacts; the pipeline achieves 99.6% accuracy and balanced accuracy of 0.90 on the resulting 11,374-source optical baseline, with excellent calibration (ECE = 0.002). XClass is modular, generalizable to any HST filter configuration, and will be applied to M31 and M33 in a companion paper.

</details>


### [7] [A Multi-Wavelength Picture of the Surprisingly Short 2024/25 Outburst from EXO 0748-676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00495)
*Amy H. Knight,Lauren Rhodes,Noel Castro Segura,Douglas J. K. Buisson,Aru Beri,David M. Russell,Kevin Alabarta,Maria C. Baglio,Jakob van den Eijnden,Matthew J. Middleton,Payaswini Saikia,Chris Done,Rob Fender,Fraser Lewis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: EXO 0748-676在2024年3月结束16年宁静期后爆发，但仅持续约10个月便于2025年1月回归宁静。本文联合9台观测设备，首次对此次爆发进行射电到X射线多波段研究，揭示其光变与光谱行为不同于典型瞬变低质量X射线双星，表现为未完成态跃迁爆发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EXO 0748-676在经历16年宁静期后于2024年3月意外爆发，且与1985-2008年的长期爆发形成鲜明对比，仅约10个月即回归宁静。作者希望借助多波段观测，理解此次异常短促爆发的物理性质及其与前次爆发的差异。

Method: 汇总来自9台观测设施、覆盖射电到X射线波段的数据，对2024/25爆发开展首个多波段研究；分析X射线与光学光变曲线，利用光学数据研究相位曲线与辐照主导的光变；使用XMM-Newton与NICER的X射线观测和光谱拟合，包括含团块状电离吸收体的模型；并进行X射线颜色分析以判定光谱态。

Result: 与1985年爆发不同，X射线和光学波段的上升与衰减均被完整捕捉。X射线和光学流量在爆发期间保持相对稳定，缺乏典型瞬变低质量X射线双星爆发的特征。光学数据显示存在偏斜的相位曲线和辐照主导的光变，直到外盘氢复合在约11000K触发爆发末期快速衰减。XMM-Newton和NICER发现早期存在严重遮蔽，软X射线贡献被抑制；含团块状电离吸收体的光谱模型可描述该遮蔽。X射线颜色分析表明，EXO 0748-676在约10个月爆发中大部分时间处于暗弱硬态，末期亮度略有增加，但从未进入软态。

Conclusion: 2024/25爆发表现出未完成态跃迁爆发的特征：系统长期处于暗弱硬态，未进入软态，且爆发持续时间远短于1985年爆发。早期遮蔽的成因仍不确定，但多波段数据共同表明该爆发与典型瞬变低质量X射线双星爆发显著不同。

Abstract: After sixteen years in quiescence, EXO 0748-676 unexpectedly returned to outburst in March 2024. However, in stark contrast to its previous outburst (1985 - 2008), EXO 0748-676 returned to quiescence in January 2025, after ~ 10 months in outburst. Here, we collate data from nine observing facilities, covering wavelengths from radio to X-ray, to provide the first multi-wavelength study of the 2024/25 outburst. Unlike the 1985 outburst, both the rise and decay are fully captured at X-ray and optical wavelengths. The light curves do not exhibit the hallmarks of a canonical transient low-mass X-ray binary outburst. Instead, the X-ray and optical fluxes remain reasonably stable during the 2024/25 outburst. The wealth of optical data of EXO 0748-676 reveals a skewed optical phase curve and an irradiation-dominated optical light curve which lasts until the onset of hydrogen recombination in the outer disc at ~11000K, triggering a rapid fade at the end of the outburst. X-ray observations by XMM-Newton and NICER revealed that EXO 0748-676 was heavily obscured early on, with spectra exhibiting a dampened soft X-ray contribution. We find that a spectral model including a clumpy, ionised absorber describes the obscuration, although the exact cause of the early-time obscuration remains uncertain. X-ray colour analysis indicates that EXO 0748-676 spent most of the ~10 month outburst in a faint, hard state. EXO 0748-676 increased marginally in brightness towards the end of the outburst, but never entered a soft spectral state. This behaviour is characteristic of a failed transition outburst.

</details>


### [8] [X-ray Through Radio Observations and Modeling of the Soft X-ray Flash GRB 250419A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00508)
*Genevieve Schroeder,Anna Y. Q. Ho,Gavin P. Lamb,Tanmoy Laskar,Daniel A. Perley,Ore Gottlieb,Tomas Ahumada,Igor Andreoni,Aleksandra Bochenek,Michael Bremer,Michael Camilo,Jonathan Carney,S. Bradley Cenko,Alessandra Corsi,Michael W. Coughlin,James Freeburn,Erica Hammerstein,Rahul Jayaraman,Mansi M. Kasliwal,Noel Klingler,Brendan O'Connor,Cassie Sevilla,Gokul P. Srinivasaragavan,Robert Stein,Jada L. Vail,Sylvain Veilleux,Lin Yan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对X射线闪（XRF）GRB 250419A开展了X射线、光学与射电（毫米至厘米波段）多波段观测，发现其具有无色散拐折、可能双射电谱成分及异常平缓的光学衰减，可由低动能、高洛伦兹因子喷流（含能量注入或边缘偏离视角）解释，表明部分XRF是经典伽马射线暴向低能端的延伸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

The afterglow is similar to the long-duration gamma-ray burst population, but slightly underluminous, and the optical afterglow has an unusually shallow decay phase at $\lesssim2\,$d. We conclude that at least some X-ray flashes are an extension of classical gamma-ray bursts down to lower energies.

We find that the combined effect of different delay times (t_delay) and observer angles (theta_obs) can produce radio afterglow, as observed for AT2018hyz. The further off-axis from the jet core an observer is, the shorter is the delay inferred between the optical disruption and launching of the jet. This presents a degeneracy between different theta_obs and tdelay. We estimate the sky projection of the jet for these different combinations to break this degeneracy. The spread in sky images ranges from 0.6 mas for a 30 deg off-axis observer to 0.3 mas for an observer perpendicular to the jet core. Future VLBI observations should be able resolve these structures. Thus a combined observation of the light curve and sky images for AT2018hyz (and other jetted TDEs) can be used to narrow down the observer angle and any delay in launching of the underlying jet.

Its properties resemble those of the novae V1494 Aql and V1716 Sco, but not those of the persistent SSS Cal 87. No significant 35-s periodicity is detected.

We conclude that these enigmatic spectra form an intermediate class between the SSe and SSa categories of Ness et al. (2013), together with V1494 Aql and V1716 Sco. Future modelling will be required to determine the relative importance of photoionisation, charge exchange, and other processes in shaping this class of intermediate SSS spectra.

Abstract: Pulsar timing array (PTA) experiments have seen evidence for a nanohertz-frequency gravitational wave background (GWB) through coordinated radio timing observations of millisecond pulsars. We apply this methodology to the NANOGrav source list of 85 pulsars observed with the Green Bank Telescope, Very Large Array, Canadian Hydrogen Intensity Mapping Experiment (CHIME), and the Deep Synoptic Array (DSA), a radio dish array under construction in Nevada. Finally, we solve for the optimal integration time per pulsar, subject to a fixed time budget, that maximizes the GWB signal-to-noise. We show that there is little benefit to optimizing over integration time for these pulsars observed with DSA. Through Monte Carlo realizations of the PTA, we show that the GWB sensitivity improves by $\sim 5\%$ for time-optimized observations compared to the equal-time-per-source scenario with an early-science, fixed source list.

Conclusion: 该RAG流水线可帮助研究人员、编辑和审稿人快速定位领域任意角落，为文献调研提供高效工具。

Abstract: The high-energy and astroparticle physics literature has grown into an enormous body of knowledge. Gaining a comprehensive understanding of this literature is an important step in research, but is a challenging task. Before making meaningful advances, it is important to establish what has already been done and identify open questions. However, this process is becoming increasingly difficult given the sheer volume of publications. Conducting a focused literature survey on a narrow topic is particularly challenging. We develop an open-source Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline to produce concise, targeted summary reports with citations, grounded in the database of arXiv papers, enabling researchers, editors, and referees to rapidly orient themselves within any corner of the field. Specifically, we embed approximately 230K hep-ph and astro-ph.HE papers to enable a hybrid retrieval that captures both keyword matches and semantic similarity. The fused candidate set is then refined using a cross-encoder reranker. Following retrieval, the papers are passed to a Large Language Model (LLM), for which we use Google DeepMind's open-source Gemma-4-E4B model. The LLM first acts as a judge to evaluate the relevance and then synthesizes a coherent and grounded report with references. This modern AI-driven approach allows us to go beyond the capabilities of classical keyword search on arXiv or Inspire-HEP.

</details>

Possible observational manifestations, thermal properties, recombination signatures, and magnetic field effects are discussed. We explicitly distinguish the weak-field, large-radius Newtonian classification used for the outer flow from the strong-field region near the throat, where the dynamics should be treated in a general-relativistic and magnetohydrodynamic framework. We further discuss the specific energy and angular momentum inherited from the inner edge of the accretion flow, the possible modification of these quantities by magnetic stresses and energy-extraction processes, and the effects of an intrinsic dipolar magnetic field of the wormhole.

In this work, we explore an alternative mechanism: the gravitational scattering of compact binaries populating the nuclear region of the host galaxy, by an intermediate-mass black hole or secondary supermassive black hole. We specifically consider populations of neutron star-neutron star (NS-NS), neutron star-black hole (NS-BH), and neutron star-white dwarf (NS-WD) binaries, and analyse the ejection of bound systems from the nuclear region through a gravitational-slingshot effect. We proceed analytically to derive the conditions for binary survival and to parametrise the post-encounter velocity. To quantify the associated merger offsets, we numerically integrate the post-slingshot trajectory of the binary centre-of-mass in host-galaxy potentials that include the primary SMBH, the stellar component, and the dark-matter halo, considering both elliptical-like and spiral-like galaxy profiles. Our parameter sampling shows that NS-NS and NS-WD binaries typically merge farther from the galactic centre than NS-BH systems, with approximately 30-45% of successful ejections merging at three-dimensional distances exceeding 100 kpc; these extreme-offset mergers occur within $\sim$0.1-10 Gyr after ejection. These results motivate the analysed mechanism as a candidate channel for observed extreme-offset transients.

Conclusion: 该策略可在低计数率下给出有竞争力的暗物质衰变限制，并随更多指向观测的累积而进一步收紧，同时提示可能存在尚未解释的谱线特征。

Abstract: Searches for radiative decays of keV-mass dark matter have been limited by energy resolution. We propose a strategy for the XRISM/Resolve low count rate observations, and search the 3.48-3.56 keV band for a decay line from the Milky Way halo. Despite a lower exposure, our bounds are stronger than those from XRISM observations of galaxy clusters. We also identify a residual at E = 3.5311 keV with ~3sigma local significance, coincident with the previously reported unidentified feature, and having no plausible atomic or instrumental counterpart. These bounds will continue to tighten as additional pointings become available.

</details>


### [22] [Stationarity and angular momentum conservation in pulsar spin noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01929)
*Reginald Christian Bernardo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于双组分模型分析脉冲星自旋噪声：在能同时观测壳层与超流两态时，非稳态扩散角动量模型与稳态守恒角动量模型可区分；仅能观测单态时推断依赖于先验与数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星自旋噪声的物理机制尚不明确，其中角动量动力学是否守恒（即自旋噪声是否稳态）缺乏可检验的建模与判别方法。

Method: 构建物理驱动的双组分自旋游走模型：一是总角动量呈扩散的非稳态奇异模型，二是以守恒总角动量为基准的稳态最简模型；并发展可扩展的高斯过程方法进行推断。

Result: 基于模拟数据，当具备全态观测（壳层与超流转动状态的同步独立数据）时，两模型完全可检验且可区分；当仅有一个组分可观测时，稳健推断与预测更困难，且依赖先验与数据质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为检验脉冲星自旋噪声的稳态性提供了可行路径，有望通过连续引力波观测与射电计时的联合实现，但在单态观测情形下结论受限。

Abstract: We present an analysis of pulsar spin noise based on physically-motivated two-component models of spin wandering. We focus on two models, distinguished by their total angular momentum dynamics: a singular, nonstationary model with a diffusive total angular momentum, and a minimal, stationary model anchored on a conserved total angular momentum. We develop scalable Gaussian process methods and, using mock data, show that the two models are fully testable and distinguishable with full-state observations, i.e., simultaneous independent data on the crust and the superfluid rotational states. This paves a path to testing stationarity in pulsar spin noise, potentially achievable with joint continuous gravitational wave observations and radio timing of pulsars. However, robust inferences and predictions are harder to achieve, and depend on the priors and data quality, when only one component is observationally accessible.

</details>


### [23] [Intrinsic versus observation induced spin nonstationarity in pulsar timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01978)
*Reginald Christian Bernardo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文开发了一种高斯过程方法，利用脉冲星计时相位数据直接检验中子星自旋噪声的平稳性，比较单分量非平稳与最小平稳双分量模型，并揭示仅观测地壳时的模型可辨识限度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双分量模型中，中子星自旋噪声是否平稳取决于角动量是内部守恒还是通过地壳与超流体上相互独立的随机力矩与外界交换。射电脉冲星计时只能观测地壳（相位），因此需要直接从相位数据检验平稳性的统计方法。

Method: 从奇异非平稳双分量模型和最小平稳双分量模型的解析相位均值与协方差出发，证明地壳相位协方差具有半可分结构，构造了随观测数线性伸缩的似然函数，并对计时模型进行解析边缘化。对两个模型的模拟相位数据用全状态观测和仅地壳观测进行分析，并微分到局部自旋频率。

Result: 积分、去除计时模型和微分既不产生也不破坏平稳与非平稳自旋噪声的区分，但决定有限含噪数据中能保留多少差异。同时观测两分量时，两模型可被果断区分并恢复生成参数；仅观测地壳时，两模型在大部分参数空间上拟合同样好，贝叶斯因子量级为1且受先验限制。

Conclusion: 最小模型通过由超流体惯性分数设定的单一因子，将隐藏的超流体相位与观测地壳相位联系起来；若由glitch独立估计该分数，则可形成可证伪预言，由未来连续引力波对内部的观测检验。

Abstract: Whether spin noise of a neutron star is stationary is, in two-component models, a question of whether stellar angular momentum is conserved internally or exchanged externally through independent stochastic torques on the crust and superfluid. The question is posed in the two components' angular velocities, but radio pulsar timing observes only the crust, through its rotational phase after a deterministic timing model is fitted. We develop a Gaussian process method for testing spin noise stationarity directly with such phase data. Starting from the exact analytical phase means and covariances of a singular, nonstationary and a minimal, stationary two-component model, we show that the crust phase covariances are semiseparable and construct a likelihood that scales linearly with the number of observations, with the timing model marginalized analytically. We analyze simulated phase data from both models using full-state and crust-only observations, and after differentiation into local spin frequencies. Integration, timing model removal and differentiation neither create nor destroy the distinction between stationary and nonstationary spin noise, but determine how much survives in a finite, noisy data set. With both components observed, the models are distinguished decisively and the generating parameters are recovered. With the crust alone, both models describe the observed phase equally well over much of parameter space, and the Bayes factors are of order unity and prior-limited. The minimal model nonetheless ties the hidden superfluid phase to the observed crust phase through a single factor set by the superfluid inertia fraction, so an independent estimate of this fraction, as from glitches, turns it into a falsifiable prediction that a continuous gravitational wave observation of the interior could test.

</details>


### [24] [The Impact of a Clumpy Ambient Medium on the Dynamics and Synchrotron Emission of AGN Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01983)
*Ivan Almeida,Christian Fendt,Bhargav Vaidya*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用3D相对论磁流体模拟研究AGN喷流在clumpy ISM中的早期演化，发现clumpy介质显著增强质量负载与同步辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGN相对论喷流预期穿过非均匀的ISM，但小尺度气体不均匀性在塑造其早期演化中的作用仍不明确。

Method: 使用PLUTO进行3D狭义相对论磁流体模拟，跟踪洛伦兹因子为10的pc尺度喷流。对比均匀介质与两个clumpy介质模型（云体积填充因子分别为0.1%和1%）。基于拉格朗日宏观粒子计算扩散激波加速产生的同步辐射图谱与光谱。

Result: 喷流-云相互作用使流动偏转、增强激波形成、产生比均匀ISM更不对称的cocoon。夹带质量从均匀情况的0.43 M☉增至clumpy情况的5.3和8.8 M☉。clumpy ISM还改变湍流与cocoon内物质混合。动力学差异导致更亮且更不规则的同步辐射；相同注入喷流下，clumpy模型产生更强的频率积分同步辐射，SED差异最大出现在亚毫米到红外/光学波段。

Conclusion: 纳入小尺度ISM结构能更完整地描述年轻AGN喷流的早期动力学演化及其辐射特性。

Abstract: Relativistic jets from AGN are expected to propagate through an inhomogeneous ISM, but the role of small-scale gas inhomogeneities in shaping their early evolution remains uncertain. We investigate this problem using 3D special-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations with PLUTO, following a jet with a Lorentz factor of 10 propagating through a pc-scale medium. We compare a homogeneous ambient medium with two clumpy ambient-medium models, motivated by dense gas in the nuclear ISM, in which clouds occupy volume filling factors of $0.1$ and $1$ per cent. We compute synthetic synchrotron maps and spectra from Lagrangian macro-particles that have been accelerated by diffusive shock acceleration. Jet-cloud interactions deflect the flow, enhance shock formation, and produce a more asymmetric cocoon than in the homogeneous ISM case. Altogether, this results in a substantially stronger mass loading: the entrained mass increases from $0.43$ M$_{\odot}$ in the homogeneous run to $5.3$ and $8.8$ M$_{\odot}$ in the clumpy runs. The clumpy ISM also modifies the turbulence and the material mixing within the jet cocoon. The dynamical differences we find translate into brighter and more irregular synchrotron emission. For the same injected jet, the models with a clumpy medium produce stronger frequency-integrated synchrotron emission than the homogeneous run, with the largest differences in the SED occurring from the sub-mm to the infrared/optical bands. Our results demonstrate that including a small-scale ISM structure provides a more complete description of the early dynamical evolution of young AGN jets and their radiative properties.

</details>

### [25] [A multi-scale immuno-epidemiological behavioral model for influenza-like illness: connecting scales through symptom scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00862)
*Binod Pant,Summer Atkins,Necibe Tuncer,Hana M. Dobrovolny*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型多尺度模型，让有症状个体的传播行为由系统症状评分（受宿主体内免疫动态驱动）动态调节，而非由群体层面病例数驱动。结果表明，更强的疾病驱动行为改变会推迟并降低流行高峰，且疫情轨迹在多种连接函数下仍与标准SEIR模型定性相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多传染病中，行为改变并非群体对病例数上升的反应，而是个体基于自身病情感知触发的个人反应。然而多数模型未能刻画疾病严重程度如何驱动这一反应。现有大多数多尺度模型通过病毒载量的连接函数将宿主体内病毒载量与群体传播率关联，但病毒载量常与症状严重程度不一致，可能不适合用于在群体层面指导行为。

Method: 构建一个新型多尺度模型：有症状个体依据系统症状评分（由肌肉酸痛、疲劳、头痛、发热感构成的综合指标，且受宿主体内免疫动态影响）动态调整其传播率，无症状个体不改变行为。模型在多种连接两尺度的连接函数选择下进行模拟，并与标准SEAR/SEIR模型及由群体层面反馈（如报告病例或死亡数）驱动的行为改变模型进行对比。

Result: 更强的疾病驱动行为改变会推迟并降低流行高峰。在多种不同的连接函数下，所得疫情轨迹与不含行为改变的标准SEIR模型在定性上保持相似。这与由群体层面反馈驱动的行为改变模型形成对比，后者可产生平台期、肩峰或疫情下降期延长等定性上不同的特征。当采用症状评分的指数函数作为连接函数时，标准SEIR模型能够较好地复现多尺度模型在给定时间点的总感染人数。

Conclusion: 以症状严重程度（而非病毒载量或群体病例反馈）驱动行为改变的多尺度建模，能更真实地反映个体病情感知对传播的影响；其疫情轨迹与标准SEIR模型定性一致，且在特定连接函数下可被标准SEIR近似复现，为融合免疫与流行病尺度的建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: For many infectious diseases, behavior change is not a population-level reaction to rising case counts, but a personal one, triggered by how sick an individual feels. Yet most models fail to capture how illness severity drives this response. Multi-scale models that link the immunological scale to the epidemiological scale seem well-suited for capturing this behavior. Most multi-scale models link within-host viral load to population-level transmission rates through linking functions that are a function of viral load. However, viral load is often misaligned with symptom severity and thus possibly ill-suited for the purpose of informing behavior at a population level. We present a novel multi-scale model in which symptomatic individuals dynamically modify their transmission based on systemic symptom scores (a composite measure of muscle ache, fatigue, headache, and feverishness informed by within-host immune dynamics) while asymptomatic individuals do not change behavior. We find that stronger illness-driven behavior change delays and lowers the epidemic peak. Moreover, the resulting epidemic trajectory remains qualitatively similar to that of a standard SEIR model without behavior change, across several distinct choices of linking functions connecting the two scales. This stands in contrast to models where behavior change is driven by population-level feedback (e.g., reported cases or deaths), which can produce qualitatively distinct signatures such as plateaus, shoulders, or an elongated epidemic decline. In the case when an exponential function of symptom score is used as a linking function, we show that the standard SEIR model can closely reproduce the total number of infectious individuals at a given point in time generated through the multi-scale level.

</details>

### [26] [Bounded-Fidelity Sim-as-Demo-Stage: Mocap Handoff for Governance Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00008)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"有界保真度的 sim-as-demo-stage"设计模式：在 LLM 驱动机器人的治理基准测试中，通过在同一手递手（抓取/携带/放置）环节内显式抑制接触物理，消除与治理性质无关的审计链分歧。使用 MuJoCo mocap-body 与 220 行 Python 适配器实现，1000 次重放全部字节一致（mocap 组 1000/1000，接触力基线 993/1000 发散），且鲁棒于时间步长与包络边缘抖动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sim-to-real 研究通常以物理保真度为首要目标，但用于 LLM 驱动机器人治理基准测试时，接触保真度反而成为负担：物体交接处的接触力积分噪声会引入与所测治理属性结构无关的审计链分歧，破坏基准的可复现性。因此需要一种在交接包络内抑制接触物理、其余部分保留完整动力学的替代方案。

Method: 提出"有界保真度的 sim-as-demo-stage"设计模式，在显式括起的手递手包络（grasp/carry/place）内抑制接触物理，其余处保留完整动力学。实现上使用 MuJoCo 的 mocap-body 原语，由 220 行 Python 适配器驱动，治理桥通过结构化意图（structured intents）调用。将审计链稳定性形式化为重放间哈希事件日志的字节相等，并识别出两个可推出该稳定性的结构包络性质。

Result: 每种姿态 N=1000 次重放：mocap 变体仅产生 1 个不同审计链哈希（1000/1000 字节一致；Wilson 95% CI [0.997, 1.000]）；接触力基线产生 584 个不同哈希（993/1000 发散；CI [0.987, 0.998]）。时间步长扫描（1、2、5、10 ms）显示分歧率为结构性而非调参假象，各步长下稳定在 0.985。包络边缘时序抖动（±10 仿真步，1400 次重放）零分歧；审计链在 K∈{1,2,3} 个顺序交接物体下均保持字节相等（1500 次重放），每次取放的额外开销呈亚线性。

Conclusion: 该模式使基准设计者以近零工程成本获得审计链可复现性，且分歧是结构性的，不受时间步长与边缘抖动影响，并具良好可扩展性。论文同时划定了其有害适用边界（sim-to-real 验证、策略训练、接触丰富任务），以避免误用。

Abstract: Sim-to-real research pursues physics fidelity as a primary objective: simulators are judged by how closely they reproduce real-world contact dynamics. For governance benchmarking of LLM-driven robots, where the simulator demonstrates that an admission/policy/contract/audit pipeline behaves correctly, contact fidelity at object handoffs (grasp, carry, place) becomes a liability: contact-force integration noise injects audit-chain divergence that is structurally unrelated to the governance property under test. We propose bounded-fidelity sim-as-demo-stage, a design pattern that suppresses contact physics within explicitly bracketed handoff envelopes while preserving full dynamics elsewhere. The construction uses MuJoCo's mocap-body primitive driven by a 220-line Python adapter that the governance bridge invokes via structured intents. We formalise audit-chain stability as byte-equality of the hashed event log across replays and identify two structural envelope properties that imply it. Across N=1000 replays per posture, the mocap variant produces one distinct audit-chain hash (1000/1000 byte-identical; Wilson 95% CI [0.997, 1.000]); the contact-force baseline produces 584 distinct hashes (993/1000 diverged; CI [0.987, 0.998]). A timestep sweep (1, 2, 5, 10 ms) shows the divergence is structural, not a tuning artefact: it stays at 0.985 at every timestep. Envelope-edge timing jitter (+/-10 simulation steps, 1,400 replays) produces 0 divergence, and audit chains remain byte-equal across K in {1, 2, 3} sequentially handed-off objects (1,500 replays) with sub-linear per-pick-and-place overhead. The pattern gives benchmark designers audit-chain reproducibility at near-zero engineering cost; we also map where it is harmful (sim-to-real validation, policy training, contact-rich tasks) so it is not mis-deployed.

</details>


### [27] [Multi-Reference Path Tracking Control for an Agricultural Tractor with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00057)
*Marcel Moll,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）的拖拉机路径跟踪控制器，将分段线性参考路径的多段直接纳入目标函数，并给出可行参考段的选择方法。经田间试验验证，求解器平均3.45 ms收敛，弯曲路径跟踪的平均绝对横向偏差为6.1 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导拖拉机沿预定参考路径行驶是精准农业的关键环节，需要一种能够直接在优化问题中处理分段线性路径、并具备实时性的路径跟踪控制方法。

Method: 构建基于非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）的路径跟踪控制器，将分段线性参考路径的多段直接嵌入目标函数；同时提出从完整路径中选取可用参考段的方法；通过配备Tractor Implement Management转向接口的拖拉机开展田间试验进行评估。

Result: NMPC求解器平均在3.45 ms内收敛；对弯曲参考路径的跟踪平均绝对横向偏差（cross-track error）为6.1 cm。

Conclusion: 该NMPC控制器具备良好的实时性与跟踪精度，能够有效实现拖拉机的分段线性路径跟踪，适用于精准农业场景。

Abstract: Guiding a tractor along a predefined reference path is a key component of precision agriculture. This study develops a path tracking controller based on Nonlinear Model Predictive Control, which incorporates multiple segments of a piecewise-linear reference path directly into the objective function. In addition, methods for selecting viable reference segments from the full path are presented. The control system is evaluated during a field test with a tractor controlled via the Tractor Implement Management steering interface. The NMPC solver converged on average after 3.45 ms and tracked the curved reference path with a mean absolute cross-track error of 6.1 cm.

</details>


### [28] [Probabilistic Plan Legibility with Off-the-shelf Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00065)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向任意PDDL领域的可读性规划方法，通过二阶心智理论估计观察者视角，将可读性研究扩展到经典规划，并验证了可读性与效率之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可读性规划旨在从观察者视角出发，生成能最大限度区分目标与候选目标的规划。已有可读性研究难以直接应用于经典规划，且往往需要构建专门（ad-hoc）的规划器，限制了方法的通用性。

Method: 将先前的可读性研究扩展到经典规划，提出适用于任意PDDL领域的可读性规划方法，无需构建专门的规划器；并提出通过二阶心智理论（second order theory of mind）连接规划者与观察者的任务空间，以估计观察者视角。

Result: 在多个PDDL规划领域进行基准测试。结果表明，规划可读性与规划效率之间总体呈权衡关系；但并非所有规划领域都能以相同方式提升可读性；实验证明需要一个正则化因子来平衡可读性与效率。

Conclusion: 可读性规划可应用于人-机器人协作场景，使自主机器人通过生成可读性规划隐式传达其目标；但需引入正则化因子在不同领域间权衡可读性与效率。

Abstract: Legible planning is the creation of plans that best disambiguate their goals from a set of other candidates from an observer's perspective. In this paper we propose a method for legible planning for arbitrary PDDL domains, by extending previous research on legibility to classical planning without requiring to construct ad-hoc planners. We also discuss how the observer perspective may be estimated through a second order theory of mind that connects the planner's and the observer's task spaces. Our solution can for example be deployed in human-robot teaming scenarios, where an autonomous robot in a team can implicitly communicate its goal by producing legible plans. We present benchmark results on several PDDL planning domains. Our results generally show that plan legibility is a trade-off with plan efficiency, however, not all planning domains allows to increase legibility in the same way and a regularizing factor to balance legibility and efficiency was proved necessary.

</details>


### [29] [HumanoidTTT: Test-Time Capability Reuse for Efficient Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00198)
*Jingtai Yang,Yining Wu,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumanoidTTT框架，实现连续人形控制中的测试时能力复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在持续部署中会反复请求相同运动能力，但介入动作会改变机器人进入状态，导致已验证动作无法盲目重放；同时已验证能力不断累积，而存储有界，需决定保留哪些能力。

Method: 提出HumanoidTTT框架，包含Selective Full-Motion Reuse（仅从经认证的适用进入状态直接复用已验证的完整动作，绕过重新生成）与Test-Time Capability Consolidation（以后续部署复用为反馈，在有界的Full-Motion Store中自适应决定哪些合格能力的持久化）。

Result: 实验显示零不安全接受，端到端相比重新生成加速16.4倍；在线整合相比冻结版本每200次请求多避免13.2次生成器调用。

Conclusion: HumanoidTTT实现了已验证运动能力的可靠高效复用，并在持续部署中自适应保留有用能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in motion generation and whole-body tracking have enabled humanoid robots to execute increasingly diverse motions, yet the same motion capabilities may be requested repeatedly during continual deployment. Reliable reuse is challenging because intervening motions can change the robot's entry state, making previously successful motions unsafe to replay blindly. Meanwhile, validated capabilities accumulate during deployment, while bounded storage requires deciding which ones are worth retaining. To address these challenges, we present HumanoidTTT, a framework for test-time capability reuse in continual humanoid control. Specifically, we introduce Selective Full-Motion Reuse, which authorizes direct reuse of validated complete motions only from certified applicable entry states, allowing accepted reuse to bypass fresh generation. We further introduce Test-Time Capability Consolidation, which adapts which qualified capabilities persist in a bounded Full-Motion Store using subsequent deployment reuse as feedback. Experiments demonstrate zero unsafe accepts and a 16.4$\times$ end-to-end speedup over fresh generation, while online consolidation improves avoided generator calls by 13.2 per 200 requests over its frozen counterpart. Overall, HumanoidTTT enables reliable and efficient reuse of validated motion capabilities while adaptively retaining useful capabilities throughout continual deployment. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/HumanoidTTT. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/HumanoidTTT.

</details>


### [30] [Real-Time Whole-Body Safe Motion Generation for Multi-Segment Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00220)
*Fangju Yang,Siyi Ma,Tonghao Guan,Tingcong Liu,Hang Yang,Zhengqiang Zhang,Jian S. Dai,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向平面多段肌腱驱动连续体机器人的统一驱动空间框架，结合能量法变曲率建模与多点CBF-QP，实现实时全身无碰撞运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动连续体机器人的实时运动生成需要精确刻画非均匀弯曲并实现全身碰撞避免，现有方法难以同时兼顾建模精度与实时安全性。

Method: 构建能量法变曲率模型以捕捉空间变化的肌腱间距与弯曲刚度，并推导解析雅可比用于微分逆运动学与安全监控；引入多点CBF-QP在障碍运动与驱动速度约束下保证主干间隙，其决策维度仅取决于独立驱动段数。

Result: 模型与GVS参考高度吻合，最大曲率误差为5.223×10⁻² m⁻¹；在100次MuJoCo试验中，静态与动态场景下的无碰撞成功率分别达96%与100%（无CBF约束时约为60%与80%）；穿洞测试验证了受限环境中的安全运动；在600个主干监测点下平均控制步耗时6.66 ms。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在保证建模精度的同时实现实时全身安全运动生成，适用于受限与动态环境中的连续体机器人控制。

Abstract: Real-time motion generation for tendon-driven continuum robots requires accurate modeling of nonuniform bending and whole-body collision avoidance. This paper presents a unified actuation-space framework for planar multi-segment tendon-driven continuum robots. An energy-based variable-curvature model captures spatially varying tendon spacing and bending stiffness and provides analytical Jacobians for differential inverse kinematics and safety monitoring. A multipoint CBF-QP enforces backbone clearance under obstacle motion and actuation-velocity bounds, while its decision dimension depends only on the number of independently actuated segments. The model closely agrees with GVS references, with a maximum curvature error of $5.223 \times 10^{-2}\,\mathrm{m}^{-1}$. Over 100 MuJoCo trials, the proposed method achieves collision-free success rates of 96% and 100% in static and dynamic scenarios, respectively, compared with approximately 60% and 80% without CBF constraints. Hole-traversal tests further demonstrate safe motion in constrained environments. With 600 backbone monitoring points, the mean control-step time is 6.66 ms, demonstrating real-time whole-body safe motion generation.

</details>


### [31] [DriftOPD: Sequence-Level Reverse-KL Distillation for One-Step VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00317)
*Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Sunwoo Park,Jangho Park,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DriftOPD，一种无需教师、无需rollout的序列级on-policy蒸馏框架，仅用离线数据和一步动作生成即可实现VLA动作专家的长时域优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型的动作专家在滚动时域控制下生成短动作块，chunk级训练只优化局部动作似然，未显式考虑长时域任务成功率；而序列级强化学习虽可解决该问题，却依赖策略rollout与闭环交互，对真实机器人操作代价高昂。

Method: 将序列级反向KL散度分解为chunk级反向KL项与刻画当前动作长时域效应的未来势项；前者用一步drifting目标优化，后者用从离线示教中学得的Q函数critic优化，从而仅凭离线数据与一步动作生成实现序列级优化。

Result: 在多种VLA架构的仿真与真实世界操作任务中，DriftOPD总体优于现有一步蒸馏基线，任务成功率可与多步教师策略相当。

Conclusion: 长时域行为可被有效蒸馏进一步VLA动作专家，无需在线交互或独立教师。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly rely on action experts that generate short action chunks under receding-horizon control. While chunk-level training is convenient across robot embodiments, it optimizes local action likelihood without explicitly accounting for long-horizon task success. Sequence-level reinforcement learning can address this limitation, but typically requires policy rollouts and closed-loop interaction, which are costly for real-robot manipulation. We introduce DriftOPD, a teacher-free, rollout-free framework for sequence-level on-policy distillation of continuous VLA action experts. We show that the sequence-level reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence decomposes into a chunk-level reverse-KL term and a future-potential term that captures the long-horizon effect of the current action. DriftOPD optimizes these two terms using a one-step drifting objective and a Q-function critic learned from offline demonstrations, respectively, enabling sequence-level optimization with only offline data and one-step action generation. Across multiple VLA architectures in simulation and real-world manipulation, DriftOPD generally outperforms existing one-step distillation baselines while achieving task success performance comparable to multi-step teacher policies. These results demonstrate that long-horizon behavior can be effectively distilled into one-step VLA action experts without online interaction or a separate teacher.

</details>


### [32] [Retrospective Open-Vocabulary Memory for Long-Term Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00330)
*Jiaming Wang,Zhiwei Xue,Chen Jizhuo,Peng Shiqi,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ECROM，将长期开放词汇记忆建模为删失观测下的概率推断，按"观测机会"加权证据，实现长期物体流行度估计与主动搜索先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期物体搜索需要从对变化环境的重复但不均匀观测中学习物体通常出现的位置。现有记忆方法未能正确处理观测机会不均等（未看见不等于不存在）的问题，且无法应对仅在查询时才指定的开放词汇概念。

Method: 将回顾式开放词汇记忆形式化为删失观测（censored observations）下的概率推断：核心思想是对每个观测机会推理证据——一次检测或未检测只应按机器人观测该位置的"机会大小"成比例地影响信念。基于该原则提出ECROM，用于估计仅在查询时给定概念的长期流行度（prevalence），并将所得信念直接转化为主动搜索的搜索先验。

Result: 在十个HM3D家庭中构建受控长期基准，可独立改变物体放置与重复遍历中的观测机会。ECROM在留出查询上较最强竞争记忆方法提升support-level AP 4.5个点，搜索SPL提升4.2个点。

Conclusion: 按观测机会加权的概率推断能有效解决非均匀观测下的长期开放词汇记忆问题，并直接服务于主动搜索；基准、数据集与代码将开源。

Abstract: Long-term object search requires learning where objects usually appear from repeated but uneven observations of a changing environment. We formulate retrospective open-vocabulary memory as probabilistic inference from censored observations, where the key idea is to reason with evidence per opportunity: a detection or non-detection should influence belief only in proportion to the robot's opportunity to observe the corresponding location. We introduce ECROM, which uses this principle to estimate long-term prevalence for concepts specified only at query time and converts the resulting belief directly into an active-search prior. To evaluate this problem, we introduce a controlled long-term benchmark in ten HM3D homes that independently varies object placement and observation opportunity across repeated traversals. ECROM improves support-level AP on held-out queries by 4.5 points and search SPL by 4.2 points over the strongest competing memory in each metric. The benchmark, dataset, and code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [33] [IndoorBEV: A Lightweight Real-Time LiDAR BEV Perception System for Indoor Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00355)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IndoorBEV：一种轻量级室内LiDAR感知框架，通过高度感知BEV表示与几何条件特征融合，在精度、延迟与内存之间取得良好折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在严格延迟与内存约束下需理解杂乱三维环境；现有point/voxel方法计算开销大，而传统BEV虽高效却丢失垂直几何信息。

Method: 用统计高度特征与多频高度编码汇总每个BEV单元的垂直点分布；轻量编码器融合几何特征、多尺度局部表示与紧凑全局场景上下文；解耦稠密预测头同时输出语义BEV图与有向目标框。

Result: 仅0.6M参数、2.3MB存储；AGX Orin上单次推理21.52MB GPU内存，200ms截止期下平均延迟169.6ms，超期率1.8%；在仿真场景、真实机器人扫描及开源室内点云数据集上表现良好折中。

Conclusion: 在紧凑BEV表示中显式编码垂直几何，是实现资源高效室内LiDAR感知的有效途径。

Abstract: Efficient indoor LiDAR perception is challenging because mobile robots must understand cluttered three-dimensional environments under strict latency and memory constraints. Existing point-based and voxel-based methods often incur substantial computational overhead, whereas conventional bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations improve efficiency at the cost of discarding vertical geometric information. We present IndoorBEV, a lightweight LiDAR perception framework that mitigates this tradeoff through a height-aware BEV representation and geometry-conditioned feature fusion. IndoorBEV summarizes the vertical point distribution in each BEV cell using statistical height features and multi-frequency height encoding, allowing informative three-dimensional cues to be processed efficiently by two-dimensional convolutions. A lightweight encoder then integrates complementary geometric features with multi-scale local representations and compact global scene context. Decoupled dense prediction heads jointly produce semantic BEV maps and oriented object bounding boxes. IndoorBEV contains only 0.6M parameters and requires 2.3 MB of model storage. On an NVIDIA AGX Orin, it uses 21.52 MB of GPU memory per inference and achieves a mean latency of 169.6 ms under a 200 ms perception deadline, with a deadline miss ratio of 1.8\%. Evaluations on simulated scenes, real-world robot scans, and an open-source indoor point-cloud dataset demonstrate a favorable tradeoff among perception accuracy, latency, and memory consumption. These results indicate that explicitly encoding vertical geometry within a compact BEV representation provides an effective approach to resource-efficient indoor LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [34] [DexPolicy: Scheduled Exploration for Trajectory-Guided Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00360)
*Haoyu Wang,Siyuan Qian,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexPolicy将探索噪声尺度显式设为训练步数的退火函数，在灵巧手操作中显著提升目标成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作RL需先发现手-物接触再精确控制物体，但服务于前者的动作噪声会干扰后者；在ViViDex类轨迹引导设定中，基线PPO运行5M步后噪声仍停在初始水平，说明需要显式控制探索尺度。

Method: DexPolicy将探索尺度设为训练步数的显式函数，从宽探索退火至窄探索，同时固定损失、架构、奖励与优化器设置。研究三种策略优化设定：PPO、无critic的GRPO续训、流参数化PPO变体（FPO）。

Result: 五个YCB物体、三个种子下，平均确定性Target成功率：FPO 49.4%→68.1%，GRPO 14.1%→45.4%，PPO 32.0%→35.7%。RealMan RM75+Inspire/RH56手上，三个物体共360次试验：FPO 25.0%→85.0%，GRPO 10.0%→63.3%，PPO 8.3%→43.3%。PPO组件筛选显示噪声控制优于所测优化器收缩；所选PPO调度在三个物体上平均Target成功率高于同端点的线性衰减。

Conclusion: 训练回报、确定性Target成功率与执行噪声容忍度三者解耦；调度应以目标执行条件下的最终任务成功率来评判，且需按任务与策略优化设定分别评估。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for dexterous manipulation must discover finger-object contacts and then control the object precisely; the action noise that serves the first goal can interfere with the second. In trajectory-guided settings such as ViViDex, where RL refine hand-object trajectories from human video, our baseline PPO runs end near their initial action noise after 5M steps, motivating explicit control of exploration scale. DexPolicy makes that scale an explicit function of training steps, annealing from broad to narrow exploration while holding loss, architecture, reward, and optimizer settings fixed. We study three policy-optimization settings: PPO, critic-free GRPO continuation, and a flow-parameterized PPO variant (FPO). Across five YCB objects and three training seeds, mean deterministic Target success rises from 49.4% to 68.1% (FPO), 14.1% to 45.4% (GRPO), and 32.0% to 35.7% (PPO). On a RealMan RM75 arm with an Inspire/RH56 hand, 360 trials over three objects raise mean Target success from 25.0% to 85.0% (FPO), 10.0% to 63.3% (GRPO), and 8.3% to 43.3% (PPO), with one trained model per object-method condition. PPO component screening favors noise control over the tested optimizer contraction; the selected PPO schedule yields higher mean Target success than linear decay with the same endpoints on three tested objects. Training return, deterministic Target success, and tolerance to execution noise dissociate; schedules should therefore be judged by terminal task success under the intended execution conditions, per task and policy-optimization setting. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/DexPolicy. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/DexPolicy.

</details>


### [35] [DeepJEPA: Scaling World Models from Within](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00368)
*Zijian Jin,Yunbei Zhang,Yuanzhe Liu,Ming Liu,Baian Chen,Weirui Ye,Shilong Liu,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeepJEPA 将世界模型中的循环更新深度视为测试时的内在扩展轴，按需为每个候选与 rollout 步骤分配计算，在决策关键转移处加深思考，从而以极少额外计算（平均每转移 1.00–1.26 次更新）取得优于或匹敌固定深度规划器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型规划器向外扩展（rollout 更远、采样更多轨迹、优化更久），但对每次想象转移赋予相同计算量。作者指出这种"均匀加深每个转移"的方式浪费计算，且可能损害规划，因为有用的细化集中在少数决策关键事件上。

Method: 提出 DeepJEPA：一种权重共享（weight-tied）的联合嵌入预测世界模型，将转移深度作为测试时的内在扩展轴，学习判断对每个候选与每个 rollout 步骤是否值得再执行一次循环更新（即何时进入额外深度）。

Result: 在五个视觉控制设置中，DeepJEPA 优于或匹配最强固定深度规划器，而平均每转移仅需 1.00–1.26 次更新；额外计算集中在接触起始与持续物体交互阶段，此时潜空间修正可改变进入规划器精英集的候选及最终动作选择。表征探测进一步表明，规划性能提升并不依赖物体状态解码能力的一致提升。

Conclusion: DeepJEPA 将世界模型扩展重新定义为"在能改变规划决策之处分配内部计算"的问题：在决策关键转移处加深思考，而非让每条 rollout 均匀变深或变长。

Abstract: World-model planners typically scale outward by rolling farther, sampling more trajectories, or optimizing longer, while assigning the same computation to every imagined transition. We show that making every transition uniformly deeper wastes computation and can degrade planning because useful refinement is concentrated at a small set of decision-critical events. We introduce DeepJEPA, a weight-tied joint-embedding predictive world model that treats transition depth as an inner test-time scaling axis and learns when another recurrent update is worth computing for each candidate and rollout step. Across five visual-control settings, DeepJEPA improves or matches the strongest fixed-depth planner while averaging only 1.00-1.26 updates per transition. Its additional computation concentrates at contact onset and sustained object interaction, where latent corrections can change which candidates enter the planner's elite set and which action is selected. Representation probes further show that improved planning does not require uniformly better object-state decodability. DeepJEPA therefore reframes world-model scaling as a problem of allocating internal computation where it can change the planner's decision: think deeper at decision-critical transitions instead of making every rollout uniformly deeper or longer.

</details>


### [36] [Whole-Body Aerial Grasping and Lifting via Partial Visual Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00404)
*Jiaye Jin,Rui Jin,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Yi Wang,Jiayan Zhao,Kun Cao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出循环教师-学生框架，单一策略联合控制飞行、机械臂与夹爪闭合，学生用双视角点云与本体感知实现闭环抓取提升，仿真成功率最高99.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中抓取-提升任务需在部分观测下完成接近、捕获与提升的全身体协调。训练早期接近阶段的失败会减少后阶段样本暴露；执行中可见性变化又使对准与闭合时机难以把握。目标是消除显式任务阶段输入，并解决阶段衔接与闭合时机问题。

Method: 采用循环教师-学生框架：特权教师通过强化学习训练，并配以关键状态课程，先暴露捕获与提升状态再与正常接近轨迹衔接；将教师行为蒸馏为循环视觉学生，用双视角点云与本体感知替代特权目标状态，并整合观测历史进行闭环控制。另设专门闭合目标，基于持续"就绪"序列监督闭合时机。仿真采用条件触发锁存、虚拟附着与基于力旋量的负载加载模型。

Result: 在8,996个完成的仿真回合中，冻结学生策略在标称、物理/控制随机化、以及额外相机随机化条件下的全任务成功率分别为99.97%、97.14%和95.84%；标称条件下锁存计数加权的每种子90百分位捕获对准误差均值为8.12 mm。

Conclusion: 关键状态课程与教师-学生蒸馏可在无显式阶段输入下实现稳健的全身体空中抓取-提升，闭环视觉学生具备对物理、控制与相机随机化的泛化能力，闭合时机可由就绪序列有效监督。

Abstract: Aerial grasp-and-lift tasks require whole-body coordination across approach, acquisition, and lifting under partial target observations. Early approach failures can limit exposure to later task stages during training, while changing visibility complicates alignment and closure timing during execution. We present a recurrent teacher-student framework that learns a single policy in simulation to jointly command flight, arm motion, and gripper closure without an explicit task-phase input. A privileged teacher learns through reinforcement learning with a critical-state curriculum that exposes acquisition and lifting states before connecting them to normal approach trajectories. Its behavior is distilled into a recurrent visual student that replaces privileged target states with dual-view point clouds and proprioception, integrating observation history for closed-loop control. A dedicated closure objective supervises closure timing from sustained model-defined readiness sequences. Training and primary evaluation use a simulated acquisition-and-payload model with condition-triggered latching, virtual attachment, and wrench-based payload loading for short-distance lifting. Across 8,996 completed simulation episodes under this model, the frozen student achieves full-task success rates of 99.97%, 97.14%, and 95.84% under nominal, physics/control-randomized, and additional camera-randomized conditions, respectively. The nominal latch-count-weighted mean of per-seed 90th-percentile alignment errors at acquisition is 8.12 mm.

</details>


### [37] [Towards a General Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Model via Egocentric Whole-Body Human Data Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00438)
*Chongyang Xu,Zhao Wu,Jin Chen,Yiming Jiang,Jinhui Ye,Yuming Jiang,Shifeng Zhang,Ziliang Feng,Mu Xu,Yilun Chen,Li Lu,Steven C. H. Hoi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出500小时人类全身移动操作数据集HumanVerse-500及三阶段训练的人形全身视觉-语言-动作策略λ₀，实现人类经验向人形机器人全身控制的规模化迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形全身操作虽进展迅速，但现有从第一视角人类视频获得的监督对全身运动及手-物交互协调的覆盖有限，而通过人形遥操作获取此类监督成本高、难以规模化。因此作者探索如何利用人类经验支持人形移动操作的规模化学习。

Method: 构建HumanVerse-500数据集：500小时开放世界环境下多样化人类移动操作行为，采用轻量可穿戴系统同步第一视角视频与身体、手部运动。基于该数据集开发λ₀全身人形VLA策略，采用三阶段训练：先学习多样化第一视角数据集的交互，再用HumanVerse-500协调身体与手部运动，最后适配下游任务与机器人本体。跨阶段学习共享表征空间实现人类经验迁移，用领域特定接口处理人机状态与动作差异。

Result: 在SIMPLE基准和4个真实世界移动操作任务上评估λ₀，取得最先进性能，并进一步分析其规模化行为、泛化能力及各训练阶段的贡献。

Conclusion: 人类数据可有效支持下游人形全身控制，所提数据集、策略与训练范式为该方向提供可行路径，作者将开源代码、模型与数据以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body manipulation has advanced rapidly, enabling policies to coordinate locomotion, posture, bimanual interaction, and dexterous hand movements. Meanwhile, egocentric human videos provide diverse examples of everyday interactions across objects and scenes, offering scalable supervision without robot operation. However, existing supervision from these videos provides limited coverage of whole-body movement and coordination with hand-object interaction, while obtaining such supervision through humanoid teleoperation is also costly and difficult to scale. We therefore explore how human experience can support scalable learning of humanoid loco-manipulation. To support this study, we introduce HumanVerse-500, a 500-hour dataset of diverse human loco-manipulation behaviors in open-world environments, collected with a lightweight wearable system that synchronizes egocentric video with body and hand motion. Building on this dataset, we develop $λ_0$, a whole-body humanoid vision-language-action policy, through three-stage training that first learns interaction from diverse egocentric datasets, then coordinates body and hand motion using HumanVerse-500, and finally adapts the policy to downstream tasks and robot embodiments. Across these stages, $λ_0$ learns a shared representation space for human experience transfer, while domain-specific interfaces handle differences between human and robot states and actions. We evaluate $λ_0$ on SIMPLE and 4 real-world loco-manipulation tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance, and further analyze its scaling behavior, generalization, and training-stage contributions to understand how human data support downstream whole-body humanoid control. We will release our code, models, and data to support further research.

</details>


### [38] [ScaffoldM3C: A Multimodal Sequential Monte Carlo Framework for Generative Stable Construction Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00487)
*Gadiel Sznaier Camps,Chengyang He,Guillaume Sartoretti,Eduardo Montijano,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出ScaffoldM3C，将积木式3D结构构建建模为概率式下一块生成任务，引入多模态条件与脚手架辅助token，并结合序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）维护多条装配序列，实现更小、更快、更稳定的自治构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自治构建物理可实现的3D结构面临组合动作空间、可互换组件、等终态装配序列以及构建过程严格稳定性要求等挑战。现有基于微调大语言模型的文本生成式构建方法不支持多模态（文本、图像、草图）条件，忽视脚手架对中间结构稳定化的实际作用，且推理速度慢。

Method: 将构建形式化为具有多个潜在装配动作和多种任务条件模态的概率式下一块生成任务；显式引入辅助脚手架块token以考虑脚手架效用。提出ScaffoldM3C——一种多模态、轻量级、自回归的积木式稳定构建模型，输出一组下一步候选块；基于候选利用序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）维护可能装配序列的种群，以同时考虑多条潜在不同的装配方向。通过扩展StableText2Brick数据集，加入图像条件提示和脚手架稳定化构建序列进行训练。

Result: ScaffoldM3C相比竞争基线小4倍，推理时获得5倍至20倍加速，同时构建质量与最先进方法相当且整体稳定性更高。通过仿真和真实机器人装配演示验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: ScaffoldM3C通过多模态条件、脚手架token与SMC序列搜索的结合，在保持构建质量的同时显著提升推理效率与结构稳定性，并可迁移至真实机器人装配场景。

Abstract: Autonomously constructing physically realizable 3D structures remains a significant challenge due to combinatorial action spaces, interchangeable components, equifinal assembly sequences, and strict stability requirements during construction. State-of-the-art methods fine-tune large language models for text-based generative construction. However, these approaches do not allow for Multimodal (text, image, sketch) conditioning, overlook the practical role of scaffolding for stabilizing intermediate structures, and suffer from slow inference speeds. Therefore, we formulate construction as a probabilistic next-block generation task with multiple potential assembly actions and multiple potential task conditioning modalities. Concurrently, we explicitly consider the utility of scaffolding by introducing an auxiliary scaffold block token. We present Scaffold Multimodal Monte Carlo (ScaffoldM3C), a multimodal, lightweight, auto-regressive model for stable block-based construction, that proposes a set of next-step candidate blocks. Leveraging these candidates, we utilize Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) to maintain a population of possible assembly sequences, allowing us to consider multiple, potentially different, assembly directions simultaneously. We train our multimodal architecture by extending the StableText2Brick dataset to contain image conditioning prompts and scaffold-stabilized build sequences. ScaffoldM3C is 4x smaller than competing baselines, yielding a 5x to 20x speedup during inference, while achieving comparable construction quality to state-of-the-art methods and higher overall stability. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through simulations and real-world robot assembly demonstrations.

</details>


### [39] [Same Scene, Different Task: Skill Alignment for Compositional Generalization in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00524)
*Taegeun Yang,Youngju Na,Yoonki Cho,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CRAFT，通过“反事实配对”训练缓解 VLA 模型的视觉捷径问题，将已演示技能的执行监督迁移到未演示技能组合上，从而提升组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型难以泛化到微调演示中未出现的技能组合，即使每个组成技能都已分别被演示过。作者关注一种失败模式——视觉捷径：微调时视觉观测可能成为指令的代理，策略倾向于执行与相似观测相关联的已演示组合，而非指令要求的组合。

Method: 利用反事实配对进行训练：固定某条演示观测不变，仅改变指令以指定一个未被演示的组合。由于这些配对缺少对应的演示动作目标，CRAFT 借助可在同一技能不同执行间复用的技能表征，把已演示执行中“所需技能”的监督迁移到反事实配对的动作目标上，而非直接复用原动作。

Result: 在三种 VLA 模型和两个仿真基准上，CRAFT 提升了未演示组合的成功率，同时保持了对已演示组合的高成功率；在真实机器人上也改善了组合泛化表现。

Conclusion: 以技能表征为中介的反事实监督可有效缓解视觉捷径，为 VLA 模型的组合泛化提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often struggle to generalize to skill combinations absent from their fine-tuning demonstrations, even when every constituent skill has been demonstrated. We focus on a vision shortcut as one failure mode: during fine-tuning, visual observations can serve as a proxy for the instruction, so a policy may execute a demonstrated combination associated with similar observations rather than the instructed combination. This motivates training with counterfactual pairs formed by holding a demonstration observation fixed while changing the instruction to specify an undemonstrated combination. These pairs, however, lack corresponding demonstrated action targets. Crucially, the currently required skill has already been demonstrated, but actions from those executions cannot serve as direct targets because the same skill can require different actions across observations. We propose CRAFT, which transfers supervision from demonstrated executions of the required skill to counterfactual pairs using skill representations that can be reused across executions of the same skill. Across three VLA models and two simulation benchmarks, CRAFT improves success on undemonstrated combinations while maintaining high success on demonstrated ones; it also improves compositional generalization on a real robot. Project website: https://taegeunyang.github.io/craft/

</details>


### [40] [Does Continual Imitation Learning Remain Grounded? A Language-Perturbed Benchmark for Robotic Task Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00542)
*Siddeshwar Raghavan,Ziqin Yuan,Fengqing Zhu,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个诊断协议，研究机器人持续模仿学习中语言引导行为随任务序列的变化，揭示持续学习性能强并不等于语言 grounding 可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续模仿学习通常只评估是否忘记旧技能，但保留任务表现并不代表行为仍受语言正确引导——策略可能依赖场景线索、物体关联或记忆化的任务结构。因此需要一个能把"任务能力"与"语言敏感性"分开考察的评估协议。

Method: 为 LIBERO 的 Goal、Spatial、Object、Long 四个套件构造"保义"和"变义"两类指令变体；策略实验聚焦 LIBERO-Goal，在每个持续学习阶段后评估 Original 与 Paraphrase 指令；在保持各方法原始假设的前提下比较代表性持续模仿学习方法，并将任务能力与语言敏感性解耦，提出语义鲁棒性、目标适应性和语言敏感性三类诊断指标。

Result: 实验表明，持续学习表现优异并不总能转化为可靠的语言 grounding；新提出的诊断指标能判断保留下来的技能是否仍被其指令正确引导，是对标准学习与遗忘指标的有效补充。

Conclusion: 仅用学习/遗忘指标评估持续模仿学习不足以保证语言 grounding，所提基准协议与诊断指标可作为补充评估手段，用于检验持续学习后行为是否仍与语言一致。

Abstract: Continual imitation learning evaluates whether a robot can learn new knowledge without forgetting previously learned skills. However, retaining task performance does not ensure the behavior remains grounded in language because policies may rely on scene cues, object associations, or memorized task structure. We introduce a benchmark protocol to study how language-guided behavior changes as robotic policies learn successive tasks. We construct meaning-preserving and meaning-changing instruction variants for the Goal, Spatial, Object, and Long suites of LIBERO. Policy experiments focus on LIBERO-Goal, evaluating Original and Paraphrase instructions after each continual-learning stage. We compare representative continual imitation learning methods under their original assumptions while separating task competence from language sensitivity. The proposed diagnostics complement standard learning and forgetting metrics by measuring semantic robustness, goal adaptation, and language sensitivity. Results show that strong continual-learning performance does not always translate to reliable language grounding, and our diagnostics help determine whether retained skills remain correctly guided by their instructions. Additional materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/stillgrounded

</details>


### [41] [Token-World: World Modeling in Vision-Language Model Token Space for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00575)
*Chuyao Fu,Xiaowei Chi,Yuhan Rui,Yu-kai Wang,Zezhong Qian,Xiaojie Zhang,Yunfan Lou,Kevin Zhang,Kuangzhi Ge,Chak Wing Mak,Zhiyang Chen,Athena Zhuoming Zhong,Hongyang Chen,Haoran Li,Yike Guo,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Token-World，将VLM视觉token压缩为紧凑token状态并在该低维空间进行动作条件动态建模，再映射回策略接口表示，用于VLA世界模型仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA世界模型仿真通常预测未来RGB观测再重新编码为策略输入，仿真与下游策略执行之间是间接接口；本文探索能否在源自VLM视觉token的紧凑、面向策略的状态中建模世界动态。关键挑战是原始VLM视觉token维度高，难以高效且准确地进行自回归动态建模。

Method: Token-World：动作条件世界模型。先将VLM特征压缩为紧凑token状态，在该降维空间学习未来动态，再将预测状态映射回原始的面向策略表示以供下游使用。并通过消融验证紧凑表示设计与维度的影响。

Result: 在操作类基准上，Token-World相比近期世界模型仿真器提升开环特征保真度与策略-动作一致性，长时域rollout退化更慢；闭环评估中其仿真策略性能与参考策略性能的相关性高于Ctrl-World（r=0.794 vs. 0.583），且仿真延迟更低。

Conclusion: 在面向策略的紧凑VLM token状态中建模世界动态可行且有效，兼顾保真度、长时域稳定性与仿真效率；紧凑表示设计与维度对预测未来状态有显著影响。代码将于项目页发布。

Abstract: A common approach to world-model simulation for vision-language-action (VLA) systems is to predict future RGB observations and then re-encode them into policy inputs, introducing an indirect interface between simulation and downstream policy execution. We instead investigate whether world dynamics can be modeled in a compact, policy-oriented state derived from VLM visual tokens. A key challenge is that raw VLM visual tokens are high-dimensional, making efficient and accurate autoregressive dynamics modeling challenging. To address this, we introduce Token-World, an action-conditioned world model that compresses VLM features into a compact token state, learns future dynamics in this reduced space, and maps predicted states back to the original policy-facing representation for downstream use. Across manipulation benchmarks, Token-World improves open-loop feature fidelity and policy-action consistency over recent world-model simulators, with slower degradation over long rollout horizons. In closed-loop evaluation, its simulated policy performance correlates more strongly with reference policy performance than Ctrl-World ($r=0.794$ vs.\ $0.583$), while requiring lower simulation latency. Ablations further show that compact-representation design and dimensionality substantially affect future-state prediction. Code will be available at https://chuyaofu.github.io/Token-World/.

</details>


### [42] [When Reasoning Helps Action: Monitoring and Steering Chain-of-Thought in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00601)
*Sathwik Karnik,Joseph JR. Lee,Aryaman Gupta,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出评估CoT作为VLA运行时安全接口的两条轴线——可纠正性与可行动性，并给出TRUST方法，发现推理正确率提升并不必然带来具身任务性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备推理能力的VLA策略会输出CoT轨迹，看似能解释并引导动作，从而形成潜在的运行时安全接口；但尚不清楚这种接口何时能真正改善具身行为，因此需要界定评估维度。

Method: 提出TRUST（Token-level Reward for Utility-Steered Chain-of-Thought）：离线训练的价值模型由部分前缀预测最终推理正确性，据此监控并有选择地引导冻结VLA策略的推理生成；并在Alpamayo 1.5驾驶VLA与DeepThinkVLA操作VLA上评估。同时定义可纠正性（不可靠推理能否在生成中被检测并改进）与可行动性（推理纠正能否带来方向正确的行为变化）两条评估轴。

Result: Alpamayo 1.5上，TRUST监控正确性准确率88.9%，推理正确率由75.9%提升至90.0%；在AlpaSim困难子集上碰撞率降低30.4%、最大轨迹误差降低11.5%，优于算力匹配的Best-of-4。DeepThinkVLA上，抓取状态判断正确率由69.3%升至90.2%，动作选择判断由68.8%升至85.9%，但LIBERO-Plus闭环任务性能基本不变。分析显示Alpamayo 1.5存在意图一致的行为效应，而DeepThinkVLA效应有限。

Conclusion: 推理正确性的提升并不自动等同于具身性能提升；将CoT用作运行时安全接口时，应同时评估可纠正性与可行动性。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled VLA policies expose chain-of-thought (CoT) traces that appear to explain and guide their actions, creating a potential interface for runtime safety through reasoning monitoring and correction. In this work, we define and operationalize two evaluation axes for assessing when this interface can improve embodied behavior: correctability, which measures whether unreliable reasoning can be detected and improved during generation, and actionability, which measures whether reasoning corrections produce behaviorally meaningful changes in the intended direction. To enable correctability, we introduce Token-level Reward for Utility-Steered Chain-of-Thought (TRUST), an offline-trained value model that predicts eventual reasoning correctness from partial prefixes and uses these estimates to monitor and selectively steer reasoning generation in frozen VLA policies. On the Alpamayo 1.5 driving VLA, TRUST monitors correctness with 88.9% accuracy and improves reasoning correctness from 75.9% to 90.0%. On a baseline-defined challenging subset in AlpaSim, TRUST reduces collision rate by 30.4% and maximum trajectory error by 11.5% relative to the unsteered policy, outperforming a compute-matched Best-of-4 baseline. On the DeepThinkVLA manipulation VLA, TRUST improves the correctness of grasp-state claims from 69.3% to 90.2% and action-choice claims from 68.8% to 85.9%, yet closed-loop task performance on LIBERO-Plus remains largely unchanged. Empirical analysis reveals intent-consistent behavioral effects in Alpamayo 1.5 but limited effects in DeepThinkVLA, helping interpret these different task-level outcomes. Together, our results show that gains in reasoning correctness do not automatically imply gains in embodied performance, motivating evaluation of correctability and actionability when using CoT as a runtime safety interface.

</details>


### [43] [TacDyn-WAM: Learning Implicit Tactile Dynamics in a Heterogeneous Visuo-Tactile World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00638)
*Enyi Wang,Mingxin Wang,Quan Shi,Hetian Guo,Hongyu Wang,Xi Wang,Bin Qian,Yupeng Zheng,Wenxuan Song,Houde Liu,Yong Xu,Cheng Chi,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacDyn-WAM：异质视触世界动作模型，预测隐式触觉动力学而非重建未来触觉观测，在UniVTAC达81.5%成功率，真机五任务达71.0%（预训练后85.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉世界动作模型多沿用视频生成流程，通过迭代去噪重建未来触觉观测。该方式在部署漂移下不可靠：接触位置或力的微小变化会显著改变触觉像素，即使底层接触演化本身仍可预测。

Method: 提出TacDyn-WAM，一种异质视触世界动作模型，预测隐式触觉动力学而非重建观测。学习TacRep动力学感知触觉目标空间，通过对触觉片段的掩码时空预测训练，并用关系结构蒸馏正则化。视觉专家与隐式触觉动力学专家在各自目标空间预测未来视觉与触觉表征，经联合注意力交互；触觉专家单次前向预测多时间跨度的未来表征及其变化，只读触觉记忆提供当前触觉状态。

Result: UniVTAC上仅用提供演示达81.5%平均成功率，达到SOTA水平，与大规模视触轨迹预训练模型具竞争力。消融证实两条触觉通路及TacRep优于像素重建与静态替代方案。五个真机任务平均成功率71.0%，适度规模预训练提升至85.0%。

Conclusion: 预测隐式触觉动力学（而非重建像素）的表征空间建模可提升部署鲁棒性与数据效率，为视触世界动作模型提供有效范式。

Abstract: World action models improve robotic manipulation by conditioning actions on predicted futures, yet existing tactile variants largely inherit video-generation pipelines that reconstruct future tactile observations through iterative denoising. Such prediction can become unreliable under deployment drift: small changes in contact position or force may substantially alter tactile pixels even when the underlying contact evolution remains predictable. We introduce TacDyn-WAM, a heterogeneous visuo-tactile world action model that predicts implicit tactile dynamics rather than reconstructing future tactile observations. It learns TacRep, a dynamics-aware tactile target space trained through masked spatio-temporal prediction on tactile clips and regularized by relational structure distillation. A visual expert and an Implicit Tactile Dynamics Expert predict future visual and tactile representations in separate target spaces while interacting through joint attention; the tactile expert predicts future representations and their changes at multiple horizons in a single forward pass, and a read-only tactile memory supplies the current tactile state. On UniVTAC, TacDyn-WAM achieves an average success rate of 81.5% using only the provided demonstrations, reaching state-of-the-art-level performance and remaining competitive with models pretrained on large-scale visuo-tactile trajectories. Ablations confirm the benefits of both tactile pathways and TacRep over pixel-reconstruction and static alternatives. On five real-robot tasks, TacDyn-WAM reaches 71.0% average success, and modest-scale pretraining raises it to 85.0%, further validating our method.

</details>


### [44] [Toward Humanoid Robots in Construction: A Teleoperation Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00718)
*Parastoo Ali Pour,David R. Martin,Chang Min Hur,Bo Zhang,Tommy Zhou,Brandon Thomas Lichter,Shane Stanfield,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向Unitree G1人形机器人的遥操作系统，将XR上半身控制与脚踏式移动相结合，使单一操作者可同时操作与行走，并在两项建筑施工任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续存在的劳动力短缺、危险作业环境以及人形机器人自主性不足，促使作者研究遥操作作为近期可行方案，以减轻工人身体负担并生成高质量示教数据。

Method: 构建融合XR（扩展现实）上半身控制与脚踏式移动的单一操作者遥操作系统，部署于Unitree G1人形机器人；从O*NET职业数据库选取两项具有代表性的建筑施工任务进行评估，并与人工基线对比任务成功率与完成时间。

Result: 工具搬运任务成功率达100%，表面喷漆任务成功率为80%；但相比人工执行，遥操作所需时间显著更长。

Conclusion: 该XR加脚踏式移动的遥操作方案可在建筑施工任务上实现较高成功率，验证了遥操作作为近期人形机器人实用路径的可行性，但效率仍受限于完成时间较长的问题。

Abstract: We present a teleoperation system that enables a single operator to perform construction tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, combining extended reality (XR) based upper body control with pedal-based locomotion to enable simultaneous manipulation and locomotion. Motivated by persistent labor shortages, hazardous working conditions, and challenges in humanoid autonomy, we investigate teleoperation as a practical near-term approach for reducing physical strain on workers while generating high quality demonstration data. We evaluate the system on two representative construction tasks drawn from O*NET occupational database, and report task success and completion time relative to a manual baseline. The system achieved 100% success on tool transport and 80% success on surface painting, with teleoperation requiring substantially more time compared to manual execution.

</details>


### [45] [CF-JEPA: Improving Robustness of JEPA World Models via Controllability Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00727)
*Morgan Byrd,Robert Wright,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CF-JEPA，一种将潜在空间分解为可控与不可控子空间的 JEPA 式世界模型，通过把干扰信息归入不可控区域来提升视觉控制任务在干扰条件下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉智能体需从无关背景中分离有用信息。JEPA 式潜在世界模型虽不做像素级重建，理论上更自然，但仍对干扰信号敏感，并会出现潜在坍缩（latent collapse），因此需要一种能有效隔离干扰的建模方式。

Method: 提出 Controllability Factorized JEPA（CF-JEPA）：在 JEPA 世界模型框架下，将潜在空间分解为可控子空间与不可控子空间。干扰信息被捕获进不可控区域，而控制相关的潜在信息用于下游任务。

Result: 在 2D 与 3D 控制任务上，标称条件下性能与基线相当；在受干扰条件下性能更优，且 CF-JEPA 是唯一未发生潜在坍缩的模型。此外在模拟机器人任务的受干扰场景中进行了验证，体现了该方案的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 可控性因子化的潜在空间分解可有效缓解干扰敏感性与潜在坍缩问题，在受干扰的视觉控制与机器人任务中具有实用性和优越性。

Abstract: Controlling an agent with vision requires being able to separate useful information from irrelevant background information. JEPA-style latent world models seem like a natural approach for this, as they do not perform pixel-level reconstruction; however, they are still sensitive to these distractor signals and experience latent collapse. In this work, we introduce Controllability Factorized JEPA (CF-JEPA), a JEPA-style world model which splits the latent space into controllable and uncontrollable subspaces. This factorization allows us to capture all the distractor information into the uncontrollable region, while we use the control-relevant latent information for our task. With this, we show comparable performance across 2D and 3D control tasks under nominal conditions and improved performance under distracted conditions, where CF-JEPA is the only model that does not experience latent collapse. We also validate our model under distracted conditions for a simulated robot task, highlighting the practical application of such a scheme.

</details>


### [46] [Measuring Asset and Scene Reconstruction Effects in Real-to-Sim Robot Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00731)
*Sanya Verma,Luca Cilio,Velissarios Christodoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过提升环境重建质量（度量尺度几何、人工物理参数、自建场景splat），仿真的机器人策略评估得分与真实评估的相关性从r=0.51提升至r=0.90，平均得分误差降低10.56个百分点，缩小了sim-to-real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真是机器人策略评估的低成本、易复现替代方案，但其价值取决于仿真结果能否准确反映真实机器人的表现。本文旨在验证高质量重建流程是否能降低仿真与真实评估之间的一致性问题。

Method: 构建同一双臂机器人单元的两个仿真版本：一是"人工重建"（度量尺度物体几何、投影纹理、人工物理参数、自建场景splat）；二是"默认重建"（开源方案：单图生成式网格、引擎默认物理、高斯splat场景）。两者使用相同物体照片和场景视频。2个策略各跑5个任务，共10个任务-策略组合（cell），每个cell在每种重建中各运行20次，其他设置固定。两个重建版本均针对同一批真实试验评分，由第三方评估者打分。

Result: 10个cell的仿真与真实均值之间：人工重建Pearson相关r=0.90，默认重建r=0.51；平均得分误差人工重建为6.97个百分点，默认为17.54个百分点，降低10.56个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过更高视觉保真度、人工物理参数和度量尺度提升环境重建质量，可使仿真更贴合真实世界，缩小sim-to-real差距。作者公开了测试框架、逐次试验得分、各运行配置以及两个重建版本的资产和场景。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is increasingly used alongside real-world evaluation of robot policies because it is cheaper and easier to repeat; however, its value depends on how closely its outcomes track the real robot's. We test whether our reconstruction pipeline, combining metrically scaled object geometry, authored physical parameters and scene reconstruction, reduces disagreement between simulated and real robot scores relative to a default open-source recipe. We constructed two simulated versions of one bimanual robot cell: an authored reconstruction, using object geometry at estimated metric scale, projected textures, authored physics and our own scene splat; and a baseline, referred to as the default reconstruction, using the open-source recipe of a generative single-image mesh, engine-default physics and a Gaussian-splat scene. Both reconstructions use the same object photographs and scene video. Two policies ran five tasks each, giving ten task-policy pairs, which we call cells; each cell was run twenty times in each reconstruction with all other settings held fixed. Both reconstructions were scored against the same real trials, graded by a third-party evaluator. Pearson correlation between the ten simulated and real cell means is r = 0.90 for the authored reconstruction and 0.51 for the default.
