At every control step, learned predictors estimate whether the nominal tracking policy and the abort policy remain viable over a short horizon, and a least-sacrificial hierarchy keeps the most task-ambitious behavior that remains viable. In simulation with randomized disturbances, VAPS sharply reduces head contact and hand contact, which are the dominant sources of hardware damage, with both a Unitree G1 and a LimX Oli; on the LimX Oli, we validate the viability predictors and the full VAPS controller for side-flip motions. VAPS Pareto-dominates the strongest single-network alternatives we could train, including an end-to-end safe-tracking policy and students distilled from VAPS's own oracle-routed decisions, in both task success and head impact. We also show that VAPS is a powerful framework to supervise undertrained policies and protect the hardware.

</details>


### [81] [ALFRED: Requirement-driven development of an open-source mobile manipulator for long-term plant monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01477)
*Ciarán Miceal Johnson,Christopher Quail,Garry Ellard,Alistair McConnell,Steve Tonneau,Fernando Auat Cheein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ALFRED——一款基于商用组件、开源设计的移动操作机器人平台，通过四次迭代设计满足长期户外重复部署需求，并完成了一年的月度森林调查任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟踪作物与森林的季节性变化需要重复观测同一植株。地面机器人可近距离作业，机械臂能提供更多视角，但长期野外数据集背后的机器人极少公开设计文件，且机器人自身结构如何限制机械臂可达范围、遮挡传感器，在不同版本间鲜有比较。

Method: ALFRED采用商用组件搭建：Ackermann转向底盘，搭载六自由度机械臂、LiDAR、RGB-D相机、RTK GNSS与IMU，安装于可重构铝型材框架上，运行容器化ROS软件。研发围绕六项重复户外部署需求（耐久性、模块化、可维护性、感知可达性、续航、可复现性），历经四次迭代；对后三个版本进行基于模型的分析（可达位姿分析、射线投射）。

Result: 基于模型的分析显示，机械臂可达位姿的可用比例从34.0%提升至60.0%，最终达66.1%；射线投射表明仅最终版本能同时保持框架安装LiDAR前后水平视野无遮挡。ALFRED完成了一年528次月度森林调查穿越，未遗漏任何计划采集，期间经历电池退化、为其他研究重新配置及ALFRED 2.0并行开发，每次版本切换约耗时六小时。

Conclusion: ALFRED验证了开源移动操作平台在长期户外部署中的可行性；同时表明机械模块化的可靠性取决于与之同步维护的机器人描述文件（robot description）。

Abstract: Tracking seasonal change in crops and forests requires observing the same plants repeatedly. Ground robots can do this at close range, and a manipulator gives their sensors more viewpoints. Yet the robots behind long-term field datasets are rarely released with their design files, and how a robot's own structure limits arm reach and occludes its sensors is seldom compared between builds. We present ALFRED, an open-source mobile manipulator built from commercially available components. It carries a six-degree-of-freedom arm, LiDAR, RGB-D cameras, RTK GNSS and an IMU on an Ackermann-steered base, all mounted on a reconfigurable aluminium strut frame, and runs containerised ROS software. It was developed through four builds against six requirements for repeated outdoor deployment: durability, modularity, repairability, sensing reach, endurance and reproducibility. Model-based analysis of the last three builds shows the usable share of the arm's reachable poses rising from 34.0% to 60.0% and then 66.1%, and ray casting shows that only the final build keeps the frame-mounted LiDAR's horizontal view clear both forwards and backwards. ALFRED completed a year of monthly forest surveys (528 traversals) without missing a scheduled collection. This was despite battery degradation, reconfiguration for another researcher's study, and the parallel development of ALFRED 2.0 for autonomous crop-row operation, with each switch between builds taking about six hours. The deployment also showed that mechanical modularity is only as dependable as the robot description that tracks it.

</details>


### [82] [OpenSpace Lab Solution to the IROS 2026 Indoor Exploration Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01505)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Dong Li,Zezhou Sun,Yuxuan Xu,Siyu Teng,Yuchen Li,Jianjian Yang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenSpace Lab 在 IROS 2026 智能信息收集竞赛中获得单机器人公开赛道第 1 名，单/多机器人私有赛道均第 3 名。方法结合地图补全预测的全局规划、基于剩余时间的探索策略与多机器人效用驱动目标选择，单机公开赛道覆盖率达 61.04%，私有赛道分别为 39.53% 和 39.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内未知环境探索需要在有限作业时间内最大化地图覆盖率，同时兼顾返航约束与多机器人协同中的重复搜索问题，为此提出了面向该竞赛的探索方案。

Method: 单机器人框架利用预训练的地图补全预测进行全局规划，优先探索未探索区域，并引入基于剩余时间的探索策略，将返航约束纳入决策过程以协调覆盖率与作业时间。多机器人探索采用效用驱动的目标选择策略，权衡观测收益、移动成本与预算约束，并利用共享地图与意图信息消除冗余搜索、提升协同效率。

Result: 单机器人公开赛道覆盖率达 61.04%；单机器人与多机器人私有赛道覆盖率分别为 39.53% 和 39.91%。团队获得单机器人公开赛道第 1 名，单机器人和多机器人私有赛道均获第 3 名。

Conclusion: 该方案在单/多机器人室内探索任务中取得了竞赛领先成绩，验证了地图补全预测、剩余时间探索与效用驱动协同策略的有效性；基于本报告的完整论文正在准备投稿，源代码将在全文录用后于指定仓库公开。

Abstract: This report presents the \textbf{OpenSpace Lab}'s solution to the Competition on Intelligent Information Gathering for Single and Multi-Robot Systems Workshops, organized as part of the IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026. Our team reached 1st place in the Single-Robot Public Track and 3rd place in both the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks. The single-robot framework utilizes pre-trained map completion predictions for global planning to prioritize unexplored areas. To reconcile map coverage with limited operation time, we introduce a remaining-time-based exploration strategy that integrates homing constraints into the decision-making process. For multi-robot exploration, we utilize a utility-driven target selection strategy that balances observation gains, movement costs, and budget constraints, leveraging shared map and intent data to eliminate redundant search and maximize coordination efficiency. Our solution reached a 61.04\% coverage rate in the Single-Robot Public Track, while reaching 39.53\% and 39.91\% coverage in the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks, respectively. An extended full-length paper based on this report is currently being prepared for submission, and the source code will be released upon acceptance of the full manuscript at https://github.com/OpenSpace-Lab/Indoor-Exploration-IROS2026.

</details>


### [83] [Completion Aware Guidance for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01559)
*Seungyeon Kim,Junhoo Lee,Baekseung Kim,Minkyu Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文揭示了世界动作模型（WAMs）的"任务未完成想象"问题根源在于短段控制适配，并提出免训练采样方法CAG加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs能够预测视觉未来与机器人动作，但存在"任务未完成想象"缺陷：预测看似合理且与动作一致，却遗漏了完成任务所需的关键状态转移。作者希望证明该失败并非世界模型骨干固有，而是短段（short-chunk）控制适配带来的偏差。

Method: 提出Completion Aware Guidance（CAG），一种免训练的采样引导方法，在生成过程中引导采样朝向任务完成，从而抑制对局部合理但非完成性转移的偏好。

Result: 在代表性WAMs上，CAG将RoboTwin 2.0子集的成功率从64%提升至70%，零样本仿真中从69%提升至75%，同时将任务未完成想象比例从79%降至40%。

Conclusion: 任务未完成想象源于短段控制适配而非世界模型本身，通过免训练的完成感知引导可显著改善任务成功率并大幅减少非完成性想象。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) predict visual futures and robot actions, yet they remain susceptible to task-incomplete imagination, where plausible, action-consistent predictions omit the transition needed for task completion. In this paper, we show that this failure is not inherent to the world model backbone, but emerges when adapted for short-chunk control, which can repeatedly favor plausible local continuations over task-completing transitions. To address this, we introduce Completion Aware Guidance (CAG), a training-free sampling method that guides generation toward task completion. Across representative WAMs, CAG improves success from 64% to 70% on a RoboTwin 2.0 subset and from 69% to 75% in zero-shot simulation, while reducing task-incomplete imagination from 79% to 40%.

</details>


### [84] [LiDARFlow: Real-Time Panel-Based MAV Guidance in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01573)
*João Machado,Zeynep Bilgin,Matthieu Verdoucq,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖机载感知的微型飞行器引导算法，将源于空气动力学势流理论的panel方法与在线构建的障碍表示结合，实现未知杂乱环境中的平滑避障，并通过室内飞行实验验证其轻量性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型飞行器在未知、杂乱环境中执行任务时，需要在仅有机载感知的条件下实现实时避障，且机载计算资源有限，因此亟需一种计算轻量、可在线运行的引导方法。

Method: 基于源自气动势流理论的panel formulation生成由局部感知障碍产生的平滑无碰撞引导向量；利用机载LiDAR测量在线构建并更新障碍表示，以将该方法扩展至未知环境；将所得避障场与标称引导向量场融合，生成最终控制输入。

Result: 在室内飞行实验中针对航点导航与方向引导两种场景进行验证，飞行器在仅使用机载感知的情况下均成功完成任务并实时避开所有障碍；结果表明该方法计算轻量、适合机载实现，点云处理被识别为主要实际限制。

Conclusion: 结果支持在未知环境中实现轻量级机载引导的可行性，同时指出点云处理是当前实际应用中的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: This paper presents a guidance algorithm for micro aerial vehicles operating in unknown, cluttered environments using only onboard sensing. The method is based on a panel formulation originally derived from aerodynamic potential-flow theory and generates smooth, collision-free guidance vectors from locally perceived obstacles. The approach is extended to unknown environments by constructing and updating the obstacle representation online from onboard LiDAR measurements. The resulting obstacle-avoidance field is integrated with a nominal guiding vector field to produce the final control input. The system is experimentally validated in indoor flight tests under two scenarios: waypoint navigation and directional guidance. In both cases, the vehicle successfully completes its task while avoiding all obstacles in real time using only onboard perception. The results demonstrate that the method is computationally lightweight and suitable for onboard implementation, with pointcloud processing identified as the main practical limitation. These results support the feasibility of lightweight onboard guidance in unknown environments.

</details>


### [85] [ReCo: Response-Consistent Locomotion with Policy-Aware MPC for Legged Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01612)
*Kuankuan Sima,Yichao Gao,Chenxi Gu,Kefan Zhao,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReCo：将响应一致性运动策略与策略感知MPC耦合，实现连续腿式操作，仿真中位置与姿态RMSE分别降低28.7%和27.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续腿式操作要求末端执行器在基座持续行走时保持精确跟踪。RL+MPC是合适方案：学习策略提供鲁棒运动，MPC协调基座与手臂补偿跟踪误差。但MPC只能补偿其可预测的基座运动，而学习策略的指令响应会随步态相位、接触状态与负载变化，导致补偿不准确。

Method: 提出ReCo框架，耦合响应一致的运动控制与策略感知MPC。通过响应塑形（response shaping）在随机化动力学下训练策略，使其对指令的响应一致且可重复；随后辨识闭环响应模型，使MPC能够联合规划运动指令与手臂动作。

Result: 在仿真基准上，ReCo相对各指标最佳基线，将位置RMSE降低28.7%、姿态RMSE降低27.4%。真实世界实验展示了机载的连续腿式操作，基座与手臂协同运动。

Conclusion: 通过使策略响应可预测、可重复，并将该响应模型嵌入MPC，ReCo有效提升了腿式操作中末端跟踪精度，并成功迁移至真实平台。

Abstract: Continuous legged manipulation requires accurate end-effector tracking while the base keeps walking. Combining reinforcement learning (RL) with model predictive control (MPC) suits this task: the learned policy provides robust locomotion, while MPC coordinates the base and arm to compensate for tracking errors. However, MPC can compensate only for base motion that it can predict, and a learned policy's command response varies with gait phase, contact, and payload. We present ReCo, a framework that couples response-consistent locomotion with policy-aware MPC for legged manipulation. Response shaping trains the policy to respond to commands consistently and repeatably across randomized dynamics. An identified closed-loop response model then lets MPC jointly plan locomotion commands and arm motion. On the simulation benchmark, ReCo reduces position and orientation root-mean-square error (RMSE) by 28.7% and 27.4% relative to the best baseline for each metric. Real-world experiments demonstrate onboard continuous legged manipulation with coordinated base and arm motion.

</details>


### [86] [SonarVoxNet: Diver Detection in 3D Bounding Box using 3D Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01644)
*Eugene Park,Jiwon Lee,Seyoung Kan,Trung Dong,Xiaomin Lin,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SonarVoxNet 与 Diver3D：前者是首个基于 3D 声呐、可输出9自由度全姿态定向包围盒的潜水员检测器，后者是首个公开的含完整3D姿态标注的3D声呐潜水员数据集；实验证明从偏航单轴到完整SO(3)旋转表示的转变是提升检测精度的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助潜水员的自主水下航行器需持续跟踪潜水员的三维位置与全身姿态。但水下视觉感知不可靠；前视声呐虽常用却丢失了姿态估计所需的高度维信息；新近商用的3D声呐虽保留高度信息，但点云稀疏且噪声大，而现有检测器面向稠密LiDAR数据以及仅绕偏航轴旋转、保持直立的目标（如车辆、行人），无法表征自由俯仰与横滚的潜水员。

Method: 1) SonarVoxNet：将体素化编码器与无锚框的center-based检测头适配到3D声呐数据，用连续的6D旋转参数化替代传统的仅偏航旋转表示，预测完整的9自由度定向包围盒。2) Diver3D：在天然洞穴潜水场景采集，是首个公开的、为多种非直立姿态潜水员提供完整3D姿态标注的3D声呐数据集。3) 通过对骨干网络与检测头进行受控消融实验定位关键因素。

Result: 消融实验表明，实现精确3D声呐潜水员检测的主导因素是旋转表示从仅偏航（yaw-only）转变为完整SO(3)。该转变显著提升检测精度并降低姿态误差。

Conclusion: 结果证明仅凭3D声呐即可恢复潜水员的全身姿态，为后续潜水员姿态估计与潜水员-机器人交互研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) assisting human divers must continuously track not only the diver's 3D position but also their full-body orientation. However, vision-based perception is unreliable underwater, and forward-looking sonar -- despite being widely used -- discards the elevation information needed for orientation estimation, posing a fundamental limitation. Recently commercialized 3D sonar preserves elevation but produces sparse, noisy returns, and existing detectors are built for dense LiDAR data and for targets that remain upright and rotate only about the yaw axis (e.g., vehicles, pedestrians), making them unable to represent a freely pitching and rolling diver. To address this gap, we present two contributions. First, SonarVoxNet adapts a voxel-based encoder and an anchor-free center-based detection head to 3D sonar data, replacing the conventional yaw-only rotation representation with a continuous 6D rotation parameterization to predict full 9-DoF oriented bounding boxes -- to our knowledge, the first 3D sonar diver detector to do so. Second, Diver3D is the first public 3D sonar dataset with full 3D orientation labels for divers in diverse, non-upright poses, collected at a natural cave-diving site. Through controlled ablations over the backbone and detection head, we show that the dominant factor behind accurate 3D sonar-based diver detection is the transition from yaw-only rotation to full-SO(3) rotation. This transition substantially improves detection accuracy and reduces orientation error. These results demonstrate that full-body diver orientation is recoverable from 3D sonar alone, laying the groundwork for future work on diver pose estimation and diver-robot interaction.

</details>


### [87] [Learning a Resolution-Consistent Jacobian Field for Bio-Inspired Rigid-Soft Finger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01668)
*Tianyou Liang,Haisen Zeng,Shanjun Chen,YiMing Zhu,Zhongyue Lu,Zirong Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Jacobian Flow Matching (JFM) 结构化学习框架，基于条件流匹配学习分辨率一致的雅可比场，实现腱驱动刚软耦合灵巧手指的高精度单步与长时程预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动刚软耦合灵巧手指具有强非线性与构型相关敏感性，建模困难；离散时间控制中基于雅可比的运动学算法依赖逐点局部线性近似，其性能对传感器采样频率与控制器更新频率敏感。

Method: 提出 JFM 框架，基于 Conditional Flow Matching (CFM)，将驱动-运动转换建模为动力流，学习分辨率一致的雅可比场；支持单步预测与基于 ODE 积分的连续 rollout，实现跨时间分辨率的一致推理。

Result: 在腱驱动刚软手指上，单步预测抑制异常值并提升精度，全局平均 RMSE 相比离散雅可比学习基线降低超过 53%；长时程预测中，稀疏采样 (Stride = 8) 下 ODE 积分恢复的轨迹 RMSE 中位数降低 14.43%，误差方差降低 24.87%。

Conclusion: 学习的流式雅可比场为刚软耦合非线性系统的离线多步轨迹优化提供了有效的局部模型。

Abstract: Bio-inspired tendon-driven rigid-soft coupled dexterous fingers exhibit strong nonlinearity and configuration-dependent sensitivity, making accurate modeling challenging. In discrete-time control, Jacobian-based kinematic algorithms typically rely on point-wise local linear approximations, which makes their performance sensitive to sensor sampling frequency and controller update frequency. To address this issue, we propose Jacobian Flow Matching (JFM), a structured learning framework based on Conditional Flow Matching (CFM), to learn a resolution-consistent Jacobian field that models actuation-to-motion transitions as a dynamical flow. The proposed framework supports both single-step prediction and continuous rollout via ODE integration, enabling consistent inference across temporal resolutions. Experiments on a tendon-driven rigid-soft finger show that the proposed method suppresses outlier errors and improves single-step prediction accuracy, reducing the global average RMSE by over 53% compared with a baseline discrete Jacobian learning approach. For long-horizon prediction, trajectories recovered via ODE integration achieve higher fidelity under sparse sampling (Stride = 8), reducing the RMSE median by 14.43% and the error variance by 24.87%. These results demonstrate that the learned flow-based Jacobian field provides an effective local model for offline multi-step trajectory optimization in rigid-soft coupled nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Leaderboard Scores: A Deployment-Focused Protocol for Interpretable Tracking Evaluation in Pedestrian-Centric Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01682)
*Dominik Wojcikiewicz,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向部署的"仅跟踪器"评测协议，用共享检测隔离跟踪器行为，在JRDB上评测六种开源跟踪器与PedRefTrack，发现漏检后轨迹延续是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在行人间导航需要快速可用、在漏检下仍空间可信、保持身份且满足嵌入式算力预算的轨迹。聚合跟踪指标（如HOTA）难以揭示轨迹在何时、以何种方式失效；而不同检测器输入又会混淆跟踪器与检测器质量的影响，因此需要隔离跟踪器本身行为的评测方法。

Method: 提出面向部署、仅针对跟踪器的评测协议：所有跟踪器共享同一检测结果以隔离跟踪器行为，直接评测初始化、检测器缺失时的延续、身份恢复、近邻关联，以及随负载变化的跟踪步运行时，同时保留HOTA作为补充性聚合指标。在JRDB数据集上用六个开源跟踪器及自研轻量Pedestrian Reference Tracker（PedRefTrack）验证，并加入GT辅助变体以估计理想关联与运动下剩余的跟踪器侧差距。

Result: 固定检测下，非GT跟踪器HOTA仅在24.26%–29.67%区间，但能力画像差异明显。检测缺失1.0 s后，无GT辅助的跟踪器在不超过一半的合格案例中能维持空间正确且身份一致的输出，说明漏检延续是所测属性中的主导性限制。近邻失效较少，主要在最短间距时上升。NVIDIA Jetson Orin上跟踪步运行时呈重尾且对负载敏感，拥挤帧中若干跟踪器低于10 Hz实时目标。

Conclusion: 该协议为刻画以行人为中心场景中跟踪器的行为与部署适用性提供了可复现方法，代码与评测脚本已开源。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating among pedestrians need trajectories that become available quickly, remain spatially credible through missed observations, preserve identity, and fit within an embedded computing budget. Aggregate tracking scores provide limited insight into when and how trajectories fail, while varying detector inputs can confound tracker and detector quality. We present a deployment-focused, tracker-only evaluation protocol that uses shared detections to isolate tracker behavior and directly evaluates initialization, detector-gap continuation, identity recovery, close-neighbor association, and load-dependent tracker-step runtime, while Higher Order Tracking Accuracy (HOTA) is retained as a complementary aggregate measure. We apply the protocol to the JackRabbot Dataset and Benchmark (JRDB) using six open-source trackers and our lightweight Pedestrian Reference Tracker (PedRefTrack), together with a GT-assisted variant that estimates the remaining tracker-side gap under idealized association and motion. Under fixed detections, the non-GT trackers span only 24.26%-29.67% HOTA yet exhibit markedly different capability profiles. After 1.0 s without detector support, no tracker without GT assistance maintains spatially correct, same-identity output in more than half of eligible cases, making missing-observation continuation the dominant limitation among the tested properties. Close-neighbor failures are smaller and increase mainly at the shortest separations. Tracker-step runtime on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin is heavy-tailed and load-sensitive, causing several trackers to fall below the 10 Hz real-time target in crowded frames. The protocol provides a reproducible way to characterize tracker behavior and deployment suitability in pedestrian-centric environments. Code and evaluation scripts are released at https://github.com/SCAI-Lab/tracker_eval.

</details>


### [89] [ActiveWAM: Evidence-Aware Active Vision for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01698)
*Renjun Wu,Luzhou Ge,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出ActiveWAM，一个联合学习观测与操作的world-action model，将主动视觉操作建模为"证据感知的保留-获取"问题，并通过训练时反演约束冻结视频先验，无需测试时反演或候选排序。同时发布RoboTwin-AV基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉操作需同时控制相机与末端执行器，但相机运动决定了有限观测窗口内哪些证据可见：获取新视角可能移走任务关键线索，保留视角则放弃潜在有用观测。因此需要一种能权衡"保留还是获取视角"的策略。

Method: 将问题形式化为evidence-aware retain-acquire问题；提出ActiveWAM统一world-action model，联合学习观测与操作。采用训练时反演，用承载任务的源证据与可见时序变化约束冻结的视频先验，避免测试时反演与候选排序。部署时策略从视角感知历史生成双臂及pan/tilt动作（含stay与reacquisition），并由新测RGB观测更新上下文；未来视频预测作为协同训练信号，动作生成不需解码未来视频或最优视角标注。

Result: 在TAVIS上分布外成功率比最强基线提升最高17.0个百分点；在RoboTwin-AV上比Fast-WAM高出20.0个点；在真实厨房物理任务上超出其26.7个点。同时构建了含50个任务、具备可执行pan/tilt控制与自动生成示范的RoboTwin-AV基准。

Conclusion: 通过将主动视角控制与操作统一到world-action model中，并以训练时反演替代推理时的视角搜索，ActiveWAM在仿真与真实任务上均显著优于基线，且无需最优视角标注，验证了证据感知视角决策在主动视觉操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Active vision manipulation requires a policy to control both its camera and its end-effectors, yet camera motion determines which evidence remains visible within finite observation windows. Acquiring a new view can displace task-critical cues, while retaining a view forgoes potentially useful observations. We formulate this as an evidence-aware retain--acquire problem and present ActiveWAM, a unified world--action model that learns observation and manipulation jointly. To this end, we propose training-time inversion which constrains a frozen video prior by task-bearing source evidence and visible temporal changes, eliminating the need for test-time inversion or candidate ranking. At deployment, the policy generates bimanual and pan/tilt actions-including stay and reacquisition behaviors-from view-aware history, and updates context from newly measured RGB observations. Future-video prediction serves as a co-training signal, while action generation requires neither future-video decoding nor optimal viewpoint annotations. We introduce RoboTwin-AV, a 50-task benchmark with executable pan/tilt control and automatically generated demonstrations. ActiveWAM improves TAVIS out-of-distribution success by up to 17.0 percentage points over the strongest baselines, achieves 20.0 additional points over Fast-WAM on RoboTwin-AV, and outperforms it by 26.7 points on real-world physical kitchen tasks.

</details>


### [90] [Query-Conditioned Articulation Estimation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01726)
*Abdelrhman Werby,Fabio Scaparro,Kai O. Arra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出QueryArt，从单张RGB图像、2D查询点和相机内参估计关节物体的3D运动学参数；通过预测相对于查询点、以查询点深度为单位的关节几何，实现尺度可识别性，再用一次深度测量恢复米制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人交互与操作需要估计关节物体（articulated objects）的运动学参数，但机器人通常只能获得当前观测信息，往往是一张未见物体的单张RGB图像。现有单图方法将关节部件分割与关节估计耦合，预测易受漏检和错误部件关联影响；同时回归米制3D几何，而单视图只能确定到尺度级别，导致目标不可辨识。

Method: QueryArt以单张RGB图像、一个2D查询点和相机内参为输入，预测相对于查询点、并以查询点深度为单位的3D关节几何，使目标仅从图像即可辨识；随后在查询点进行一次深度测量提供尺度，恢复米制关节参数。模型在精心构建的合成与真实关节数据集混合上训练，并在多个基准上与近期基线方法比较。

Result: 在多个基准上，QueryArt在大多数关节指标上优于近期基线，包括在分布外数据上。在移动操作平台的真实场景评估中，跨16个物体部件、5类视角共57次操作试验，取得70.2%的成功率。

Conclusion: QueryArt解耦了关节部件分割与关节参数估计，利用尺度可辨识的相对几何表示结合单次深度测量恢复米制参数，在基准和真实机器人操作中均表现良好，代码与视频已公开。

Abstract: Enabling robots to estimate the kinematic parameters of articulated objects unlocks a wide range of capabilities for interaction and manipulation. The estimation has to happen from the information the robot currently observes, often just a single RGB image of an object it has never seen before. Current single-image approaches couple articulation part segmentation with articulation estimation, making their predictions vulnerable to missed detections and incorrect part associations, and they regress metric 3D geometry that a single view fixes only up to scale. We present QueryArt, a model that estimates articulation parameters from a single RGB image, a 2D query point, and camera intrinsics. QueryArt is trained to estimate the 3D articulation geometry relative to the queried point and in units of its depth, which keeps its target identifiable from the image alone. A single depth measurement at the query point then supplies the scale and recovers the metric parameters. We train QueryArt on a curated mixture of synthetic and real-world articulation datasets. We evaluate QueryArt on several benchmarks and compare it against recent baselines. QueryArt outperforms recent baselines on most articulation metrics, including on out-of-distribution data. To demonstrate the model's capabilities in real-world settings, we evaluate QueryArt on a mobile manipulator across 57 manipulation trials spanning 16 object parts and five viewpoint classes, achieving a 70.2% success rate. We provide code and videos at: https://abwerby.github.io/queryart/

</details>


### [91] [World Motion Models: Flexible Sequence Modeling of SE(3) Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01742)
*Jiahui Lei,Qianqian Wang,Trevor Darrell,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出World Motion Models（WMMs），用稀疏SE(3)位姿轨迹统一建模动态3D世界，将多实体耦合分布转化为带逐token噪声水平的flow-matching序列建模问题，用统一网络通过不同掩码支持预测、补全、控制等多种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赋予人工代理空间智能，需要具备对动态3D世界的全面生成式先验。现有4D建模表示往往任务专用、实体分离，难以统一处理关节物体、人体、手-物交互、分段刚体场景运动、相机运动乃至机器人状态与动作，也难支持任意实体与时间步之间的灵活条件生成。

Method: 1) 观察动态场景元素可由一组刚性SE(3)轨迹良好近似，将其作为4D建模的最小且具表达力的基元；用稀疏SE(3)位姿轨迹表示"什么在何时何处（过去、现在、未来）"；2) 该表示把关节物体、人体、手-物交互、分段刚体动态、相机运动及机器人状态/动作统一到共享空间；3) 将多实体联合分布建模为序列建模问题，采用flow-matching并给每个token设置不同噪声水平；4) 引入context token机制处理非序列条件，实现任意实体、任意时间步之间的any-to-any边缘条件化；5) 不同任务仅通过在同一网络上施加不同掩码实现。

Result: 在3D视觉与机器人学的6个多样化应用上开展实验，涵盖未来预测、运动补全（infilling）、模型预测控制、逆运动学、跨本体重定向（cross-embodiment retargeting）与策略学习，WMMs均取得强劲性能，验证了其通用性与灵活性。

Conclusion: 以稀疏SE(3)轨迹为统一基元并结合带逐token噪声的flow-matching与context token条件机制，可将多种4D动态建模与机器人任务归约为同一网络上的掩码差异，WMMs为动态3D世界的生成式先验提供了一个统一、灵活且有效的框架。

Abstract: Equipping artificial agents with spatial intelligence requires a comprehensive generative prior over the dynamic 3D world. We propose World Motion Models (WMMs) that capture "what was, is, and will be where across time" via sparse SE(3) pose trajectories. WMMs are built on the observation that elements of dynamic scenes can be well approximated by a set of rigid SE(3) trajectories, a minimal yet expressive primitive for 4D modeling. This representation unifies articulated objects, human bodies, hand-object interactions, piecewise-rigid scene dynamics, camera motion, and even robot states and actions into a single shared space. Given this representation, we cast the joint distribution of these entities as a flexible sequence modeling problem, utilizing flow-matching with per-token noise levels. Coupled with a context token mechanism for non-sequential conditioning, this formulation supports any-to-any marginal conditioning across an arbitrary number of entities and time steps. Tasks such as future prediction, motion infilling, model-predictive control, inverse kinematics, cross-embodiment retargeting, and policy learning all reduce to the application of different masks over the same network. Experiments on 6 diverse applications of 3D vision and robotics demonstrate the versatility and flexibility of WMMs with strong performance.

</details>


### [92] [End-to-End Learning vs. Modular Architectures: Comparative Insights into Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01746)
*Kartik B. Kapse*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对自动驾驶中的端到端学习与模块化架构进行系统比较，并提出四维架构选择框架，在10个已发布系统上验证，推荐准确率为7/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统设计中，端到端学习与模块化架构是两大主流范式，但缺乏系统性的比较与选择依据。作者希望厘清二者的优势、局限与权衡，并为不同应用场景提供可操作的架构选型指导。

Method: 文献综合与比较分析：从可解释性、可扩展性、鲁棒性和实际适用性等维度对比两种范式；进而提出"四维架构选择框架"，涵盖安全、运行环境、数据/计算资源、部署情境四个方面，共十二项二元判据；并使用十个已发表的自动驾驶系统进行验证。

Result: 端到端学习强调简洁性与动态环境下的适应性，但透明度不足且高度依赖大规模数据集；模块化架构具备更强的可解释性与任务特定优化能力，却面临集成复杂与可扩展性挑战。所提出的框架在十个系统中正确推荐了7个已部署架构，并指出混合架构是弥合两者缺陷的有前景方向。

Conclusion: 两种范式各有优劣，不存在普适最优方案；混合架构结合二者优势，是未来研究的重要方向。四维框架为架构选型提供了初步可行的决策工具，但验证规模有限，仍需更广泛实证。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems have become a central focus of intelligent transportation research, with End-to-End Learning and Modular Architectures offering two prominent design paradigms for their implementation. E2E Learning uses deep learning algorithms to map raw sensory inputs directly to driving actuators, providing a streamlined and adaptable solution. while Modular Architectures employ a pipeline-based approach, dividing the system into distinct subsystems for perception, cognition, planning, and control. This paper presents a comprehensive comparative analysis of these paradigms, focusing on their strengths, limitations, and trade-offs to provide insights into their suitability for various autonomous driving applications. The study evaluates key factors such as interpretability, scalability, robustness, and real-world applicability. While End-to-End Learning emphasizes simplicity and adaptability in dynamic environments, it lacks transparency and is highly dependent on large datasets. Conversely, Modular Architectures offer superior interpretability and task-specific optimization, but face challenges related to integration complexity and scalability. To address these limitations, hybrid approaches that combine the strengths of both paradigms have emerged, offering a promising direction for overcoming these challenges. Beyond this comparative synthesis, following work proposes a Four-Dimensional Architecture Selection Framework, comprising twelve binary criteria across safety, operating environment, data/computational resources, and deployment context, and validate it against ten published autonomous driving systems, correctly recommending 7/10 deployed architectures. This work synthesizes existing literature to highlight key trade-offs between the paradigms and identifies hybrid architectures as a promising direction for future research.

</details>


### [93] [FlashDexRetarget: Accelerating Dexterous Manipulation Data Generation through Multi-Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01849)
*Kyungmin Lee,Sibeen Kim,Dongyoon Hwang,Yoonsang Oh,Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,I Made Aswin Nahrendra,Jaegul Choo,Hojoon Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashDexRetarget：基于强化学习的高成功率、高效灵巧手动作重定向框架，在50个动作基准上达90%成功率，比采样基线高约2.5倍，训练算力比RL基线少至多100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手-物体演示是可复用的灵巧操作数据源，但跨本体迁移需要物理可行的重定向。现有基于物理的方法在重定向成功率、动作专属训练效率或两者兼有上存在局限，因此需要一个高成功率且高效的方案。

Method: 提出基于RL的FlashDexRetarget框架。为提升可学习性，融合物体点云观测、手-物距离特征与未来轨迹编码，并配以监督物体运动与参考手-物关系的互补奖励；为加速学习，采用左右手分离的actor-critic网络，并将off-policy算法FlashSAC适配到灵巧动作跟踪。

Result: 在涵盖单物体与双物体交互的50个动作基准上成功率达90%，约为所评估采样基线成功率的2.5倍，同时训练算力比所评估RL基线少至多100倍。在XHand与Sharpa Wave Hand上均取得一致增益，组件消融验证了各设计选择贡献。200、500、1000个动作的扩展实验表明方法在大规模下保持稳定并更高效。真实采集演示的定性回放展示了框架对录制人手操作数据的适用性。

Conclusion: FlashDexRetarget通过互补观测特征、奖励设计与分离式actor-critic结合FlashSAC，实现了高成功率且高算效的灵巧手动作重定向，并具备良好的跨手型泛化性与大规模扩展性。

Abstract: Human hand-object demonstrations offer a reusable source of dexterous robot manipulation data, but transferring them across embodiments requires physically feasible retargeting. Existing physics-based approaches face limitations in retargeting success, motion-specific training efficiency, or both. To address these limitations, we introduce FlashDexRetarget, an RL-based framework for high-success, efficient dexterous motion retargeting. To make the demonstrated interaction easier to learn, we combine object point-cloud observations, hand-object distance features, and future trajectory encodings with complementary rewards that supervise object motion and reference hand-object relationships. To further accelerate learning, we employ separate left- and right-hand actor critic networks and adapt the off-policy algorithm, FlashSAC to dexterous motion tracking. On a benchmark of 50 motions spanning single-object and two-object interactions, FlashDexRetarget achieves a 90% success rate, approximately 2.5x that of the evaluated sampling-based baselines, while requiring up to 100x less training compute than the evaluated RL-based baselines. Evaluations on both XHand and Sharpa Wave Hand show consistent gains, and component-wise ablations examine the contributions of our design choices. Beyond the 50-motion benchmark, experiments with 200, 500, and 1,000 motions demonstrate that our method remains stable at larger scales and produces successful retargeted motions more efficiently as the training set grows. Qualitative replay results using real-world-captured demonstrations further illustrate the applicability of our framework to recorded human manipulation. Videos and code are available at https://davian-robotics.github.io/FlashDexRetarget/

</details>


### [94] [ChunkVLA-AM: Parallel Action Chunking for Vision-Language-Action Robot Control in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01856)
*Zhugang Liu,Kaichuang Zhang,Jinman Zhang,Pu Sun,Martha Asare,Jose Hernandez,Maxim Ermolinsky,Efren Saenz,Qi Lu,Jinghao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将 OpenVLA-OFT 部署于 FAIRINO FR3 机械臂的增材制造固定工作单元，采用云边架构与分块闭环推理，在 42 次实物 A 到 B 物体搬运试验中成功 39 次（成功率 92.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型统一了视觉感知、语言理解与动作生成，为增材制造（AM）自动化带来机遇，但部署面临两大障碍：将模型适配到未见过的机器人本体成本高昂，且环境变化会导致性能退化。因此需要一套面向特定 AM 工作单元的可落地部署框架。

Method: 1) 数据管线将单目真实世界示范数据转换为 OpenVLA 兼容的 TFDS/RLDS 数据集，以适配 FR3 本体；2) 运行时每次推理请求预测 8 步的 7 维动作块，FR3 以开环方式执行整块动作后再采集新观测，形成块间闭环反馈；3) 系统采用云边架构，FR3 客户端通过 FastAPI 接口将观测流式传输至远程推理服务器。

Result: 42 次物理 A 到 B 物体搬运试验（红、蓝目标各半）中成功 39 次（92.9%）；3 次失败均发生在最终放置阶段，原因是释放高度控制不足导致物体倾倒。光照扫描确定在 0-255 尺度上 85-125 为低误差亮度区间，其中亮度 95 时平均空间误差最低。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了 OpenVLA-OFT 在 AM 固定工作单元中跨本体部署的可行性，云边分块推理架构可实现较高的实物任务成功率；后续改进重点在于释放高度控制以消除放置阶段的失败，并将运行光照约束在 85-125 亮度范围内以维持低空间误差。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models unify visual perception, language understanding, and action generation, offering new opportunities for automation in additive manufacturing (AM). However, deployment in AM remains challenging because adapting these models to unseen robot embodiments is costly, and performance can degrade under environment changes. In this work, we present a framework for deploying OpenVLA-OFT on a FAIRINO FR3 robot in a fixed AM workcell. A data pipeline converts monocular real-world demonstrations into OpenVLA-compatible TFDS/RLDS datasets to support adaptation to the FR3 embodiment. At runtime, each inference request predicts an eight-step chunk of 7-D actions. The FR3 executes each chunk open loop before capturing a new observation, providing closed-loop feedback between chunks. The system uses a cloud-edge architecture in which the FR3 client streams observations to a remote inference server through a FastAPI interface. In 42 physical A-to-B object-transfer trials, evenly split between red and blue targets, the system succeeded in 39 (92.9%). All three failures occurred during final placement, when insufficient release-height control caused the object to topple. An illumination sweep identified a low-error luminance range of 85-125 on a 0-255 scale, with the lowest mean spatial error at 95.

</details>


### [95] [Towards Physical Underwater Robotic Assistance for Scuba Diver Movement in Confined Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01906)
*Demetrious T. Kutzke,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个通过推进器驱动的可穿戴机器人RADMCS，利用单目深度估计与力反馈触觉，帮助潜水员在与海底结构（珊瑚礁、仪器、未爆物等）作业时保持固定距离。基于8名潜水员的IRB水下实验（封闭水域与海洋）验证了阈值敏感度、距离保持及形态适配，结果表明仅需最大推力10%即可实现机器人引导人体运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜水员通常被教导控制深度以避免快速升降带来的气体栓塞与气压伤等伤害，但许多水下任务（如珊瑚礁、水下钻探仪器、未爆物等海底结构附近作业）需要的是横向控制，即维持与结构的固定距离。现有推进辅助外骨骼关注的是推进助力，而非方向性引导，因此缺乏一种能提供定向引导的可穿戴机器人方案。

Method: 提出RADMCS（受限空间中的机器人辅助潜水员运动），一种可穿戴机器人：通过单目深度估计的感知技术感知与海底结构的距离，并利用潜水推进器的力反馈向潜水员施加触觉反馈，从而实现推进器驱动的方向引导。系统具有小型紧凑的外形，作为可扩展的基础平台。开展IRB批准的水下人体实验（8名潜水员参与者），包括封闭水域游泳设施与海洋环境中的阈值敏感度测试、封闭水域中的距离保持实验，以及海洋中的形态、适配与功能测试。

Result: 实验表明，相对较低的推力值（最大推力的10%）即可通过机器人引导产生的体感实现对人体运动方向的控制，验证了方案的可行性；并在封闭水域成功进行距离保持实验，在海洋环境中完成形态、适配与功能测试。

Conclusion: RADMCS是首个提供推进器驱动定向引导（区别于推进助力）的可穿戴机器人方案，其紧凑形态构成可扩展的基础平台，未来可支持更复杂的控制与导航行为；低成本推力即可实现有效的触觉方向引导。

Abstract: Scuba divers are taught to control their depth to avoid rapid ascents and descents, which could result in serious injuries such as gas embolisms and barotrauma. However, many underwater tasks necessitate lateral control, maintaining distance between subsea structures such as coral reefs, submerged drilling instrumentation, or unexploded ordnance. In this work, we discuss a first-of-its-kind wearable robotic solution providing thruster-actuated directional guidance to a diver, as distinct from prior propulsive-assistance exoskeletons. We introduce ``Robotic Assisted Diver Movement in Confined Spaces'' (RADMCS), a wearable robot that assists divers in maintaining a fixed distance from subsea structures by leveraging perception techniques in monocular depth estimation and force-feedback from submersible thrusters to provide haptic feedback. Its small and compact form factor creates a foundational platform that could be expanded to include more sophisticated control and navigation behaviors. We present results from Institutional Review Board (IRB) in-water studies with eight human scuba diver participants on threshold sensitivity tests in both a closed-water swimming facility and ocean environments; distance-maintaining experiments in a closed-water facility; and form, fit, and function testing in the ocean. We demonstrate that relatively low thrust values (10 percent of maximum) allow robotic direction of a human's movement using the physical sensation of the robot's guidance.

</details>


### [96] [Robot Learning on Discrete Surfaces: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01910)
*Matteo Dalle Vedove,Fares J. Abu-Dakka,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的离散黎曼框架，使机器人学习可直接在多面体表面网格上进行，涵盖DMP、GP与黎曼流匹配三种范式，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体表面普遍存在，但多数机器人学习与运动生成框架仅把表面当作约束，忽略其内在几何；对CAD与三维重建常用的多面体网格而言，其离散几何结构未被利用。

Method: 基于离散微分几何，定义跨面、边、顶点均良定义的指数/对数映射、平行移动与环境空间投影；并实例化于三种学习范式：(i) DMP，采用改进指数映射计算与固定切锥力项编码加平行移动；(ii) 高斯过程，构造带可容许性控制的测地核，无需光滑性假设即可在任意网格位置回归；(iii) 黎曼流匹配，使用网格原生算子。

Result: 仿真中相对最先进方法取得更优表现：DMP在跨表面泛化与稳定性上优于既有网格方法，GP可实现任意网格位置回归，RFM在生成质量上优于谱基线并缩短训练时间；并在两个真实机器人场景中验证——跨不同表面泛化用户绘制轨迹，以及在RGB-D重建表面上规划抛光运动。

Conclusion: 该离散黎曼框架统一支持多面体网格上的机器人学习与运动生成，兼顾理论与实用性，具备真实机器人应用可行性。

Abstract: All the objects composing our world are enclosed within surfaces. Yet, most robot learning and motion generation frameworks treat surfaces as constraints ignoring their intrinsic geometry. This gap is acute for polyhedral meshes--the standard output of CAD and 3D reconstruction--whose discrete geometric structure remains unexploited. In this paper, we propose a unified discrete Riemannian framework that enables robot learning directly on polyhedral surface meshes. Using discrete differential geometry, we define logarithmic and exponential maps, parallel transport, and ambient-space projections that remain well-defined across faces, edges, and vertices. We instantiate the framework in three learning paradigms: (i) Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs), an improved exponential-map computation and a fixed-tangent-cone forcing-term encoding with parallel transport yield better cross-surface generalisation and stability over prior mesh-based approaches. (ii) Gaussian Process (GP), a geodesic-based kernel with practical admissibility control, enables regression at arbitrary mesh locations without smoothness assumptions. (iii) Riemannian Flow Matching (RFM), mesh-native operators improve generative quality over spectral baselines while reducing training time. The framework is validated in simulation against state-of-the-art methods and demonstrated on two real-robot scenarios: generalising user-drawn trajectories across different surfaces and planning polishing motions on RGB-D-reconstructed surfaces.

</details>


### [97] [TouchTherm: Building Multimodal Digital Twins of Objects for Tactile and Thermal Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01943)
*Yitao Zhang,Hong Ying,Haoran Guo,Xiaoying Zhou,Guanyu Chen,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchTherm框架从真实物体构建可用于仿真的视觉-触觉-热学多模态资产：融合结构光扫描与光度立体法重建微观表面结构用于光学触觉渲染，并通过多视角红外视频重建物理正则化的动态热场，支持触觉识别与VR温度反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D数据集与重建方法主要表征物体尺度的几何与视觉外观，忽略了用于高保真触觉渲染的微观表面结构，以及用于温度感知交互的瞬态温度动态，难以满足机器人仿真与虚拟现实对触觉和热学物理线索的需求。

Method: 提出TouchTherm框架。视觉与触觉重建方面，结合结构光扫描与光度立体法获得的多视角法线图，将法线图配准到扫描几何并转换到切线空间，恢复局部微观高度场用于光学触觉渲染，粗网格用于碰撞检测。热学重建方面，在受控加热后采集自然冷却过程中的同步多视角红外视频，重建物理正则化的动态热场。

Result: 在20个物体上的实验表明：重建的微观高度场保留了主要表面结构，并恢复了粗几何之外的更高频细节；热场在30s和45s时的留出表面温度MAE分别为0.465°C和0.592°C。所得触觉资产支持从触觉观测到真实物体的合成-真实识别，基于手套的VR系统展示了空间与时间变化的温度反馈。

Conclusion: TouchTherm在多模态感官仿真与温度感知虚拟交互方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Robotic simulation and virtual reality increasingly require object assets that capture not only visual geometry but also the physical cues underlying tactile and thermal interaction. Existing 3D datasets and reconstruction methods primarily represent object-scale geometry and visual appearance, overlooking microscale surface structure for high-fidelity haptic rendering and transient temperature dynamics for temperature-aware interaction. We present TouchTherm, a framework for constructing simulation-ready visuo-tactile-thermal object assets from real-world objects. For visual and tactile reconstruction, we combine structured-light scanning with multiview normal maps obtained from photometric stereo. The normal maps are registered to the scanned geometry and transformed into tangent space to recover local micro-height fields for optical tactile rendering, while the coarse mesh handles collision detection. For thermal reconstruction, we capture synchronized multiview infrared videos of natural cooling following controlled heating and reconstruct a physics-regularized dynamic thermal field. Experiments on 20 objects show that the reconstructed micro-height fields preserve dominant surface structures and recover higher-frequency details beyond the coarse geometry, while the thermal fields achieve held-out surface-temperature MAEs of 0.465 degrees C and 0.592 degrees C at 30 s and 45 s, respectively. The resulting tactile assets support synthetic-to-real object recognition from tactile observations, while a glove-based VR system demonstrates spatially and temporally varying thermal feedback. These results highlight the potential of TouchTherm for multimodal sensory simulation and temperature-aware virtual interaction.

</details>


### [98] [Training-Free Diffusion Planning with Analytical Local Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01959)
*Michael Y. Fatemi,Jinhao Liang,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练的扩散式运动规划器，用解析局部评分替代学习到的全局轨迹评分，实现高效、平滑、无碰撞的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式规划器依赖大规模可行轨迹数据训练，导致其与地图强绑定，难以在缺乏高质量示范的场景中部署。因此需要一种无需训练、同时保留扩散迭代精修优势的规划方法。

Method: 提出免训练扩散规划器，用基于障碍、平滑度、速度与智能体间可行性等项的解析局部评分，替代学习到的全局轨迹评分。其核心观察是轨迹评分可仅由相邻路点与邻近约束之间的局部交互重建，从而得到分解式去噪过程，兼顾经典轨迹方法的优化结构与扩散模型的迭代精修行为。

Result: 在大量复杂环境和多智能体规划任务上，解析评分在有限算力下生成平滑可行轨迹。例如在 GPU 上于 6 秒内为 100+ 障碍物环境中的 300+ 智能体生成可行路径，优于强学习基线和优化基线。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了对学习式扩散规划器所需数据的要求，在复杂几何约束下兼具高效率与高质量轨迹生成能力。

Abstract: Path finding and multi-robot motion planning require trajectories that are smooth, goal-directed, and collision-free in environments with complex geometric constraints. Recent diffusion-based planners have shown that trajectory generation can be cast as iterative denoising which has opened the doors to learning-based approaches that can handle multi-modal trajectory distributions and refine entire trajectories. However, a key limitation is that diffusion planners require training on large collections of feasible trajectories, rendering them map-specific, and difficult to deploy when high-quality demonstrations are unavailable. This paper introduces a training-free diffusion-based motion planner that replaces learned global trajectory scores with analytical local scores derived from obstacle, smoothness, velocity, and inter-agent feasibility terms. The proposed idea relies on a key observation: the score of a trajectory can be reconstructed by considering only local interactions between neighboring waypoints and nearby constraints. This structure exploitation yields a decomposed denoising procedure that retains the optimization structure of classical trajectory methods while inheriting the iterative refinement behavior of diffusion models. Experiments on a large collection of complex environments and large multi-agent planning tasks show that the proposed analytical score produces smooth and feasible trajectories within limited computational costs, for example in generating feasible paths for 300+ agents in environments containing 100+ obstacles in under 6 seconds on a GPU, outperforming strong learning-based and optimization baselines, while avoiding the data requirements of learned diffusion planners.

</details>


### [99] [BLT*: Informed Belief Localization Trees for Uncertainty-Aware Planning on Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01972)
*Elliot Preston-Krebs,Abhishek Goudar,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Informed BLT*，一种基于采样的信念空间规划算法，可在具有点云观测的大规模户外数字孪生中扩展；采用2-Wasserstein度量、各向同性高斯信念，支持高效连接、转向与重连，并复用测量信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大规模户外数字孪生中点云观测下的信念空间规划问题，现有方法在扩展性和效率上受限，需避免重复传播观测并复用已计算的测量信息。

Method: 将RRT*与Informed RRT*适配到信念空间，使用2-Wasserstein（W₂）度量；假设各向同性高斯信念，使采样信念状态可高效连接，同时考虑可用信息与概率碰撞约束；支持无须反复传播观测的转向与重连；并提出生成语义标注数字孪生的框架，用于基于点云定位的真实环境规划。

Result: 在仿真环境与数字孪生中进行实验，显示在多数地图上初始解发现更快，且代价收敛具竞争力。

Conclusion: Informed BLT*有效提升信念空间规划的扩展性与效率，适用于大规模户外数字孪生中的点云观测规划任务。

Abstract: We present Informed Belief Localization Trees* (Informed BLT*), a sampling-based belief space planning (BSP) algorithm that scales to large outdoor digital twins with point-cloud observations. We adapt RRT* and Informed RRT* to belief space using the $2$-Wasserstein ($W_2$) metric. Assuming isotropic Gaussian beliefs, sampled belief states can be connected efficiently while accounting for available information and probabilistic collision constraints. This enables steering and rewiring without repeatedly propagating observations, and allows previously computed measurement information to be reused. We present a framework to generate semantically labelled digital twins for planning in real-world environments with point-cloud-based localization. Experiments in simulated environments and digital twins show faster initial solution discovery in most maps with competitive cost convergence.

</details>


### [100] [H-SPAR: Hydrodynamic-aware Simulation for Particle Transport and Autonomous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01985)
*Navid Zarrabi,Nariman Yousefi,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H-SPAR 是一个将时空变化流场、拉格朗日粒子输运、概率采样与 ROS 2/Gazebo 无人水面艇自主性统一起来的海洋机器人仿真框架，用于在一致水动力条件下联合评估任务成本与采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境机器人采样需同时考虑水流对机器人运动与粒子输运的双重影响，但现有海洋机器人仿真器通常将流场、自主性与采样目标分开建模，难以联合评估任务成本与采样性能。

Method: H-SPAR 集成时空变化速度场、拉格朗日粒子输运、概率采样以及基于 ROS 2/Gazebo 的 USV 自主性；在闭环载体执行过程中，由共享的预计算流场同时驱动粒子平流与水流诱导力。通过路径规划实验对比 SVF-RRT* 与常规 RRT*，并通过覆盖实验考察扫描方向对采样率的影响。

Result: 规划层面，水流感知规划器 SVF-RRT* 的上游成本较常规 RRT* 降低 69.4%；但在时变水流下的执行阶段，该降幅降至 41.7%，原因在于规划时忽略了流场时间变化、载体运动约束与路径偏差。在完整 H-SPAR 配置下，扫描方向变化可使粒子采样率相差最高 22.2%。

Conclusion: 应在一致水动力条件下将规划、载体执行、粒子输运与采样结合起来评估；仅凭规划层面的成本优势不足以反映实际执行性能。项目网页与开源代码已公开。

Abstract: Environmental robotic sampling requires considering the dual influence of water currents on robotic motion and particle transport. Existing marine robotics simulators generally model flow, autonomy, and sampling targets separately, limiting joint evaluation of mission cost and sampling performance. H-SPAR integrates spatially and temporally varying velocity fields, Lagrangian particle transport, probabilistic sampling, and ROS 2/Gazebo-based uncrewed surface vehicle (USV) autonomy. In this work, shared precomputed flow fields drive particle advection and current-induced forces during closed-loop vehicle execution. Path-planning experiments show that the existing current-aware planner SVF-RRT* achieves 69.4% lower upstream cost than conventional RRT* at the planning level, but this reduction falls to 41.7% during execution under time-varying currents, reflecting temporal flow variation, vehicle motion constraints, and path deviation omitted during planning. Coverage experiments show that sweep orientation changes the particle-sampling rate by up to 22.2% under the complete H-SPAR configuration. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating planning, vehicle execution, particle transport, and sampling together under consistent hydrodynamic conditions. The project webpage is available at https://sites.google.com/view/h-spar, and the open-source code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/naviiidz/h-spar-sim.

</details>


### [101] [UniWAM: Unified World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02054)
*Jiayi Chen,Wenxuan Song,Jingbo Wang,Shuai Zhou,Xicheng Gong,Zehua Fan,Ziyang Zhou,Junwu E,Haodong Yan,Fuhao Li,Qize Yu,Xu Huang,Pengwei Wang,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一架构 UniWAM，联合学习物理世界语义理解、视觉生成与动作预测，在多类评测中达到 SOTA，并发现人机协同训练的 log-linear 缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）受益于预训练 VLM 的理解与推理能力，但仅靠动作监督难以充分建模世界动力学；世界-动作模型（WAM）虽继承视频生成模型的时空先验，却在语义理解与分布偏移下的推理能力上受限。两者优势互补但未能统一。

Method: 构建统一架构 UniWAM，集成物理推理器、世界生成器与动作预测器三者联合学习。数据侧建立面向人类自我中心数据与机器人数据的严格清洗与标注流水线；用自然语言表示低层动作以适配视觉-语言组件并保留语言能力，设计预训练配方，将 VQA、人类自我中心数据与机器人示范的互补监督分配给相应组件。后训练阶段采用未来视觉噪声增强以降低对精确未来预测的依赖，并用历史条件流匹配以编码动作历史初始化动作生成，从而减少去噪步数。

Result: 在分布内性能、鲁棒性、泛化性、指令跟随及长时程任务执行等多类评测中取得 SOTA；揭示统一人-机器人协同训练的 log-linear 缩放规律，验证大规模混合数据预训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 统一语义理解、视觉生成与动作预测的一体化架构能够有效弥补 VLA 与 WAM 各自短板，语言化动作表示与互补监督预训练、后训练降噪设计共同提升效率与性能，人机混合大规模预训练具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models benefit from the understanding and reasoning capabilities of pretrained vision-language models, but action-only supervision provides limited grounding in world dynamics. Conversely, world-action models inherit spatiotemporal priors from video generation models, yet remain limited in semantic understanding and reasoning under distribution shifts. We introduce UniWAM, a unified architecture that integrates a physical reasoner, a world generator, and an action predictor to jointly learn semantic understanding of the physical world, visual generation, and action prediction. To ensure the quality of the training data, we developed a rigorous data cleaning and annotation pipeline for both human egocentric data and robot data. To adapt the vision-language component to embodied tasks while preserving its inherited language capabilities, we represent low-level actions in natural language and introduce a pre-training recipe that assigns complementary supervision from visual question answering (VQA) data, human egocentric data, and robot demonstrations to the appropriate model components. During post-training, future visual noise augmentation reduces reliance on precise future predictions, while history-conditioned flow matching uses encoded action history to initialize action generation. Together, these designs significantly reduce denoising steps while maintaining performance. UniWAM achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across multiple evaluations, including in-distribution performance, robustness, generalization, instruction following, and long-horizon task execution. Furthermore, we uncover a log-linear scaling law of unified human-robot co-training, demonstrating the effectiveness of large-scale pre-training on a mixture of human and robot data.

</details>


### [102] [HumanoidToolBench: Benchmarking Humanoid Tool Use from Selection to Mobile Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02089)
*Kyochul Jang,Seohyeon Park,Ohchul Kwon,Sangjun Park,Junhyeok Choi,Seungyeop Yi,Chaeyun Kim,Sangkyu Lee,Idan Szpektor,Avi Caciularu,Jongmin Park,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HumanoidToolBench 人形机器人工具使用基准（18 任务、3 场景、3 执行层级、2 工具集模式）与 ToolBook 数据集（3.1k 演示），揭示策略在工具选择与任务完成间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人硬件与学习方法进步，人形机器人需要工具来完成超出自身物理极限的任务。成功使用工具要求先选择合适的工具，并协调操作乃至移动来完成作业。然而现有基准未能在人形机器人上联合评估这些能力。

Method: 构建 HumanoidToolBench：覆盖三个场景、三个执行层级与两种工具集模式的 18 项任务；并采集 ToolBook 数据集，包含在仿真环境与真实 Unitree G1 上收集的 3.1k 条演示。在仿真中评测 7 个策略，在真机上评测 3 个策略，并对 GR00T N1.7 进行针对性探测。

Result: 评测显示策略在“选择合适工具”与“完成任务”之间存在显著性能差距；GR00T N1.7 探测表明：面对未见工具时选择准确率下降，并会在无关指令下仍持续执行任务。

Conclusion: 工具选择与任务执行的联合能力是人形机器人工具使用的关键瓶颈，该基准与数据集为后续研究提供了统一评估平台，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: As robotic hardware and learning methods advance, humanoids need tools to perform tasks beyond their inherent physical limits. Successful tool use requires selecting a suitable tool and coordinating manipulation and, when needed, locomotion to complete the task. Existing benchmarks do not jointly evaluate these capabilities on a humanoid. We introduce HumanoidToolBench, an 18-task benchmark spanning three scenarios, three execution levels, and two tool-set modes, together with ToolBook, a dataset of 3.1k demonstrations collected in simulation and on a real Unitree G1. Evaluation of seven policies in simulation and three on the real robot reveals substantial gaps between selecting a suitable tool and completing the task. Focused GR00T N1.7 probes show reduced selection accuracy on unseen tools and continued task execution under unrelated instructions. Code and data are available at https://snu-pi.github.io/HumanoidToolBench/.

</details>


### [103] [GlassGuard: Verified Glass Plane Mapping for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02110)
*Hanwen Guo,Zhengzhi Lin,Yusen Xie,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GlassGuard 是一个面向导航的平面建筑玻璃重建框架，融合视觉与 LiDAR 证据，在保证玻璃覆盖率的同时抑制自由空间污染，在九个楼宇级场景中实现 82% 覆盖率并显著减少错误体素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明与镜面表面对 LiDAR SLAM 与导航构成挑战：激光可能穿透玻璃，导致碰撞边界在地图中缺失。已有工作尝试重建缺失表面，但不准确的障碍物放置会产生相反失效，即污染可通行的自由空间。因此重建需同时满足两个要求：覆盖玻璃且不污染自由空间。

Method: 提出 GlassGuard，一个导航导向的平面建筑玻璃重建框架。以玻璃覆盖率与自由空间污染双重指标定义成功，并贯穿候选验证与全局地图构建。具体流程：基础视觉模型提供玻璃实例掩码；结构性 3D 线索生成度量平面假设；无深度的 2D 投影几何检验平面朝向；通过检验的平面进入统一的全局地图。

Result: 在九个楼宇级场景中评估，涵盖多样玻璃结构、空间尺度与光照条件，累计超过 1 小时、2.1 km 的真实机器人行进。全景版本达到 85% 总玻璃覆盖率；在相同针孔输入下达到 82% 覆盖率，对比基线最高仅 61%，同时每帧错误体素数减少 5-17 倍。与导航规划器结合的定性示例显示，重建平面可阻挡穿过玻璃的路径，同时保持可通行路线开放。

Conclusion: GlassGuard 通过视觉与 LiDAR 互补证据的联合验证，在玻璃覆盖与自由空间污染之间取得平衡，满足导航导向的玻璃重建需求，并在真实楼宇级场景中优于现有基线。

Abstract: Transparent and specular surfaces pose a serious challenge to LiDAR-based SLAM and navigation because laser returns may pass through glass, leaving collision boundaries absent from the map. Prior work attempts to reconstruct the missing surfaces, but inaccurate obstacle placement can create the opposite failure: contamination of traversable free space. Recognizing this dual requirement, we present GlassGuard, a navigation-oriented framework for reconstructing planar architectural glass from complementary visual and LiDAR evidence. We formulate success in terms of both glass coverage and free-space contamination and apply this principle throughout proposal verification and global map construction. A foundation vision model provides glass-instance masks, structural 3D cues generate metric plane hypotheses, and depth-free 2D projective geometry checks their orientations before they enter a consolidated global map. We evaluate GlassGuard in nine building-scale scenes spanning diverse glass structures, spatial scales, and lighting conditions, with more than one hour and 2.1 km of real-world robot traversal. GlassGuard achieves 85% of total glass coverage for its panoramic version. Under identical pinhole inputs, GlassGuard achieves 82% total coverage, compared with at most 61% for the evaluated baselines, while producing 5-17x fewer false voxels per frame. Qualitative examples with a navigation planner illustrate the reconstructed planes blocking paths through glass while leaving traversable routes open. The project page is available at https://glassguardproject.github.io/.

</details>


### [104] [SkeleWAM: Skeleton World-Action Modeling for Efficient Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02120)
*Juyi Sheng,Hua Wang,Mengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkeleWAM 用稀疏3D骨架（机器人关节、物体中心、交互点）作为世界动作模型的状态表示，在线由RGB-D与本体感知构建，支持动作生成与未来骨架预测，LIBERO-Plus上以57.1M参数达到85.9%成功率，超越Cosmos-Policy 3.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）多以视频或学习到的视觉隐变量预测未来，交互几何仅被隐式表示，且保留了与控制无关的外观信息，导致状态冗余、参数效率低。作者希望寻找一个统一、紧凑、显式几何的状态空间。

Method: 提出SkeleWAM：将操作场景表示为由机器人关节、物体中心与交互点构成的稀疏3D骨架，在线从当前RGB-D观测与机器人本体感知构建。该骨架作为统一几何状态同时驱动动作生成与未来骨架预测；未来骨架预测为动作学习提供额外几何监督，无需视觉重建。推理时直接由当前骨架与语言指令生成动作，并采用Medoid Action Consensus (MAC) 作为随机动作采样的辅助共识策略。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上，SkeleWAM仅用57.1M参数取得85.9%的总体成功率，比Cosmos-Policy高出3.7个百分点，体现了较强的鲁棒性与参数效率。

Conclusion: 稀疏3D机器人—物体结构是一种有效的状态空间，可支撑鲁棒且参数高效的世界动作学习；未来骨架预测可作为几何监督替代视觉重建，为WAM提供更贴合控制需求的状态表示。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) combine robot action generation with future state prediction. Existing WAMs typically predict videos or learned visual latents, which represent interaction geometry only implicitly and may retain appearance information unrelated to control. We introduce SkeleWAM, a compact WAM that represents a manipulation scene as a sparse 3D skeleton composed of robot joints, object centers, and interaction points. Constructed online from current RGB-D observations and robot proprioception, the skeleton provides a unified geometric state for action generation and future skeleton prediction. Future skeleton prediction provides additional geometric supervision for action learning without requiring visual reconstruction. At inference, SkeleWAM generates actions directly from the current skeleton and language instruction, while Medoid Action Consensus (MAC) serves as an auxiliary consensus strategy for stochastic action samples. On LIBERO-Plus, SkeleWAM achieves an overall success rate of 85.9% with 57.1M parameters, outperforming Cosmos-Policy by 3.7 percentage points. These results demonstrate that sparse 3D robot--object structure provides an effective state space for robust and parameter-efficient world action learning. The project is available at https://skelewam-project.github.io/.

</details>


### [105] [DuoMind: Enabling Distributed Multi-Robot Coordination with Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02161)
*Hanchu Zhou,Dechen Gao,Hang Wang,Brendan Lynch,Boqi Zhao,Qiyao Ma,Raman Goyal,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DuoMind——一种基于语义通信的分布式分层多机器人协调框架，并结合新构建的RoboPoly基准验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）与视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）推动了通用机器人的快速进展，但现有研究多集中于单机器人场景。将此类能力扩展至多机器人系统面临两大挑战：一是需要协调长时程行为，二是需保持可靠且精细的执行。此外，多机器人协调领域缺乏相应基准。

Method: 提出DuoMind分布式分层框架：每台机器人配备基于VLA的低层动作模型（负责精细执行）与基于VLM的高层编排器（负责高层推理与智能体间协调）。每个规划步中，编排器综合任务指令、局部观测及来自其他机器人的消息进行推理，随后输出给动作模型的低层指令以及发送给同伴机器人的语义消息，从而结合VLM的语义推理能力与VLA的精确动作生成能力。同时构建RoboPoly基准，包含需在分布式控制下协调闭环执行的长时程操作任务。

Result: 在RoboPoly与RoboTwin上的实验表明，DuoMind提升了多机器人任务性能；消融研究证实了分层编排与语义通信的贡献。

Conclusion: DuoMind通过融合VLM语义推理与VLA精确执行的分层架构及语义通信机制，有效提升了多机器人长时程协调能力，其有效性在自建RoboPoly基准及RoboTwin上得到验证；分层编排与语义通信为关键贡献因素。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and vision-language-action models (VLAs) have recently driven rapid progress in general-purpose robots, yet most progress has focused on single-robot settings. Extending these capabilities to multi-robot systems remains challenging because robots must coordinate long-horizon behaviors while maintaining reliable, fine-grained execution. We introduce DuoMind, a distributed hierarchical framework for multi-robot coordination through semantic communication. Each robot uses a VLA-based action model for low-level execution and a VLM-based orchestrator for high-level reasoning and inter-agent coordination. At each planning step, the orchestrator at each robot reasons over the task instruction, local observations, and messages received from other robots. It then generates low-level instructions for the action model and semantic messages for peer robots. This architecture exploits the complementary strengths of pretrained models by combining the semantic reasoning capabilities of VLMs with the precise action-generation capabilities of VLAs. To address the scarcity of benchmarks for multi-robot coordination, we further develop RoboPoly, a benchmark comprising long-horizon manipulation tasks that require coordinated, closed-loop execution under distributed control. Experiments on RoboPoly and RoboTwin demonstrate that DuoMind improves multi-robot task performance, while ablation studies confirm the contributions of hierarchical orchestration and semantic communication. More details are available on our project page.

</details>


### [106] [Watch, Infer, Coordinate: Inferring Robot Partner Constraints for Zero-Shot Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02170)
*Suyu Ye,Zheyuan Zhang,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Tianmin Shu,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Nakul Agarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出WIC（Watch, Infer, Coordinate）基准与推理方法，让助手机器人通过观察搭档与另一机器人的协作行为，推断其物理约束，并在新任务中实现零样本协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人协作中，硬件退化或执行器故障会限制机器人可可靠执行的动作，且这些限制可能对搭档未知。助手需要从协作演示中推断搭档的物理约束能力，但演示只展示"做了什么"而非"本可以做什么"；且在物理耦合任务中，另一机器人可能补偿其限制，使约束更难从被约束机器人的单独行为中识别。

Method: 核心洞察是约束会塑造团队的联合行为，因此两个机器人的动作都对被约束搭档的能力具有信息量。作者构建了WIC基准，涵盖三种物理耦合的操作设置，并提出一种推理方法，利用观测到的联合行为对候选约束进行评分。

Result: 在全部三种设置中，该方法显著提升了约束推断与零样本协调性能，接近拥有真实约束信息的oracle水平。

Conclusion: 通过观察联合协作行为推断搭档的物理约束是可行且有效的，可支持在新任务上的零样本协调，性能接近oracle。

Abstract: Robots operating in the physical world will increasingly need to coordinate with other robots, particularly in manipulation tasks where an object may be too large or heavy for a single robot to carry alone. Physical limitations caused by hardware degradation or actuator faults can restrict the actions a robot can reliably execute, yet these limitations may be unknown to its partner. We study whether a helper can infer a robot partner's physical constraints from observing it coordinate with another robot, then use the inferred capability to coordinate with the same partner on a new task. This is difficult because a demonstration shows what the constrained robot did, but not what it could have done. In physically coupled tasks, the other robot may also compensate for its limitations, making those limitations difficult to identify from the constrained robot's behavior alone. Our key insight is that these constraints shape the joint behavior of the team, making the actions of both robots informative about the constrained partner's capability. We introduce Watch, Infer, Coordinate, a benchmark spanning three physically coupled manipulation settings, together with an inference approach that scores candidate constraints using observed joint behavior. Across all three settings, our method substantially improves constraint inference and zero-shot coordination, approaching an oracle with access to the true constraints.

</details>


### [107] [InterEvolve: Test-Time Evolution of Reward Programs for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02196)
*Zhuo Lin,Sirui Xu,Liuyu Bian,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 InterEvolve 框架，利用冻结控制器在测试时通过 LLM 改写奖励程序与数值优化调参，实现人形机器人 loco-manipulation 技能的进化与复用，并成功部署于真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制器只能完成训练过的任务，面对新任务需重新训练。作者认为广谱控制器本身已蕴含新任务所需的大部分能力，关键在于设计一个既能表达接触丰富、多阶段交互，又具备可执行性与可度量性、可借助执行反馈指导规划的"规划—控制"接口。

Method: InterEvolve 包含两部分：1) 物体感知的前向-后向（FB）行为基础模型，其在冻结身体先验上的物体残差能将关于身体或物体的新奖励在测试时转化为 loco-manipulation 行为；2) 以奖励程序（带完成条件的分阶段奖励及可调常数）定义任务，由 LLM 代理基于执行反馈和已验证程序技能库改写程序结构，数值优化器调整常数，候选程序在并行仿真场景中验证并迭代改进。

Result: 实验表明人工设计的奖励未能充分释放 FB 模型的 loco-manipulation 能力，而 InterEvolve 进化出的程序可释放该能力并产生新策略；在仿真中可为多样任务、复杂场景和长时程组合生成行为，进化技能可基于自视角机载感知在真实 Unitree G1 上自主运行。

Conclusion: 通过规划与控制的表达性接口结合 LLM 结构搜索与数值调参，可在不重训的前提下实现人形机器人技能的测试时进化与复用，并具备从仿真到真实硬件的迁移能力。

Abstract: We study test-time evolution for humanoid loco-manipulation: solving tasks that a controller was never trained for by repurposing its existing skills, improving from its own attempts, and retaining what it learns, without retraining. Our key insight is that a broad controller already holds much of the competence a new task needs, and that this competence becomes accessible through an interface between planning and control that is expressive enough to specify contact-rich, multi-stage interactions, yet executable and measurable enough that execution feedback can guide planning from experience. InterEvolve realizes this interface with two components. First, we develop an object-aware forward-backward (FB) behavioral foundation model, whose object residuals on a frozen body prior turn a new reward about the body or objects into loco-manipulation behavior at test time. Second, we specify tasks as reward programs: staged rewards with completion conditions and tunable constants. A large language model (LLM) agent revises the program structure in context, drawing on execution feedback and a skill library of verified programs, while a numerical optimizer tunes its constants. With every candidate verified across parallel simulation scenarios, the program explores new ways to induce, repurpose, and compose the controller's existing motor competence for the task at hand, and thus improves over iterations. Experiments show that human-designed rewards leave much of the FB model's loco-manipulation competence untapped, whereas the programs InterEvolve evolves release it, sometimes through novel strategies. It further produces behaviors for diverse tasks, complex scenes, and long-horizon compositions in simulation, and evolved skills run autonomously on a physical Unitree G1 from egocentric onboard perception.

</details>


### [108] [Reconstruct, Practice, Go Real: Guided Self-Improvement for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02204)
*Yen-Jen Wang,Haozhe Jiang,Shuying Deng,Haoru Xue,Weirui Ye,Rocky Duan,Nika Haghtalab,S. Shankar Sastry,Pieter Abbeel,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RPG框架，通过离线数据构建仿真练习任务、利用执行反馈与特权状态诊断失败，自主改进符号技能与系统提示，冻结权重下将22项操作任务成功率从28.6%提升至95.0%，并在30次真机试验中全部成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建跨多样任务的可靠机器人能力需要大量人力来开发与维护技能、设计奖励，并将感知与控制集成。论文希望在不更新模型权重的前提下，实现机器人执行系统的自主改进，降低人力成本。

Method: RPG（Reconstruct, Practice, Go Real）框架：从离线数据集中识别可操作能力，并在仿真中构建相关练习任务；练习阶段利用执行反馈、仿真器特权状态和数据集视频诊断失败，据此开发新的可复用符号技能、细化已有技能并修订系统提示；通过跨任务评估对单个候选改动与合并修订进行检验后再保留复用；测试时由多模态LLM使用所得系统提示与技能库协调感知与机器人控制。

Result: 在22项操作任务的留出初始条件上，成功率从首轮练习后的28.6%提升至15轮后的95.0%，优于所有评估基线，包括ASPIRE（75.5%）和由GPT-6 Astra Pro驱动的CaP-Agent0（60.0%）。经统一的标定与硬件适配流程后，冻结系统在全部30次物理试验中成功，涵盖三项任务各十次试验。

Conclusion: 无需更新模型权重，仅通过仿真练习中的自主诊断与技能、提示的迭代修订，即可显著提升机器人操作性能并有效迁移到真实硬件。

Abstract: Building reliable robot capabilities across diverse tasks requires substantial human effort to develop and maintain skills, design rewards, and integrate perception with control. We present Reconstruct, Practice, Go Real (RPG), a framework for autonomous improvement of robot execution systems without updating model weights. RPG identifies manipulation capabilities in an offline dataset and constructs related practice tasks in simulation. During practice, RPG uses execution feedback, privileged simulator state, and available dataset videos to diagnose failures. It develops new reusable symbolic skills, refines existing skills, and revises the system prompt based on these diagnoses. Cross-task evaluation tests individual candidate changes and merged revisions before they are retained for reuse. At test time, a multimodal LLM uses the resulting system prompt and skill library to coordinate perception and robot control. On held-out initializations of 22 manipulation tasks, RPG improves task success from 28.6% after the first practice round to 95.0% after 15 rounds, outperforming all evaluated baselines, including ASPIRE (75.5%) and CaP-Agent0 powered by GPT-6 Astra Pro (60.0%). After a common calibration and hardware-adaptation procedure, the frozen system succeeds in all 30 physical trials, with ten trials on each of three tasks. Project Website: https://rpg-robot.github.io/

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Integrating a Novel Kumaraswamy-Teissier Distribution with VARMA: A Hybrid Framework for Rainfall Modeling and Forecasting in the Northwest Himalayas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00060)
*Kamana Mishra,Neeraj Poonia,Tanmay Kayal,Sarita Azad*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出KTD-VARMA混合框架用于山区降雨建模：先构建三参数Kumaraswamy-Teissier分布（KTD）刻画极端降雨并用MLE、MPS与贝叶斯MCMC估计参数，再将KTD作为正态化变换结合VARMA建模时空依赖，实现更精确的多站点预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山区降雨建模需要能同时刻画强偏态与极端值的灵活统计工具，而传统模型在极端值拟合与多站点时空依赖建模上存在不足。

Method: 基于Kumaraswamy-G生成元构造三参数KTD分布，推导其统计性质，采用最大似然、最大乘积间距及贝叶斯MCMC方法估计参数并给出可信区间与HPD区间；将KTD作为偏态降雨序列的正态化变换，对变换后数据建立VARMA模型以捕捉时空依赖。

Result: KTD优于其子模型；重现期分析显示明显空间差异（Dehradun极端最频繁，Mandi与Kangra中等，Shimla与Nainital极端月总量重现期较长）；经季节调整后，KTD-VARMA预测精度高于原始数据模型与单变量ARIMA，VARMA的RMSE最低。

Conclusion: KTD-VARMA框架是兼具极值分析与多站点改进预测的综合工具，为山区水文风险评估提供了稳健方法。

Abstract: Rainfall modeling in mountainous regions requires flexible statistical tools capable of capturing strong skewness and extremes. This study proposes a novel hybrid KTD-VARMA framework to address this challenge. The framework first introduces the Kumaraswamy-Teissier Distribution (KTD), a new three-parameter model derived via the Kumaraswamy-G generator, to statistically characterize extreme rainfall. Its properties are derived, and parameters are estimated via maximum likelihood, maximum product spacing, and a Bayesian (MCMC) approach along with credible and HPD intervals. The KTD provides a superior fit compared to its sub-models. The return-period analysis highlights clear spatial contrasts: Dehradun experiences the most frequent extremes, Mandi and Kangra exhibit moderate extremal behavior, while Shimla and Nainital display long return periods for extreme monthly totals. Further, the KTD serves as a normalizing transformation for the skewed rainfall series and KTD-transformed data are then modeled using a Vector Autoregressive Moving Average (VARMA) model to capture spatio-temporal dependencies. This integrated methodology, presented here for the first time, yields more accurate forecasts after seasonal adjustment than models using raw data or univariate ARIMA, with VARMA achieving the lowest RMSE. The work establishes the KTD-VARMA framework as a comprehensive tool for both extreme value analysis and improved multi-site forecasting, providing a robust approach for hydrological risk assessment in mountainous regions.

</details>


### [110] [Counting the Uncounted: Population-Level Surveillance of Documented Pregnancy and Fetal Harm in Police Crash Narratives with a System One Model (Jev)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00213)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究首次在人口尺度上通读德克萨斯州2017–2025年全部5,018,079份警方碰撞叙述，借助正则筛选与校准决策模型Jev识别孕妇涉事碰撞，估计出约5,467起记录妊娠的碰撞（95%区间5,306–5,577），其中58起记录胎儿伤害，并给出孕龄15–49岁女性驾驶员的碰撞率及与生命统计的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机动车碰撞是美国创伤性胎儿死亡的主要原因，但现有碰撞数据库不记录妊娠状态。已有估计来源有限：全国调查样本置信区间宽、创伤登记仅覆盖受伤者、少数州的记录链接。警方叙述在现场需要时会记录妊娠信息，但尚无研究在人口规模上阅读这些文本。

Method: 通读德克萨斯州2017–2025年全部5,018,079份警方碰撞叙述；用正则表达式筛出6,840个候选；由校准的System One决策模型Jev按八问模式阅读候选（仅返回概率、不返回文本），确认5,333份记录孕妇涉事，并提取角色、孕周与碰撞后状况；对剩余文本抽取499,306份做"存在性"判读以估计漏筛；对按概率分层的样本进行盲法人工裁定，得到加权灵敏度0.999、特异度1.000；再用Rogan-Gladen估计量与bootstrap推断总体。

Result: 确认5,333份叙述记录妊娠；校正漏筛后估计5,467起记录妊娠的碰撞，95%区间5,306–5,577，其中58起记录胎儿伤害。个体层面记录得到每1,000名15–49岁女性驾驶员1.13–1.45起的碰撞率。对照生命统计的活产期望，叙述仅记录到预期怀孕驾驶员的2.56%–3.09%。记录病例状态与驾驶员本人记录的受伤关联最强，比值比21.7。

Conclusion: 警方碰撞叙述可在人口尺度上提供妊娠涉事碰撞的补充监测信息，但记录覆盖率远低于活产期望，且高度依赖现场是否记录到驾驶员本人受伤，说明叙述记录存在显著选择性，需与生命统计等来源结合解读。

Abstract: Motor-vehicle crashes are a leading cause of traumatic fetal death in the United States, yet no crash database records pregnancy. Existing estimates come from national investigation samples with wide intervals, trauma registries seeing only the injured, or record linkage in a few states. Police narratives record pregnancy whenever it matters at the scene, and no study has read them at population scale. We read every police crash narrative in Texas from 2017 through 2025, 5,018,079 in total. A regular-expression screen flags 6,840 candidates, which Jev, a calibrated System One decision model, reads under an eight-question schema, returning probabilities and no text. It confirms 5,333 as documenting a pregnant person involved in the crash, with role, stage and post-crash condition. The same model reads 499,306 of the remainder under the presence question, to estimate what the screen missed. Blind human adjudication of a probability-stratified sample gives weighted sensitivity 0.999 and specificity 1.000, from which the Rogan-Gladen estimator and a bootstrap give 5,467 crashes with a documented pregnancy, with a 95% interval of 5,306 to 5,577, including 58 crashes documenting fetal harm. Person-level records give a rate of 1.13 to 1.45 per 1,000 female drivers aged 15 to 49. Measured against a live-birth expectation from vital statistics, narratives document 2.56% to 3.09% of expected pregnant drivers, and documented-case status is most strongly associated with the driver's own recorded injury, at an odds ratio of 21.7.

</details>


### [111] [Efficient Convex Optimization Methods for State-Space Heartbeat Dynamics Models with Gamma Generalized Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00884)
*Sabrina Liu,Andrew S. Perley,Todd P. Coleman*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种心跳动力学的动态状态空间统计模型，用凸优化高效求解最优状态估计，用于评估自主神经与心血管健康。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部平均方法难以捕捉心率模式中的急剧瞬变，需要一种能同时刻画心跳动力学并支持生理意义分析的统计框架。

Method: 构建状态空间模型，将Gauss-Markov过程先验与带log-link函数的gamma发射分布结合；用静态模型估计参数，采用交替方向乘子法高效求解最大后验潜状态估计；通过偏自相关函数在模型规模与拟合度间权衡进行模型选择。

Result: 合成心跳数据中模型能准确恢复潜在权重；在倾斜床数据集上比传统局部平均方法更好地捕捉心率模式的急剧瞬变；结合呼吸相位等信息可提取信息论指标（如呼吸性窦性心律不齐）以量化器官间耦合。

Conclusion: 该心跳动力学建模统计框架高效且能提供具有生理意义的洞见。

Abstract: Objective: This work proposes a dynamic state-space statistical model of heartbeat dynamics whose optimal state estimate can be solved efficiently with convex optimization and used to assess autonomic and cardiovascular health. Methods: This state-space model combines a Gauss-Markov process prior with gamma emissions using a log-link function. Parameters are estimated using a static version of the model, and alternating direction method of multipliers is used to efficiently find the optimal maximum a priori latent state estimate. Model selection is performed by finding the tradeoff between model size and model fit using the partial autocorrelation function. Results: We demonstrate in synthetically generated heartbeats that this model is able to closely recover the underlying latent weights. Then, we show using a tilt table dataset that this statistical model is able to better capture sharp transients in heart rate patterns than traditional local averaging methods. In addition, combining heart beat timings with information from other organs such as the respiratory phase, we show that our statistical modeling framework enables the extraction of information-theoretic measures to quantify inter-organ coupling such as respiratory sinus arrythmia. Conclusion: This statistical framework for modeling heartbeat dynamics provides an efficient framework that can provide physiologically meaningful insights. Significance: This work broadens potential tools that can be used to assess heartbeat dynamics.

</details>


### [112] [On relations between the Cochran--Mantel--Haenszel test and Augmented Inverse Probability Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01362)
*Ekkehard Glimm*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文从因果推断视角重新诠释Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel（CMH）检验，证明其统计量是平均治疗效应AIPW估计量的标准化形式，并比较条件与无条件方差，说明在等比例分配下CMH检验对弱零假设渐近保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验中因果推断方法日益普及，而经典CMH检验传统上仅被解释为分层logistic回归中共同优势比的检验，缺乏因果推断意义上的解读，需厘清其与AIPW等因果估计方法的关系。

Method: 针对二值终点、各层治疗分配比例相同的分层随机试验，将CMH统计量的分子与总体平均因果风险差的AIPW估计量相联系，并比较该估计量的条件方差与无条件方差，讨论方差估计及其对检验的影响。

Result: CMH检验统计量可视为AIPW因果平均治疗效应估计量的标准化版本，不依赖共同优势比模型；在等治疗分配下，CMH检验对零平均治疗效应的弱零假设是渐近保守的；并明确了经典条件CMH检验与Wald型AIPW检验之间的关系。

Conclusion: CMH检验具有更一般的因果推断解释，其有效性不局限于共同优势比假设；实际应用中应认识其在弱零假设下的渐近保守性，并正确理解其与Wald型AIPW检验的联系。

Abstract: This note revisits the Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel (CMH) test in light of the increasing use of causal inference methods in clinical trials. It shows that the CMH test statistic can be interpreted as a standardized version of the augmented inverse probability weighted (AIPW) estimate of the causal average treatment effect. Consequently, it is not restricted to an interpretation as a test of the common-odds ratio in a stratified logistic regression model. The note then discusses conditional and unconditional variances for this estimator as well as variance estimation and its implications for testing. The results clarify the relationship between the classical conditional CMH test and Wald-type AIPW tests, and show that, under equal treatment allocation, the CMH test is asymptotically conservative for the weak null hypothesis of zero average treatment effect. %This note revisits the Cochran--Mantel--Haenszel (CMH) test in light of the increasing use of causal inference methods in clinical trials. For stratified randomized trials with binary endpoints and a common treatment allocation fraction across strata, we show that the numerator of the CMH statistic is proportional to an augmented inverse probability weighted estimator of the population-average causal risk difference. This provides an interpretation of the CMH test that does not depend on the common-odds-ratio model often used to motivate it. We then compare conditional and unconditional variance concepts for this estimator and discuss their implications for testing. The results clarify the relationship between the classical conditional CMH test and Wald-type AIPW tests, and show that, under equal treatment allocation, the CMH test is asymptotically conservative for the weak null hypothesis of zero average treatment effect.

</details>
