### [113] [Emergence of electronic modes and triplet pairing from spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulators in the Kanamori-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00110)
*Masanori Kohno*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过研究双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，阐明了自旋为1的反铁磁绝缘体的能带结构如何以及为何随掺杂和温度发生变化，并揭示了轨道简并体系中涌现电子模的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能带绝缘体在掺杂（化学势移动）或升温时能带结构基本不变，而Hubbard和Kondo晶格模型描述的Mott与Kondo绝缘体却会从带边向能隙中涌现具有动量偏移磁色散关系的电子模式。为理解大自旋反铁磁绝缘体中自旋、电荷与轨道自由度的相互作用如何导致涌现电子模式，作者开展本研究。

Method: 采用双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，结合弱跳跃有效理论、选择定则分析和数值计算进行系统研究。还考察了自旋或电荷扰动下非平衡态中电子模式的涌现与特性。

Result: 在典型参数区间，掺杂诱导的各轨道模式分别反映不同类型的自旋模式，而温度诱导的模式在两个轨道中主要反映低能自旋模式，两者均呈现动量偏移的自旋模式色散关系。若跳跃参数符号不同，涌现模式的动量区间可在轨道间存在差异。对于较小的轨道间库仑排斥，掺杂后可形成在位自旋三重态对，导致破对能隙，而温度诱导的电子模式仍反映低能自旋模式且无破对能隙。

Conclusion: 研究全面揭示了自旋、电荷与轨道自由度在大自旋反铁磁绝缘体中的相互作用如何导致涌现电子模式，为探索强关联体系中的非常规量子现象开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The electronic band structures of conventional band insulators generally remain unchanged under doping via chemical-potential shifts or increasing temperature. However, in Mott and Kondo insulators described by models such as the Hubbard and Kondo lattice models, distinct electronic modes with momentum-shifted magnetic dispersion relations have been shown to emerge from band edges into the gap. Here, by investigating the two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model, this study elucidates how and why the band structures of spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulators change with doping and temperature, revealing emergent spectral features characteristic of orbitally degenerate systems. The nature of the emergent electronic modes is clarified using weak-hopping effective theory, selection-rule analysis, and numerical calculations. In a typical parameter regime, the doping-induced modes in each orbital reflect distinct types of spin modes, while the temperature-induced modes primarily reflect the low-energy spin mode in both orbitals; in both cases, the emergent modes exhibit momentum-shifted spin-mode dispersion relations. The momentum regimes of these emergent modes can differ between orbitals if signs of the hopping parameters differ. For small inter-orbital repulsion, onsite spin-triplet pairs can form after doping, leading to a pair-breaking gap, while the temperature-induced electronic modes continue to reflect the low-energy spin mode without a pair-breaking gap. Moreover, the emergence and characteristics of electronic modes in nonequilibrium states by spin or charge perturbations are elucidated. These findings provide a comprehensive understanding of how the interplay among the spin, charge, and orbital degrees of freedom in large-spin antiferromagnetic insulators leads to emergent electronic modes, opening avenues for exploring unconventional quantum phenomena in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [114] [Causality violations in cluster GW+DMFT: Exact Lehmann moments and the necessity of non-local vertex corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00324)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文以最小 Hubbard 团簇的精确对角化为基准，严格检验 $GW$ 与 $GW$+DMFT 自能的解析结构，证明在阻挫三角环上二者均违反 Matsubara 因果性条件，说明仅注入局域自能不足以恢复因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $GW$ 近似已知违反因果性，会在复频率上半平面产生非物理极点；$GW$+DMFT 试图通过注入精确局域自能来修正这一问题，但该混合方案能否真正保证因果性缺乏严格检验，尤其在具有阻挫的团簇上。

Method: 在最小 Hubbard 团簇上采用精确对角化：先分析二格点 dimer，再研究非二分的三格点 Hubbard 环；通过解析求解精确 Lehmann 矩，推导自能高频尾的闭式表达式 $C=U^2\mathrm{Var}(d)I$，并对比 $GW$+DMFT 中 $Λ$ 的局域截断与完全不可约顶点的精确渐近行为。作者以无插值的实轴精确自能作为主要因果性判据，解析延拓作为辅助证据。

Result: 发现"dimer anomaly"：二格点 dimer 上反演对称性迫使非局域自能为纯实数，从而掩盖了因果性破坏。在三格点阻挫环上，非局域自能出现有限虚部，$GW$ 与 $GW$+DMFT 均违反 Matsubara 因果性条件。$GW$+DMFT 虽满足必要的高频条件 $C\succeq0$，却在有限频率处仍违反符号条件；其局域截断的 $Λ$ 未能修正非局域 RPA 共振。两项诊断逻辑上相互独立。

Conclusion: 局域自能修正不足以保证阻挫团簇上的因果解析结构；高频条件与有限频率符号条件相互独立，仅满足前者并不充分。这为 $GW$+DMFT 等混合方法的适用范围提供了严格的反例基准。

Abstract: The $GW$ approximation is known to violate causality, yielding self-energies with unphysical poles in the upper half of the complex frequency plane. The combined $GW$+DMFT approach attempts to cure this by injecting the exact local self-energy. Using exact diagonalization on minimal Hubbard clusters, we rigorously benchmark the analytic structure of the $GW$ and $GW$+DMFT self-energies. We demonstrate a ``dimer anomaly'': on the two-site dimer, inversion symmetry forces the non-local self-energy to be purely real, masking the causality violation. Moving to the non-bipartite three-site Hubbard ring, where hopping frustration yields a finite imaginary part in the non-local self-energy, we prove that both $GW$ and $GW$+DMFT violate the Matsubara causality condition. By analytically evaluating the exact Lehmann moments, we derive a closed-form expression for the high-frequency tail of the self-energy, $C = U^2\,\mathrm{Var}(d)\,I$, and show that standard methods fail to capture the exact equal-time two-particle correlators required to enforce it. The equal-time correlators that govern this tail are precisely those encoded in the high-frequency asymptotics of the fully irreducible vertex $Λ$; nevertheless, our intermediate-frequency analysis demonstrates that the local-only truncation of $Λ$ in $GW$+DMFT leaves non-local RPA resonances uncorrected. The two diagnostics are logically independent: $GW$+DMFT satisfies the necessary high-frequency condition $C\succeq0$ yet still violates the Matsubara sign condition at finite frequencies. Taking the interpolation-free real-axis evaluation of the exact self-energy as the primary causality certificate, with rational continuation as supporting evidence, we establish a rigorous benchmark demonstrating that local self-energy corrections are insufficient to guarantee a causal analytic structure on frustrated clusters.

</details>


### [115] [Anyon Proliferation and Anyon Superconductivity in Higgsing Transitions via Conformal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00452)
*Diego García-Sepúlveda,Da-Chuan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于共形嵌入讨论 (2+1) 维 Chern-Simons Higgsing 相变及其与任意子增殖的关系。相变在减小规范群的同时保持或增大内禀拓扑序；当拓扑序增大时，两相通过任意子凝聚区分，并由 Abelian 拓扑序过渡到非 Abelian 拓扑序。作者分析了 SO(N)_2 ↔ SU(N)_1 家族与 Spin(16)_1 → Spin(9)_2 相变，提出未 Higgs 相中候选任意子与 Higgs 相可凝聚通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 Chern-Simons Higgsing 相变如何在规范群减小时保持或增大内禀拓扑序，并借助共形嵌入理解任意子增殖以及从 Abelian 到非 Abelian 拓扑序的转变机制。特别是刻画 SO(N)_2 ↔ SU(N)_1 家族中两半经典区域拓扑序相同但整体 U(1) 对称性实现不同的情形。

Method: 采用共形嵌入方法构造 (2+1)d Chern-Simons Higgsing 相变；分析 SO(N)_2 ↔ SU(N)_1 家族（N≥3）与 Spin(16)_1 → Spin(9)_2 相变；指出标量表示相对 UV Chern-Simons 能级非整（non-integrable），故不能直接标记未 Higgs 相任意子；借助启发式伴随屏蔽（adjoint screening）提出未 Higgs 相候选任意子；通过 Higgs 子群下的分支（branching）确定 Higgs 相可凝聚任意子的通道。

Result: 在 SO(N)_2 ↔ SU(N)_1 家族中，两半经典区域具有等价内禀拓扑序但有不同整体 U(1) 对称性实现。对奇 N，这些是从费米子 1/N Laughlin 态到电荷 2e 任意子超导（伴随相同手征 Z_N 拓扑序）的非 Abelian 相变实现。构造了 Spin(16)_1 → Spin(9)_2 相变，其可凝聚代数包含一个非 Abelian 任意子，但该任意子自融合中并不包含所有允许的可规范（gaugable）通道。

Conclusion: Chern-Simons Higgsing 相变可通过共形嵌入统一描述，任意子增殖与任意子凝聚决定拓扑序的保持或增大。SO(N)_2 ↔ SU(N)_1 与 Spin(16)_1 → Spin(9)_2 例子表明，未 Higgs 相任意子需借助伴随屏蔽等启发式方法识别，而 Higgs 相可凝聚任意子由 Higgs 子群分支给出；某些可凝聚代数可含非 Abelian 任意子而不含全部可规范通道。

Abstract: We discuss (2+1)d Chern-Simons Higgsing transitions based on conformal embeddings and their relation to the proliferation of anyons. These transitions preserve or enlarge the intrinsic topological order despite reducing the gauge group. When the topological order is enlarged, the two phases differ by anyon condensation, and the transition takes an Abelian topological order to a non-Abelian one. In the $SO(N)_{2} \hookrightarrow SU(N)_{1}$ family with $N \geq 3$, the two semiclassical regimes have equivalent intrinsic topological orders but distinct realizations of a global $U(1)$ symmetry. For odd $N$, these are non-Abelian realizations of transitions from fermionic $1/N$ Laughlin states to charge-$2e$ anyon superconductivity coexisting with the same chiral $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ topological order. The scalar representations are always non-integrable with respect to the UV Chern-Simons level and therefore do not directly label anyons of the un-Higgsed phase, despite defining Wilson lines in the UV. Using heuristic adjoint screening, we propose candidate anyons in the un-Higgsed phase, while branching under the Higgs subgroup reveals channels corresponding to condensable anyons of the Higgs phase. We further construct a $\mathrm{Spin}(16)_{1} \to \mathrm{Spin}(9)_{2}$ transition admitting a condensable algebra that contains a non-Abelian anyon but not all of the allowed gaugable channels in its self-fusion.

</details>


### [116] [Near-infrared magnetic linear and non-reciprocal directional dichroism in the chiral antiferromagnet SmFe3(BO3)4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00534)
*B. Beke,B. Szász,I. A. Gudim,D. Szaller,S. Bordács*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用偏振分辨磁光光谱研究SmFe3(BO3)4中反铁磁序对近红外光学响应的影响，观察到Sm3+ 4f-4f跃迁的磁线性二色性、磁场可控性及非互易方向二色性，可用于探测反铁磁序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究反铁磁序如何影响稀土铁硼酸盐SmFe3(BO3)4的近红外光学响应，并寻找探测反铁磁序参量面内取向的光学手段。

Method: 采用偏振分辨磁光光谱技术，在奈尔温度以下测量Sm3+ 4f-4f跃迁的磁线性二色性；通过磁场旋转易面反铁磁基态的序参量，推导偏振选择定则；并在对称性允许的环面几何下探测非互易方向二色性。

Result: 在奈尔温度以下观察到Sm3+ 4f-4f跃迁显著的磁线性二色性；通过磁场旋转序参量实现线性二色性的磁场调控，并确定偏振选择定则；在面内磁场垂直于二重旋转轴时于环面几何中检测到若干4f-4f跃迁的非互易吸收。

Conclusion: 非互易吸收表明电偶极与磁偶极激发之间存在强干涉；所观测的光学各向异性为探测反铁磁序的面内取向提供了可行途径，可实现稀土铁硼酸盐中反铁磁序参量的成像与时间分辨研究。

Abstract: We investigated the influence of antiferromagnetic (AFM) order on the near-infrared optical re- sponse of SmFe3(BO3)4 by polarization-resolved, magneto-optical spectroscopy. Below the Néel temperature, we observed pronounced magnetic linear dichroism at the 4f -4f transitions of Sm3+. By rotating the order parameter of the easy-plane AFM ground state using magnetic fields, we demonstrated the magnetic field control of linear dichroism, and deduced polarization selection rules. In addition, we detected non-reciprocal directional dichroism for several 4f -4f transitions. The non-reciprocal absorption appears in the symmetry-allowed toroidal geometry when the in- plane magnetic field is normal to one of the two-fold rotation axes. These results indicate a strong interference between electric- and magnetic-dipole excitations for transitions showing non-reciprocal absorption. The observed optical anisotropies provide a promising path to detect the in-plane orien- tation of the AFM order, enabling imaging and time-resolved studies of the AFM order parameter in rare-earth ferroborates.

</details>


### [117] [Mechanism of Incommensurate Magnetic Order in $\mathrm{BaCo_2(AsO_4)_2}$: Interplay of Frustrated Further-Neighbor Exchanges and Bond-Directional Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00626)
*Mohammad-Hossein Zare,Mehdi Biderang,Hamid Mosadeq*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展的 J1-K-Γ-Γ'-J2-J3 模型结合第一性原理、Luttinger-Tisza 解析与扭曲边界精确对角化，揭示 BaCo2(AsO4)2 零场非公度螺旋磁序源于交换阻挫与键各向异性的协同作用，而非异常占优的 Kitaev 耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝钴酸盐 BaCo2(AsO4)2 的零场非公度磁序微观机制尚未解决，尤其是键向 Kitaev 型相互作用与交换阻挫的相对贡献存在争议。

Method: 构建含 XXZ 型交换各向异性的扩展 J1-K-Γ-Γ'-J2-J3 模型，用第一性原理约束最近邻参数，并结合 Luttinger-Tisza 解析方法与扭曲边界条件下的精确对角化，绘制 (J2, J3) 参数空间的经典与量子相图。

Result: 沿 Γ→M 方向传播的非公度螺旋相并非需要异常主导的 Kitaev 耦合，而可由远邻耦合 (J2, J3) 驱动的交换阻挫与中等离对角键各向异性 (Γ, Γ') 的协同作用自然产生；量子区间中，该非公度态与面外铁磁 (FMz) 相竞争，FMz 经量子序由无序机制选择性稳定。

Conclusion: 研究调和了关于 BaCo2(AsO4)2 磁相互作用相互矛盾的解释，确立了其非公度基态的微观起源与稳定范围。

Abstract: The microscopic mechanism governing the zero-field incommensurate magnetic order in the honeycomb cobaltate $\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2$ remains a significant unresolved problem, particularly concerning the relative contributions of bond-directional Kitaev-type interactions and exchange frustration. This study investigates an extended $J_1\text{--}K\text{--}Γ\text{--}Γ'\text{--}J_2\text{--}J_3$ model, which incorporates XXZ-type exchange anisotropies on the honeycomb lattice. Nearest-neighbor parameters are constrained by ab initio electronic structure calculations. By integrating the analytical Luttinger-Tisza approach with exact diagonalization calculations under twisted boundary conditions, we delineate the classical and quantum phase diagrams across the $(J_2, J_3)$ parameter space. We demonstrate that the stabilization of the experimentally observed incommensurate spiral phase, propagating along the $Γ\to M$ direction, does not necessitate an anomalously dominant Kitaev coupling. Instead, this phase arises naturally from the synergistic interplay between exchange frustration, driven by further-neighbor couplings ($J_2, J_3$), and intermediate off-diagonal bond anisotropies ($Γ, Γ'$). In the quantum regime, we elucidate the competition between this incommensurate manifold and an out-of-plane ferromagnetic ($\mathrm{FM}_z$) phase, which is selectively stabilized via a quantum order-by-disorder mechanism. Our findings reconcile conflicting interpretations of the magnetic interactions in $\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2$ and establish the microscopic origin and stability range of its incommensurate ground state.

</details>


### [118] [The half-filled optical Su-Schrieffer-Heeger-Hubbard model with uniaxial strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00681)
*Jonah Huang,James Neuhaus,Benjamin Cohen-Stead,Steven Johnston,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用各向异性跃迁模拟单轴应变下的SSH-Hubbard模型，在半满条件下绘制了电子-声子耦合与Hubbard耦合平面内的相图，确定了键序波(BOW)形成的临界温度，并给出BOW相中方向依赖的能隙与谱函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变可通过改变键长、跃迁幅度和晶格对称性直接调控电子相，而SSH哈密顿量描述的电子-声子耦合体系对应变场尤为敏感，因此适合系统研究应变诱导效应。应变会破坏90°旋转对称性并改变BOW简并度，但该效应对半满相图与临界温度的影响尚需明确刻画。

Method: 采用各向异性跃迁来建模单轴应变，破坏90°旋转对称性并将BOW相简并度由四种降到两种；同时引入在位Hubbard U以描述反铁磁关联（SSH相互作用本身亦微弱驱动该关联）。在半满条件下，以电子-声子耦合与Hubbard耦合为平面绘制相图，计算BOW形成的临界温度，并通过解析延拓评估谱函数。

Result: 单轴应变将BOW相简并度降至两种；确定了半满相图中BOW区域及其临界温度；谱函数显示BOW相中存在方向依赖的能隙形成。

Conclusion: 应变作为调控参量可有效解除BOW相的部分简并并诱导各向异性的能隙行为，SSH-Hubbard模型为理解应变调控的电子-声子耦合相提供了可计算的框架。

Abstract: Strain offers a direct route to control electronic phases by altering bond lengths, hopping amplitudes, and lattice symmetries. These aspects make electron-phonon (e-ph) coupled systems a natural setting to study strain-induced effects. The Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) Hamiltonian describes the coupling between itinerant electrons and lattice degrees of freedom that modulate hopping and is thus expected to be strongly affected by strain fields. At half-filling and on a bipartite lattice, the SSH interactions drive dominant bond-order-wave (BOW) correlations at low- temperature in which short bonds with high kinetic energy alternate with long bonds with low kinetic energy along four possible patterns. We model uniaxial strain through anisotropic hopping, which breaks the $90^\circ$ rotational symmetry and reduces the BOW phase degeneracy to two. We also include an on-site Hubbard U that promotes antiferromagnetic correlations that are also (weakly) driven by the SSH interaction itself. We map out the half-filled phase diagram in the plane of the e-ph and Hubbard couplings, and determine the critical temperature of BOW formation. Using analytic continuation, we also evaluate the spectral function and demonstrate directionally dependent gap formation in the BOW phase

</details>


### [119] [Local geometry of the Fermi surface and its effect on the electronic characteristics of normal metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00810)
*N. A. Zimbovskaya*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文考察费米面几何中的精细特征（如近圆柱形、近抛物面形条带或局部平坦化）对金属电子性质（尤其是磁电输运性质）的影响，指出这些特征在特定条件下可显著改变常规金属或层状导电结构对高频外场的响应，且这些效应强烈依赖于扰动传播方向和/或外加磁场方向，并讨论了实验观测的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米面整体拓扑之外的精细几何特征（近圆柱、近抛物面条带、局部平坦区）往往在常规理论中被忽略，但其可能对金属的高频电磁响应产生非平凡影响，因此有必要系统考察这些细节结构的物理后果。

Method: 以费米面几何的局域特征为出发点，分析其对电子性质（主要为磁电输运/ galvanomagnetic 性质）的影响，重点研究常规正常金属与具有金属导电性的层状结构在高频外场扰动下的响应行为，并考察该响应与扰动传播方向及外加磁场方向的依赖关系。

Result: 在特定条件下，近圆柱形条带、近抛物面形条带或局部平坦区域等精细特征能显著改变金属对高频外场扰动的响应方式；所讨论的效应对外场传播方向和/或磁场方向高度敏感。

Conclusion: 费米面的精细几何特征可成为调控金属及层状金属结构高频电磁响应的重要机制，其方向敏感性为实验验证提供了判据，文中进一步讨论了观测这些效应的实验可能性。

Abstract: Fine features in the Fermi surface geometry, such as nearly cylindrical or nearly paraboloidal strips or local flattenings, are examined as regards their effect on the electronic, mainly galvanomagnetic, properties of metals. It is shown that under certain conditions, these features may significantly change the way a conventional normal metal or a layered structure with metallic conductivity responds to high- frequency external disturbances. All of the effects considered appear to be very sensitive to the disturbance propagation direction and/or to that of the external magnetic field. Experimental possibilities of observing the described effects are discussed.

</details>


### [120] [Robustness of the Verwey transition against remanent strain-induced defects in magnetite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01337)
*M. A. Gala,K. J. Grzywa,K. Komędera,R. Zalecki,A. Baczmański,Z. Kąkol,A. Pacanowska,C. M. N. Kumar,R. Rodriguez Lamas,C. Detlefs,C. Yildirim,C. Marin,J. Debray,G. Beutier,L. Ortega,A. Kozłowski,N. Barišić,J. E. Lorenzo,W. Tabiś*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究结合暗场X射线显微镜与交流磁化率测量，发现单轴压缩在化学计量比单晶磁铁矿中产生的线状缺陷与残余应变场并不会改变Verwey转变温度及其锐度，从而将点缺陷与扩展滑移缺陷的作用区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁铁矿的Verwey转变对晶格不完美和外部扰动高度敏感，已有研究表明掺杂、偏离氧化学计量比及辐照点缺陷会降低转变温度$T_{\rm V}$。然而，由单轴应力产生的残余应变场和扩展缺陷结构的作用此前基本未被探索，因此需要系统研究其对该相变的影响。

Method: 对化学计量比单晶磁铁矿沿同一$[011]$方向进行单轴压缩并卸载，其中加载至$200~\mathrm{MPa}$产生稳定的线状缺陷与残余应变场，加载超过断裂阈值则产生更致密的缺陷网络和机械断裂；另对沿$[001]$方向压缩断裂的样品测量交流磁化率。结合暗场X射线显微镜（DFXM）的实空间成像、晶格取向映射与残余应变映射，以及交流磁化率来表征缺陷结构与相变行为。

Result: DFXM的实空间成像、晶格取向映射和残余应变映射均显示压缩后存在显著残余结构无序。但所有压缩或断裂样品的Verwey转变温度$T_{\rm V}$及转变锐度均未发生可测量的变化，这与点状无序对$T_{\rm V}$的强烈抑制形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 塑性变形产生的扩展线状缺陷与残余应变场不会可测量地扰动导致Verwey转变的宏观电子有序，这表明在磁铁矿中点缺陷与扩展滑移相关缺陷对Verwey转变的影响存在本质区别。

Abstract: The Verwey transition in magnetite is a benchmark electronically driven phase transition that is highly sensitive to lattice imperfections and external perturbations. Doping, deviations from oxygen stoichiometry, and irradiation-induced point defects are known to lower the Verwey transition temperature, $T_{\rm V}$. By contrast, the role of remanent strain fields and extended defect structures generated by uniaxial stress has remained largely unexplored. Here we combine dark-field x-ray microscopy (DFXM) and ac magnetic susceptibility to determine how strain-induced defects affect the Verwey transition in stoichiometric single-crystalline magnetite after uniaxial compression and unloading. The DFXM measurements were performed on two samples compressed along the same $[011]$ direction: loading to $200~\mathrm{MPa}$ generated stable line-like defects and remanent strain fields, whereas loading beyond the fracture threshold produced denser defect networks and mechanical fracture. To test the effect of a different loading geometry, we also measured the ac susceptibility of a sample fractured by compression along $[001]$. Real-space DFXM imaging, lattice-orientation mapping, and residual strain mapping show substantial remanent structural disorder after compression. Nevertheless, neither the transition temperature $T_{\rm V}$ nor the sharpness of the transition changes measurably in any of the compressed or fractured samples. This behavior contrasts with the strong suppression of $T_{\rm V}$ by point-like disorder. Our results show that extended line-like defects and remanent strain fields produced by plastic deformation do not measurably perturb the macroscopic electronic ordering responsible for the Verwey transition, thereby distinguishing the effects of point defects from those of extended slip-related defects in magnetite.

</details>


### [121] [Na$_2$Ir$^\text{IV}$Cl$_6$: The Missing Member of the Perfectly Cubic Vacancy Ordered $A_2$IrCl$_6$ Family, Another Potential $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ Ground State Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01371)
*Siddhartha Sankar Soren,Aditya Patra,Rounak Das,Ritwik Das,Tanmay Chanda,Jens Buck,Indra Dasgupta,Sugata Ray*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 首次合成出具有fcc结构的无水Na₂IrCl₆单晶，反铁磁转变温度为1.91 K，纠正了此前"无水"单斜相的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空位有序双钙钛矿（VODP）铱酸盐虽研究逾百年，但其精确晶体结构与磁基态仍存争议。Na基VODP铱酸盐存在异常的水合依赖的结构与磁性不确定性，导致具有fcc反铁磁性的立方Na₂IrCl₆始终无法稳定。作者希望填补这一空白，并提供一个研究强Kitaev相互作用的平台。

Method: 通过合成与综合表征手段制备无水Na₂IrCl₆单晶；利用热重分析（TGA）确认晶格中不含水并评估热稳定性；对晶体结构与磁性进行测试以确定其晶体相和磁转变行为。

Result: 成功获得无水Na₂IrCl₆单晶，呈fcc结构，反铁磁转变温度为1.91 K；TGA证实无晶格水，晶体热稳定至453 K；该立方相与此前报道的"无水"单斜Na₂IrCl₆形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该工作首次稳定了立方Na₂IrCl₆反铁磁体，凸显了Na基VODP铱酸盐中结构与物理性质的强关联，为探索潜在的完美Jeff=1/2八面体及最近邻Ir离子对间的强Kitaev相互作用提供了新平台。

Abstract: Vacancy ordered double perovskites (VODPs) have been known for more than 100 years, but their exact crystal structures and magnetic ground state remained controversial till date. Anhydrous VODP iridates ($A_2$Ir$X_6$; $A$ = K$^+$, (NH$_4$)$^+$, Cs$^+$, Rb$^+$, and $X$ = F$^-$, Cl$^-$, Br$^-$), having $face-centered~cubic$ (fcc) antiferromagnetic ground state, offer a chemically well-defined platform to investigate the influence of on-site Coulomb repulsion, crystal field, spin-orbit coupling, and competing Heisenberg and Kitaev interactions. Interestingly, among these, the Na-based VODP iridates seem to possess an unusual hydration dependent structural and magnetic uncertainty. As a result, a cubic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ system having fcc antiferromagnetism could not be stabilized ever. Here, for the first time, we report the synthesis and comprehensive characterization of anhydrous Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ single crystals having fcc structure with antiferromagnetic transition temperature of 1.91 K. Thermogravimetric analysis establishes the absence of lattice water and also demonstrates thermal stability of the crystals up to 453 K. The cubic phase reported here contrasts with previously described 'anhydrous' monoclinic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ and highlights the strong structural and physical property relationship in Na-based VODP iridates. This new material offers an exciting playing ground for exploring another potential perfect $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ octahedra and the consequent strong Kitaev interaction within the nearest-neighbor (nn) pairs of Ir ions.

</details>


### [122] [Room-temperature Magnetoelastic Coupling in UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01551)
*Mingyu Xu,Tomasz Klimczuk,M. Brian Maple,Weiwei Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在铀基金属间化合物UIr₄Al₁₅中，于略高于室温处观察到巨磁弹耦合，晶格参数与特定化学键长在磁转变区出现显著异常，为室温附近磁性与晶格强关联的稀有平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子与结构自由度的相互作用是量子材料中诸多涌现现象的根源，但金属体系中的磁弹耦合通常较弱，且局限于低温或微观尺度。因此，寻找在室温附近具有强磁弹耦合的金属材料具有重要意义。

Method: 采用温度依赖的单晶X射线衍射，并结合各向异性磁化率测量，对UIr₄Al₁₅中与磁有序耦合的结构畸变进行直接解析。

Result: 在无晶体学对称性破缺的情况下，晶格参数和选定的化学键长在磁转变区附近出现明显异常，显示出晶体结构对磁取向的异常敏感性。

Conclusion: UIr₄Al₁₅是一种罕见的在室温附近磁性与晶格畸变强耦合的平台材料，为磁性响应的量子材料及功能性磁传感应用提供了新机遇。

Abstract: The interplay between electronic and structural degrees of freedom underpins many emergent phenomena in quantum materials, yet magnetoelastic coupling in metallic systems is typically weak and confined to low temperatures or microscopic length scales. Here, we report giant magnetoelastic coupling slightly above room temperature in the uranium-based intermetallic compound UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$. Using temperature-dependent single-crystal X-ray diffraction together with anisotropic magnetic susceptibility measurements, we directly resolve subtle but reproducible structural distortions coupled to magnetic alignment. Despite the absence of crystallographic symmetry breaking, pronounced anomalies emerge in lattice parameters and selected chemical bond distances near the magnetic transition region, revealing an unusual sensitivity of the crystal structure to magnetic orientation. The coupling enables direct probing of how atomic distances and local chemical bonding govern the electronic and magnetic states in a bulk intermetallic material. Our results establish UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ as a rare platform in which magnetism and lattice distortions are strongly intertwined near room temperature, opening new opportunities for magnetically responsive quantum materials and functional magnetic sensing applications.

</details>


### [123] [Mass-asymmetry-controlled exciton dressing and dissociation in a quantum lattice model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01594)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在极性材料中，中性激子通过电子与空穴形变势之和与声子耦合，其弹性激子-声子顶点因电荷中性而由电子-空穴干涉正则化。作者通过对Holstein-激子模型的精确对角化研究该干涉的非微扰归宿，构建了包含内层极化子交叉与解离边界的相图，并证明等质量情形下对称激子基态受交换选择定则保护而免于声子修饰，而质量不对称会破坏该保护并引发强局域极化子云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性激子的声子耦合源于电子和空穴形变势之和，由于电荷中性使总源项消失，激子-声子顶点被电子-空穴干涉所正则化。然而这种干涉在非微扰强耦合下的最终归宿尚不清楚，同时激子内层极化子修饰与解离之间的关系也缺乏统一理解，作者因此希望通过精确对角化建模来厘清这些问题。

Method: 作者构建了Holstein-激子模型，并对其进行精确对角化以非微扰地处理激子-声子耦合。他们将质量不对称性参数化，使其与小极化子原子极限解耦，从而得以扫描并绘制出包含内部极化修饰交叉与解离边界的regime map。此外，作者通过解析证明结合数值验证，分析了等质量情形下声子源在电子-空穴交换下的奇偶性对基态的影响，并追踪了质量不对称增大时选择定则的破坏。

Result: 对于等质量激子，当声子源在电子-空穴交换下为奇（中性情形）时，对称激子基态因精确的交换选择定则而被保护，不发生声子修饰；随着质量不对称增大，该选择定则被破坏，激子获得强局域极化子云。解离边界则由库仑束缚与极化子稳定化之间的全局能量平衡决定，几乎与内部极化修饰无关。悖论性的是，正是保护激子免于修饰的对称性使其失去了极化子稳定化，从而在强耦合下被推向解离。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Holstein-激子模型中激子-声子干涉的非微扰相图，揭示了交换选择定则对激子声子修饰的保护作用及其在质量不对称下的破坏，并阐明了解离边界由全局能量平衡而非内部修饰决定。作者将结果置于晶格激子-极化子模型背景下讨论，指出其对质量对称与质量不对称极性半导体中激子谱线锐化与展宽差异具有启示意义。

Abstract: In a polar material, a neutral exciton couples to phonons through the sum of the electron and hole deformation potentials. Because the total source vanishes by charge neutrality, the elastic exciton-phonon vertex is regularized by electron-hole interference. Here we determine the non-perturbative fate of this interference by exact diagonalization of a Holstein-exciton model. By parameterizing the mass asymmetry to decouple it from the small-polaron atomic limit, we map a regime map comprising an internal dressing crossover and a dissociation boundary. We prove analytically and verify numerically that for equal masses, the symmetric exciton ground state is protected from phonon dressing by an exact exchange selection rule, provided the phonon source is odd under electron-hole exchange (the neutral case). As mass asymmetry increases, this selection rule is broken and the exciton acquires a strong local polaronic cloud. We show that the dissociation boundary, conversely, is set by a global energy balance between Coulomb binding and polaronic stabilization, and is nearly independent of the internal dressing. Paradoxically, the very symmetry that protects the exciton from dressing denies it polaronic stabilization, driving it toward dissociation at strong coupling. We discuss these results in the context of lattice exciton-polaron models and their implications for sharp versus broad excitonic lines in mass-symmetric versus mass-asymmetric polar semiconductors.

</details>


### [124] [Why polar excitons stay sharp: parity protection of the center-of-mass recoil channel in exciton-phonon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01600)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文指出传统冻结质心近似忽略了 exciton–phonon 问题的关键运动学自由度，恢复精确质心反冲后出现一个普适开放、无参数的 1s→1s 吸收通道（反冲动量 q_*），其速率随 N_LO(T) 变化。该通道受电子-空穴相消干涉控制，线宽随质量不对称度按 γ∝F_{1s,1s}(q_*)^2 消失，并给出弹性缀饰的精确抑制律 S_X/S_ind=η^2(6−η^2)/5。由此建立散射机制层级：质量不对称材料（GaAs, η=−0.74）反冲通道活跃（2.2 meV）；质量对称材料（FAPbI3 η=0，MAPbI3 η=−0.11）被干涉抑制（0.00、0.12 meV），说明钙钛矿观测到的 27–40 meV 线宽不能归因于质心反冲，而需内部态改变等非弹性通道，其中 η-稳健的 1s→np 共振是主要候选。Fröhlich 常数 α 本身不足以作为品质因子，投影到关联激子后控制参数是 η、q_*a_X 与 Rydberg 失谐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性半导体中 Fröhlich 相互作用是主导的电子-声子耦合，但卤化物钙钛矿等材料的激子共振却异常尖锐，与常规预期不符。作者认为标准冻结质心处理遗漏了决定性的运动学自由度，需要恢复精确质心反冲来重新审视该问题，并厘清何种机制真正主导钙钛矿的谱线展宽。

Method: 在氢原子型 Fröhlich 模型框架下，放弃冻结质心近似，恢复激子的精确质心（COM）反冲；推导反冲动量 q_*=√(2M_ex ħω_LO)/ħ 处的 1s→1s 吸收通道，分析其速率随 N_LO(T) 的标度；通过电子-空穴干涉研究反冲线宽 γ_LO^recoil 与质量不对称度 η 的关系，推导弹性缀饰的抑制律 S_X/S_ind=η^2(6−η^2)/5；并对具体材料（GaAs、FAPbI3、MAPbI3）计算反冲线宽。

Result: 发现一个普适开放、无参数的 1s→1s 反冲吸收通道，速率随 N_LO(T) 标度；反冲通道由电子-空穴相消干涉控制，线宽按 γ_LO^recoil∝F_{1s,1s}(q_*)^2 随质量不对称度消失，弹性缀饰满足精确抑制律 S_X/S_ind=η^2(6−η^2)/5。数值上：质量不对称的 GaAs（η=−0.74）反冲通道活跃，γ_LO^recoil=2.2 meV；质量对称的 FAPbI3（η=0）与 MAPbI3（η=−0.11）反冲被干涉抑制，分别为 0.00 与 0.12 meV。而已观测的钙钛矿线宽 27–40 meV 远大于反冲贡献。

Conclusion: 质心反冲不足以解释钙钛矿中观测到的 27–40 meV 线宽，因此需要内部态改变等其他非弹性通道，其中构造性、对 η 稳健的 1s→np 共振是本模型内的首要候选。Fröhlich 常数 α 单独不足以作为品质因子；投影到关联激子后，决定性的控制参数是质量不对称度 η、q_*a_X 和 Rydberg 失谐。该理论确立了激子-声子散射机制的层级结构。

Abstract: In polar semiconductors the Fröhlich interaction is the dominant electron--phonon coupling, yet excitonic resonances in materials such as halide perovskites remain anomalously sharp. We show that standard frozen-center-of-mass treatments of the exciton--phonon problem miss the decisive kinematic degree of freedom: restoring the exact center-of-mass (COM) recoil reveals a universally open, parameter-free $1s\to1s$ absorption channel at recoil momentum $q_*=\sqrt{2M_{\rm ex}\hbarω_{\rm LO}}/\hbar$, whose rate scales as $N_{\rm LO}(T)$. We prove that this recoil channel is controlled by destructive electron--hole interference: the recoil linewidth vanishes with the mass asymmetry as $γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}\propto\mathcal{F}_{1s,1s}(q_*)^2$, and the elastic dressing obeys the exact suppression law $S_X/S_{\rm ind}=η^2(6-η^2)/5$ within the hydrogenic Fröhlich model. The theory establishes a hierarchy of scattering regimes. In mass-asymmetric materials (GaAs, $η=-0.74$) the recoil channel is active ($γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}=2.2$~meV); in mass-symmetric materials (FAPbI$_3$, $η=0$; MAPbI$_3$, $η=-0.11$) it is killed by interference (0.00 and 0.12 meV), showing that the observed 27--40 meV perovskite linewidths cannot be accounted for by COM recoil and therefore require internal-state-changing and other inelastic channels, of which the constructive, $η$-robust $1s\to np$ resonance is the leading candidate within the present model. The Fröhlich constant $α$ alone is therefore insufficient as a figure of merit: after projection onto the correlated exciton, the controlling parameters are $η$, $q_*a_X$, and the Rydberg detuning.

</details>


### [125] [Frustration-induced multiferroicity in hauerite MnS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01990)
*Vilmos Kocsis,Harish K. Singh,Kranthi K. Bestha,Yaqian Guo,Maxim Mostovoy,Jeroen van den Brink,Oleg Janson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: MnS₂在48 K以下形成共线反铁磁序，伴随晶胞加倍并破缺反演对称性，产生高达360 μC/m²的铁电极化，从而成为II型多铁体；其微观机制为S₂二聚体在受阻磁序诱导下获得偶极矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黄铁矿型磁性绝缘体MnS₂中磁序与铁电性之间的耦合关系，明确其是否具备II型多铁性及相应微观起源。

Method: 基于第一性原理/理论计算分析MnS₂的磁结构、对称性破缺与电极化，并考察S₂二聚体的偶极矩来源、时间反演对称性对铁电畴的影响以及畴翻转的能量势垒。

Result: MnS₂在48 K以下出现共线反铁磁序，伴随晶胞加倍和反演对称性破缺，产生最高360 μC/m²的铁电极化；微观上S₂二聚体因邻近Mn位点的受阻磁序而获得偶极矩；时间反演对称性保持铁电畴不变，畴翻转需克服由磁交换能标度决定的高能垒。

Conclusion: MnS₂被确认为一种II型多铁体，其铁电性源于磁序诱导的S₂二聚体偶极矩，且铁电畴受时间反演对称性保护、翻转势垒较高。

Abstract: Pyrite-type mineral hauerite MnS$_2$ is a magnetic insulator with localized $S=\frac52$ moments. Below 48 K, it develops a collinear antiferromagnetic order, accompanied by unit-cell doubling and breaking the inversion symmetry. We demonstrate that the latter gives rise to a ferroelectric polarization of up to 360 $μ$C/m$^2$ rendering MnS$_2$ a type-II multiferroic. Microscopically, the electronic polarization originates from S$_2$ dimers that acquire a dipole moment induced by the frustrated magnetic ordering of neighboring Mn sites. We show that time-reversal symmetry leaves ferroelectric domains intact, and that domain switching necessitates overcoming a high energy barrier set by the magnetic exchange energy scale.

</details>


### [126] [Magnetic Phase Diagrams and Spin Hamiltonian of Monoclinic $α$-RuCl$_3$ from Angle-Dependent Torque Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02006)
*Daniel Antoniou,Danrui Ni,John S. Pearce,Robert J. Cava,Amalia I. Coldea,Radu Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了亚100微米级单斜α-RuCl₃单晶的磁性，其低温保持单斜结构并在14 K发生磁相变；通过高灵敏压电悬臂力矩测量（最高16 T）构建了三个正交晶面内的磁相图，发现其与菱面体样品存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-RuCl₃是研究自旋轨道纠缠j_eff=1/2及Kitaev等键依赖各向异性相互作用导致的非常规磁性的候选材料，但以往研究多聚焦于会发生单斜-菱面体结构相变的大尺寸单晶，对低温保持单斜结构的小尺寸晶体的磁性认识不足。

Method: 采用高质量、亚100微米直径的单晶（低温仍为单斜），利用高灵敏压电悬臂力矩测量，在最高16 T磁场下对磁场在三个正交晶面内旋转的力矩数据进行采集；通过连续改变磁场角度和幅值，借助原始力矩数据及其对角和磁场的高阶导数的异常来探测相变；并将力矩数据与考虑单斜晶格对称性的α-RuCl₃平均场计算进行对比。

Result: 获得的磁相图与菱面体样品相比存在诸多差异，表现为旋转对称性降低、抑制自发磁序所需磁场显著更高，以及出现独特的场诱导相；提出了覆盖所有已探索磁相图区域的最小磁结构模型。

Conclusion: 小尺寸单斜α-RuCl₃单晶展现出与菱面体样品不同的磁相图与场诱导相行为，晶格对称性对磁性有重要影响，所提出的最小磁结构模型可解释各相区。

Abstract: The layered honeycomb $α$-RuCl$_3$ has been much explored as a candidate to display unconventional cooperative magnetism between spin-orbit entangled $j_{\rm eff}=1/2$ Ru$^{3+}$ magnetic moments stabilized by strongly-frustrated, Kitaev and other bond-dependent anisotropic interactions. Most studies so far have focused on relatively large single crystals that undergo a structural phase transition from monoclinic to rhombohedral upon cooling below 150 K and a magnetic transition to zigzag order around 7 K. Here we study the magnetism of high-quality, very small (sub 100 $μ$m diameter) single crystals, which remain monoclinic upon cooling to low temperatures and display a sharp magnetic transition at 14 K. Using highly-sensitive piezo-cantilever magnetic torque measurements in fields up to 16 T, we report a comprehensive study of the magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic field rotated in three orthogonal crystallographic planes. Our extensive torque data collected upon varying almost continuously the angular orientation of the field as well as the field magnitude, allows clear detection of phase transitions via anomalies in the raw torque data and its higher order derivatives with respect to both angle and field magnitude. The obtained magnetic phase diagrams show many differences compared to rhombohedral samples, displaying reduced rotational symmetry, substantially higher fields required to suppress the spontaneous magnetic order, and distinct field-induced phases. We provide a direct comparison of the torque data with mean-field calculations for magnetic Hamiltonians appropriate for $α$-RuCl$_3$ allowing for the monoclinic lattice symmetry and propose minimal magnetic structure models for all explored regions of the magnetic phase diagrams.

</details>

### [127] [Two-Line Elimination and the Weierstrass Group Law in Arbitrary Characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00028)
*Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文用多项式分解、形式微分与二次插值证明了一般Weierstrass形式椭圆曲线的弦-切运算满足结合律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦-切运算（chord–tangent operation）为椭圆曲线群律的核心，其结合律通常借助几何论证，需处理不同情形且在特殊特征下易出问题；本文旨在给出一个统一、纯代数且适用于任意特征的证明。

Method: 在一般Weierstrass形式下，结合多项式分解、形式微分（用形式偏导数刻画切触，避免除以2、3等）与二次插值；通过主恒等式消去连续加法S=P⊕Q与T=S⊕R中出现的两条非竖直直线Y=ℓ(X)与Y=m(X)，构造c∈K与首一二次多项式h∈K[X]，并利用表达式(Y−ℓ)(Y−m)=(X−x_S)(h−cY)+Y²+A(X)Y−G(X)进行分析；当c≠0时，q=h/c的图像过P、Q、R与−T，两种加括号方式由普通或Hermite插值确定同一二次多项式，从而给出同一点。

Result: 证明了弦-切运算的结合律在任意特征下成立；当c≠0时，两条加括号路径对应同一个插值抛物线q=h/c，其对P、Q、R及−T的插值由普通或Hermite插值唯一确定，故最终点相同；对短Weierstrass形式，另给出简证，将q等同于Zwegers构造中的插值抛物线。

Conclusion: 该证明是纯代数的，适用于所有特征，包括小特征情形；通过将结合律归约为唯一的插值抛物线问题，统一并简化了弦-切运算结合律的论证，并与Zwegers的构造相容。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a field and let $E/K$ be a nonsingular projective curve in general Weierstrass form. We prove associativity of its chord--tangent operation by combining polynomial factorization, formal differentiation, and quadratic interpolation. The main identity eliminates the two nonvertical lines $Y=\ell(X)$ and $Y=m(X)$ occurring in the successive additions $S=P\oplus Q$ and $T=S\oplus R$. There are $c\in K$ and a monic quadratic polynomial $h\in K[X]$ such that \[
  \ell+m+\Apol=c(X-x_S),
  \qquad
  \Gpol+\ell m=(X-x_S)h, \] and \[
  (Y-\ell)(Y-m)
  =(X-x_S)(h-cY)
  +Y^2+\Apol(X)Y-\Gpol(X). \] When $c\ne0$, the graph of $q=h/c$ contains $P,Q,R$, and $-T$. The two bracketings determine the same quadratic by ordinary or Hermite interpolation and therefore yield the same final point. The proof applies in every characteristic, since tangency is expressed through the formal partial derivatives of the Weierstrass equation. For the short Weierstrass form, a separate concise proof identifies $q$ with the interpolating parabola in Zwegers's construction.

</details>


### [128] [PageRank on Lubotzky--Phillips--Sarnak graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00109)
*Shenghao Hua*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文比较LPS图上带独立几何停时的随机游走返回概率与无限正则树上的对应量，用McKay测度刻画树值、Ihara zeta函数刻画环路带来的额外返回，并在平均游走长度与最短环长相当的区间估计该修正项；无条件得到含双对数因子的上界，在GRH下修正项与顶点数倒数同阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机游走在LPS图（Ramanujan图）上的返回概率通常由树的McKay测度主导，但图中的短环会带来额外返回贡献。当平均游走长度与最短环长可比时，游走有不可忽略的概率绕环一周，此时环修正项变得重要，需要定量刻画其大小。

Method: 以独立几何停时截断随机游走，将返回概率分解为无限正则树的McKay测度主项与由Ihara zeta函数描述的环路修正项；在平均游走长度与最短环长可比的标度区间内，对环修正进行解析估计，并结合数论工具（涉及二次Dirichlet L函数）处理素数层（prime levels）上的求和与矩。

Result: 无条件情形下，证明修正项（乘以顶点数后）在素数层上具有带双对数因子的上界，且有界固定矩；在二次Dirichlet L函数的广义黎曼假设下，修正项与顶点数的倒数同阶。

Conclusion: 结果表明：在临界标度区间内，LPS图上几何截断随机游走返回概率相对正则树值的偏差受最短环主导，其大小在GRH下恰为顶点数倒数量级，而无条件下仅有双对数损失的上界。

Abstract: We compare return probabilities for random walks stopped at an independent geometric time on Lubotzky--Phillips--Sarnak graphs and on the infinite regular tree, with the McKay measure giving the tree value and the Ihara zeta function describing the additional returns caused by cycles. We estimate this correction when the mean walk length is comparable to the length of the shortest cycle, a regime in which walks long enough to traverse a cycle receive non-negligible weight.
  Unconditionally, we prove an upper bound with a double logarithmic factor and bounded fixed moments over prime levels after multiplication by the number of vertices. Under the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis for quadratic Dirichlet $L$-functions, the correction is of the same order as the reciprocal of the number of vertices.

</details>


### [129] [Some Methods to Solve the Diophantine Equation ay^2=b(x^k-c^k)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00119)
*Xiaodong Zhuang,Nikos E. Mastorakis*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究丢番图方程 ay²=b(x^k-c^k) 的求解，针对不同 k 提出便于计算机算法实现的方法，将原方程降次以提高整数解搜索效率，并给出 ay²=b(x^2n-c^2n) 无解的一个充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丢番图方程 ay²=b(x^k-c^k) 的直接求解与整数解搜索效率较低，需要针对不同 k 设计可算法化的求解途径，并给出判定无解的充分条件。

Method: 按 k 的不同情形分类讨论；构造便于计算机算法实现的方法；将原方程约化为低次方程；针对 ay²=b(x^2n-c^2n) 给出无解的充分条件。

Result: 提出了不同 k 情形下的求解方法，通过降次约化提升了搜索可能整数解的效率，并得到了 ay²=b(x^2n-c^2n) 无解性的一个充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为 ay²=b(x^k-c^k) 类丢番图方程提供了分类求解与降次约化思路，兼具算法实现便利性与搜索效率优势，并为特定情形的不可能性判定提供了充分条件。

Abstract: The solution of the Diophantine equation ay^2=b(x^k-c^k) is studied in different cases of k. Methods are proposed for different cases of k, which are convenient for computer algorithm implementation. The original equation is reduced to lower-degree equations, which can improve the efficiency in searching possible integer solutions. A sufficient condition of the unsolvability of ay^2=b(x^2n-c^2n) is presented.

</details>


### [130] [An explicit family of twists of the Carlitz module with unbounded analytic rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00379)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文否定了Grishkov与Logachev提出的关于Carlitz模扭动的解析秩是否有界的问题：对每个q≥3，答案是负的，且在q=p^s、n为p的幂时解析秩等于(q/p)^n(p-1)^(n-1)，无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grishkov与Logachev在其综述（问题1.4）中提问：对固定有限域F_q，Carlitz模各扭动的解析秩是否有界。本文旨在回答该问题，并进一步处理综述的问题3.6.1至3.6.4。

Method: 对q=p^s与n≥1定义P_{q,n}=(θ^(q^n)-θ)^(q-2)；通过对Grishkov-Logachev矩阵M(P_{q,n},1,k)的有向图进行分析，显式给出其回路集合：两两不交、均不与含t的项相交，并与E^n在循环旋转下的轨道一一对应（E为[0,q-2]中使p不整除l+1的l的集合）。由此得到L(C_P,t,T)不依赖t，并按轨道分解为形如1-wt(ν)T^per(ν)的因子之积。

Result: L(C_P,t,T)与t无关，分解为每个轨道对应一个因子1-wt(ν)T^per(ν)的乘积；解析秩等于权重为1的轨道上p^(v_p(per(ν)))之和。当n为p的幂时，该和等于(q/p)^n(p-1)^(n-1)，随n无界；因此对每个q≥3，Carlitz模扭动的解析秩无界。

Conclusion: Grishkov-Logachev问题1.4的答案为否定：解析秩无界。该论证同时回答了其综述中问题3.6.1至3.6.4（针对该族）。

Abstract: Grishkov and Logachev have asked (Problem 1.4 of their recent survey) whether, for a fixed finite field F_q, the analytic ranks of the twists of the Carlitz module are bounded. We answer this in the negative for every q >= 3. For q = p^s and n >= 1 put P_{q,n} = (theta^(q^n) - theta)^(q-2). We show that the directed graph of the matrix M(P_{q,n},1,k) of Grishkov-Logachev has a completely explicit set of circuits: they are pairwise disjoint, none of them meets an entry involving t, and they are in bijection with the orbits of E^n under cyclic rotation, where E is the set of l in [0,q-2] with p not dividing l+1. Consequently L(C_P, t, T) does not depend on t, it factors as a product of one term 1 - wt(nu) T^per(nu) per orbit, and the analytic rank equals the sum of p^(v_p(per(nu))) over the orbits of weight 1. When n is a power of p this equals (q/p)^n (p-1)^(n-1), which is unbounded. The argument also answers questions 3.6.1 to 3.6.4 of the survey for this family.

</details>


### [131] [New bounds for the integer Chebyshev constant of [0,1]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00386)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将 [0,1] 上整数 Chebyshev 常数的区间从 1.39e-3 缩窄到 4.56e-4，证明 0.4222286000 ≤ t_Z([0,1]) ≤ 0.4226846975，上下界均由显式证书给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自 Pritsker (2005) 和 Flammang (2014) 的工作以来，[0,1] 的整数 Chebyshev 常数一直被限制在 0.4213 ≤ t_Z([0,1]) ≤ 0.422685 这一区间内，长期未能进一步收紧。本文旨在显着缩小该区间宽度，并给出可独立验证的显式证书。

Method: 上界由含 92 个因子及显式整数指数的整数多项式构造证书给出；下界由 947 个单元上的离散测度与包含 206 个不可约多项式的库给出，所用不等式中"为可除性付出代价"的项均被显式写出。

Result: 证明了 0.4222286000 ≤ t_Z([0,1]) ≤ 0.4226846975，将区间宽度从 1.39e-3 缩小到 4.56e-4。两个自包含脚本作为附加文件，可仅凭证书重新推导上下界。

Conclusion: 通过显式证书构造，显著改善了 [0,1] 上整数 Chebyshev 常数的上下界，且结果可由所附脚本独立复核。

Abstract: The integer Chebyshev constant of [0,1] has been bracketed by 0.4213 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.422685 since the work of Pritsker (2005) and of Flammang (2014). We prove
  0.4222286000 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.4226846975,
  narrowing the interval from 1.39e-3 to 4.56e-4. Both bounds are established by explicit certificates. The upper bound is carried by an integer polynomial with 92 factors and explicit integer exponents; the lower bound by a discrete measure on 947 cells together with a library of 206 irreducible polynomials, through an inequality in which the terms paying for divisibility are made explicit. Two self-contained scripts, included as ancillary files, re-derive both bounds from the certificates alone.

</details>


### [132] [On a problem on Ramanujan-Sato series of level 11](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00551)
*John M. Campbell*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了关于11级Ramanujan-Sato级数系数$c_{11}(n)$的长期公开问题，给出了一个与低层级情形直接类比的双重二项式求和公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Cooper等人2015年提出以来，确定11级Ramanujan-Sato级数系数$c_{11}(n)$（对所有非负整数$n$的固定嵌套有限二项式求和表达式）的显式公式一直是悬而未决的问题，且该问题在Cooper 2017年教材及2025年Contemp. Math.论文中再次被强调，与低层级级数已知的系数公式形成类比缺口。

Method: 作者引入并证明了$c_{11}(n)$的一个双重二项式求和公式，该公式与低层级Ramanujan-Sato级数的系数公式直接类比。

Result: 成功给出并证明了$c_{11}(n)$的双重二项式求和表达式，填补了11级情形长期缺失的显式系数公式。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了Cooper等人提出的开放问题，使11级Ramanujan-Sato级数的系数公式与低层级公式实现统一类比。

Abstract: A longstanding open problem concerning Ramanujan-Sato series is given by determining an expression for the coefficients $c_{11}(n)$ associated with Ramanujan-Sato series of level 11 with a fixed, nested, finite binomial sum for all nonnegative integers $n$, by direct analogy with the known formulas for the coefficients for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series (and this is clarified and made explicit in our paper). This open problem was given by Cooper et al. in 2015 [J. Math. Anal. Appl.], in Cooper's 2017 textbook on Ramanujan's theta functions, and in Cooper's 2025 contribution to the Contemp. Math. series. This problem seems to have remained open. We succeed in solving this problem, by introducing and proving a double binomial sum formula for $c_{11}(n)$ that is directly analogous to the coefficient formulas for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series.

</details>


### [133] [Perfect squares in reciprocal-sum sequences and primes that are inert in quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00765)
*Mateo Matijasevick,Santiago Rodríguez,Gregorio Salazar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究满足 x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+...+x_n) 的正有理数序列，刻画何时 c·a_n+b_n 恒为完全幂：当且仅当 k=h² 且 h 的每个素因子 ≡3 (mod 4)，此时 c=2/h 且均为平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有理数递归序列中约化分数的分子分母组合何时恒为完全幂，揭示该算术现象与二次域中素元素惰性之间的深刻联系。

Method: 关键变换 t_n=(x_0+...+x_n)/h 满足 t_{n+1}=t_n+1/t_n，在该递归下约化分数不约分，由此得到 c·a_n+b_n 的精确公式；并借助 Q(√-k) 中素元素的惰性条件分析不变量 b_n²-(4/k)a_n²。

Result: 证明了 c·a_n+b_n 对所有序列和所有 n≥2 恒为完全幂当且仅当 k=h² 且 h 的素因子均 ≡3 (mod 4)，此时取 c=2/h 且幂为平方；每一素因子至多破坏一项。对任意 k，不变量 b_n²-(4/k)a_n² 恒为有理平方，且为整数平方当且仅当 k 的平方部分的素因子满足 Q(√-k) 中的惰性条件。

Conclusion: 结果推广到递归 x_{n+1}=h²/(x_0+...+x_n+nμh)，其中高斯整数的作用由二次域 Q(√(μ²-4)) 承担，涵盖 Q(√-3) 及所有实二次域。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a positive integer, and consider the sequences of positive rationals with $x_0\in\N$ and $x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+x_1+\dots+x_n)$. Write $x_n=a_n/b_n$ in lowest terms. We show that there is a rational constant $c>0$ such that $c\,a_n+b_n$ is a perfect power for every such sequence and every $n\ge2$ if and only if $k=h^2$ and every prime factor of $h$ is congruent to $3$ modulo $4$; in that case $c=2/h$ and the powers are squares. The proof rests on the observation that $t_n=(x_0+\dots+x_n)/h$ satisfies $t_{n+1}=t_n+1/t_n$, a recursion under which reduced fractions never cancel. This yields an exact formula for $c\,a_n+b_n$, shows that along any single sequence each prime factor of $h$ spoils at most one term, and leads to two generalizations. For arbitrary $k$ the invariant $b_n^2-\frac4k a_n^2$ is always a rational square, and it is always an integer square exactly when the primes dividing the square part of $k$ satisfy an inertness condition in $\Q(\sqrt{-k})$. For the recursions $x_{n+1}=h^2/(x_0+\dots+x_n+nμh)$ the role of the Gaussian integers is played by the quadratic fields $\Q(\sqrt{μ^2-4})$, which include $\Q(\sqrt{-3})$ and every real quadratic field.

</details>


### [134] [Congruence Jumping in Polynomial Divisibility Systems: Arithmetic Structure and Solution Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00808)
*Max A. Alekseyev,Dmitry I. Khomovsky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究 y|A(x), x|B(y) 的整数解，提出"同余跳跃"商变换方法，给出互反多项式规则、二次情形分类及 Pell 型线性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数系数多项式满足双向整除关系 y|A(x)、x|B(y) 的整数解结构复杂，现有方法多依赖互素、首一或单位等限制性假设，缺乏对商变换改变多项式对的一般性刻画。

Method: 引入"同余跳跃"（congruence jumping）——通过商变换在改变多项式对的同时求解；建立控制此类跳跃的互反多项式规则，给出多项式状态整数可规范化的精确判据；利用伴曲面恒等式构造无限整商链；对二次情形采用定量分母界、例外单侧无限链分类及重整为固定圆锥上的 Vieta 动力学。

Result: 在无需互素、首一或单位假设下得到一般互反多项式规则；构造出显式混合次数无限整商链；二次情形获得有限链的定量分母界，分类出非整圆锥参数的例外非平凡单侧无限链，证明二次状态变化归结为固定圆锥上的 Vieta 动力学；构造出具有无穷多正整点的真正非单位四循环，并给出其一致 Pell 型线性化；通过 Pell 轨道与变状态阶梯分析一个非单位整除系统；另给出关系格判据将部分解集化为有限除子搜索。

Conclusion: 同余跳跃与伴曲面恒等式为双向整除系统提供了统一的构造与分类框架；二次情形受圆锥上的 Vieta/Pell 动力学刚性约束，可归约为有限搜索。

Abstract: Let $A$ and $B$ be polynomials with integer coefficients. We study nonzero integer solutions of $$y\mid A(x),\qquad x\mid B(y),$$ using quotient transformations that may change the polynomial pair. We call this process \emph{congruence jumping}. We establish a general reciprocal-polynomial rule governing such jumps, without coprimality, monicity, or unit assumptions, and give exact criteria for when the resulting polynomial states can be normalized integrally. Companion-surface identities provide a mechanism for constructing infinite integral quotient chains, including an explicit mixed-degree example.
  The quadratic case is considerably more rigid. We obtain a quantitative denominator bound for finite chains, classify the exceptional nonconstant one-sided infinite chains with nonintegral conic parameter, and show that changing quadratic states reduce to ordinary Vieta dynamics on a fixed conic. We further construct a genuinely nonunit recurrent four-cycle with infinitely many positive integral points and show that its dynamics admits a uniform Pell-type linearization. A motivating nonunit divisibility system is analyzed through Pell orbits and changing-state ladders. Finally, an independent relation-lattice criterion reduces certain solution sets to a finite divisor search.

</details>


### [135] [Multiplicative recurrence of Piatetski--Shapiro and nil-Bohr sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00843)
*Sun-Kai Leung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广Frantzikinakis–Klurman–Moreira的圆法，给出自然数集乘法回复性的充分判别准则，并应用于小指数Piatetski–Shapiro集与正则无理nil-Bohr集，同时研究完全积性函数在这些集合上的取值分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法回复性是数论与遍历理论交叉中的核心概念，而小指数Piatetski–Shapiro集与nil-Bohr集等具体集合的乘法回复性此前缺乏统一、可操作的判别工具；此外，完全积性函数在稀疏集合上的取值分布也尚待刻画。

Method: 借用并改造Frantzikinakis–Klurman–Moreira发展的圆法，建立自然数集为乘法回复的一个充分性判别准则，并将其应用于小指数的Piatetski–Shapiro集和正则无理nil-Bohr集。

Result: 得到乘法回复性的充分判别准则；据此证明了小指数Piatetski–Shapiro集与正则无理nil-Bohr集满足乘法回复性；进一步给出模1完全积性函数沿上述集合的取值分布结果。

Conclusion: 圆法可作为研究乘法回复性的有效统一框架，所得准则兼具普适性与可验证性，并为完全积性函数在稀疏集合上的分布研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: Adapting the circle method developed by Frantzikinakis--Klurman--Moreira, we establish a sufficient criterion for a set of natural numbers to be multiplicatively recurrent. We apply this criterion to Piatetski--Shapiro sets with small exponents and to regular irrational nil-Bohr sets. We also study the value distribution of unimodular completely multiplicative functions along these sets.

</details>


### [136] [Transcendence of dendric beta-expansions with pure discrete spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00856)
*Jihyuk Seo,Wolfgang Steiner*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明一类具有代数基β的β-展开数（其基属于纯离散谱的树枝移位，且替换指令序列满足特定组合条件）的超越性，涵盖任意字母表上的Arnoux-Rauzy序列与Cassaigne-Selmer序列，方法结合Rauzy分形与子空间定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究β-展开数的超越性，扩展已知超越性结果到更广的代数基情形，即基所对应的移位系统为纯离散谱的树枝移位（dendric shifts），从而统一并推广Arnoux-Rauzy序列、Cassaigne-Selmer序列等经典情形。

Method: 利用Rauzy分形刻画代数基β对应的替换系统的几何/算术结构，并结合子空间定理（Subspace Theorem）进行超越性论证；证明依赖于替换指令序列（directive sequences）上的特定组合条件。

Result: 在所述组合条件下，证明了相应β-展开数的超越性；该结果适用于任意字母表上的Arnoux-Rauzy序列以及Cassaigne-Selmer序列。

Conclusion: Rauzy分形与子空间定理的结合为研究代数基下β-展开数的超越性提供了有效框架，将超越性结果推广至纯离散谱树枝移位的广泛类别。

Abstract: We prove the transcendence of numbers that have $β$-expansions in algebraic bases $β$ belonging to dendric shifts with purely discrete spectrum, under certain combinatorial conditions on their directive sequences of substitutions. This includes Arnoux-Rauzy sequences on arbitrary alphabets and Cassaigne-Selmer sequences. The proof uses Rauzy fractals and the subspace theorem.

</details>


### [137] [Powers in orbits and semigroups generated by polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00893)
*Alina Ostafe,Dat T. Tran*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究数域上多项式（或有限多项式族生成半群）轨道中完全幂的存在性；修正并推广了Ahmadi等(2012)的结果，证明轨道中完全幂的乘法平移可由有限算法检测，并给出轨道元素满足特定乘法关系的有限性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注数域上多项式迭代轨道及有限多项式族生成半群的轨道中完全幂的出现问题。此前Ahmadi等(2012)给出相关结果，但存在需要修正与推广之处，本文旨在完善该结论并扩展其适用范围。

Method: 以数域上多项式（及有限族生成的半群）的轨道为研究对象，采用算法性方法处理完全幂的乘法平移，并对轨道元素间的乘法关系进行有限性分析。

Result: 修正并推广了Ahmadi等(2012)的结果；证明轨道中完全幂的乘法平移可以由有限算法检测；得到轨道元素满足某些乘法关系时的有限性结论。

Conclusion: 轨道中完全幂的乘法平移具有可判定性（有限算法可检测），且满足特定乘法关系的轨道元素具有有限性，推广了既有文献的相关结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the presence of perfect powers in orbits of polynomials, or of semigroups generated by a finite family of polynomials, defined over a number field. In particular, we correct and generalize a result of Ahmadi et al. (2012), showing that multiplicative shifts of perfect powers in orbits can be detected by a finite algorithm. We also give a finiteness result on elements in orbits satisfying certain multiplicative relations.

</details>


### [138] [Translation invariant quadratic forms in $8$ variables and dense sets of primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01091)
*Zhaochen Pei,Maoru Tan,Weiwen Zhang,Lilu Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文在平移不变不定二次型及素数变量的设定下，改进了Green关于二次型方程素数解的密度条件，将所需素数集合的相对密度降低至对数对数尺度的负幂次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Green近期在二次型方程素数解方面取得了进展。本文旨在发展Green的工作，进一步降低使方程Q(p_1,...,p_8)=0存在素数解所需的素数集合密度门槛。

Method: 对平移不变的不定八元二次型Q，给定素数集合A（大小为X内素数的子集），通过发展Green的近期工作，运用圆法等解析数论工具，在Q满足温和条件时研究素数解的存在性。

Result: 证明了只要素数集合A的相对密度满足|A|/(X/log X) ≫_Q (log log X)^{-c}（c为某绝对常数），就存在两两不同的素数p_1,...,p_8 ∈ A使得Q(p_1,...,p_8)=0。即密度条件下界为对数对数尺度的负幂次。

Conclusion: 本文将所需素数集合的相对密度门槛降至(log log X)^{-c}量级，显著弱化了对素数分布密度的要求，推广并发展了Green的相关结果。

Abstract: Let $Q$ be a translation invariant indefinite quadratic form in $8$ variables. Let $\mathcal{P}$ be the set of all primes. Let $\mathcal{A}\subset\mathcal{P}\cap\{1,\dots,X\}$. By developing the recent work of Green, we prove that subject to a mild condition on the form $Q$ there exist pairwise distinct primes $p_{1},\dots,p_{8}\in \mathcal{A}$ satisfying $Q(p_{1},\dots,p_{8})=0$ provided that \[\frac{|\mathcal{A}|}{X/\log X}\gg_{Q}(\log\log X)^{-c}\] for some absolute constant $c>0$.

</details>


### [139] [Construction of parametrized families of general pseudo-bimagic and bimagic squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00915)
*Ajai Choudhry*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首次（除一特殊族外）给出参数化的6×6伪双魔方与8×8双魔方族，并推广到任意正整数m的6m阶伪双魔方与8m阶双魔方多参数族，附带数值实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双魔方的参数化族此前除一个6×6伪双魔方族外尚无发表结果，作者希望填补这一空白，先构造低阶（6×6、8×8）参数化族，再通过阶数倍增推广至任意倍数阶。

Method: 构造6×6伪双魔方与8×8双魔方的参数化族；利用这些族，对任意正整数m，构造6m阶伪双魔方与8m阶双魔方的多参数族。

Result: 得到6×6和8×8的参数化族，以及6m阶伪双魔方和8m阶双魔方的多参数族；并给出6×6、12×12伪双魔方与8×8双魔方的数值例子。

Conclusion: 将双魔方的参数化构造从孤立的低阶例子推广为可任意倍增阶数的多参数族，显著扩展了已知双魔方构造的范围。

Abstract: A magic square of order $n$ is a square array of $n^2$ distinct integers such that the integers of every row, every column and the two diagonals have the same common sum. If a magic square remains a magic square even when each integer is replaced by its square, it is called bimagic. If, however, all the rows and all the columns of the aforementioned square of squares add up to a common sum, but the two diagonals do not, the original magic square is called pseudo-bimagic. Except for one family of $6 \times 6$ pseudo-bimagic squares, parametrized families of bimagic squares have not been published. In this paper we construct parametrized families of $6 \times 6$ pseudo-bimagic squares and $8 \times 8$ bimagic squares. We use these families to construct, for any positive integer $m$, multi-parameter families of pseudo-bimagic squares of order $6m$ and bimagic squares of order $8m$. We also give numerical examples of $6 \times 6$ and $12 \times 12$ pseudo-bimagic squares and an $8 \times 8$ bimagic square.

</details>


### [140] [Baire-category transfer between Engel and Pierce expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00946)
*Min Woong Ahn*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造Engel展开与Pierce展开数字序列之间的显式对应（将第$n$位Engel数字加$n-2$），证明其诱导一个从单位区间无理数集到该集某个可数稠密子集补集上的同胚，从而给出两个展开之间关于Baire范畴的转移原理，并确定保持哪些Borel类。进一步将该同胚识别为Moroz(2027)两个映射的复合，研究第二个映射（把修改的Engel展开映为Pierce展开）的连续性：确定其不连续点集、证明每个不连续点均为跳跃型、并证明该映射无处单调，从而证实Moroz的相关猜想。应用上刻画了使Pierce展开数字级数在余贫集上发散的非增位置依赖权重，并关联Engel与Pierce展开数字序列的收敛指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engel展开与Pierce展开是两类经典的数字展开，已有研究多关注各自性质，二者之间的结构联系尚缺乏显式刻画。本文旨在建立两者数字序列之间的显式对应，并借同胚与转移原理揭示其在Baire范畴、Borel类及收敛性方面的联系，同时验证Moroz(2027)提出的关于相关映射连续性、间断与单调性的猜想。

Method: 构造显式对应：将第$n$位Engel展开数字加$n-2$，得到修改Engel展开；证明该对应诱导单位区间无理数集到其某可数稠密子集补集上的同胚；据此建立Baire范畴转移原理并确定保持的Borel类；将该同胚识别为Moroz(2027)两映射的复合，分析第二个映射的连续性（不连续点集、跳跃型间断、无处单调性）；并应用于位置依赖权重的发散性与收敛指数。

Result: 建立了Engel与Pierce展开之间的显式对应并证明其为同胚；得到Baire范畴转移原理并确定了保持的Borel类；确定了第二个映射的全部不连续点，证明每个不连续点均为跳跃且在稠密处无处单调，从而证实Moroz(2027)的相关猜想；刻画了使Pierce展开数字级数在余贫集上发散的非增位置依赖权重，并建立了Engel与Pierce展开数字序列收敛指数之间的关系。

Conclusion: Engel与Pierce展开通过“第$n$位数字加$n-2$”的显式变换紧密联系，该变换诱导同胚并给出范畴转移原理；相关映射的不连续结构与无处单调性得到完整刻画，验证了既有猜想，并为展开数字级数的发散性与收敛指数研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct an explicit correspondence between Engel and Pierce expansion digit sequences, obtained by adding $n-2$ to the $n$th Engel expansion digit, and show that it induces a homeomorphism from the set of irrationals in the unit interval onto the complement of a countable dense subset of this set. This yields a transfer principle for Baire category between the two expansions, and we determine exactly which Borel classes are preserved. We then identify this homeomorphism with the composition of two maps considered by Moroz (2027), and study the continuity of the second map, which sends modified Engel expansions to Pierce expansions. We determine its set of discontinuities, show that every discontinuity is a jump, and prove that the map is nowhere monotone. In particular, this confirms, for this map, the nowhere monotonicity and the continuity outside the exceptional set conjectured by Moroz (2027). As applications, we characterize the non-increasing position-dependent weights for which the associated series of Pierce expansion digits diverges on a comeager set, and we relate the convergence exponents of the Engel and Pierce expansion digit sequences.

</details>


### [141] [Indefinite theta vectors and generalized Nahm sums of arbitrary rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00967)
*Dandan Chen,Siyu Yin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为任意秩 \(r\geq2\) 的一类带符号广义 Nahm 和构造了不定 theta 表示，给出权重为 1 的模变换定律，并将四个相关乘积向量的各分量表示为广义 Nahm 和的有限组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将带符号广义 Nahm 和与不定 theta 函数及模形式理论联系起来，通过构造 theta 向量及其实解析完备化，揭示这类多重和所满足的模变换性质，并由此得到乘积向量分量的多重和表示。

Method: 对任意秩 \(r\geq2\) 的带符号广义 Nahm 和，选取带符号分支并进行显式归一化与边界修正，使其中一个分支给出与 signature \((1,1)\) 格相伴的 \((2r-1)\) 分量全纯 theta 向量的偶数分量；用 Euler 型多重和给出奇数分量，从而得到关于秩与分量指标一致的公式。利用 Zwegers 理论确定实解析完备化的权重 1 模变换定律，给出 \(S\)-变换的显式有限正弦矩阵；将非全纯修正分解为 unary theta 函数与一维误差函数级数的有限乘积之和，并计算完备化的反全纯导数，结合完备化变换定律得到全纯向量的非齐次变换定律。还计算两个带符号分支的线性组合，将其表示为 Jacobi 乘积商的差；对两族可对称化矩阵及其对偶作进一步求值。

Result: 构造出带符号广义 Nahm 和的不定 theta 表示，其偶数与奇数分量分别由归一化带符号分支和 Euler 型多重和给出，公式对秩和分量指标统一。确定了实解析完备化的权重 1 模变换定律及 \(S\)-变换的显式有限正弦矩阵，得到非全纯修正的分解和反全纯导数，以及全纯向量的非齐次变换定律。还将两个带符号分支的线性组合表示为 Jacobi 乘积商之差；结合已知结果，对两族可对称化矩阵及其对偶，给出了四个相关乘积向量每个分量的多重和表示，这些表示为具有指定矩阵与对称化子的广义 Nahm 和的有限组合，其中对偶族允许带符号求和与半周期平移。

Conclusion: 该工作将带符号广义 Nahm 和纳入不定 theta 与模变换框架，建立了统一的分量公式和模变换定律，并给出乘积向量分量的多重和表示，推广了相关已知结果。

Abstract: We construct indefinite theta representations for a family of signed generalized Nahm sums of arbitrary rank \(r\geq2\). After explicit normalization and boundary correction, one signed branch gives the even components of a \((2r-1)\)-component holomorphic theta vector associated with a lattice of signature \((1,1)\). Euler-type multiple sums provide the odd components, yielding formulas uniform in both the rank and the component index. Using Zwegers' theory, we determine the weight-one modular transformation laws of the real-analytic completion, with an explicit finite sine matrix for the \(S\)-transformation. We decompose the nonholomorphic correction into a finite sum of products of unary theta functions and one-dimensional error-function series, and compute the antiholomorphic derivative of the completion. Together with the completed transformation laws, these formulas give the inhomogeneous transformation law of the holomorphic vector. We also evaluate a linear combination of two signed branches as a difference of Jacobi product quotients. This identity and further evaluations for two families of symmetrizable matrices and their duals, combined with known results, give multiple-sum representations for every component of four associated product vectors. These representations are finite combinations of generalized Nahm sums with the prescribed matrices and symmetrizers; in the dual families, signed sums and half-period translates are allowed.

</details>


### [142] [The sixth moment of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01035)
*Alexandre de Faveri,Mayank Pandey*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了黎曼ζ函数在临界线上的新大值估计，首次改进了Hardy-Littlewood(1923)关于 $|\zeta(1/2+it)|\geq T^{1/8}$ 测度的上界，并据此改进了 $4<k\leq 12$ 时ζ函数 $k$ 阶矩的上界，特别给出六阶矩的新估计 $\int_0^T|\zeta(1/2+it)|^6dt\ll_\varepsilon T^{5/4-1/60+\varepsilon}$。核心工具是带平方根相位的指数和及其扰动的新的的大值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ζ函数大值估计与矩问题是解析数论的核心问题。关于 $|\zeta(1/2+it)|\geq T^{1/8}$ 的测度上界自Hardy-Littlewood(1923)以来长期未获改进，同时 $k$ 阶矩（尤其 $4<k\leq 12$ 区间）的精确上界也亟待推进。作者旨在通过新的解析工具突破这些长期停滞的结果。

Method: 核心方法是建立带平方根相位（square-root phases）的指数和及其扰动的新的的大值估计。这类指数和出现在ζ函数短二阶矩（short second moment）的展开中，作者利用该大值估计来推导ζ函数在临界线上的大值测度上界与矩估计。

Result: (1) 首次改进Hardy-Littlewood(1923)关于集合 $\{t\in[T,2T]:|\zeta(1/2+it)|\geq T^{1/8}\}$ 测度的上界；(2) 对每个 $4<k\leq 12$ 得到改进的 $k$ 阶矩上界；(3) 特别地证明六阶矩估计 $\int_0^T|\zeta(1/2+it)|^6dt\ll_\varepsilon T^{5/4-1/60+\varepsilon}$。

Conclusion: 通过引入平方根相位指数和的新大值估计，本文在ζ函数大值测度与矩估计两个经典问题上取得了实质性进展，突破了自1923年以来未被改进的界，并为相关指数和理论提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We prove new large value estimates for the Riemann zeta function on the critical line. For instance, we improve the upper bound on the measure of $t\in [T, 2T]$ with $|ζ(1/2 + it)| \geq T^{1/8}$ for the first time since Hardy-Littlewood (1923). Our results imply improved upper bounds on the $k$-th moment of zeta for every $4 < k \leq 12$. We show in particular that
  \begin{equation*}
  \int_{0}^{T} |ζ(\tfrac{1}{2} + it)|^6 \,d t \ll_\varepsilon T^{\frac{5}{4} - \frac{1}{60} + \varepsilon}.
  \end{equation*}
  The main ingredient is a new large value estimate for exponential sums with square-root phases, and certain perturbations. Those arise in the expansion of the short second moment of zeta.

</details>


### [143] [Sharp $p$-adic Extrema and Least Extremal Rows for Restricted Binomial GCDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01328)
*John Fairfax-Ball*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文研究受限二项式系数最大公约数 G(N;m) 的 p 进赋值上确界，证明其最大值恰为 r_p(m)，并给出取等构造；对 m=p^a+1 精确确定最小极值行 T_p(m)=p^{3a}+1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限二项式最大公约数族在文献中已有记载，但其 p 进赋值（p-adic valuation）的确切上界与取等条件尚不完整；作者希望确定 v_p(G(N;m)) 在所有可行行 N 上的最大值，并刻画达到该最大值的行，尤其是最小极值行 T_p(m)。

Method: 方法上结合 Kummer 进位定理（carry theorem）与显式首位数字见证（leading-digit witness）证明普适上界 v_p(G(N;m))≤r_p(m)；用乘法阶（multiplicative-order）构造给出取等行；随后对 m=p^a+1 通过独立的严格最小性论证证明在 p^{3a}+1 以下不存在取等行，并用针对唯一最坏首位数字模式的备用见证处理边界情形。

Result: 主要结果：对所有可行行 N，v_p(G(N;m)) 的最大值恰为 r_p(m)（r_p(m) 为使 m<p^r 的最小正整数），并给出构造性取等行；取等性在最小极值行 T_p(m) 定义之前即已建立。对特殊族 m=p^a+1（a≥2），精确确定 T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1。

Conclusion: 论文确定了受限二项式最大公约数 p 进赋值的精确上确界与达到该值的行结构，并对 m=p^a+1 给出最小极值行的显式公式；同时明确说明该受限 GCD 族与已有文献的关系以及文献检索的局限性。

Abstract: For integers $m\ge 2$ and rows $N>m$ divisible by $m$, consider the restricted binomial greatest common divisor $G(N;m)=\gcd\{\binom Nk:0<k<N,\ m\mid k\}$. Fix a prime $p$ with $p\nmid m$, and let $r_p(m)$ be the least positive integer $r$ such that $m<p^r$. We prove that the largest possible value of $v_p(G(N;m))$, as $N$ ranges over all admissible rows, is exactly $r_p(m)$, and we give a constructive equality row. Attainment is established before the least extremal row $T_p(m)$ is defined. For the special family $m=p^a+1$ with $a\ge2$, we determine that least row exactly: $T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1$. The proof combines Kummer's carry theorem with an explicit leading-digit witness for the universal upper bound, a multiplicative-order construction for equality, and a separate strict-minimality argument below $p^{3a}+1$ with a fallback witness for the unique worst leading-digit pattern. The selected-GCD family itself is known in the literature; the relation to earlier results and the limits of the documented literature search are stated explicitly.

</details>


### [144] [A Prime-Power Dichotomy for Nekrasov--Okounkov Hook Lengths and $t$-Core Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01422)
*Meenu Sharma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Nekrasov–Okounkov钩长生成函数系数$a_t(n)$与$t$-core分拆生成函数系数$b_t(n)$之间的模$t$同余当且仅当$t$为素数幂，并在$t=p$素数时强化到模$p^2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两类分拆理论生成函数（Nekrasov–Okounkov钩长函数与$t$-core分拆函数）的系数在模算术意义下的等价性，确定其成立的确切模数条件。

Method: 通过比较$a_t(n)$与$b_t(n)$的生成函数，利用模算术与素数幂结构分析，证明同余$a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t$对所有$n\ge 0$成立的充要条件，并对素数情形提升模数至$p^2$。

Result: 证明了尖锐的素数幂二分性：对任意整数$t\ge 2$，同余$a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t$对所有$n\ge 0$成立当且仅当$t$为素数幂；当$t=p$为素数时同余可强化为模$p^2$；对$t=6$及所有非素数幂的$t$，同余不成立。

Conclusion: 素数幂恰好是使这两类分拆理论系数序列在模意义下算术等价的模数，刻画了二者等价性的精确边界。

Abstract: Let $a_t(n)$ denote the coefficients of the Nekrasov--Okounkov hook length generating function $F_{t^2}(x)$, and let $b_t(n)$ denote the coefficients of the $t$-core partition generating function $C_t(x)$. We prove a sharp prime-power dichotomy: for every integer $t\ge 2$, the congruence $$ a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t $$ holds for all $n\ge 0$ if and only if $t$ is a prime power. When $t=p$ is prime, the congruence strengthens to modulo $p^2$. For $t=6$, and more generally for every integer that is not a prime power, the congruence fails. This identifies prime powers as the exact moduli for which these two partition-theoretic coefficient sequences are arithmetically equivalent.

</details>


### [145] [Tridimensional character sums with polynomial arguments and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01524)
*Étienne Fouvry,Igor E. Shparlinski,Ping Xi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究素数模下含多项式的三重特征和，在两种情形下给出了非平凡上界，并应用于短区间内2×2不可约特征多项式矩阵的计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作源于Ganguly与Rajan（2023）关于F_p上元素取自短区间的2×2矩阵中具有不可约特征多项式的计数问题的一个中间结果，旨在改进相应特征和的估计以缩短可用区间长度。

Method: 研究形如∑_{a~A}∑_{b~B}∑_{c~C} α(a,b)β(c)χ(f(a)+bc)的特征和，其中f∈Z[X]次数k较小，α、β为复系数；对两种系数与次数情形分别建立非平凡上界：情形(1) β≡1、k=2,3,4,5 且 A,B,C>p^{1/8+ε}；情形(2) β一般、k=2,3 且 A,B,C>p^{1/6+ε}。

Result: 在所述两种情形下均证明了该三重特征和的非平凡上界；据此可将具有不可约特征多项式的2×2矩阵计数推广到更短的区间段。

Conclusion: 所得新界改进了原有中间结果，使得相关矩阵计数问题可在更短的segment长度下成立。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a large prime and $χ$ a non-trivial Dirichlet character modulo $p$. We study the character sum \[ \sum_{a \sim A} \sum_{b \sim B} \sum_{c \sim C} α(a,b) β(c)χ(f(a) + bc), \] where $z \sim Z$ means $Z\le z < 2Z$, $f \in \mathbb{Z}[X]$ is of small degree $k$, and $\boldsymbolα=(α(a,b))_{a\sim A,b\sim B}$ and $\boldsymbolβ=(β(c))_{c\sim C}$ are two complex coefficients. We prove non-trivial upper bounds for this sum in either of the two cases: (1) $\boldsymbolβ\equiv1$, $k=2,3,4,5$ and $A,B,C>p^{\frac{1}{8}+\varepsilon}$, (2) $\boldsymbolβ$ general, $k=2,3$ and $A,B,C>p^{\frac{1}{6}+\varepsilon}$, where $\varepsilon>0$ is fixed.
  This work was originally motivated by an intermediate result of Ganguly and Rajan (2023) on counting $2\times2$ matrices over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with irreducible characteristic polynomials, where the entries are in short segments. The new bounds here allow us to count such matrices in much shorter segments.

</details>


### [146] [Uniform estimate of Lang-Weil type for counting points of schemes over finite rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01748)
*Kien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限环 Z/p^m Z 上有限型 Z-概型的点数估计，建立了以 m、低一阶点数 X(Z/p^{m-1}Z) 及 p-adic Igusa 局部 zeta 函数极点为参数的统一估计，可视为 Lang-Weil 估计的归纳版本，并与 Igusa 指数和猜想预期界相吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Igusa 指数和猜想与 Lang-Weil 估计的启发，作者希望对有限环 Z/p^m Z 上的概型点数给出统一的、关于 m≥2 与 p 一致的估计，并借此处理 Igusa 指数和猜想在有理奇点情形下的相关问题。

Method: 在通常的奇点消解与 Denef 关于 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数公式之外，引入 Hermite 插值公式研究 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数的分子；并通过大量 p-adic 分析，用适当的广义对数典范阈值给出 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数非平凡极点实部的上界。

Result: 给出了 X(Z/p^m Z) 关于 m、X(Z/p^{m-1}Z) 及 X 的 p-adic Igusa 局部 zeta 函数极点的估计，该估计在 m≥2 与 p 上一致。当 X_Q 不是仅有有理奇点的局部完全交时，估计与 Igusa 指数和猜想的预期界一致；当 X_Q 是仅有有理奇点的局部完全交时，估计非常接近预期界。

Conclusion: 所得估计可视为概型在有限环上计点的 Lang-Weil 估计的归纳版本，并类比于 Cluckers、Mustaţǎ 与作者 2019 年关于非有理奇点多项式指数和模素数幂的估计；同时借助同一方法可逼近有理奇点情形下的 Igusa 指数和猜想，且所建立的 p-adic 分析上界为后续应用提供了工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the number of points of $\mathbb{Z}$-schemes $X$ of finite type over the finite rings $\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z}$ for positive integers $m$ and prime numbers $p$. Motivated by Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums and the Lang-Weil estimate, we establish an estimate of $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z})$ in terms of $m$, $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^{m-1}\mathbb{Z})$ and poles of the $p$-adic Igusa local zeta function of $X$ uniformly in $m\geq 2$ and $p$. Our estimate might be viewed as an inductive version for counting points of schemes over finite rings of the Lang-Weil estimate. Our estimate agrees with the expected bound of Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums if $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ is not a locally complete intersection having only rational singularities. This can be regarded as an analogous version of the estimate of exponential sums modulo powers of primes of polynomials with non-rational singularities given by Cluckers, Mustaţǎ and the author in 2019. In the case that $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ is a locally complete intersection having only rational singularities, our estimate is very close to the expected bound of Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums. To prove our estimate, beside using the usual tools such as resolutions of singularities and Denef's formula for Igusa local zeta functions, we also use the Hermit interpolation formula to study the numerators of Igusa local zeta functions. By the same method, we are able to approach Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums in the case of rational singularities. Lastly, to have further applications of our result in the future, we study heavily on $p$-adic analysis to give an upper bound of the real parts of non-trivial poles of Igusa local zeta functions in terms of suitable generalized log canonical thresholds.

</details>


### [147] [Quadratic distances in even dimensions over prime fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01795)
*Thang Pham,Chun-Yen Shen,Dung The Tran,Boqing Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文研究有限域上非退化二次型在点集上的距离集（差分集）大小的下界估计，证明在Witt指数为m-1的条件下，当|E|≥p^m时|Δ_Q(E)|有独立于p、m、Q的下界，并在平面情形m=1给出达到对数因子最优的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域F_p^{2m}上二次型Q的距离集Δ_Q(E)的大小，是对经典欧氏距离问题在有限域情形的类比，旨在刻画集合的二次型差分能量分布及其扩展性质的下界。

Method: 设p为奇素数，m≥1，Q为F_p^{2m}上Witt指数为m-1的非退化二次型，定义Δ_Q(E)={Q(x-y):x,y∈E}。通过解析数论与加性组合工具（如特征和、傅里叶分析等）对Δ_Q(E)的基数进行下界估计。

Result: 当|E|≥p^m时，|Δ_Q(E)|≫ p/log(2+p^{m+1}/|E|)，隐含常数绝对且独立于p、m、Q；在平面情形m=1下，对1≤|E|≤p，有|Δ_Q(E)|≫|E|/log(2|E|)，该结果在差一个对数因子的意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 论文给出了有限域二次型距离集大小的有效下界，推广至一般维数与Witt指数情形，并在平面情形得到对数因子意义下最优的估计。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime, let $m\geq1$ be an integer, and let $Q$ be a nondegenerate quadratic form on $\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$ with Witt index $m-1$. For a nonempty set $E\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$, write $Δ_Q(E)=\{Q(x-y):x,y\in E\}$. We prove that, whenever $|E|\geq p^m$, $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg \frac{p}{\log\bigl(2+p^{m+1}/|E|\bigr)}, $$ with an absolute implied constant independent of $p$, $m$ and $Q$. In the planar case $m=1$, we also prove that $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg\frac{|E|}{\log(2|E|)}, \qquad (1\leq |E|\leq p), $$ which is optimal up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>


### [148] [Averages of Artin characters and their relation to the Davenport--Heilbronn theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01837)
*Victor Ahlquist*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将三次域计数函数的误差项改进到 Ο_ε(X^{θ+ε})，θ<2/3；在GRH下进一步改进，并对光滑计数、一水平密度、中心点非零比例及函数域情形给出新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 Bhargava、Taniguchi、Thorne 关于三次域计数函数误差项的既有结果，并在GRH条件下获得更强估计；同时推进 Artin L-函数族一水平密度的容许支撑范围及中心点非零比例。

Method: 将 Bhargava、Shankar、Tsimerman 的判别式缩减筛法与对应于 Artin L-函数 ζ_K(s)/ζ(s) 的 Artin 特征平均相联系（K 为三次域）；该方法是一水平密度研究的自然推广，并可处理有限多个分裂条件。

Result: 无条件得到误差项 Ο_ε(X^{θ+ε})，θ<2/3；在 Dedekind zeta 函数的GRH下改进为 Ο(X^{2/3-1/51+ε})；光滑计数问题在GRH下为 Ο(X^{1/2+ε})；一水平密度容许支撑由 σ<2/5 改进到 σ<1（GRH下），并证明至少75%的L-函数在中心点非零；函数域 F_q(T)（q与6互素）上无条件得到非零比例至少75%及计数误差 Ο(X^{1/2+ε})。

Conclusion: 本文通过筛法与 Artin 特征平均的联系显著改进了三次域计数误差项，并在GRH下进一步优化；一水平密度支撑与中心点非零比例均获提升，函数域情形结果无条件成立。

Abstract: We bound the error term in the counting function of cubic extensions of $\mathbb{Q}$ by $\mathcal{O}_ε\left(X^{θ+ε}\right)$, with $θ< 2/3$, improving upon results due to Bhargava, Taniguchi, and Thorne. The proof proceeds by relating a certain discriminant-reducing sieve, due to Bhargava, Shankar, and Tsimerman, to averages of Artin characters associated with the Artin $L$-function $ζ_K(s)/ζ(s)$, with $K$ a cubic field. Conditional on the Generalised Riemann Hypothesis (GRH) for Dedekind zeta functions $ζ_K(s)$, we obtain further savings and show that the error term is $\mathcal{O}\left(X^{2/3-1/51+ε}\right)$. Moreover, for the analogous smooth counting problem, we obtain a bound $\mathcal{O}(X^{1/2+ε})$ for the error term, conditionally improving results of Shankar, Södergren, and Templier. Our arguments can also handle finitely many splitting conditions.
  The methods we use to study cubic fields are natural extensions of methods which are used to study the one-level density associated with the above family of Artin $L$-functions. In this direction, we improve the admissible support $[-σ,σ]$, from the previously known admissible value $σ< 2/5$, to $σ< 1$, conditional on the GRH. This also proves that at least $75\%$ of these $L$-functions are non-vanishing at the central point, conditionally improving upon results of Shankar, Södergren and Templier.
  Finally, one may study similar questions over a rational function field $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$, with $q$ coprime to $2$ and $3$. Here, we obtain a proportion of non-vanishing of at least $75\%$ for the analogous family of $L$-functions. Furthermore, we obtain a bound $\mathcal{O}(X^{1/2+ε})$ for the error term in the counting function of cubic function fields, improving previous results of the author. Over function fields, our results are unconditional.

</details>


### [149] [Square products of factorials and a conjecture of Erdős and Graham](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01899)
*Fedir Yudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究了$n!$作为$k$个不同阶乘乘积中最大因子且该乘积为完全平方数的问题，确定了$D_k(X)$对每个$k$的增长阶：$D_3(X)=\kappa_3\sqrt X+O_\varepsilon(X^{2/5+\varepsilon})$，且$D_5(X)\asymp D_6(X)\asymp X$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős和Graham提出了$3\le k\le6$时$D_k(X)$的增长阶问题，并猜想$D_6(X)\gg X$。本文旨在确定所有$k$对应的$D_k(X)$增长阶。

Method: 对$k=3$，平衡Pell方程的均匀界与Gallagher大筛法，并用Weil界提供剩余类限制，得到$2/5$指数。对$k=5,6$，限制到含有大于$X^{1-\alpha}$素因子的整数，结合Matomaki-Radziwill-Shao-Tao-Teravainen关于素数等分布估计与大筛法排除更短表示。附录中用有限阿贝尔群零和定理构造完全$m$次幂。

Result: $D_3(X)=\kappa_3\sqrt X+O_\varepsilon(X^{2/5+\varepsilon})$，显式常数$\kappa_3=2.7097\ldots$；$D_5(X)\asymp D_6(X)\asymp X$。结合经典结果，确定了所有$k$的$D_k(X)$增长阶。

Conclusion: 完整确定了$D_k(X)$对每个$k$的增长阶，验证了Erdős-Graham关于$D_6(X)\gg X$的猜想，并给出$D_3(X)$的渐近公式及显式常数。

Abstract: For $n\ge2$ let $F(n)$ be the least $k\ge2$ such that $n!$ is the largest factor in a product of $k$ distinct factorials that is a perfect square, and let $D_k(X)$ be the number of $n\le X$ with $F(n)=k$. Erdos and Graham asked for the order of growth of $D_k(X)$ for $3\le k\le6$, and conjectured that $D_6(X)\gg X$. We prove that $D_3(X)=κ_3\sqrt X+O_\varepsilon(X^{2/5+\varepsilon})$ with an explicit constant $κ_3=2.7097\ldots$, and that $D_5(X)\asymp D_6(X)\asymp X$. Together with classical facts, this determines the order of growth of $D_k(X)$ for every $k$. The exponent $2/5$ comes from balancing a uniform bound for Pell equations against Gallagher's larger sieve, with residue restrictions supplied by the Weil bound. For five and six factors we restrict to integers with a prime factor exceeding $X^{1-α}$, where $α>0$ is small and fixed. We exclude shorter representations by combining an equidistribution estimate for primes of Matomaki, Radziwill, Shao, Tao and Teravainen with the large sieve. In an appendix we use a zero-sum theorem for finite abelian groups to construct, for every $m\ge2$, perfect $m$-th powers that are products of a bounded number of distinct factorials with arguments given by fixed affine functions.

</details>


### [150] [Supersingularity and Superspeciality Verification of Abelian Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01924)
*Maria Corte-Real Santos,Gioella Lorenzon,Krijn Reijnders*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首次研究超奇异阿贝尔曲面的判定问题，提出$O(\log p)$的蒙特卡洛算法验证$\mathbb{F}_p$上阿贝尔曲面是否超奇异（失败概率可忽略），并在阶光滑时给出确定性算法；额外给出任意维阿贝尔簇极值性与超特殊雅可比矩阵的验证算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超奇异阿贝尔曲面在基于同源的密码学中至关重要，但目前没有高效算法验证给定的阿贝尔曲面是否超奇异，这是一个关键空白。

Method: 通过对$\mathbb{F}_p$上超奇异雅可比矩阵结构的细致分析，推导出验证算法；并将方法推广到任意维阿贝尔簇的极值性验证和超特殊雅可比验证。

Result: 给出$O(\log p)$的蒙特卡洛算法，失败概率可忽略；若阶光滑则给出确定性（conclusive）算法；还可高效验证任意维阿贝尔簇是否minimal或maximal，以及任意维雅可比是否超特殊。

Conclusion: 本文开启了超奇异阿贝尔曲面高效判定这一研究方向，提供了高效算法，并拓展到更高维情形。

Abstract: Supersingular abelian surfaces are essential in isogeny-based cryptography. Despite this, we have no efficient algorithm to verify if a given abelian surface is supersingular. In this work, we initiate this research topic by giving an efficient Monte Carlo algorithm to verify if an abelian surface over $\mathbb{F}_p$ is supersingular in $O(\log p)$ with negligible failure probability, and an efficient conclusive algorithm if the order is smooth. We derive this algorithm by a careful analysis on the structure of supersingular Jacobians over $\mathbb{F}_p$. Furthermore, we derive efficient algorithms to verify if an abelian variety of any dimension is minimal or maximal, and to verify if a Jacobian of any dimension is superspecial.

</details>


### [151] [Infinite free pro-p products as absolute Galois groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01966)
*Tamar Bar-On*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明广义Mel'nikov判别法，并将其用于刻画自由投射有限群与自由pro-p群作为绝对Galois群实现时与pro-p群层乘积的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mel'nikov判别法是Galois理论中判定群是否为绝对Galois群的重要工具（Haran也曾证明），但需要更一般的形式来处理自由投射有限群及自由pro-p群的结构问题。

Method: 证明Mel'nikov判别法的推广形式，并将其应用于自由投射有限群与自由pro-p群（作为pro-p群层的自由乘积）的绝对Galois群实现问题。

Result: 建立了广义Mel'nikov判别法，并给出自由投射有限群与自由pro-p群的自由乘积（相对于pro-p群层）的绝对Galois群实现之间的关联。

Conclusion: 广义Mel'nikov判别法可用于统一处理自由投射有限群和自由pro-p乘积作为绝对Galois群的实现问题。

Abstract: In this paper we prove a generalized version of Mel'nikov's Criterion, which has also been proved by Haran, and use it in order to connect the realization as absolute Galois groups of free pronite and free pro-p products of a sheaf of pro-p groups.

</details>


### [152] [On the prime geodesic theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01998)
*Alberto Acosta Reche*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了模群（模曲线）素数测地线定理的误差项，并将结果推广到有理数域上任意不定四元数代数的任意同余子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 素数测地线定理的误差项刻画了闭测地线计数与主项之间的偏差，与Selberg特征值问题、自守L函数零点分布等密切相关，改进误差项是该领域的核心问题之一。此前结果多局限于模群或较窄的群类。

Method: 通过自守形式/L函数的解析方法（如Selberg迹公式与L函数零点区域的估计）处理闭测地线计数的误差，并将论证推广至一般不定四元数代数的同余子群所对应的算术双曲曲面。

Result: 在该设定下获得了比已有结果更优的误差项估计，且适用范围从模群扩展到任意不定四元数代数上的任意同余子群。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并改进了素数测地线定理的误差项，说明所给方法在更一般的算术双曲曲面族上有效。

Abstract: We improve the error term in the prime geodesic theorem for the modular group. More generally, our result applies to any congruence subgroup of any indefinite quaternion algebra over $\mathbb{Q}$.

</details>


### [153] [On The Index of Polynomial Compositions over Valued Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02111)
*Anuj Jakhar,Ravi Kalwaniya,Shanta Laishram,Prabhakar Yadav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究由多项式复合 f(h(x)) 定义的代数数域的单基因性（是否存在幂整基）。在任意 Krull 赋值环上，当 f 首一、h 为首一三项式时，给出了复合多项式的判别式显式公式，并用其刻画复合域的单基因性；进一步推广到多项式迭代，得到二项式迭代的单基因性判别准则，构造出新的无限族单基因域，并给出定量结果与实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断代数数域是否具有幂整基是经典问题，但对由多项式复合与动力迭代定义的域较为困难。已有方法难以处理复合结构下判别式与单基因性的关系，因此需要一个能显式刻画复合与迭代情形单基因性的理论框架。

Method: 在任意 Krull 赋值环上考虑首一多项式 f(x) 与首一三项式 h(x) 的复合 f(h(x))。推导该复合多项式判别式的显式公式，并据此建立单基因性判别条件：将复合的单基因性与 f(x) 的单基因性以及 h(x) 临界值上的显式无平方条件联系起来。随后把方法推广到多项式迭代，导出二项式迭代的单基因性准则。

Result: 得到复合多项式判别式的显式公式及 f(h(x)) 生成单基因域的充要条件；建立复合单基因性与 f 的单基因性、h 临界值无平方性之间的关系；给出多项式迭代的推广及二项式迭代的单基因性判别准则，由此构造出新的无限族单基因域，并提供定量结果与若干实例验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为复合与迭代多项式定义的单基因域提供了统一且显式的判别工具，将问题归结为 f 的单基因性与 h 临界值的无平方条件，并能够批量生成新的单基因域族。

Abstract: Determining whether an algebraic number field admits a power integral basis is a classical problem, but it can be difficult for fields defined by polynomial compositions and dynamical iterates. In this paper, we study the monogeneity of compositions $f(h(x))$, where $f(x)$ and $h(x)$ are monic polynomials over an arbitrary Krull valuation ring and $h(x)$ is a trinomial. We derive explicit formulas for the discriminant of the composition and use them to characterize when $f(h(x))$ generates a monogenic field. In particular, we relate the monogeneity of the composition to that of $f(x)$ and to explicit square-free conditions on the critical values of $h(x)$. We further extend the results to polynomial iteration and obtain a criterion for the monogeneity of binomial iterates, yielding new infinite families of monogenic fields. Finally, we give quantitative results and illustrate our criteria with several examples.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Inverse Optimal Control with Convex Features and Box Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00033)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang*
