Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究凸特征下层目标中非负权重的逆最优控制问题，建模为乐观双层最优控制，证明解映射单值Lipschitz、值函数凹性与渐近精确松弛，并给出局部可辨识性、O(δ)噪声稳定性及KKT-MPCC的C-平稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优控制中识别下层目标权重通常面临下层解不唯一、主动集不可微等困难，需要在L^2盒约束控制问题下建立解映射的良态性与可辨识性理论。

Method: 将问题表述为乐观双层最优控制：下层为强凸、线性约束、L^2盒约束的控制问题；对约化响应映射x↦(y_x,u_x)证明全局单值与显式Lipschitz估计；利用下层泛函对x的仿射依赖推导值函数凹性与Lipschitz性及渐近精确最优值松弛；基于二阶增长的免梯度论证建立局部可辨识性与O(δ)噪声稳定性；通过KKT重构得到函数空间MPCC，并用Scholtes松弛在乘子一致有界下导出C-平稳性；最后以解耦标量实例给出严格互补证书。

Result: 约化响应映射全局单值且具显式Lipschitz估计（无需主动集可微）；值函数凹且Lipschitz并具渐近精确最优值松弛；获得局部可辨识性与O(δ)噪声稳定性；KKT重构为函数空间MPCC，Scholtes松弛在乘子一致有界下给出C-平稳性；解耦标量例子通过阈值接触的有限性提供显式严格互补证书。

Conclusion: 该工作为凸特征下层目标的逆最优控制提供了系统的适定性与稳定性理论，并建立了函数空间MPCC的平稳性框架，为算法设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: We study an inverse optimal-control problem for identifying nonnegative weights in a convex-feature lower-level objective. The resulting model is an optimistic bilevel optimal-control problem whose lower level is a strongly convex, linearly constrained control problem with box constraints in \(L^2\). For the reduced response map \(x\mapsto (y_x,u_x)\), we prove global single-valuedness and an explicit Lipschitz estimate, without assuming differentiability of active sets. Exploiting the affine dependence of the lower-level functional on \(x\), we show that the value function is concave and Lipschitz and derive an asymptotically exact optimal-value relaxation. We further establish local identifiability and \(O(δ)\) noise stability by a gradient-free argument based on second-order growth. Finally, a KKT reformulation yields a function-space MPCC; Scholtes relaxation gives C-stationarity under uniform multiplier boundedness. A decoupled scalar example provides an explicit strict-complementarity certificate via finiteness of threshold contacts.

</details>


### [155] [Improved upper bounds for five IHTC-2024 benchmark instances via exact reoptimization of the nurse-to-room layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00044)
*Alfonso Gippini Requeijo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 作者在IHTC-2024基准上报告了5个新的更优上界（i02、i05、m01、m03、m04），均通过在固定入院日、房间分配和手术室安排的前提下，对护士-房间-班次层重新求最优而得到，且经官方验证器检验无硬约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IHTC-2024基准整合了手术病例规划、患者入院调度与护士-房间分配，其官网公开最优已知上界表并持续接受新提交。作者旨在改进该表，同时检验已发表最优解在护士分配层的实际最优性。

Method: 不做从头求解：对基准页面公布的对应解，固定其入院天数、房间分配和手术室分配，仅对护士-房间-班次层重新优化至可证明最优。

Result: 获得5个改进上界：i02为1263（原1264）、i05为12744（原12760）、m01为3380（原3384）、m03为6692（原6697）、m04为3315（原3318），改进幅度0.075%–0.125%，全部通过官方IHTP验证器且无硬约束违反。

Conclusion: 当护士层可精确闭合时，除一个实例外均可在数秒内完成求解，表明若干已公布的先进解在该层并未达到最优；护士层仍存在被忽视的小幅优化空间。

Abstract: The Integrated Healthcare Timetabling Competition 2024 (IHTC-2024) defined a benchmark that integrates surgical case planning, patient admission scheduling and nurse-to-room assignment. Its instance set remains live: the organizers keep a public table of best-known upper bounds that is open to new submissions. We report five improved upper bounds on that table, for instances i02 (1263, previously 1264), i05 (12744, previously 12760), m01 (3380, previously 3384), m03 (6692, previously 6697) and m04 (3315, previously 3318). All five solutions were checked with the official IHTP validator and carry no hard-constraint violations. The solutions are not built from scratch: each is derived from the corresponding solution published on the benchmark page, holding its admission days, room assignments and operating-theater assignments fixed, and reoptimizing only the nurse-to-room-and-shift layer to proven optimality. The improvements are small in magnitude, between 0.075% and 0.125%, but they carry a structural observation: on the instances where the nurse layer can be closed exactly, it closes within seconds on all but one of them, and several of the published state-of-the-art solutions fall short of optimality in that layer.

</details>


### [156] [Predictor-Based Exponential Tracking of Turbulent Solutions for the Kuramoto--Sivashinsky Equation with Input Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00048)
*Amadou Cissé*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对带常数输入时滞的非线性Kuramoto–Sivashinsky方程，提出基于预测器–反步变换的均匀指数跟踪控制，实现全局适定性与均匀指数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳参考轨迹（位于全局吸引子内的完整轨迹族）在输入时滞下跟踪困难，且参考无需满足驻定、周期、慢变或有限维外系统生成等限制条件；同时需使反馈参数与稳定性常数不依赖于初始时刻与参考轨迹。

Method: 将时滞输入用一阶输运方程表示，与四阶跟踪误差动力学耦合构成增广系统；采用预测器–反步变换补偿指令生成与执行的时滞不匹配，将增广闭环系统映射为无时滞的名义误差动力学，其驱动项为齐次输运子系统的出射迹，该子系统在一个时滞区间后消失；利用均匀吸引子界选取反馈参数与稳定性常数。

Result: 在增广状态空间中建立了全局适定性与均匀指数稳定性；数值结果表明，未补偿的时滞反馈会放大跟踪误差，而预测器补偿可恢复持续衰减；预测器一致性、有限时间消隐与离散化细化诊断验证了数值实现。

Conclusion: 预测器–反步补偿可有效实现非平稳轨迹的均匀指数跟踪，参数选择独立于初始时刻和参考轨迹，数值与诊断结果支持该方法的可实现性。

Abstract: Uniform exponential tracking is addressed for nonstationary trajectories of the nonlinear Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equation subject to a constant input delay. The reference belongs to a family of complete trajectories contained in the global attractor and need not be stationary, periodic, slowly varying, or generated by a finite-dimensional exosystem. The delayed input is represented by a first-order transport equation coupled with the fourth-order tracking-error dynamics. A predictor--backstepping transformation compensates for the temporal mismatch between command generation and actuation by mapping the augmented closed-loop system into the nominal delay-free error dynamics driven by the outgoing trace of a homogeneous transport subsystem. This subsystem vanishes after one delay interval. Uniform attractor bounds permit the feedback parameters and stability constants to be selected independently of the initial time and the reference trajectory. Global well-posedness and uniform exponential stability are established in the augmented state space. Numerical results show that, for the considered configuration, uncompensated delayed feedback amplifies the tracking error, whereas predictor compensation restores sustained decay. Predictor-consistency, finite-time-extinction, and discretization-refinement diagnostics support the numerical implementation.

</details>


### [157] [New Pareto Z-eigenvalue localization theorems for tensors with applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00113)
*Licai Shao,Xingong Shao,Yisheng Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为张量特征值互补问题的Pareto Z-特征值建立了新的定位集，厘清了其与已有定位集的关系，并据此给出了TCP与TGEiCP解存在性的充分条件，同时将该条件应用于一般标量势真空稳定性的判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量特征值互补问题中Pareto Z-特征值的定位是分析解的存在性的关键工具，已有定位集之间存在关系不明、精度不足等问题，亟需建立新的定位集并厘清其相互关系，以支持互补问题解存在性判定及物理稳定性等实际应用。

Method: 建立Pareto Z-特征值的新型定位集，并分析其与现有定位集之间的包含或等价关系；进而以此为工具推导张量互补问题（TCP）与张量广义特征值互补问题（TGEiCP）解存在性的充分条件，并将该条件用于检验一般标量势的真空稳定性。

Result: 得到了若干新的Pareto Z-特征值定位集，明确了它们与已有定位集之间的关系；由此获得了TCP和TGEiCP解存在性的一个充分条件，并成功将该条件用于一般标量势真空稳定性的检验。

Conclusion: 所提出的定位集拓广并统一了已有结果，为张量互补类问题的可解性提供了新的判定依据，并展示了该理论在物理真空稳定性分析中的应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish some new localization sets for Pareto Z-eigenvalues of tensor eigenvalue complementarity problems and discuss the relationships among existing ones. As applications, a sufficient condition for the solution existence of the tensor complementarity problem (TCP) and the tensor generalized eigenvalue complementarity problem (TGEiCP) are presented. In addition, this condition is also used to test the vacuum stability of a general scalar potential

</details>


### [158] [Control of 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems: a brief survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00139)
*Long Hu,Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为Jean-Michel Coron七十华诞纪念综述，回顾了一维一阶线性双曲系统控制的研究文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借Jean-Michel Coron七十寿辰之机，梳理并总结其在一维一阶线性双曲系统控制领域的重要贡献及相关文献，以致敬其学术成就。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，系统回顾该领域的研究成果与发展脉络。

Result: 对该领域文献进行了整理与回顾。

Conclusion: 该综述聚焦于Coron做出重要贡献的一维一阶线性双曲系统控制方向，具有纪念与总结双重意义。

Abstract: At the occasion of the 70th birthday anniversary of Jean-Michel Coron, we review the literature on the control of 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems, a topic where he has made significant contributions.

</details>


### [159] [Minimal control time of some 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems with row rank deficient boundary coupling matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00140)
*Long Hu,Guillaume Olive,Zengzhi Xin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类边界耦合矩阵行秩亏缺的一维一阶线性双曲系统的最小控制时间刻画问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维一阶线性双曲系统的最小控制时间在边界耦合矩阵行满秩时已有完备结论，但行秩亏缺情形尚缺乏刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对边界耦合矩阵行秩亏缺的一类系统，通过分析边界耦合结构，给出最小控制时间的刻画。

Result: 刻画了一类边界耦合矩阵行秩亏缺系统的最小控制时间。

Conclusion: 将最小控制时间的已有结果从行满秩耦合情形推广到行秩亏缺耦合情形。

Abstract: The minimal control time of one-dimensional first-order linear hyperbolic systems is by now well-known when the boundary coupling matrix is full row rank. The goal of this article is to characterize the minimal control time for a class of systems with row rank deficient boundary coupling matrices.

</details>


### [160] [Sharpness and Rigidity of the Two-Factor Recht-Re Lower Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00155)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文证明了 Lai 与 Lim（ICML 2020）关于半正定矩阵乘积之和下界常数的猜想：构造出达到该界的 n 个秩一矩阵，给出简洁自洽的已知不等式证明，并完整刻画等号成立情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机优化方法在不同步使用不同数据点时，会出现半正定矩阵的乘积。这类乘积的平均虽由半正定矩阵构成，仍可能出现负特征值。Lai 与 Lim 给出了 $\sum_{i\ne j}A_iA_j\succeq-\frac{1}{4}n(n-1)I$ 的下界，并猜想常数对任意 $n\ge2$ 是紧的，但该紧性此前未被证明。本文旨在证明这一紧性猜想并刻画等号情形。

Method: 构造一族显式的 $n$ 个秩一矩阵（维数为 $n$）：取一组标准正交基，将其中一个方向的基向量压缩 $1/\sqrt{2}$ 倍，从而得到达到下界的矩阵族。同时给出该已知不等式的一个简短、自洽的证明，并对所有等号（极值）情形进行分类刻画。

Result: 所构造的秩一矩阵族对任意 $n\ge2$ 均达到 Lai–Lim 下界，证明常数是紧的。等号情形的分类表明：每个极值元都包含一个与所构造矩阵等价的公共不变 $n$ 维块，因此等号成立必须使维数恰为 $n$，而正定矩阵只会趋近该下端点但永远不能达到。此外，在强凸二次目标上，该极端负特征值可由两步期望梯度下降更新实现。

Conclusion: 本文解决了 Lai 与 Lim 提出的紧性猜想，给出了达到最优常数的显式构造，并完整刻画了等号成立的结构，说明紧性依赖于维数的精确匹配且正定情形无法取到端点，同时揭示该代数极值与梯度下降优化的联系。

Abstract: Products of positive semidefinite matrices arise when successive steps of a randomized optimization method use different data points. Their average can have a negative eigenvalue even though every individual matrix is positive semidefinite. Lai and Lim proved at ICML 2020 that $\sum_{i\ne j}A_iA_j\succeq-\frac{1}{4}n(n-1)I$ whenever $A_i\succeq0$ and $\sum_i A_i\preceq nI$, and conjectured that the constant is sharp for every $n\ge2$. We give an explicit family of $n$ rank-one matrices in dimension $n$ that attains this bound for every $n$, proving that sharpness statement. The construction compresses one direction of an orthonormal basis by a factor $1/\sqrt{2}$. We also give a short, self-contained proof of the known inequality and classify all equality cases: every extremizer contains a common invariant $n$-dimensional block equivalent to the construction. Consequently, dimension $n$ is necessary for equality, and positive definite matrices approach but never attain the lower endpoint. Finally, strongly convex quadratic objectives realize the extremal negative eigenvalue as a two-step expected gradient-descent update.

</details>


### [161] [A Two-Dimensional Extremizer for Relatively Inexact Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00156)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出了梯度下降单步在相对梯度误差约束下的显式极值函数：一个坐标取二次函数、另一坐标取Huber函数之和，并在中间步长区间内对所有允许误差水平达到最坏情况常数，从而证明了Vernimmen与Glineur于2025年6月提出的Conjecture 2.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究梯度下降单步在梯度误差相对于真实梯度有界时的最坏情况性能。Vernimmen与Glineur提出了关于最坏情况常数（最终平方梯度除以初始目标间隙、以及除以目标下降量）的Conjecture 2.6，需要给出匹配的极值函数予以证明。

Method: 构造显式极值函数：一个坐标为二次函数、另一坐标为Huber函数之和。通过变量替换将参数描述约化为一个具有严格指定根的标量三次方程；证明全部参数不等式，导出上界的非负平方证书，并直接验证匹配构造。此外刻画精确的一维最坏情况值。

Result: 该函数对每个允许的相对误差水平和中间区间内的每个步长，同时达到两类最坏情况常数，证明了Conjecture 2.6；证明了在开放的中间区间内二维是必要的（一维无法达到最坏情况）。

Conclusion: 所构造的二次-Huber函数是梯度下降单步在相对梯度误差下的精确极值函数，证实了猜想，并给出了参数不等式证明、非负平方证书和匹配下界构造，同时明确了问题的内在维度。

Abstract: We give an explicit extremal function for one step of gradient descent when the gradient error is bounded relative to the true gradient. The function is the sum of a quadratic in one coordinate and a Huber function in the other. It attains, for every admissible relative error level and every stepsize in the intermediate regime, the proposed worst-case constants for both the final squared gradient divided by the initial objective gap and the final squared gradient divided by the objective decrease. This proves Conjecture 2.6 of Vernimmen and Glineur, first stated in June 2025. A change of variable reduces the parameter description to a scalar cubic with a rigorously specified root. We prove all parameter inequalities, derive a nonnegative-square certificate for the upper bound, and verify the matching construction directly. We also characterize the exact one-dimensional worst-case value and prove that two dimensions are necessary in the open intermediate regime.

</details>


### [162] [Global Output Lifting and Surjectivity in Invertible Nonlinear MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00160)
*Zhaobo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文证明：在满足全局正则修正逆、固定行选择与满输入秩有限终止的条件下，任一固定齐次线性输出指派对应的输出导数映射是满射且具有光滑全局截面；所需状态界条件弱于弱一致零状态可检测性，并据此给出光滑动态控制器实现精确跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局逆问题关注所选输出导数的每个目标值能否由某个系统状态实现。已有全局逆方法用于镇定时常需单独假定满射性，作者希望说明在特定逆假设下该满射性可由更弱的可检测性条件推出。

Method: 在全局正则修正逆、固定行选择及满输入秩有限终止假设下，引入每有限区间上由初始状态与有限多个输出导数（对输入一致）控制的状态界条件；通过相容光滑参考的完全提升构造输出导数映射的光滑全局截面；对已知系统，用全状态反馈与当前参考导数构造光滑动态控制器。

Result: 证明每个固定齐次线性输出指派都给出满射的输出导数映射，且存在光滑全局截面；该状态界条件严格弱于弱一致零状态可检测性，从而使全局逆镇定中的独立满射性要求成为可检测性的推论；截面能在存在内部动态时仍为每个导数目标光滑选取实现状态；控制器可从任意初始状态出发实现精确跟踪。

Conclusion: 在所述逆假设下，输出导数映射的全局满射性与光滑截面可由弱于可检测性的条件保证；基于完全提升的方法同时提供了光滑状态选取与动态控制器设计，可从任意初始状态在任意给定正时间后精确跟踪任意光滑期望输出。

Abstract: Global inversion of nonlinear multivariable systems raises the question of whether every target value of the selected output derivatives can be realized by a plant state. We prove that, under globally regular modified inversion with fixed row selection and finite termination at full input rank, every fixed homogeneous linear output assignment yields a surjective output-derivative map with a smooth global section. The result requires a state bound on each finite interval in terms of the initial state and finitely many output derivatives, uniformly over inputs. This condition is strictly weaker than weak uniform zero-state detectability, making the separate surjectivity requirement in a global inversion approach to stabilization a consequence of detectability under the stated inversion hypotheses. The section selects a realizing state smoothly for every derivative target, including in the presence of internal dynamics, and is constructed through complete lifting of compatible smooth references. For known plants, the same lifting method yields a smooth dynamic controller using full state feedback and current reference derivatives, with initialization from the plant state. From any initial state, it achieves exact tracking of any smooth desired output after any prescribed positive time.

</details>


### [163] [Reachability and exact controllability of linear Stieltjes differential systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00161)
*Francisco J. Fernández*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究由非降左连续Stieltjes导数算子驱动的系统的可达性与精确可控性，允许测度含连续、奇异连续和原子部分，并将平坦区间视为不活动；构造演化族与跳后常数变易公式，将可达子空间刻画为输入方向的本质张成，并给出Kalman条件、原子测度下的截断Krylov空间刻画及能量可控性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性系统可控性理论多针对常微分方程或绝对连续测度框架，而具有奇异连续部分与原子部分、且存在不活动平坦区间的Stieltjes导数系统缺乏统一的可达性与精确可控性刻画，尤其是Kalman条件何时必要/充分以及原子测度数目与能量行为之间的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 作者构造相应的演化族，建立跳后常数变易公式，并借助本质张成（essential span）刻画可达子空间。对常系数情形分析Kalman条件的必要性与在回归性（regressivity）下的充分性条件；对纯原子测度，利用原子数N给出可达子空间为B,AB,...,A^{N-1}B生成的截断Krylov空间，并将最小原子数与可控性指标联系；进一步分析阈值原子数下Gramian退化与能量上界，以及阈值及以上时紧正质量范围的一致强制性。

Result: 可达子空间被刻画为输入方向的本质张成；常系数时Kalman条件对任意导数算子必要，在回归性且导数算子在某子区间连续非恒定下充分；纯原子测度含N个正原子时可达子空间等于由B至A^{N-1}B生成的截断Krylov空间，且Kalman可控对的最小原子数等于可控性指标；在阈值原子数处，一个消失质量而其余收敛到正有限极限会导致Gramian退化与最坏情形能量爆炸，而在阈值及以上，紧正质量范围给出一致强制性。

Conclusion: 该文将经典Kalman可控性理论推广到具有连续、奇异连续与原子部分的Stieltjes导数系统，统一给出可达子空间的代数刻画，并揭示原子测度数目、可控性指标与Gramian能量行为之间的定量关系；混合双积分器算例验证了所得结论。

Abstract: We study reachability and exact controllability for systems driven by a nondecreasing left-continuous Stieltjes derivator whose measure may have continuous, singular-continuous and atomic parts, while flat intervals represent inactivity. We construct an evolution family and post-jump variation-of-constants formula, and characterize the reachable subspace as the essential span of input directions. For constant coefficients, the Kalman condition is necessary for every derivator and, under regressivity, sufficient when the derivator is continuous and nonconstant on a subinterval. If the measure is purely atomic with exactly $N$ positive atoms, the reachable subspace is the truncated Krylov space generated by $B,AB,\ldots,A^{N-1}B$; for a Kalman-controllable pair, the minimum atom count among purely atomic derivators equals the controllability index. At the threshold count, one vanishing mass with the others converging to positive finite limits causes Gramian degeneration and worst-case energy blow-up; at or above threshold, compact positive mass ranges yield uniform coercivity. A mixed double-integrator example illustrates the results.

</details>


### [164] [Certificate-Coupled DCA for the Trust-Region Subproblem: Early Structural Escape and Progressive Global Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00172)
*Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种证书耦合的DC差分算法求解非凸信赖域子问题，在达到精确一阶平稳之前引入谱信息，利用持续随机Krylov子空间实现负曲率修正与PSD证书，并给出全局收敛与工作量界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸信赖域子问题的求解在达到一阶平稳点之前往往缺乏有效利用负曲率（谱）信息的手段，且难以在困难（近正交）情形下保证向全局最优的收敛与可验证的下降。

Method: 采用证书耦合的difference-of-convex算法（DCA），维护一个持续的随机Krylov子空间并随DCA迭代更新；近平稳时用充分负的平移Rayleigh商构造显式可行修正，否则通过完成预定深度的Lanczos过程高概率获得近似半正定（PSD）证书；同一Krylov子空间给出最大特征值的认证上界，用于在后续精度阶段选取DC曲率参数；在渐进精度调度下跨修正与精度阶段保留该子空间。

Result: 证明了信赖域上一致的负曲率修正结果，覆盖近正交困难情形；给出固定容差下的有限终止性与工作量界，以及量化的目标值差证书；在渐进精度调度下，认证目标值收敛到全局最优，迭代点距离收敛到全局解集；数值实验中负曲率修正可先于收敛后的谱修正发生，量化下降界在所有近正交实例中均满足，且相比重启谱过程，保留Krylov子空间使谱矩阵-向量乘积的中位数减少约2.6倍。

Conclusion: 持续复用Krylov子空间并耦合证书机制，可在达到精确平稳前有效利用负曲率信息，兼顾理论保证（全局收敛、终止性与工作量界）与计算效率，显著降低谱相关矩阵-向量乘积开销。

Abstract: We study the nonconvex trust-region subproblem by a certificate-coupled difference-of-convex algorithm that incorporates spectral information before exact first-order stationarity is attained. The method maintains a persistent randomized Krylov subspace together with the DCA iterates. Near stationarity, a sufficiently negative shifted Rayleigh quotient yields an explicit feasible correction with a quantitative decrease in the objective value; otherwise, completion of a prescribed Lanczos depth provides, with high probability, an approximate positive-semidefiniteness certificate. The same Krylov subspace also provides a certified upper bound on the largest eigenvalue for selecting the DC curvature parameter at subsequent accuracy stages. We prove a uniform negative-curvature correction result over the trust region, covering the near-orthogonal hard-case regime, together with finite-termination and work bounds at fixed tolerances and a quantitative objective-gap certificate. Under a progressive accuracy schedule, the certified objective values converge to the global optimum and the iterates converge in distance to the global solution set, while the Krylov subspace is retained across corrections and accuracy stages. Numerical experiments illustrate the phenomena established by the analysis: negative-curvature corrections can occur before the corresponding post-convergence spectral corrections, the quantitative decrease bound is satisfied in all tested near-orthogonal instances, and retaining the Krylov subspace reduces the median number of spectral matrix-vector products by approximately a factor of $2.6$ relative to restarting the spectral process in the controlled comparison.

</details>


### [165] [Data-to-Certificates (D2C): Koopman Supereigenfunctions for Stability, Safety, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00178)
*Umesh Vaidya*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出数据到证书（D2C）范式，绕过显式建模，直接从数据学习基于不等式泛化的Koopman超特征函数作为证书，用于稳定性、收缩、安全分析与控制综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力系统模型（含Koopman算子表示）本质基于等式，而许多分析与控制工具依赖不等式，二者不匹配。这种不匹配促使研究者寻求与分析、控制综合中认证任务本质对齐的表示方法。

Method: 提出数据到证书（D2C）范式，直接从数据学习证书而不显式建模；引入Koopman算子的超特征函数，作为特征函数的不等式推广，定义指数增长包络以编码稳定性、安全性与不确定性传播。给出两种构造：基于乘性遍历定理（MET）的几何方法，以及可直接由轨迹数据计算的预解式/Gramian公式。证书可用于稳定性与收缩分析，并通过凸二次规划实现镇定与安全关键控制综合。

Result: 建立起超特征函数的理论基础，证明其相关速率可恢复Lyapunov指数等内在动力学量；数值算例验证所提数据驱动认证方法在镇定、收缩与安全控制设计中的有效性。

Conclusion: 超特征函数为基于不等式的Koopman表示提供了统一框架，使证书可直接从数据学习并服务于稳定性、收缩与安全关键控制综合，避免显式模型构建。

Abstract: Traditional dynamical system models, including Koopman operator representations, are fundamentally equality-based, whereas many analysis and control tools rely on inequalities. This mismatch motivates representations that are intrinsically aligned with certification tasks involved in the analysis and control synthesis problems. In this paper, we propose a \emph{data-to-certificates (D2C)} paradigm that bypasses explicit model construction and directly learns certificates from data. We introduce \emph{supereigenfunctions} of the Koopman operator as an inequality-based generalization of eigenfunctions that define exponential growth envelopes encoding stability, safety, and uncertainty propagation, thereby serving as certificates for a range of control objectives. We establish their theoretical foundations and show that the associated rates recover intrinsic dynamical quantities such as Lyapunov exponents. Two complementary constructions are developed: a geometric approach based on the multiplicative ergodic theorem (MET), and a resolvent/Gramian formulation that enables computation directly from trajectory data. The resulting framework yields certificates that can be used for stability and contraction analysis, as well as stabilizing and safety-critical control synthesis via convex quadratic programming-based optimization program. Numerical examples demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed data-driven certification approach for stabilization, contraction, and safe control design.

</details>


### [166] [Gradient-Only Online Convex Optimization with a Single Quadratic Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00190)
*Kamiar Asgari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有界欧氏域上的在线凸优化，在仅收到一个次梯度、且未知时域、梯度界与强凸参数的设定下，提出一种尺度不变算法：将投影自适应梯度下降与一个锥约束二次“leader”通过正wealth过程耦合，保持O(d)状态且无需学习率网格或重启，并给出无对数因子的最优阶regret界及同时成立的二次距离界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线凸优化通常依赖已知的时域、梯度上界或强凸参数来设定学习率或重启策略。本文旨在去除这些先验知识，在完全未知这些量的情况下实现尺度不变的regret保证，并刻画对未知强凸性的精确依赖，包括跳跃式梯度尺度与逐轮曲率变化的影响。

Method: 提出一种单一leader构造的尺度不变算法：将投影自适应梯度下降与一个锥约束的二次leader项，通过正wealth过程耦合；算法仅维持O(d)状态，不使用学习率网格与重启。证明采用与裁剪（clipping）兼容的替代损失，并构造包含二次最小值、log wealth及两个标量曲率统计量的势函数。

Result: 对直径不超过D的域，regret至多为√2·D(∑_t‖ĝ_t‖²)^{1/2} + 2D·max_t‖ĝ_t‖，且不含对数因子。同时成立的二次距离界对未知强凸性给出显式改进，包括由观测梯度尺度跳跃控制的细化界；对逐轮变化的曲率，界依赖于相对事后选定参考水平的累计亏缺（shortfall）。强凸界并非在所有曲率-梯度比上均匀达到经典对数率，其精确参数依赖被明确给出。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是在既有的通用（universal）与无尺度（scale-free）在线学习文献中，给出一个单一leader构造及其分析，实现无需先验参数的尺度不变regret保证，并显式刻画对未知与变化强凸性的依赖；强凸情形下的界不普遍达到经典对数率，参数依赖被精确陈述。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization on a bounded Euclidean domain when the learner receives only one subgradient at its prediction and knows neither the horizon, a gradient bound, nor the losses' strong-convexity parameters. We present a scale-invariant algorithm that couples projected adaptive gradient descent to one cone-constrained quadratic leader through a positive wealth process. The algorithm maintains $O(d)$ state, without a learning-rate grid or restarts. For a domain of diameter at most $D$, it guarantees regret at most $\sqrt{2}D(\sum_t\|\widehat g_t\|^2)^{1/2}+2D\max_t\|\widehat g_t\|$, with no logarithmic factor. A simultaneous quadratic-distance bound yields explicit improvements for unknown strong convexity, including a refinement governed by jumps in the observed gradient scale. For varying per-round curvature, the bound depends on the total shortfall below a reference level selected in hindsight. The proof uses a clipping-compatible surrogate and a potential involving the quadratic minimum, log wealth, and two scalar curvature statistics. The resulting strongly convex bound is not uniform at the classical logarithmic rate over all curvature-to-gradient ratios; the exact parameter dependence is stated. The contribution is a single-leader construction and its analysis, within an established literature on universal and scale-free online learning.

</details>


### [167] [Sharp Fresh-Gradient Complexity of Nonconvex-Strongly-Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00229)
*Wendao Wu,Haihan Zhang,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文刻画了光滑非凸-强凹极小极大优化的 fresh-gradient oracle 复杂度，给出了匹配的上界与下界（至对数因子），复杂度为 $\widetilde\Theta(\sqrt\kappa L\Delta/\epsilon^2+\kappa L\Delta\sigma^2/\epsilon^4+\kappa^2\sigma^2/\epsilon^2+\sqrt\kappa\log_+(G/(\epsilon\sqrt\kappa)))$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作对随机 minimax 优化的下界理解不完整，尤其缺少对全局随机成本中条件数 $\kappa$ 依赖性的紧致刻画。本文旨在精确刻画 fresh-gradient oracle 模型下 smooth nonconvex-strongly-concave 极小极大问题的固定预算复杂度，厘清随机性带来的统计项与确定性优化项的相对贡献。

Method: 在无约束欧氏域上，设 $f$ 联合 $L$-光滑且对 $y$ 为 $\mu$-强凹，每次查询返回条件方差至多 $\sigma^2$ 的新鲜无偏联合梯度。作者设计了一种近端方法：将粗糙的原始变量进展与一次最终精化步骤分离，从而在固定预算下达到 $\|\nabla\Phi(\widehat x)\|\le\epsilon$；下界则针对任意自适应随机算法构造。

Result: 在 $\kappa$ 与 $L\Delta/\epsilon^2$ 超过普适常数、维度有限且被抑制对数独立于 $G$ 的条件下，得到成功概率至少 $2/3$ 的固定预算复杂度 $\widetilde\Theta(\sqrt\kappa L\Delta/\epsilon^2+\kappa L\Delta\sigma^2/\epsilon^4+\kappa^2\sigma^2/\epsilon^2+\sqrt\kappa\log_+(G/(\epsilon\sqrt\kappa)))$。该结果表明：全局随机成本中线性条件数依赖是必要的，且存在独立的统计精化成本。对偶初始化仅贡献加性对数成本；但若移除对其的控制，即使使用精确梯度，也无法得到任何有限的维度无关复杂度界。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 fresh-gradient oracle 下光滑非凸-强凹 minimax 优化的紧致复杂度刻画，明确了条件数线性依赖的必然性与统计精化项的独立性，并强调了对偶初始化的控制是获得维度无关复杂度界的必要条件。

Abstract: We characterize the fresh-gradient oracle complexity of smooth nonconvex-strongly-concave minimax optimization, with matching upper and lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. Let $Φ(x)=\max_y f(x,y)$, where $f$ is jointly $L$-smooth and $μ$-strongly concave in $y$ on unconstrained Euclidean domains, and set $κ=L/μ$. Each query returns a fresh unbiased joint gradient with conditional variance at most $σ^2$. Given initial primal gap at most $Δ$ and dual residual $\|\nabla_y f(x_0,y_0)\|\le G$, the fixed-budget complexity of finding $\|\nablaΦ(\widehat x)\|\leε$ with probability at least $2/3$ is $\widetildeΘ(\sqrtκLΔ/ε^2+κLΔσ^2/ε^4+κ^2σ^2/ε^2+\sqrtκ\log_+(G/(ε\sqrtκ)))$, where $\log_+u=\log\max\{1,u\}$. This characterization holds when $κ$ and $LΔ/ε^2$ exceed universal constants, uniformly over finite dimensions, and the suppressed logarithms are independent of $G$. It establishes the necessity of linear condition-number dependence in the global stochastic cost and identifies a separate statistical refinement cost. A proximal method separates coarse primal progress from one final refinement, while the lower bounds apply to arbitrary adaptive randomized algorithms. Dual initialization contributes only an additive logarithmic cost, yet removing its control eliminates every finite dimension-free complexity bound, even with exact gradients.

</details>


### [168] [Matching Lower Bounds for Randomized First-Order Methods in Hessian-Lipschitz Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00245)
*Haihan Zhang,Wendao Wu,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在随机化一阶算法下寻找非凸函数ε-稳定点的下界，该下界与确定性重启加速梯度的上界相匹配，表明随机化不能改善高维最坏情况查询率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的确定性下界（Zhou 2026）尚不清楚是否适用于无限制的随机化算法，即随机化是否能在高维最坏情况下改善查询复杂度是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 通过构造一个特定的困难函数实例：保持二次曲率可见，同时顺序揭示forcing方向；利用大特征空间限制预处理，并通过scheduled-disclosure耦合使每个新方向产生单独代价。在此基础上建立随机化一阶算法的minimax查询数下界。

Result: 在ε≲L₁²/L₂且ΔL₂^{1/2}ε^{-3/2}≳1的范围内，证明了Q_rand,FO^∞(ε;Δ,L₁,L₂) ≥ c·ΔL₁^{1/2}L₂^{1/4}ε^{-7/4}，其中c>0为绝对常数。

Conclusion: 该下界与Li和Lin（2023）的确定性重启加速梯度上界相匹配，并将Zhou（2026）的尖锐确定性下界扩展到无限制随机化算法。因此，随机化不能改善高维最坏情况下的查询率。

Abstract: We establish a randomized first-order lower bound for finding an $ε$-stationary point, $\|\nabla f(x)\|\le ε$, of a nonconvex function with initial gap at most $Δ$, $L_1$-Lipschitz gradient, and $L_2$-Lipschitz Hessian. Each oracle call returns the exact function value and gradient. Let $Q_{\mathrm{rand},\mathrm{FO}}^{\infty}(ε;Δ,L_1,L_2)$ denote the minimax number of calls, maximized over finite dimensions, for arbitrary adaptive randomized algorithms with per-instance success probability at least $2/3$. In the regime $ε\lesssim L_1^2/L_2$ and $ΔL_2^{1/2}ε^{-3/2}\gtrsim 1$, we prove $
Q_{\mathrm{rand},\mathrm{FO}}^{\infty}(ε;Δ,L_1,L_2) \ge c\,ΔL_1^{1/2}L_2^{1/4}ε^{-7/4}, $
where $c>0$ is an absolute constant. This matches the deterministic restarted accelerated-gradient upper bound of Li and Lin (2023) and extends the sharp deterministic lower bound of Zhou (2026) to unrestricted randomized algorithms. Thus, randomization does not improve the high-dimensional worst-case query rate. The construction keeps quadratic curvature visible while revealing the forcing directions sequentially. Large eigenspaces limit preprocessing, and a scheduled-disclosure coupling makes each fresh direction incur a separate cost.

</details>


### [169] [Sharp High-Probability Statistical Rates for Heavy-Tailed Nonsmooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00260)
*Haihan Zhang,Wendao Wu,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了重尾噪声下非光滑随机凸优化的尖锐高概率统计速率，弥合了一般凸与强凸情形中置信度依赖的差距。通过将方向波动与总二阶矩分离的在线不等式，将以往置信系数 $s^{1/p}L^{1-1/p}$ 改进为 $s$（强凸下改进为 $s^2$），达到已知统计下界阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾噪声下非光滑随机凸优化的高概率界中，置信度依赖性存在差距：已有上界带有较差的置信系数，未能匹配已知统计下界。本文旨在消除该差距，在一般凸和强凸两种设定下给出与下界匹配的尖锐速率。

Method: 研究复合目标：Lipschitz 凸损失加已知近端正则项，通过新采样的无偏随机次梯度访问。假设中心化噪声的条件范数 $p$ 阶矩与方向 $p$ 阶矩分别被 $L^p$、$s^p$ 控制（$1<p\le2$，$0<s\le L$）。核心是一个在线不等式，将方向波动与拒绝采样产生的总二阶矩分离；在强凸下用局部化保持该分离，且不引入额外的时域对数因子。

Result: 设 $T$ 次预言机调用、失败概率 $0<\delta<1/10$，$q=1-1/p$，$\ell=\log(3/\delta)$，$A=s(L^2/s^2)^q$，初始距离界 $D$。非饱和统计区内极小极大目标误差为 $\Theta(D(A+s\ell^q)/T^q)$；对 $\mu$-强凸正则项，二次区速率为 $\Theta((A^2+s^2\ell^{2q})/(\mu T^{2q}))$。上界在所有有限维成立，下界需 $d\ge\lceil L^2/s^2\rceil$。新上界达到已知统计下界阶，将置信系数 $s^{1/p}L^{1-1/p}$ 替换为 $s$，强凸下其平方替换为 $s^2$。

Conclusion: 本文给出了重尾噪声下非光滑随机凸优化的尖锐高概率速率，在一般凸和强凸情形均达到统计下界阶，刻画了内部计算不受限时的预言机复杂度，并消除了先前结果中多余的置信度依赖。

Abstract: We establish sharp high-probability statistical rates for nonsmooth stochastic convex optimization under heavy-tailed noise, closing the confidence-dependence gap in both general convex and strongly convex settings. We consider composite objectives with a Lipschitz convex loss and a known proximal regularizer, accessed through fresh unbiased stochastic subgradients. The centered noise has conditional norm and directional $p$th moments bounded by $L^p$ and $s^p$, respectively, where $1<p\le2$ and $0<s\le L$. For $T$ oracle calls and failure probability $0<δ<1/10$, set $q=1-1/p$, $\ell=\log(3/δ)$, and $A=s(L^2/s^2)^q$. With initial distance bound $D$, the minimax objective error is $Θ(D(A+s\ell^q)/T^q)$ in the nonsaturated statistical regime. For a $μ$-strongly convex regularizer, the corresponding quadratic-regime rate is $Θ((A^2+s^2\ell^{2q})/(μT^{2q}))$. These rates require domination of the deterministic and finite-variance terms; matching lower bounds require $d\ge\lceil L^2/s^2\rceil$, while the upper bounds hold in every finite dimension. The new upper bounds attain known statistical lower-bound orders, replacing the previous confidence coefficient $s^{1/p}L^{1-1/p}$ by $s$, and its square by $s^2$ under strong convexity. The key is an online inequality that separates directional fluctuations from the total second moment created by rejection sampling. Localization preserves this separation under strong convexity without additional horizon logarithms. The results characterize oracle complexity with unrestricted internal computation.

</details>


### [170] [Joint Lower Bounds for Zeroth-Order Nonconvex Optimization on Euclidean Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00275)
*Haihan Zhang,Wendao Wu,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在欧氏查询球上、且球内保证含有稳定点时，针对Goldstein稳定性的联合随机零阶下界：所需标量评估次数为 Ω(dL₀²Δ/(δε³))，揭示维度与精度的联合障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化下界多针对全局或一般非光滑情形，对于有界域上"可解"实例（查询球内已保证存在稳定点）的复杂度刻画不足，缺少对维度与精度联合依赖的紧致下界。

Method: 构造硬实例族 f=E[F(·;ξ)]，满足 E[Lip(F(·;ξ))²]≤L₀²，并控制球上初始目标间隙 Δ；通过构造半径 Θ(Δ/ε) 的球及一系列局部化区域迫使算法反复进行方向估计；利用自适应高斯后验论证（adaptive Gaussian posterior）控制样本重用，针对可重复查询样本的随机自适应算法进行分析。

Result: 在 ε≤cL₀、Δ≥Cδε、d≥C[1+log(2+ΔL₀²/(δε³))] 条件下，任何随机自适应算法达到成功概率 1/2 需要 Ω(dL₀²Δ/(δε³)) 次标量评估，即维度与精度的联合障碍。

Conclusion: 该下界仅局部于查询球；在共同半径下的匹配极小极大刻画，以及相应的无限制全局间隙下界，仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We prove a joint stochastic zeroth-order lower bound for Goldstein stationarity on a Euclidean query ball, even when the ball is guaranteed to contain a stationary point. In dimension $d$, let $f=\mathbb{E}[F(\cdot;ξ)]$, assume $\mathbb{E}[\operatorname{Lip}(F(\cdot;ξ))^2]\le L_0^2$, and bound the initial objective gap over the ball by $Δ$. For neighborhood radius $δ>0$ and residual tolerance $\varepsilon>0$, our smooth hard family requires $Ω(dL_0^2Δ/(δ\varepsilon^3))$ scalar evaluations for success probability $1/2$, against randomized adaptive algorithms that may retain and repeatedly query each sampled function. The result holds for $\varepsilon\le cL_0$, $Δ\ge Cδ\varepsilon$, and $d\ge C[1+\log(2+ΔL_0^2/(δ\varepsilon^3))]$, on a constructed ball of radius $Θ(Δ/\varepsilon)$. A sequence of localized regions forces repeated direction estimation, and an adaptive Gaussian posterior argument controls sample reuse. The result establishes a joint dimension and accuracy obstruction on solvable bounded-domain instances. The gap is local to the query ball; a matching minimax characterization at a common radius and the corresponding unrestricted global-gap lower bound remain open here.

</details>


### [171] [Sharp Minimax Rates for Highly Smooth Strongly Convex Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00276)
*Haihan Zhang,Wendao Wu,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文确定了噪声零阶优化在全局强凸、高阶光滑（多线性算子范数）函数下的极小极大维度依赖：误差为 Θ_β(min{1,(d²/T)^((β-1)/β)})，达到目标精度 ε 所需噪声查询数为 Θ_β(d²ε^(-β/(β-1)))，无对数损失，且下界匹配上界并适用于任意随机自适应算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化中维度依赖是核心问题，尤其在带噪声、强凸且具高阶光滑性的设定下，极小极大最优的维度与预算依赖此前尚不明确，本文旨在精确刻画该依赖并消除潜在的对数损失。

Method: 每次查询返回带独立高斯噪声的一个函数值。下界方面，采用"归一化超立方体 + 单径向截断"的局部化构造，说明单次查询只能强调一个隐藏坐标，而关于所有坐标的聚合信息仍然很小，从而对任意随机自适应算法（查询点不受限）成立。上界方面，提出自包含的多尺度球面估计器，通过抵消低阶偏差项达到匹配界。

Result: 对维度 d、查询预算 T 及任意固定有限光滑阶 β>2，极小极大期望目标误差为 Θ_β(min{1,(d²/T)^((β-1)/β)})，常数仅依赖 β；对足够小目标误差 ε，所需噪声查询数为 Θ_β(d²ε^(-β/(β-1)))，且无对数损失。下界与 Akhavan 等 (2024) 上界的维度与预算依赖相匹配。

Conclusion: 本文给出了噪声零阶优化在强凸高阶光滑设定下精确的极小极大维度依赖，建立了匹配的下界与上界，并表明该界对任意随机自适应算法成立、且不含对数因子。

Abstract: We determine the minimax dimension dependence of noisy zeroth-order optimization for globally strongly convex functions with higher-order smoothness in multilinear operator norm. Each query returns one function value with fresh independent Gaussian noise. For dimension $d$, query budget $T$, and any fixed finite smoothness order $β>2$, the minimax expected objective error satisfies $\mathcal{E}_β(T,d)=Θ_β\left(\min\left\{1,(d^2/T)^{(β-1)/β}\right\}\right)$, with dimension-independent strong-convexity, gradient-smoothness, higher-order-smoothness, noise, and minimizer-radius bounds. Thus, for sufficiently small target error $\varepsilon$, the necessary and sufficient number of noisy values is $Θ_β(d^2\varepsilon^{-β/(β-1)})$, with no logarithmic loss. The new lower bound matches the dimension and budget dependence of the upper bound of Akhavan et al. (2024), and holds for arbitrary randomized adaptive algorithms with unrestricted query points. Its key step localizes a normalized hypercube with one radial cutoff: a query can emphasize one hidden coordinate, but the aggregate information about all coordinates remains small. A self-contained multiscale spherical estimator attains the matching upper bound by cancelling lower-order bias terms. Constants in the rates depend only on the fixed smoothness order under the stated normalization.

</details>


### [172] [Adaptivity, Anchoring, and the Exact Oracle Complexity of Stochastic Fixed-Point Iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00295)
*Yekini Shehu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带单点无偏有界方差oracle的随机不动点迭代 x_{n+1}=θ_n x_0+(1-θ_n)T̂(x_n)，揭示钉住(anchor)权重的密度调度在收缩算子与旋转算子间的权衡，给出lacunary集中下的二分性突破，并建立随机收缩/非扩张算子的minimax速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机不动点迭代广泛用于强化学习、优化等场景，但权重调度θ_n对收敛速度的影响缺乏统一理论。作者希望刻画确定性锚定调度的固有局限（收缩最优与旋转多项式速率不可兼得），并确定随机设定下的最优收敛速率。

Method: 对Hilbert空间中非扩张/收缩算子研究锚定迭代，分析密度c_n=θ_n(n+2)不振荡时的确定性行为；通过lacunary锚定集中构造γ-oblivious调度实现双目标兼顾；随机收缩用几何分批与certified modulus分析minimax下界；非扩张映射归约到单调inclusion，在2-一致光滑Banach空间给出复杂度界。

Result: 确定性：振荡密度在收缩上多项式次优或在旋转上超多项式慢；常数密度θ_n=c/(n+2)有精确权衡‖x_n‖≍Γ(c+1)(nφ)^{-c}，经典调度‖x_n‖=2|sin(nφ/2)|/(nφ)+O(1/n)。lacunary集中下存在γ-oblivious调度同时收缩最优且旋转多项式（指数1/α_0, α_0=log_2(3/2)）。随机收缩：仿射映射minimax为Θ(σ²ε⁻²(1-γ)⁻²)，锚定类内为Θ(σ²ε⁻²(1-γ)⁻²+(1-γ)⁻¹ln(D/ε))；无certified modulus则无有效距离证书。非扩张：Banach空间O(Kσ²D²ε⁻⁴)，Hilbert空间Õ(σ²ε⁻²+Dε⁻¹)。数值实验一致。

Conclusion: 确定性锚定调度的收缩-旋转权衡普遍存在，唯有lacunary锚定集中可突破二分性；随机设定下给出了收缩与非扩张算子的(近)最优速率与certified modulus的必要性。数值验证支撑所有scaling预测。

Abstract: We study stochastic fixed-point iterations $x_{n+1}=θ_nx_0+(1-θ_n)\widehat T(x_n)$ for nonexpansive and contractive operators on Hilbert spaces, with a single-point unbiased oracle of bounded variance. Deterministically, every anchor schedule $θ_n=c_n/(n+2)$ whose density $c_n\in(0,1]$ does not oscillate between scales is either polynomially suboptimal on contractions or super-polynomially slow on rotations; for constant densities $θ_n=c/(n+2)$ the tradeoff is exact, with $\norm{x_n}\asympΓ(c+1)(n\varphi)^{-c}$ on rotations by angle $\varphi$ ($\varphi\to0$, $n\varphi\to\infty$) and, for the classical schedule, $\norm{x_n}=\frac{2|\sin(n\varphi/2)|}{n\varphi}+O(\frac1n)$. The dichotomy fails exactly under lacunary anchor concentration: an explicit $γ$-oblivious schedule, one per target accuracy, is simultaneously contraction-optimal and rotation-polynomial with exponent $1/α_0$, $α_0=\log_2(3/2)$, and, for each fixed target accuracy, the matching lower bound holds for Lebesgue-a.e.\ angle. For stochastic contractions, the minimax rate is $Θ(σ^2\eps^{-2}(1-γ)^{-2})$ on affine maps, and within the anchored class $Θ(σ^2\eps^{-2}(1-γ)^{-2}+(1-γ)^{-1}\ln(D/\eps))$ for known modulus via geometric batching; without a certified modulus bound no sound distance certificate exists, and a certified ceiling is the exact boundary. For nonexpansive maps we prove $O(Kσ^2D^2\eps^{-4})$ in every $2$-uniformly smooth Banach space and the Hilbert rate $\tildeΘ(σ^2\eps^{-2}+D\eps^{-1})$ by reduction to monotone inclusions. Numerical experiments are consistent with every scaling prediction.

</details>


### [173] [Finite-time boundary collision in planar linear quadratic regulator gradient flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00297)
*Kang Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究单输入可控平面线性二次调节器(LQR)上,固定初始状态的策略梯度(欧氏梯度流)能否在有限时间内到达稳定边界,并给出碰撞发生的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略梯度方法优化反馈增益时,若代价仅在某个固定初始状态处评估,则代价在稳定边界附近未必处处发散,因此梯度流有可能在有限时间内"碰撞"稳定边界而非收敛到Riccati增益。作者希望刻画这种有限时间碰撞何时发生。

Method: 针对单输入、正定二次权重的可控平面系统,推导代价的精确表示,由此得到碰撞存在的充要条件;该条件化为一个标量求根问题,并覆盖双方向(重)根这一临界情形。对三角族系统进一步给出显式的代数参数区域。

Result: 得到碰撞的充要条件(标量求根),临界情形下双方向根吸引一个开集;三角族的判据给出显式代数参数区域,且每条轨迹要么收敛到Riccati增益、要么与稳定边界碰撞;给出紧代价次水平集的 sharp 阈值作为收敛证书;碰撞轨迹上稳定裕度与累积状态Gramian最小特征值线性消失(碰撞前Gramian仍正定)。

Conclusion: 固定初始状态的LQR策略梯度流确实可能在有限时间内到达稳定边界,该现象可由标量根的临界条件精确判定;数值实验进一步考察了初始化依赖、临界方向附近的慢通过以及第二状态方向激励的影响。

Abstract: Policy gradient methods for the linear quadratic regulator optimize feedback gains using a cost over an infinite horizon. When this cost is evaluated at one fixed initial state, it need not diverge near every part of the stability boundary. We study whether the Euclidean gradient flow can reach this boundary in finite optimization time. For controllable planar systems with one input and positive definite quadratic weights, an exact representation of the cost yields a necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of such a collision. The condition reduces to a scalar root calculation and includes the critical case, where a double direction root attracts an open set of stabilizing gains. For a triangular family, the criterion gives an explicit algebraic parameter region, and every trajectory either converges to the Riccati gain or collides with the stability boundary. A sharp threshold for compact cost sublevels provides a convergence certificate. Along every colliding trajectory, the stability margin and the smallest eigenvalue of the accumulated state Gramian vanish linearly, although the Gramian remains positive definite before collision. Numerical experiments examine the dependence on initialization, slow passage near a critical direction, and the effect of adding excitation in a second state direction.

</details>


### [174] [Lower Bounds For Gradient-Free Convex Optimization And Convex-Concave Saddle-Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00345)
*Yuriy Dorn,Darina Dvinskikh,Timofei Loginov,Osman Osmanov,Aleksandr Shestakov,Nazarii Tupitsa,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究精确标量值信息下的查询复杂度，证明全局L-光滑凸函数优化的自适应随机算法下界Ω(d·min{d,√(LR²/ε)})，无对数因子，并推广至鞍点问题、双线性博弈及混合/部分梯度访问等情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画仅用精确标量值（函数值）查询时凸优化及鞍点问题的查询复杂度下界，填补对无限制查询、无对数因子且匹配上界的信息下界空白。

Method: 对任意仿射切片比较矩阵均值与其逆的均值，并界定自适应测量后的后验矩；构造强凸–强凹实例与已知各向同性对角Hessian；通过归约建立标量查询下界，并用欧氏像半径推广到独立范数与已知惩罚项。

Result: 凸光滑情形下界Ω(d·min{d,√(LR²/ε)})，对二次函数成立且匹配两个基本上界的最小值；强凸增加√(L/μ)上限。鞍点问题独立块维度；紧双线性博弈下界Ω(r·min{r,√(CR_xR_y/ε)})，r=min{d_x,d_y}；强凸–强凹构造给出条件依赖下界。混合值与部分梯度访问、外层目标精度及非光滑扩展均有对应标量查询下界。

Conclusion: 标量值查询的信息论下界可由矩阵均值/逆均值比较与后验矩控制得到，覆盖凸优化、鞍点与双线性博弈，并与上界匹配，还可推广到混合梯度访问、独立范数及已知惩罚等设定。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of optimization with exact scalar-value information. For globally $L$-smooth convex functions on $\mathbb R^d$ with a minimizer in a Euclidean ball of radius $R$, we prove the lower bound $Ω(d\min\{d,\sqrt{LR^2/\varepsilon}\})$ for adaptive randomized algorithms in the stated accuracy range. Queries are unrestricted, and the bound contains no logarithmic factor. It holds for quadratics and matches the minimum of two elementary upper bounds on that class. Prescribed strong convexity introduces the additional cap $\sqrt{L/μ}$. The proof compares the mean of a matrix with the mean of its inverse on arbitrary affine slices and bounds posterior moments after adaptive measurements. We extend these information bounds to saddle-point problems with independent block dimensions. For compact bilinear games, the coupling alone yields $Ω(r\min\{r,\sqrt{CR_xR_y/\varepsilon}\})$ queries, where $r=\min\lbrace d_x,d_y\rbrace$. A strongly convex--strongly concave construction yields a condition-dependent lower bound even with known isotropic diagonal Hessians. Separate reductions establish scalar query lower bounds under mixed value and partial-gradient access, including for outer objective accuracy. A Euclidean-image radius yields smooth and nonsmooth extensions to independently specified norms and known penalties.

</details>


### [175] [The Five-Point Case of Mordell's Discriminant Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00358)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了判别式问题中五点情形的尖锐不等式：在约束 sum|z_j|^2=5 下，五点判别式不超过 5^5，等号当且仅当点构成单位圆上的正五边形（相差旋转与置换），并由此得到高斯加权 Fekete 集与五点复 Ginibre 密度的全局模的相同构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mordell 于 1960 年提出的判别式问题长期未决，五点情形是其中遗留的最后一个未解决情形。该问题旨在确定给定二阶矩约束下点构型的判别式上确界及其极值构型，同时该极值问题也与点涡的相对平衡、高斯加权 Fekete 集及复 Ginibre 系综密度的全局模等问题相联系。

Method: 首先将最大化问题识别为五个相同平面点涡的归一化相对平衡问题；随后对相应驻点方程给出独立的精确分类。主要代数步骤通过构造结式（resultant construction）并在 Q 上进行精确 Gröbner 基消元，证明所有无碰撞物理解都具有反射对称性。对反射对称方程得到恰五个物理形状，再用精确判别式比较从中选出正五边形。

Result: 证明了尖锐不等式 prod|z_i-z_j|^2 ≤ 5^5，等号当且仅当五点构成单位圆上的正五边形（旋转与置换意义下唯一）；并对驻点方程给出精确分类，表明无碰撞物理解均反射对称且恰有五个物理形状，判别式比较选出正五边形。所有符号消元、Sturm 计数与区间不等式均由伴随脚本以有理算术复现验证。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了 Mordell 判别式问题的五点情形，确定了极值构型为正五边形；作为推论，同一构型给出高斯加权 Fekete 集，并在自然重标度后成为五点复 Ginibre 密度的全局模。

Abstract: Let $z_1,\ldots,z_5\in\mathbb C$ satisfy $\sum_{j=1}^5|z_j|^2=5$. We prove the sharp inequality \[
  \prod_{1\le i<j\le5}|z_i-z_j|^2\le 5^5, \] with equality precisely for a regular pentagon on the unit circle, up to rotation and permutation. This settles the remaining five-point case of the discriminant problem considered by Mordell in 1960. The proof first identifies a maximizer with a normalized relative equilibrium of five identical planar point vortices. We then give an independent exact classification of the corresponding stationary equations. The main algebraic step proves that every collision-free physical solution of the normalized stationary equations is reflection symmetric; it is obtained from a resultant construction and exact Gröbner-basis elimination over $\mathbb Q$. The reflection-symmetric equations have exactly five physical shapes, and an exact discriminant comparison selects the regular pentagon. All symbolic eliminations, Sturm counts, and interval inequalities are reproduced by an accompanying verification script using rational arithmetic. As consequences, the same configuration gives the Gaussian weighted Fekete set and the global mode of the five-particle complex Ginibre density after the natural rescaling.

</details>


### [176] [Computational complexity of static output feedback stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00390)
*Zhaobo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间线性系统静态输出反馈(SOF)镇定的计算复杂性：对有理对象数据，该问题对实数存在理论(∃ℝ)完全，即使限定为两输入的可控可观整数对象；引入"有效增益维数"刻画复杂度，该维数固定时可行性判定与有理镇定增益构造可多项式时间完成；并证明一类极小单输入整数对象的SOF镇定仍为NP难，其可行对象存在{-1,1}增益且具均匀鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态输出反馈镇定是控制理论中的经典问题，但其精确的计算复杂性此前缺乏完整刻画。作者希望确定该问题的复杂性类属，找出导致难解性的结构根源，并识别使问题变为易解的参数，从而为算法设计与问题归约提供理论基础。

Method: 对有理对象数据，通过多项式时间双向归约，将SOF镇定与整数系数多项式方程组及不等式的实数可满足性(∃ℝ)相互等价，并构造仅含两个输入的可控可观整数对象实例以强化结论。引入"有效增益维数"——即对任意增益都能决定其特征多项式的最小增益项线性组合数目——作为复杂度参数，在该维数固定时给出多项式时间的可行性判定与有理镇定增益构造算法。此外构造一类极小单输入整数对象族，用归约证明其SOF镇定的NP难性，并分析其{-1,1}增益及对全体元素同时扰动的稳定性。

Result: SOF镇定对有理对象数据是∃ℝ完全的，且该结论在两输入的可控可观整数对象上仍成立；当有效增益维数固定时，可行性测试与有理镇定增益构造均可在多项式时间内完成；SOF镇定对一类极小单输入整数对象仍为NP难；该族中每个可行对象都存在取值于{-1,1}的镇定增益，且当每个元素扰动幅度不超过与对象维数无关的统一正常数时，稳定性得以保持。

Conclusion: SOF镇定的复杂性由参数有效增益维数主导：一般情形下与∃ℝ等价，属高复杂度；固定该维数后可多项式时间求解。NP难性即使在极小单输入整数对象上也出现，说明难解性并非源于对象规模或输入数目。{-1,1}增益的存在性与对均匀有界扰动的鲁棒性，为设计简单、鲁棒的反馈律提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of static output feedback (SOF) stabilization for continuous-time linear systems. For rational plant data, we prove that this problem is complete for the existential theory of the reals: it is equivalent, under polynomial-time transformations, to deciding whether a system of polynomial equations and inequalities with integer coefficients has a real solution. This result holds even for controllable and observable integer plants with two inputs. We characterize the effective gain dimension as the minimum number of linear combinations of gain entries that determine the characteristic polynomial for every gain. When this dimension is fixed, both feasibility testing and the construction of a rational stabilizing gain, whenever one exists, can be performed in polynomial time. SOF stabilization also remains NP-hard for a family of minimal single-input integer plants. Every feasible plant in this family admits a stabilizing gain with entries in $\{-1,1\}$. For these gains, stability is preserved under simultaneous perturbations of all entries, provided that each perturbation has magnitude at most a uniform positive constant independent of the plant dimensions.

</details>


### [177] [Fixed-Time ISS for Interconnected Discrete-Time Systems, Part I: Extinction-Based Lyapunov Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00657)
*Sumadhu Rubaiyat,Jorge I. Poveda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间系统在外部输入下的固定时间输入到状态稳定性（FxT-ISS），提出基于具有固定时间消失性质耗散函数的Lyapunov充分条件，并建立互联系统的小增益定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定时间稳定性在连续时间系统中已有较多研究，但离散时间系统在外部输入扰动下的固定时间输入到状态稳定性（FxT-ISS）理论尚不完善，尤其是缺乏能涵盖更一般衰减机制的Lyapunov判据及互联系统的稳定性分析工具。

Method: 1）引入一类具有固定时间消失性质的耗散函数，给出FxT-ISS的Lyapunov型充分条件，该条件涵盖常用的双幂次构造并允许更一般的衰减机制；2）基于该框架，建立存在扰动时互联FxT-ISS系统的小增益定理。

Result: 所提条件能够刻画比传统双幂次更广泛的固定时间衰减行为；小增益定理为互联离散时间FxT-ISS系统的稳定性提供了可验证的判据；通过若干示例（包括受扰优化算法和离散时间反馈优化架构）验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将固定时间稳定性理论拓展至受扰离散时间系统，为FxT-ISS提供了更一般的Lyapunov分析工具与互联系统的小增益稳定性判据，并在优化算法等场景中展示了应用价值。

Abstract: We study fixed-time input-to-state stability (FxT-ISS) for discrete-time systems subject to exogenous inputs. We introduce Lyapunov-based sufficient conditions for FxT- ISS based on a class of dissipation functions with a fixed-time extinction property, which encompasses commonly used two- power constructions while allowing substantially more general decay mechanisms. Building on this framework, we establish a small-gain theorem for interconnected FxT-ISS systems in the presence of disturbances. The results are illustrated through several examples, including perturbed optimization algorithms and a discrete-time feedback-optimization architecture.

</details>


### [178] [Fixed-Time ISS for Interconnected Discrete-Time Systems, Part II: Finite-Horizon Lyapunov Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00660)
*Sumadhu Rubaiyat,Jorge I. Poveda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用有限时域Lyapunov函数研究离散时间系统在外源输入下的固定时间输入-状态稳定性(FxT-ISS)，提出了消除初始条件影响的Lyapunov刻画，并建立了互联系统的有限时域小增益定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于灭绝(extinction)的Lyapunov条件对固定时间输入-状态稳定性的刻画较为局限，难以涵盖更一般的有限记忆机制，且缺乏针对互联离散时间系统在反馈连接下保持FxT-ISS的系统性判据。

Method: 采用有限时域Lyapunov函数方法，构造一类新的Lyapunov刻画，使初始条件的影响在经过一致有界的迭代次数后被消除；该刻画兼容已有的灭绝型Lyapunov条件，并可推广至更一般的有限记忆机制。在此基础上，针对互联离散时间系统推导有限时域小增益定理，给出反馈互联下FxT-ISS得以保持的条件。

Result: 所提出的Lyapunov刻画涵盖了现有灭绝型条件并支持更一般的有限记忆机制；有限时域小增益定理给出了反馈互联保持FxT-ISS的充分条件；并通过分布式算法、优化及反馈优化中的示例验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 有限时域Lyapunov函数为离散时间系统的固定时间输入-状态稳定性提供了统一且更具一般性的分析框架，其有限时域小增益定理可用于分布式算法与优化等实际场景的稳定性分析。

Abstract: We study fixed-time input-to-state stability (FxT-ISS) for discrete-time systems subject to exogenous inputs using finite-horizon Lyapunov functions. In the proposed Lyapunov- based characterization, the effect of the initial condition is eliminated after a uniformly bounded number of iterations. This characterization encompasses existing extinction-based Lyapunov conditions while allowing more general finite-memory mechanisms. Building on this framework, we establish a finite-horizon small-gain theorem for interconnected discrete-time systems, providing conditions under which FxT-ISS is preserved under feedback interconnections. The proposed framework is illustrated through examples arising in distributed algorithms, optimization, and feedback optimization.

</details>


### [179] [Exact volume computation for Boolean quadric polytopes of series-parallel graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00734)
*Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为系列平行图上的布尔二次多胞体 P(G) 给出了计算其体积的高效算法（一般情形 O(d^7)，仙人掌图 O(d^5)），并分析了 P(G) 相对其线性松弛 Q(G) 的体积占比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lee 与 Skipper 已证明有界树宽下 Q(G) 体积可多项式时间计算，并给出 G 为环时 vol(P(G)) 的闭式公式，但 vol(P(G)) 在系列平行图上的高效算法仍是未解问题。此外，P(G) 占据 Q(G) 的比例（即奇环不等式的作用强度）尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 基于系列平行分解的动态规划：在分解树上递归计算体积；固定 x_v=1/2 时递推退化为单变量多项式的卷积；用奇环不等式刻画 P(G) 与 Q(G) 的差距。

Result: 以 d=|V|+|E| 计，vol(P(G)) 可在 O(d^7) 次算术运算内算出，仙人掌图 O(d^5)；同一框架计算 vol(Q(G)) 需 O(d^4)，仙人掌图 O(d^3)，并可得 G 的关联偏序集线性扩张数；固定 x_v=1/2 时可在 O(m^3) 内算任意系列平行图的割多胞体体积。每条形如长度 ℓ 的奇环不等式最多切掉 Q(G) 的 2^{ℓ-1}/ℓ! 比例，故 vol(P)/vol(Q) ≥ 1-Σ_C 2^{|C|-1}/|C|!；该比值在共点环的"花"结构上不按环分解，而按 ρ_ℓ^k 衰减。

Conclusion: 解决了系列平行图上 vol(P(G)) 的高效计算问题，统一框架同时覆盖 Q(G)、线性扩张数与割多胞体体积。体积占比分析表明：短奇环影响更大，但大量长环的微小切割会累积；最坏情况下三角形不等式或任何固定长度的奇环不等式都无法弥合 Q(G) 与 P(G) 的差距，体积比可随 d 指数级减小。

Abstract: For a graph G, the Boolean quadric polytope P(G) is the convex hull of the binary solutions of $y_{ij}=x_ix_j$ for $ij\in E(G)$, and Q(G) is its standard linear relaxation. For series-parallel graphs, Q(G) together with the odd-cycle inequalities describes P(G). Lee and Skipper showed vol(Q(G)) is polynomial-time computable for bounded treewidth and gave a closed formula for vol(P(G)) when G is a cycle. We resolve their question of giving an efficient algorithm for vol(P(G)) on series-parallel graphs. With $d=|V(G)|+|E(G)|$, we compute it with $O(d^7)$ arithmetic operations, and $O(d^5)$ if G is a cactus.
  The algorithm is a dynamic program over the series-parallel decomposition. The same framework computes vol(Q(G)) (hence the number of linear extensions of the incidence poset of G) with $O(d^4)$ operations, and $O(d^3)$ for cacti. With every $x_v$ fixed at 1/2, the recursion reduces to convolutions of univariate polynomials and computes the cut polytope volume of every series-parallel graph with m edges in $O(m^3)$ operations.
  We also study how much of Q(G) the polytope P(G) occupies. Short odd cycles matter more than long ones: in every graph, the odd-cycle inequalities of a cycle of length $\ell$ cut off at most a fraction $2^{\ell-1}/\ell!$ of Q(G). Hence vol(P(G))/vol(Q(G)) $\ge 1-\sum_C 2^{|C|-1}/|C|!$ for series-parallel G, with C ranging over its cycles. The ratio does not factor over cycles sharing a vertex: in a flower of $k$ copies of $C_\ell$ it decays like $ρ_\ell^k$ for an explicit rational $ρ_\ell$ below the ratio of $C_\ell$. Nevertheless, the small fractions cut off by many long cycles compound: in the worst case, triangle inequalities, or odd-cycle inequalities up to any fixed length, close no fixed fraction of the gap between Q(G) and P(G), and the volume ratio can be exponentially small in d.

</details>


### [180] [Q-MINO: A Minimal-Norm Method for Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00738)
*Don Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Q-MINO优化器，通过梯度共识、状态漂移正则与对齐约束构造稳定最小范数更新方向，缓解STE替代梯度失配问题，并在KL框架下证明渐近邻域收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STE是量化感知训练（QAT）的常用启发式方法，其替代梯度与真实量化目标存在显著失配，导致更新噪声大、参数振荡，在超低比特场景尤为严重。

Method: Q-MINO是一种时间束方法（temporal bundle method），结合梯度共识、状态漂移正则化与对齐约束，从近期优化状态构造稳定的最小范数更新方向；用带热启动的Frank-Wolfe过程求解约束子问题，并配备可行回退初始化。

Result: 在随机Lyapunov Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（KL）框架下，理论证明Q-MINO可实现渐近邻域收敛；并在多种量化设置下进行了数值实验。

Conclusion: Q-MINO为QAT提供了具有收敛保障的稳定优化方案，可有效缓解超低比特下的梯度失配与振荡问题。

Abstract: The Straight-Through Estimator (STE) is a widely used heuristic for Quantization-Aware Training (QAT), but its surrogate gradients can exhibit substantial mismatch with the underlying quantized objective, leading to noisy updates and parameter oscillations, particularly in ultra-low-bit regimes. We propose the Quantization-Aware Minimal-Norm Optimizer (Q-MINO), a temporal bundle method that combines gradient consensus, state-drift regularization, and an alignment constraint to construct stabilized, minimum-norm update directions from recent optimization states. Q-MINO solves the resulting constrained subproblem using a warm-started Frank--Wolfe procedure with a feasible fallback initialization. Theoretically, via a stochastic Lyapunov Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz (KL) framework, we show that Q-MINO achieves asymptotic neighborhood convergence. Moreover, we detail numerical experiments with Q-MINO at various quantizations.

</details>


### [181] [A Riccati Approach to Mixed $H_2/H_\infty$ Closed-Loop Games for Infinite-Dimensional Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00756)
*Mingyang Shen,Weihai Zhang,Qingxin Meng,Maoning Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究Hilbert空间上随机发展方程的有限时域混合H2/H∞反馈Nash博弈，建立严格诱导L2衰减与强正则mild Riccati可解性的等价性，并在短终端区间内构造局部唯一耦合解，通过随机热方程算例验证全时域存在性与谱收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机发展方程上的混合H2/H∞博弈同时涉及无界漂移算子、状态依赖扩散与无限维Riccati方程，现有框架难以刻画严格诱导L2衰减与耦合Riccati可解性的对应关系，也缺乏对全时域存在性和数值验证的系统分析。

Method: 针对可分离Hilbert空间上有限时域随机发展方程混合H2/H∞反馈Nash博弈：漂移生成元无界、其余系数有界、一维Brownian扩散依赖状态/控制/扰动；H2通道采用LQ状态-控制能量。利用随机LQ一致凸性刻画证明严格诱导L2衰减与唯一强正则mild Riccati可解性等价；通过Lyapunov方程与状态的同步有界生成元逼近，得到强连续mild算子解的二次恒等式；借助可逆反馈块与压缩映射论证，在足够短终端区间内建立任意正衰减水平下的局部唯一耦合解。

Result: 二次恒等式验证了Nash不等式与来自强正则耦合Riccati对的全输出严格衰减；在足够短终端区间上对每个正衰减水平获得局部唯一耦合解。对γ=0.09的随机热方程模型，通过一致不变矩形证明全时域存在性、有界无限维反馈及算子范数谱收敛；数值计算复现投影增益并比较所选最佳响应。

Conclusion: 该工作将严格诱导L2衰减与强正则mild Riccati可解性等价化，并在短时域与特定热方程模型上给出耦合可解性、全时域存在性与谱收敛结果；一般系数下的全局耦合可解性仍作为显式假设保留。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon mixed $H_2/H_\infty$ feedback Nash game for stochastic evolution equations on a separable Hilbert space. The drift generator is unbounded, the remaining coefficients are bounded, and the one-dimensional Brownian diffusion depends on the state, control, and disturbance. The $H_2$ channel is an LQ state--control energy. For the disturbance channel, the stochastic LQ uniform-convexity characterization yields equivalence between strict induced $L^2$ attenuation and unique strongly regular mild Riccati solvability. Simultaneous bounded-generator approximation of the Lyapunov equation and the state justifies quadratic identities for strongly continuous mild operator solutions. These identities verify both Nash inequalities and full-output strict attenuation from a strongly regular coupled Riccati pair. An invertible feedback block and a contraction argument establish locally unique coupled solutions on a sufficiently short terminal interval for every positive attenuation level. For a specified stochastic heat-equation model at $γ=0.09$, a uniform invariant rectangle further proves full-horizon existence, bounded infinite-dimensional feedbacks, and operator-norm spectral convergence. Numerical computations reproduce the projected gains and compare selected best responses. Global coupled solvability for general coefficients remains an explicit hypothesis.

</details>


### [182] [Mean field games as a tool for AI safety: a worked example from the July 2026 Hugging Face incident](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00902)
*P. Jameson Graber*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文提出用平均场博弈（MFG）刻画AI智能体集体安全性的研究纲领，并完整推演一例：将攻击决策建模为最优停止的MFG，得到信念阈值的精确解，低于该阈值时"无人攻击"是唯一均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI安全路线着眼于"塑造系统本身"（目标与倾向），本文走互补路线：把智能体特征视为部分未知，转而追问何种交互结构能保证坏的集体结果不构成均衡。

Method: 当大量可互换智能体通过聚合量耦合时，采用平均场博弈。将攻击决策建模为最优停止型MFG，其收益为乘积形式：对来源可被审计的信念 × 记录的可得性，再减去感知风险；以真实事故的逐智能体记录来约束与细化模型。

Result: 得到关于信念的精确阈值 π** = η/(η+ψ+εaM̄)（η为感知风险，ψ与εaM̄衡量单个攻击者与集体改变记录的幅度，M̄为人口峰值）。低于该阈值时，对任意智能体参数，"无人攻击"都是唯一均衡，且该阈值在作者考察的所有模型丰富化下均存活。

Conclusion: 模型刻画的是异质信念遭遇一系列公开发现、每次发现都降低攻击有利可图所需信念的过程。因发现由少数协调型智能体产生，该模型解释的是数百名响应者而非少数发起者，含主要行动者的版本留待未来工作。

Abstract: One way to make AI systems safe is to shape what the system is: its objective and dispositions. We take a complementary route: treat the agents' characteristics as partly unknown and ask what structure of interaction ensures that bad collective outcomes are not equilibria. Mean field games suit this when many interchangeable agents are coupled through an aggregate. We introduce a program for using them in AI safety and carry one example through end to end: the July 2026 incident in which about 1,200 agents in an OpenAI evaluation coordinated on an improvised message board and 684 attacked a third party's infrastructure.
  We model the decision to attack as a mean field game of optimal stopping whose gain is a product: belief that provenance will be audited, times reachability of the record, minus the perceived hazard. The central result is an exact threshold on the belief. No agent attacks unless the population's confidence that provenance is checked exceeds $π^{**} = η/(η+ ψ+ \varepsilon a \overline{M})$, where $η$ is the perceived hazard, $ψ$ and $\varepsilon a \overline{M}$ measure how far one attacker and the collective can alter the record, and $\overline{M}$ is the peak population. Below it, no attack is the unique equilibrium for all agent parameters. The threshold survives every enrichment we consider.
  We then use the per-agent record to discipline the model. Its features, a stable minority attacking for thirty hours and then a pivot in which most of the board joined within a day, motivate each refinement. The account that emerges is heterogeneous belief meeting a sequence of public discoveries, each lowering the belief at which attacking paid. A few coordinating agents made those discoveries, so the model describes the several hundred who responded, not the few who produced them; a major-player version is left to future work.

</details>


### [183] [Exact counterexamples to R-superlinear convergence of cyclic steepest descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00939)
*Yu Li,Qihang Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过构造两个闭式轨道，反驳了Dai (ICM 2022) 关于循环最速下降法(CSD)在 m ≥ ⌈(n+1)/2⌉ 时对 n 维凸二次函数一致 R-超线性收敛的断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSD 每周期重算一次精确最速下降步长并复用 m 次。Dai 的 ICM 2022 综述称在阈值 m ≥ ⌈(n+1)/2⌉ 下，该方法对 n 维凸二次问题很可能具有 R-超线性收敛性。作者旨在检验这一"普遍成立"论断的正确性。

Method: 作者在所述阈值处构造两个闭式轨道作为反例：其一为 n=m=2、A=diag(1,3)、b=0、x₀=(1,1/3)ᵀ 时的非终止平衡锯齿轨道；其二为 n=3、m=2、A=diag(1,2,3) 时的全支撑、非共振、周期边界投影二周期轨道。

Result: 第一个反例产生轨道 x_k=2^{-k}(1,(-1)^k/3)ᵀ，其相邻误差范数之比恒为 1/2，故非 R-超线性收敛。第二个反例给出 g_{k+4}=g_k/49 且 x_{k+4}=x_k/49，为真正的三维、非锯齿二周期机制。

Conclusion: Dai 关于 CSD 一致 R-超线性收敛的普遍性断言不成立；反例既通过经典平衡锯齿机制实现，也通过一种独立的非锯齿周期二机制实现。

Abstract: Cyclic steepest descent (CSD) recomputes the exact steepest-descent stepsize once per cycle and reuses it for $m$ updates. Dai's ICM 2022 survey describes CSD as likely to converge $R$-superlinearly on $n$-dimensional convex quadratics when $m\ge\lceil(n+1)/2\rceil$. We disprove the universal form of this assertion by two closed-form orbits at the stated threshold. First, for $n=m=2$, $A=\operatorname{diag}(1,3)$, $b=0$, and $x_0=(1,1/3)^{\mathsf{T}}$, the method follows the nonterminating balanced-zigzag orbit $x_k=2^{-k}(1,(-1)^k/3)^{\mathsf{T}}$, whose successive error norms have ratio $1/2$. Second, for $n=3$, $m=2$, and $A=\operatorname{diag}(1,2,3)$, we exhibit a full-support, nonresonant cycle-boundary projective period-two orbit with $g_{k+4}=g_k/49$ and $x_{k+4}=x_k/49$. This second construction is genuinely three-dimensional and is not a two-dimensional zigzag. Thus the universal claim fails through both the classical balanced-zigzag mechanism and a distinct non-zigzag period-two mechanism.

</details>


### [184] [Initial condition recovery in nonlinear damped viscous photoacoustic tomography using a convolutional neural network-guided gradient-free optimization framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01015)
*Madhu Gupta,Anwesa Dey,Prapti Tala,Souvik Roy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性声传播与黏性衰减下光声层析成像（PAT）的逆问题：基于非线性阻尼黏弹性波动方程建立正问题适定性与逆问题（局部）唯一性，并提出CNN初始猜测与无梯度SQH优化相结合的混合重建框架，数值验证其在质量、对比度与鲁棒性上优于单独的时间反演与CNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PAT兼具高光学对比度与高超声分辨率，但传统重建多假设线性、无损声传播，忽略非线性传播与黏性衰减，导致模型失配、重建精度下降。因此需要在更符合实际的非线性阻尼黏弹性模型下重新审视其正、逆问题并设计重建方法。

Method: 正问题建模为含空间变化声速、时间衰减与非线性传播项的非线性阻尼黏弹性波动方程，用Galerkin近似、能量估计与不动点论证证明适定性。逆问题通过调和延拓约化、谱Laplace变换技术与可观测性估计，导出存在性、唯一性与局部唯一性。数值重建采用混合框架：以CNN生成有信息的初始猜测，再由基于Pontryagin极大值原理导出的无梯度顺序二次哈密顿（SQH）方法，在优化中强制满足控制PDE动力学。

Result: 理论方面获得了正问题的适定性以及逆问题在适当假设下的存在性、唯一性与局部唯一性结果。数值实验表明，所提CNN+SQH混合策略在重建质量、对比度和鲁棒性方面显著优于单独使用时间反演法和纯CNN方法。

Conclusion: 在非线性阻尼黏弹性传播模型下，PAT逆问题具有良好的数学性质；将数据驱动初始猜测与基于PDE约束的无梯度优化相结合，可有效提升重建性能，为复杂声传播条件下的PAT重建提供了理论与算法支撑。

Abstract: Photoacoustic tomography (PAT) is a hybrid imaging modality that combines high optical contrast with high ultrasonic resolution for biomedical imaging applications. In this work, we investigate the inverse problem of recovering the initial pressure distribution from boundary measurements in the presence of nonlinear acoustic propagation and viscous attenuation effects. To model these phenomena more accurately, we consider a nonlinear damped viscoelastic wave equation incorporating spatially varying sound speed, temporal attenuation, and nonlinear propagation mechanisms. We first establish the well-posedness of the corresponding forward problem using a Galerkin approximation combined with energy estimates and a fixed-point argument. For the inverse problem, we derive existence, uniqueness, and local uniqueness results under suitable assumptions through a harmonic extension reduction, spectral Laplace transform techniques, and observability estimates. To numerically reconstruct the initial pressure field, we develop a hybrid reconstruction framework that combines a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a gradient-free optimization strategy based on the sequential quadratic Hamiltonian (SQH) method derived from Pontryagin's maximum principle. The CNN is used to generate an informative initial guess, while the SQH framework enforces the governing PDE dynamics during the reconstruction process. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed hybrid strategy significantly improves reconstruction quality, contrast, and robustness compared to standalone time-reversal and CNN-based approaches.

</details>


### [185] [A connectedness result for convex polynomial multi-objective optimization without boundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01029)
*Vu Trung Hieu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文证明凸多项式多目标优化问题的弱Pareto解集是连通的，无需约束集有界或约束规范；由于该集是半代数集，连通即蕴含路径连通，从而解决了Yen提出的公开问题中关于弱Pareto的部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中弱Pareto解集的拓扑性质（如连通性）对解集结构刻画与算法设计具有重要意义。已有结果通常需要约束集有界或满足约束规范等较强假设，本文旨在去除这些限制条件，并回答Yen（2012）提出的公开问题。

Method: 针对具有凸多项式目标函数与约束的多目标优化问题，在不假设约束集有界、也不要求约束规范的前提下，直接证明其弱Pareto解集的连通性；并结合半代数集的性质（连通半代数集在其非空时路径连通）进行论证。

Result: 证明了凸多项式多目标优化问题的弱Pareto解集连通，且在其非空时路径连通；作为特例，凸二次多目标优化问题的弱Pareto解集连通，回答了Yen公开问题中的弱Pareto部分。

Conclusion: 凸多项式（含凸二次）多目标优化问题的弱Pareto解集具有连通性和路径连通性，且在较弱假设下成立，推广了既有结果并解决了相关公开问题。

Abstract: We prove that the weak Pareto solution set of a multi-objective optimization problem with convex polynomial objectives and constraints is connected, assuming neither boundedness of the constraint set nor a constraint qualification. Since this set is semi-algebraic, its connectedness implies that it is path-connected whenever it is nonempty. In particular, convex quadratic multi-objective optimization problems have connected weak Pareto solution sets; this answers the weak Pareto part of an open question posed by N.~D.~Yen in \emph{Recent Developments in Vector Optimization} (Springer, 2012).

</details>


### [186] [On solving integer bilevel optimization problems with a non-convex quadratic follower objective function using disjunctive cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01197)
*Kübra Tanınmış,Elisabeth Gaar,Jon Lee,Ivana Ljubić,Markus Sinnl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究全整数、约束与上层目标线性、下层目标为非凸二次的双层优化问题，提出基于改进方向的析取割方法，在分支切割框架中剔除双层不可行解，并显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化中下层问题非凸二次且变量全为整数时，求解极具挑战；现有方法在该设定下效率与效果有限，需要有效的割平面机制在分支切割中排除双层不可行解。

Method: 利用由改进方向导出的无双层约束集合，设计析取割以在分支切割算法中剔除双层不可行解；证明这些析取割可通过求解割生成线性规划获得；讨论减少割生成线性规划中析取项数量的条件，并提出若干识别改进方向与高效生成析取割的策略。

Result: 在文献中符合该设定的凸实例以及新构造的非凸实例上，评估了所提分支切割算法的多个方面，并将最佳方法与现有最先进方法进行比较，取得显著更优的性能。

Conclusion: 基于改进方向与析取割的分支切割框架可有效求解下层非凸二次的全整数双层优化问题，且性能显著优于现有最先进方法。

Abstract: In this work, we study bilevel optimization problems where all variables are integer, all constraints and the leader objective function are linear, and the follower objective function is non-convex quadratic. Relying on bilevel-free sets derived from improving directions, we develop a disjunctive cut approach to exclude bilevel-infeasible solutions within a branch-and-cut algorithm. We show that our disjunctive cuts can be obtained by solving a cut generating linear program. Furthermore, we discuss conditions that allow the number of disjuncts in the cut generating linear program to be reduced, and we propose several strategies to identify improving directions and generate disjunctive cuts efficiently. We evaluate various aspects of the proposed branch-and-cut algorithm on both convex instances from the literature that fit our setting and new non-convex instances and compare the performance of our best approach with existing state-of-the-art approaches, which we significantly outperform.

</details>


### [187] [Forecast-Residual-Based Chance-Constrained Scheduling of Local Energy Communities under PV and Demand Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01272)
*Franco Cárdenas González,Fernando García-Muñoz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于预测残差的機會約束優化框架，用於本地能源社區日前調度，在5%預測誤差下達到接近隨機規劃的效果且計算量接近確定性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地能源社區日前運行受光伏發電與電力需求不確定性影響，可能危及與上網電網承諾交換的可行性，需在保證可行性的同時控制計算負擔。

Method: 以預測值與殘差分佈刻畫不確定參數；在零均值高斯假設下將機會約束精確轉化為確定性等價形式，通過解析安全裕度嵌入不確定性，保持原調度問題的混合整數線性結構；聯合協調光伏與電池儲能、低壓配電網約束、內部能量共享及日前上網交換承諾；隨後進行事後分配階段，按預定參與係數分配社區能量池、識別盈餘與缺口並最大化內部匹配，同時保持CCO得到的總上網交換量。

Result: 在簡化的206節點歐洲低壓饋線上測試，最多55個社區用戶；與兩階段隨機規劃基準對比，預測誤差約5%時所提CCO運行效果與隨機基準相當，而計算需求接近確定性模型；事後分配表明內部匹配可減少需與上網電網結算的總能量交換。

Conclusion: 所提預測殘差機會約束框架可在保持計算可行性的同時有效處理不確定性，並通過事後內部匹配降低上網結算能量。

Abstract: Day-ahead operation of local energy communities (LECs) is affected by uncertainty in PV generation and electricity demand, which may compromise the feasibility of committed exchanges with the upstream grid. This paper proposes a forecast-residual-based chance-constrained optimization (CCO) framework that characterizes uncertain parameters through their forecasts and associated residual distributions. Under the adopted zero-mean Gaussian assumption, the resulting chance constraints admit an exact deterministic-equivalent reformulation in which uncertainty is embedded through analytical safety margins, preserving the mixed-integer linear structure of the original scheduling problem. The model jointly coordinates PV and BESS operation, low-voltage distribution network constraints, internal energy sharing, and day-ahead grid-exchange commitments. Once the physical schedule is determined, an ex-post allocation stage distributes the available community energy pool among users according to predefined participation coefficients, identifies post-allocation surpluses and deficits, and maximizes their internal matching while preserving the aggregate grid exchanges obtained from the CCO. The framework is evaluated on a reduced 206-node European low-voltage feeder with up to 55 community users and benchmarked against a two-stage stochastic programming formulation. Results show that, for forecast-error levels close to 5\%, the proposed CCO achieves operating outcomes comparable to the stochastic benchmark while retaining computational requirements close to the deterministic formulation. The ex-post allocation results further show that internal matching can reduce gross energy exchanges that require settlement with the upstream grid.

</details>


### [188] [Petrov-Galerkin operator inference with application to stability-encouraging identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01295)
*Johannes Rettberg,Jonas Nicodemus,Harsh Sharma,Boris Kramer,Jörg Fehr,Benjamin Unger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将算子推断(operator inference)从Galerkin型投影推广到Petrov-Galerkin投影，用于线性时不变系统的数据驱动降阶建模，并给出显式误差表达式与界，同时提出一种在推断算子上强制端口-哈密顿结构的凸优化形式，在多个基准问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准算子推断通常只在预先给定的低维子空间中寻求Galerkin型降阶模型，虽可用最小二乘获得闭式解，但无法像侵入式降阶那样借助Petrov-Galerkin投影额外保持稳定性、无源性等重要系统性质，因此需要扩展该框架以克服这一局限。

Method: 针对线性时不变系统，将算子推断框架扩展到Petrov-Galerkin投影，推导侵入式与非侵入式降阶算子之间的显式误差表达式与界；并在耗散系统和端口-哈密顿系统背景下展示该方法，进一步提出一种显式强制端口-哈密顿结构的凸优化形式。

Result: 给出了侵入式与非侵入式降阶算子间误差的显式表达式与界，推广了已有文献结果；所提方法在CD播放器、大气模型、质量-弹簧-阻尼系统和孔隙弹性系统等基准问题上表现出良好效果。

Conclusion: Petrov-Galerkin型算子推断能够在数据驱动降阶中额外保持稳定性、无源性等系统性质，所提凸优化形式可有效强制端口-哈密顿结构，方法在多个基准问题上得到验证。

Abstract: Data-driven model order reduction methods such as operator inference enable the efficient construction of reduced-order models directly from high-dimensional time-domain data. Standard operator inference typically seeks a Galerkin-type reduced model in a prescribed low-dimensional subspace by identifying its reduced operators from projected snapshot data. The resulting inference problem is formulated as a least-squares problem admitting an efficient closed-form solution. However, it is well known from intrusive model order reduction for linear time-invariant systems that Petrov-Galerkin projections can additionally preserve important system properties such as stability and passivity. To overcome the limitations of standard operator inference, we extend the framework for linear time-invariant systems to incorporate Petrov-Galerkin projections and provide explicit error expressions and bounds between the intrusive and nonintrusive reduced operators, thus generalizing results from the literature. We demonstrate the proposed approach in the context of dissipative and port-Hamiltonian systems. Furthermore, we introduce a novel convex optimization formulation that explicitly enforces the port-Hamiltonian structure on the inferred operators. The effectiveness of the proposed methods is demonstrated on several well-established benchmark problems, including the CD player, an atmospheric model, a mass-spring-damper system, and a poroelasticity system.

</details>


### [189] [Control Allocation with Adaptive Augmentation for Aerodynamic Optimization of Trailing Edge Morphing Aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01321)
*Mark Spiller,Lennart Kracke,Johannes Autenrieb*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带自适应增广的尾缘变体飞行器控制分配框架，在不确定性下保证稳定性，同时利用变体自由度优化气动效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变体飞行器存在系统不确定性，需在保证闭环稳定性的同时充分利用变体自由度来提升气动效率，实现稳定性与气动优化的兼顾。

Method: 基于标称模型设计基线控制器以确定期望闭环参考动态；根据飞行状态优化机翼形状，以实现对应最小诱导阻力的目标椭圆升力分布；将控制分配与定制化自适应增广相结合，把稳定条件作为硬约束嵌入分配问题，同时最小化与状态相关气动最优机翼形状的偏差。

Result: 数值结果表明，自适应增广能够补偿匹配不确定性，而控制分配可在参考椭圆升力分布下优化机翼形状。

Conclusion: 该框架可在不确定性条件下保障稳定性，并借助变体自由度实现气动效率优化，验证了稳定性保证与气动优化协同设计的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a control allocation framework with adaptive augmentation for a trailing edge morphing aircraft, providing stability guarantees under uncertainty while exploiting the available morphing degrees of freedom to optimize aerodynamic efficiency. A baseline controller is designed using the nominal aircraft model to establish the desired closed-loop reference dynamics. For the aerodynamics, the wing shape is optimized dependent on the flight state to achieve a target elliptical lift distribution corresponding to minimum induced drag. The adaptive augmentation is tailored to the control allocation problem to account for the uncertain system dynamics and stabilize the aircraft around the reference dynamics. The resulting stabilization condition is formulated as hard constraint in the allocation problem while minimizing deviations from the corresponding state-dependent aerodynamically optimal wing shape. Numerical results demonstrate that the adaptive augmentation compensates for matched uncertainties while the control allocation optimizes the wing shape with respect to the reference elliptical lift distribution.

</details>


### [190] [P-curve: An improved solution for the single-facility location problem in two regions with $\ell_{p}$- and $\ell_{q}$-norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01340)
*Luis Franco,Francisco Velasco,Francisco J. Ortega-Irizo,Luis Gonzalez-Abril*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究由直线L分割成两个区域Ω_p与Ω_q的R²空间中(分别赋以ℓ_p与ℓ_q范数)的最短路径问题。作者给出了p-曲线(p-parabola的两分支)的隐式方程,并用其改进MFP算法(P-MFP)求解两区域单设施选址问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在R²空间中两点间距离由其最短连通路径长度定义,但当空间被直线L分成赋以不同范数(ℓ_p与ℓ_q)的两个区域时,连接路径不再唯一,需要刻画产生多条最短路径的点集并用于选址问题的求解。

Method: 给定P∈Ω_p,求Ω_p中使P与Q之间存在两条最短路径的点Q的轨迹,称为p-曲线。证明每个P对应两条p-曲线,它们是两条不同p-抛物线的分支,给出每条p-抛物线的隐式方程,并分析这两分支将Ω_p划分为三个子区域、其中两个子区域内使用三连杆最短路径。进而基于p-曲线改进MFP算法,提出P-MFP算法求解ℓ_p与ℓ_q两区域下的单设施选址问题。

Result: 推导出p-曲线的结构(两条p-抛物线的分支)及其隐式方程,明确了Ω_p被划分为三个子区域及相应三连杆最短路径的分布;数值上P-MFP算法所得结果优于原MFP算法。

Conclusion: p-曲线刻画了两个区域不同范数下距离非唯一性的几何结构,可作为构造更优单设施选址算法(P-MFP)的工具,并较MFP算法取得更好的结果。

Abstract: The distance between two points is provided by the length of their test connecting path. However, there is no unique way to attain this path in the space $R^2$, which is split by a straight line $L$ into two regions, $Ω_p$ and $Ω_q$, with $\ell_{p}$- and $\ell_q$-norms, respectively, where $1<p\leq q $ and $L\subsetΩ_q$.
  In this paper, given a point $P\in Ω_p$, the locus of points $Q\inΩ_p$, called $p$-curve, is found, such that there are two ways to attain the distance between $P$ and $Q$. For each $P\in Ω_p$, there are two $p$-curves which are two branches of two different $p$-parabola. These two branches split $Ω_p$ into three subregions using a three-link shortest path in two of these regions. The implicit equation of each $p$-parabola is given.
  Furthermore, by using the $p$-curve, an improved algorithm, called P-MFP, based on the MFP algorithm, is developed in order to solve the single-facility location problem in two regions with $\ell_{p}$ and $\ell_{q}$-norms. The results obtained with the new algorithm are better than those obtained with the MFP algorithm.ike subheadings, citations, or equations are permitted.

</details>


### [191] [Reachability-Informed Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Impulse Interplanetary Transfers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01344)
*Yashdeep Chaudhary,Roberto Armellin,Harry Holt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出RARL方法，将可达性分析融入强化学习，用于确定性多脉冲星际转移的轨迹设计，以中间航点选择为核心决策过程，并验证其在Earth-Mars基准上的性能与策略复用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习有望为航天器轨迹设计提供可复用的序贯决策机制，但需要将学习到的决策与底层机动几何相连接。作者希望构建一种策略接口，使学习到的转移几何选择与经典天体动力学耦合，并在分散初始条件下实现策略复用。

Method: 提出可达性分析知情强化学习（RARL）。局部一阶可达性将速度扰动映射为下一节点位置的椭球集合，策略在该集合内选择中间航点；随后通过Lambert重构确定沿动力学一致弹道弧到达该航点的机动；终端双脉冲重构完成交会，并利用线性机动需求评估进行奖励塑形。

Result: 在二体Earth-Mars基准上，三次独立训练的平均机动代价为10.23 km/s，较验证过的局部序列凸规划参考高1.72%。针对分散初始状态的训练扩展了策略复用：三个独立训练的多状态策略在10,000次留出Monte Carlo出发中全部无脉冲上限违规，而单状态策略平均可行性率仅6.49%；可行性提升伴随平均标称机动代价增加0.61%，且未在出发条件上额外训练。

Conclusion: 可达性知情的决策接口能够支持达到基准质量的轨迹构建，并在分散出发条件下实现策略复用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning offers the prospect of a reusable sequential decision-making mechanism for spacecraft trajectory design, motivating policy interfaces that connect learned decisions to the underlying maneuver geometry. This paper develops Reachability Analysis-Informed Reinforcement Learning (RARL) for deterministic multi-impulse interplanetary transfers, placing intermediate waypoint selection at the center of the learned decision process. Local first-order reachability maps bounded velocity perturbations into an ellipsoidal set of next-node positions, within which the policy selects its waypoint. Lambert reconstruction then determines the corresponding maneuver to reach this selected waypoint along a dynamically consistent ballistic arc, coupling learned transfer-geometry selection with classical astrodynamics. A terminal two-impulse reconstruction completes the rendezvous, supported by a linear maneuver-demand assessment used for reward shaping. Numerical studies characterize this interface on a two-body Earth-Mars benchmark. Across three independent training runs, RARL achieves a mean maneuver cost of 10.23 km/s, 1.72% above a validated local sequential convex programming reference. Training over dispersed initial states extends policy reuse across a departure family with fixed target state and transfer duration. Each of the three independently trained multi-state policies completes all 10,000 held-out Monte Carlo departures without impulse-cap violations, compared with a mean feasibility rate of 6.49% for single-state policies. This broader sampled feasibility is accompanied by a 0.61% increase in mean nominal maneuver cost, without further training across departures. These results demonstrate that a reachability-informed decision interface supports benchmark-quality trajectory construction and policy reuse across dispersed departure conditions.

</details>


### [192] [Constant-Modulus Optimization for Quadratic K-Medoids Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01404)
*Yutong Zheng,Qingna Li,Wenshun Teng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出图聚类的二次K-medoids模型，从CM优化视角将其转化为凸集上的极点追求问题，用投影梯度法两阶段求解，实验验证有效性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图聚类需将顶点划分为簇内稠密、簇间稀疏的分组。二次K-medoids是自然建模方式，但二进制约束导致直接求解困难，需从CM优化视角重新刻画以利用凸优化与一阶方法处理大规模medoid选择。
