Method: 将二进制可行集识别为离散CM集，建立二次K-medoids模型与CM优化的联系；取可行集凸包并引入负平方范数惩罚以促使解落在极点上，得到简单凸集上的极点追求模型。该凸集上的投影可高效计算，从而可用投影梯度类方法。据此提出两阶段聚类：第一阶段用惩罚连续化投影梯度求解器近似求解带惩罚的medoid选择问题；第二阶段用统一的两级分配规则将选定medoids转为图划分，以Jaccard距离为主准则，必要时以最短路距离为辅准则。

Result: 在合成PPM图、SBM图及真实图数据上的数值实验表明，所提方法具有有效性和计算效率。实验还显示，medoid选择问题中更小的目标值不一定带来更准确的最终图划分。

Conclusion: 将二次K-medoids图聚类与CM优化建立联系并松弛为凸集上的极点追求问题，可实现大规模medoid选择的高效求解；但需同时评估优化性能与分配后的聚类质量，因为medoid选择目标值与最终划分精度并不一致。

Abstract: Graph clustering aims to partition the vertices of a graph into groups with dense intra-cluster connections and sparse inter-cluster connections. In this paper, we study a quadratic $K$-medoids formulation for graph clustering from the perspective of CM optimization. By identifying the binary feasible set as a discrete CM set, we establish a connection between the quadratic $K$-medoids model and CM optimization. Rather than solving the original binary problem directly, we consider the convex hull of its feasible set and introduce a negative squared-norm penalty to promote extreme-point solutions, resulting in an extreme point pursuit model over a simple convex set. The efficient projection onto this convex set enables the use of projected-gradient-based optimization for large-scale medoid selection. Based on this formulation, we propose a two-stage clustering method. In Stage~1, a penalty-continuation projected gradient solver is used to approximately solve the penalized medoid-selection problem. In Stage~2, the selected medoids are converted into a graph partition through a unified two-level assignment rule, where Jaccard distance is used as the primary criterion and shortest-path distance is used as a secondary criterion when needed. Numerical experiments on synthetic PPM and SBM graphs, together with real-world graph data, demonstrate the effectiveness and computational efficiency of our method. The experiments also show that a smaller objective value in the medoid-selection problem does not necessarily lead to a more accurate final graph partition, highlighting the importance of evaluating both optimization performance and clustering quality after assignment.

</details>


### [193] [On the Multi-Index, Multi-Rate, and Multi-Phase Dynamics of Decoder-Only Language Models: A Unified Hybrid Framework for Generative and Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01478)
*Ali Pakniyat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将解码器式大语言模型建模为多指标、多速率系统，并为其代理式工具交互提出随机混合系统框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益作为自主决策与规划回路的计算引擎，但现有系统与控制视角存在架构简化、指标混淆、工具交互描述不正式等问题，缺乏统一的控制论刻画。

Method: 形式化三个层级耦合演化指标：(i) 层深度方向超快前馈级联，层归一化视为球面投影，KV缓存经因果前缀不变性证明为精确内部状态实现；(ii) token生成步上的非受控随机差分递推，有限上下文截断诱导时间齐次马尔可夫链；(iii) 自主模式切换机制管理token生成与工具执行机制间的转换，工具调用由轨迹到达切换流形触发，随后以外生状态跳跃映射将外部观测增广至上下文串；并定义依赖提示词的可评估断言评估器，得到任务级误差过程。

Result: 在条件故障风险与误差漂移假设下，无故障历史与期望误差增长获得有界性界。

Conclusion: 所提多指标、多速率控制论表述与随机混合系统框架为LLM代理式工具交互的多阶段动力学治理奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as computational engines in autonomous decision-making and planning loops, yet their systems and control treatment remains hindered by architectural simplifications, index conflations, and informal descriptions of tool interactions. This paper presents a control-theoretic formulation of decoder-only language models as multi-index, multi-rate systems, and sets the stage for a stochastic hybrid systems framework to govern the multi-phase dynamics of agentic tool interaction. We formalize the architecture across three hierarchically coupled evolution indices: (i) an ultrafast feedforward cascade of transformer blocks across layer depth, where layer normalization is cast as a spherical projection and key--value caching is proven to be an exact internal state realization via causal prefix invariance; (ii) an uncontrolled stochastic difference recursion over token generation steps, where finite context truncation induces a time-homogeneous Markov chain; and (iii) an autonomous mode-switching mechanism governing transitions between token generation and tool execution regimes, where tool invocations are triggered upon trajectory arrival at switching manifolds, followed by exogenous state jump maps that augment the context string with external observations. By defining a prompt-dependent evaluator over successive evaluable claims, we obtain a task-level error process whose fault-free histories and expected error growth admit bounds under conditional fault-hazard and error-drift assumptions.

</details>


### [194] [McADMM: A Multi-Clique Augmented Lagrangian-Based Algorithm for Large-Scale Sparse SDPs with Bound Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01503)
*Kristo Nugraha Lian,Nehal Ahmed Shaikh,Di Hou,Xingyu Xie,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出多团分解方法McADMM，改进sGS-PADMM以高效求解大规模稀疏SDP，并通过GPU实现加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sGS-PADMM在中等规模线性SDP上表现良好，但面对大规模稀疏SDP（如n>1000）时扩展性不足。常见分解算法侧重于最小化团之间的重叠，而忽视了分解后PSD投影成本这一关键瓶颈，因此需要新的分解策略来提升大规模稀疏SDP的求解效率。

Method: 提出新的多团（multi-clique）分解方法，核心目标是降低分解后估计的PSD投影成本，而非传统方法所关注的最小化团间重叠。为此设计了一种新颖的线性空间投影方法，能够通过简单的平均步骤高效处理大量重叠约束。基于该方法实现求解器McADMM，并额外开发了其GPU版本。

Result: 在若干由重要QCQP问题松弛产生的大规模SDP实例上进行数值实验，将McADMM与其它先进分解型求解器以及未分解的sGS-PADMM进行对比，验证了其性能优势；GPU实现进一步显著加速了分解求解器。

Conclusion: 通过以降低PSD投影成本为目标的多团分解与线性空间投影技术，McADMM显著提升了对具有良好聚合稀疏模式的大规模稀疏SDP的求解能力，GPU实现可进一步带来实质性加速。

Abstract: sGS-PADMM [21, 14, 6] is a powerful and versatile class of convergent multi-block ADMM solvers for implementations on moderate-sized linear semidefinite programming (SDP) problems. In this paper, we further enhance this class of algorithms for solving SDP problems by proposing a new multi-clique decomposition approach, allowing substantial improvements in applications on large-scale sparse SDPs (e.g., where $n > 1000$) with conducive aggregate sparsity patterns. Our SDP decomposition strategy mainly aims to reduce the estimated PSD projection cost after decomposition, in contrast to common decomposition algorithms that are encumbered with minimizing the overlaps between cliques. This feature is made possible by our novel linear-space projection approach that is capable of efficiently processing a large number of overlap constraints via simple averaging steps. For the numerical experiments, we demonstrate the performance of our solver -- named McADMM for Multi-clique ADMM -- on a number of large-scale SDP instances that arise from relaxations of some important quadratically constrained quadratic programming (QCQP) problems. The performance of McADMM is contrasted against other state-of-the-art decomposition-based solvers as well as the non-decomposed sGS-PADMM to highlight our key contributions. We additionally develop a GPU implementation of McADMM and demonstrate that it can substantially accelerate the decomposed solver.

</details>


### [195] [Vector- and operator-valued backward stochastic equations with finite-variation drivers and a maximum principle for singular stochastic control in infinite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01545)
*Ying Hu,Guomin Liu,Shanjian Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究希尔伯特空间上混合正则-奇异随机控制问题（含无界随机线性算子、非凸正则控制域、状态依赖奇异系数），证明正倒向方程适定性与加权矩估计，刻画二阶伴随，并导出一阶/二阶哈密顿最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机控制理论多针对有界算子、凸控制域或有界奇异控制，难以处理无界随机系数与非凸约束下的混合正则-奇异控制问题，故需建立更一般的适定性与最优性理论。

Method: 在希尔伯特空间中，对正则控制域非凸、奇异系数状态依赖、奇异控制为无界终值的适应非降càdlàg过程的情形，先证正倒向方程适定性与加权矩估计；再用条件期望算子值倒向随机积分方程刻画二阶伴随；最后结合该伴随二次型的Itô型公式、spike变分与凸变分推导最优性条件。

Result: 得到正倒向方程的适定性与加权矩估计；获得二阶伴随的算子值倒向随机积分方程刻画及其二次型的Itô型公式；导出正则控制的二阶哈密顿条件，并证明奇异哈密顿的非负性及接触条件。

Conclusion: 该工作将混合正则-奇异随机控制的最优性分析推广至无界随机算子、非凸控制域及无界奇异控制框架，给出了含二阶伴随与接触条件的完整哈密顿刻画。

Abstract: We study a mixed regular--singular control problem for stochastic evolution equations in a Hilbert space with possibly unbounded random linear operators, a nonconvex regular-control domain, and a state-dependent singular coefficient. The singular control is an adapted nondecreasing càdlàg process whose terminal value need not be bounded. We prove well-posedness and weighted moment estimates for the forward and backward equations, and characterize the second-order adjoint by a conditionally expected operator-valued backward stochastic integral equation. An Itô-type formula for the quadratic form of this adjoint, together with spike and convex variations, yields a second-order Hamiltonian condition for the regular control, as well as nonnegativity and a contact condition for the optional singular Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [196] [Reinforcement Learning to Accelerate Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient for Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01546)
*Jinhwan Sul,Alex Oshin,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: GALLOP 用强化学习联合学习连续算法参数与离散重启决策，优化广义加速 PDHG，在六类 LP 上显著减少迭代并提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDHG 虽适合 GPU 求解大规模线性规划，但实际性能依赖算法参数、加速与重启的协调，手工调参困难。

Method: 提出 GALLOP：无需对求解器求导，用 RL 联合学习连续参数与离散重启决策；采用广义加速 PDHG 更新，结合分离的原始/对偶外推、历史修正与重启锚定，系数可独立调节；用分组 PPO 目标训练维度无关的反馈策略，按控制组分别裁剪似然比，并在重启转换时排除非活跃加速控制。

Result: 在六类 LP 族与一个公开物品放置基准上评估，主设置下迭代次数减少 1.9–5.6 倍，相比 MPAX 算法墙钟时间最高提速 16.0×；每族训练一个策略即可泛化到比最大训练实例大 3×–400× 的同族 LP，包括 1024 万变量的 Transport LP。

Conclusion: RL 驱动的参数与重启联合学习可显著提升 PDHG 求解大规模 LP 的效率，且策略具备跨规模泛化能力。

Abstract: Primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) methods solve large-scale linear programs (LPs) using GPU-friendly matrix-vector products and projections, but their practical performance depends on coordinating algorithm parameters, acceleration, and restarts. We introduce GALLOP, which uses reinforcement learning to jointly learn continuous algorithm parameters and discrete restart decisions without differentiating through the solver. Its generalized accelerated PDHG update combines separate primal and dual extrapolation, history corrections, and restart anchoring with independently adjustable coefficients. We train a dimension-agnostic feedback policy using a groupwise proximal policy optimization objective that clips likelihood ratios separately for different control groups and excludes inactive acceleration controls on restart transitions. We evaluate GALLOP on six LP families and a public item-placement benchmark. On the main evaluation settings across the six families, GALLOP reduces iteration counts by factors of $1.9$-$5.6$ and achieves up to a $16.0\times$ speedup in algorithm wall-clock time over MPAX. With one policy trained per family, the learned policies generalize without retraining to within-family LPs $3\times$-$400\times$ larger than the largest training instances, including Transport LPs with $10.24$ million variables.

</details>


### [197] [Optimal Momentum Methods for Stochastic Multilevel Compositional Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01572)
*Wei Jiang,Rui Yan,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang,Zechao Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机多层优化问题，目标为多层光滑非凸函数的嵌套复合，仅可获得各层梯度与函数值的随机估计。作者采用基于动量的估计器配合小批量追踪各层函数值，构造动量梯度估计器，实现寻找ε-稳定点的最优样本复杂度O(ε^{-4})，并进一步提出无需小批量的方法，在风险规避投资组合优化与分层倾斜经验风险最小化任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机多层优化中，由于目标为多层光滑非凸函数的嵌套复合，整体梯度的准确估计因嵌套结构而十分困难；已有文献通常依赖更强的平均光滑性假设，且需要问题相关常数来设置超参数，限制了方法的适用性与实用性。

Method: 1) 采用基于动量的估计器并配合小批量，追踪每一层的函数值，进而构造动量梯度估计器；2) 通过归一化技术，在无需问题相关常数设置超参数的情况下达到相同收敛速率；3) 为摆脱小批量需求，进一步提出无批量（batch-free）方法，引入一阶近似与裁剪（clipping）技术进行函数值估计。

Result: 建立了寻找ε-稳定点的最优样本复杂度O(ε^{-4})，且避免了先前文献常依赖的更强平均光滑性假设；归一化技术使方法无需问题相关常数即可达到相同速率；无批量方法同样取得最优速率，并在风险规避投资组合优化与分层倾斜经验风险最小化实验中验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机多层非凸优化提供了在较弱假设下达到最优样本复杂度的有效算法框架，兼顾了超参数设置的便利性与无小批量的实现方式，并通过实际应用实验证明了方法的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates stochastic multi-level optimization where the objective is a nested composition of several smooth non-convex functions. We assume that only stochastic estimates of the gradient and function values for each level are accessible. Consequently, obtaining an accurate estimate of the overall gradient is challenging due to the nested structure. To address this, we employ a momentum-based estimator with mini-batches to track the function values of each level, which are subsequently used to construct momentum gradient estimators. We establish an optimal sample complexity of $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ for finding an $ε$-stationary point, avoiding the stronger average smoothness assumption commonly relied upon in prior literature. Furthermore, by employing a normalization technique, we attain the same rate without requiring problem-dependent constants to set hyperparameters. To achieve the optimal rate without mini-batches, we further develop a batch-free method that incorporates a first-order approximation and a clipping technique for function value estimation. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our proposed methods through experiments on risk-averse portfolio optimization and hierarchical tilted empirical risk minimization.

</details>


### [198] [Convergence Analysis of STORM Under Different Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01599)
*Wei Jiang,Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Rui Yan,Lijun Zhang,Zechao Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究STORM在无平均光滑性假设下对不同目标函数的收敛性，借助辅助序列分析，证明其在非凸、凸及强凸目标下均达到标准光滑条件下的最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STORM的收敛分析依赖较强的平均光滑性假设，限制了方法的适用范围，因此需要研究去掉该假设后STORM的收敛行为。

Method: 先重新审视平均光滑性下的结果；在无平均光滑性时，构造辅助序列，并将STORM更新与其进行对比分析。

Result: 平均光滑下非凸目标获得O(T^{-1/3})，PL条件下最后迭代输出获得O(σ^2/(μT))；无平均光滑下非凸目标仍达O(T^{-1/4})（标准光滑下最优），凸和λ-强凸目标分别达到最优的O(σR/√T)和O(σ^2/(λT))。

Conclusion: STORM在无平均光滑性假设下仍具良好收敛性，可在同一STORM递推下通过不同超参数选择实现各类最优速率。

Abstract: Stochastic recursive momentum (STORM) achieves fast convergence for nonconvex optimization via the variance reduction effect, but existing analyses rely on the strong average smoothness assumption. In this paper, we study the convergence of STORM for different objectives without average smoothness. We first revisit the results under average smoothness, obtaining the $O(T^{-1/3})$ bound for nonconvex objectives and the $O(σ^2/(μT))$ bound for last-iterate output under the $μ$-Polyak--Łojasiewicz~(PL) condition. Without average smoothness, we design an auxiliary sequence and compare the STORM update with it in the analysis. With the help of this sequence, we prove that STORM still attains an $O(T^{-1/4})$ rate for nonconvex objectives, which is optimal under standard smoothness. For convex and $λ$-strongly convex objectives, we further prove averaged and last-iterate bounds with optimal rates of $O(σR/\sqrt T)$ and $O(σ^2/(λT))$, respectively. All the obtained results use the same STORM recursion with different hyperparameter choices.

</details>


### [199] [Structural stability of systems and cycle covers in random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01607)
*Mohamed Ali Belabbas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究有向step-graphon采样得到的网络拓扑能否以高概率维持线性系统的结构稳定性，通过关联循环多面体、骨架有向图与集中向量给出充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构系统理论关注哪些网络拓扑能支撑可控性或稳定性等性质；当拓扑本身随机时，问题转为概率问题——从随机模型抽取的图在多大程度上能维持该性质。这类概率可衡量性质在拓扑空间中的丰富性与鲁棒性，并判断依赖该性质的系统能否在不确定环境中可靠部署。作者针对有向graphon情形下的线性系统渐近稳定性回答这一问题。

Method: 定义两个图论性质：N要求对每个k≤n，D的某个k顶点诱导子有向图存在循环覆盖；S要求这些子图可按节点集嵌套序列V1⊂…⊂Vn=V(D)选取，且起点为带自环的单顶点。已知N是结构稳定的必要条件、S是充分条件。从有向step-graphon W采样D，对具有q节点骨架有向图S和集中向量x*的step-graphon W关联循环多面体X(S)⊆Δ_q。以x*在多面体中的位置、多面体维数、W的自环密度，以及（对S）骨架循环的排序条件来刻画Pr(N)→1和Pr(S)→1的充要条件。

Result: 给出Pr(N)→1与Pr(S)→1当n→∞时的充要条件，条件由x*在循环多面体X(S)内的位置、多面体维数、W的自环密度以及骨架循环的排序条件共同决定。

Conclusion: 对任意有向step-graphon，这些结果识别出采样拓扑以压倒性概率维持或不维持稳定动力学的参数区域，从而为随机拓扑下结构稳定性的概率刻画提供了完整判据。

Abstract: Structural system theory studies which network topologies can sustain a prescribed system property such as controllability or stability. When the topology is itself random, the relevant question becomes probabilistic: how likely is a graph drawn from a stochastic model to sustain the property? Such probabilities measure the abundance and robustness of the property across topologies, and indicate whether systems requiring it can be reliably deployed in uncertain environments. We address this question for asymptotic stability of linear systems in the directed graphon setting.
  We consider two graph-theoretic properties. The first is $\mathcal N$, and it requires that for every $k\leq n$ some $k$-vertex induced subdigraph of $D$ admits a cycle cover, and the second is $\mathcal S$, which requires that these subdigraphs can be chosen so that their node sets form a nested sequence $V_1\subset\cdots\subset V_n=V(D)$ starting from a single vertex with a loop. We have shown that $\mathcal N$ is necessary and $\mathcal S$ is sufficient for structural stability. We sample $D$ from a directed step-graphon $W$. Our main results give necessary and sufficient conditions for $\Pr(\mathcal N)\to 1$ and $\Pr(\mathcal S)\to 1$ as $n\to\infty$. In more detail, to a step-graphon $W$ with skeleton digraph $S$ on $q$ nodes and concentration vector $x^*$ we associate a cycle polytope $\vec{\mathcal X}(S)\subseteqΔ_q$. The conditions are then formulated in terms of the position of $x^*$ within $\vec{\mathcal X}(S)$, the dimension of the polytope, the loop density of $W$ and, for $\mathcal S$, an ordering condition on the cycles of the skeleton. Together these results identify, for directed step-graphons, the regime in which a sampled topology is overwhelmingly likely or unlikely to sustain stable dynamics.

</details>


### [200] [Lower Bounds for Stochastic First-Order Algorithms with Variance Reduction in Nonconvex--Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01662)
*Jiayi Song,Zi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了允许方差缩减的随机一阶算法在非凸-凹及非凸-强凹极小极大优化中的复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有下界对算法类限制过强，尤其未覆盖使用方差缩减的随机一阶算法；本文旨在刻画这类算法在非凸极小极大优化中的复杂度障碍。

Method: 针对允许方差缩减的"零尊重"（zero-respecting）算法类，在无偏随机一阶oracle（方差≤σ²）及均方光滑条件下，构造硬实例，以约束原值函数Moreau包络的梯度范数为精度度量，推导下界。

Result: 非凸-凹情形下界为Ω(L²D_YΔε⁻³ + L³D_Y²Δσ²ε⁻⁶)；非凸-强凹情形（条件数κ=L/μ）在有界方差模型下为Ω(LΔ√κ ε⁻² + LΔκσ²ε⁻⁴)，附加均方光滑（常数L̄）时为Ω(LΔ√κ ε⁻² + ΔL̄σκ^{3/2}ε⁻³)。

Conclusion: 结果给出了含噪、含方差缩减时对精度、对偶域半径和噪声的依赖关系，揭示了凹与强凹两类情形下的复杂度壁垒，且主下界对方差缩减算法依然成立。

Abstract: We establish complexity lower bounds for stochastic first-order algorithms in nonconvex--concave minimax optimization, allowing algorithms to use variance reduction. Our main contribution is a lower bound for a zero-respecting algorithm class that permits variance reduction, extending beyond the algorithmic restrictions imposed by some existing lower bounds. We consider objectives with an $L$-Lipschitz continuous joint gradient, a compact convex dual domain of Euclidean radius at most $D_Y$, and a primal value function, defined by maximizing the objective over the dual variable, with initial suboptimality at most $Δ$. The target accuracy $\varepsilon$ is measured by the gradient norm of the Moreau envelope of the constrained primal value function with parameter $1/(2L)$. Under an unbiased stochastic first-order oracle with variance at most $σ^2$ and mean-square smoothness, we prove the lower bound $Ω\!\left(L^2D_YΔ\varepsilon^{-3}+L^3D_Y^2Δσ^2\varepsilon^{-6}\right)$. This result quantifies the dependence on accuracy, dual-domain radius, and oracle noise even when variance reduction is allowed. We also establish complementary lower bounds for nonconvex--strongly-concave minimax optimization. With dual strong-concavity parameter $μ>0$ and condition number $κ:=L/μ$, we obtain $Ω\!\left(LΔ\sqrtκ\,\varepsilon^{-2}+LΔκσ^2\varepsilon^{-4}\right)$ under the bounded-variance oracle model. Under the additional mean-square smoothness condition with constant $\bar L$, we obtain $Ω\!\left(LΔ\sqrtκ\,\varepsilon^{-2}+Δ\bar Lσκ^{3/2}\varepsilon^{-3}\right)$. Together, these results identify complexity barriers across the concave and strongly concave regimes, with the main nonconvex--concave bound remaining valid for algorithms that use variance reduction.

</details>


### [201] [Routing in Line Networks with Handling Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01666)
*Gabriel Deza,Michal Tzur,Tal Raviv*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究超连通城市包裹网络中固定线路路由问题，提出RLN-HT模型，将内生的装卸停留时间纳入考虑，建立双线性广义容量流分配模型，并通过两种二阶锥松弛与残差前沿框架获得可行解与下界证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向当日达与时效敏感配送的超连通城市包裹网络，评估一组重复性枢纽间车辆服务时需要解决固定线路路由问题。车辆沿预定义的循环服务点序列（线路）运行，包裹可在不同线路间转运。转运虽扩展了路径选择，但需要装卸作业，导致车辆在服务点停留。由于处理量与路由决策相互依赖，停留时间具有内生性，进而影响线路周期时间、有效服务频率与转运等待时间，因此必须将内生停留时间纳入路由决策。

Method: 提出Routing in Line Networks with Handling Times（RLN-HT）问题，将其建模为双线性广义容量流分配模型。刻画最优停留时间及由此产生的频率作为装卸工作量的函数，从而得到仅以商品流量表达的重新表述。利用其凸子结构推导出两种二阶锥松弛，保持原可行域不变，从而给出可行解及有效下界。进一步提出残差前沿框架，刻画凸松弛目标与剩余非凸残差之间的权衡，形成构造下界证书的通用机制。

Result: 计算实验表明，忽略装卸时间会导致路由效果差甚至不可行；而考虑内生停留时间可将平均包裹运输时间最多改善26.5%。在节点数多达257、线路数达25的实例中，松弛模型求解迅速，能给出接近最优的路由并获得较小的最优性间隙；相比之下，现成的空间分支定界方法难以产生有用的界。

Conclusion: 将内生停留时间纳入固定线路网络路由对于时效敏感的城市包裹配送至关重要；所提出的双线性流量模型、二阶锥松弛及残差前沿下界证书机制，在大规模实例上能够快速求得近优路由并给出紧的下界，优于通用空间分支定界方法。

Abstract: Motivated by hyperconnected urban parcel networks for same-day and time-sensitive delivery, we study the fixed-line routing problem that arises when evaluating a candidate set of recurring inter-hub vehicle services. Vehicles follow predefined cyclic sequences of service points, or lines, and parcels may transfer between lines. Such transfers expand path choice but require loading and unloading operations that make vehicles dwell at service points. Because handled volumes are routing-dependent, dwell times become endogenous, linking routing decisions to line cycle times, effective service frequencies, and transfer waiting times. We introduce the Routing in Line Networks with Handling Times (RLN-HT) problem and formulate it as a bilinear generalized capacity-flow assignment model. We characterize optimal dwell times and the resulting frequencies as functions of handling workloads, enabling a reformulation solely in terms of commodity flows. We exploit its convex substructure to derive two second-order conic relaxations that preserve the original feasible region and therefore yield feasible incumbent solutions along with valid lower bounds. We also develop a residual-frontier framework that characterizes the tradeoff between the convex relaxation objective and the remaining nonconvex residual, yielding a general mechanism for constructing lower-bound certificates. Computational experiments show that ignoring handling times can yield poor or infeasible routing, whereas accounting for endogenous dwell times can improve average parcel travel times by up to 26.5%. In instances with up to 257 nodes and 25 lines, the relaxations solve quickly, yield near-optimal routing, and obtain small optimality gaps, whereas off-the-shelf spatial branch-and-bound struggles to produce useful bounds.

</details>


### [202] [Simplifying the computation of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives of convex functionals via $Γ$-convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01673)
*Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 在温和假设下，凸泛函的强-强二次上可微性等价于其共轭泛函的弱-⋆二次上可微性，相应次导数互为（相差标度的）共轭；该结果有助于计算弱-⋆二次次导数，并表明其在合适函数空间变换下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱-⋆二次次导数是无间隙二阶最优性条件的关键要素，但其计算通常困难。作者希望通过对偶关系来简化这类计算，并厘清其与函数空间选取的关系。

Method: 在若干温和假设下，建立凸泛函强-强二次上可微性与共轭泛函弱-⋆二次上可微性之间的等价性，并刻画相应次导数的共轭（相差标度）对应关系。

Result: 证明了上述二次上可微性的等价性，并给出次导数的共轭对应；据此可借助对偶方式计算弱-⋆二次次导数，同时证明弱-⋆二次次导数在合适的底层函数空间变换下具有不变性。

Conclusion: 该对偶等价框架为无间隙二阶最优性条件中弱-⋆二次次导数的计算提供了工具，并说明其结果不依赖于特定的函数空间设定。

Abstract: We prove (under some mild assumptions) that the strong-strong twice epidifferentiability of a convex functional is equivalent to the weak-$\star$ twice epidifferentiability of its conjugate functional. Further, the corresponding subderivatives are (up to scaling) conjugates of each other. This result can be used to facilitate the computation of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives, which are crucial ingredients in no-gap second-order optimality conditions. We also show that weak-$\star$ second subderivatives are invariant under suitable changes of the underlying function space setting.

</details>


### [203] [Recognizing Signomial Convexity is Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01812)
*Rui Zheng,Iosif Sakos,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明：判定符号多项式（signomial）在三种使优化可行的凸性形式（原变量凸、对数变换后凸、正符号多项式取对数后凸）中任一种是否成立，均为强NP难，无论在有界紧域还是全局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号多项式（实指数广义单项式的有限和）是自然的机器学习模型：既能简洁表达幂律、反比关系和乘法交互，又具备通用逼近能力和可解释参数；在AI Feynman基准的100个方程中有45个可表示为符号多项式。符号优化广泛用于工程设计、通信、经济学和机器学习，但一般情形计算不可行。凸性作为高效优化的黄金标准，为符号多项式提供三条可行路径，然而这些凸性形式是否可被有效识别此前并不清楚。

Method: 理论计算复杂性分析。以多项式凸性的gap-promise变体为起点，构造从多项式到符号多项式的归约，使得在相应对数变换下曲率间隙（curvature gap）得以保持，从而将三种凸性识别问题分别在紧域和全局情形下归约到已知的强NP难问题。

Result: 三种凸性形式（原变量凸、对数变换后凸、正符号多项式额外取对数后凸，即disciplined geometric programming所依据的结构）的识别问题均为强NP难，且在有界紧域与全局两种设定下都成立。

Conclusion: 识别符号多项式是否属于可利用凸优化求解的三种凸性形式在计算上不可行（强NP难），说明即便存在这些理论上的可行化路径，实际判定其适用性本身也是困难的；这为符号多项式建模与几何规划的计算边界提供了复杂性下界。

Abstract: Signomials---finite sums of generalized monomials with real exponents---are a natural machine learning model, combining parsimonious representations of power laws, inverse relationships, and multiplicative interactions with universal approximation and interpretable parameters. In fact, 45 of the 100 equations in the AI Feynman benchmark admit signomial representations. Moreover, signomial optimization is widely used in engineering design, communications, economics, and machine learning, but is computationally intractable in general. Convexity, the gold standard for efficient optimization, offers three routes to tractability for signomials: a signomial may be convex in its original variables, become convex after a logarithmic change of variables, or, when positive, become convex after additionally taking the logarithm of its value---the structure underlying disciplined geometric programming. We show that recognizing each form is strongly NP-hard, both on compact domains and globally. Our proofs start from gap-promise variants of polynomial convexity and develop polynomial-to-signomial reductions that preserve curvature gaps under the relevant logarithmic transformations.

</details>


### [204] [Optimal Stochastic Bilevel Optimization with First-Order Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01843)
*Linxuan Pan,Junchi Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出单环一阶方法MRT-FD求解非凸-强凸双层优化，利用p阶有限差分近似二阶导数作用，达到O(ε^{-4-2/p})查询复杂度并证明匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机一阶oracle下的非凸-强凸双层优化问题，旨在弥合该设置中随机一阶方法的上界与下界之间的复杂度差距。

Method: 提出MRT-FD：单环一阶方法，每轮同时更新上层变量、下层解及由超目标隐式微分产生的辅助响应；每次迭代对每个变量各更新一次；采用p阶有限差分近似二阶导数作用。

Result: 对任意固定有限光滑阶p≥1，MRT-FD以O(ε^{-4-2/p})次随机梯度查询找到ε-稳定点；并证明匹配的Ω(ε^{-4-2/p}) oracle下界。

Conclusion: 下界构造从带更强随机oracle的难解非凸最小化链出发，经与标量下层变量的正弦耦合提升为双层问题；因此对每个固定有限p，ε依赖达到最优，弥合了该随机一阶oracle设置下的上下复杂度差距。

Abstract: We study nonconvex--strongly-convex bilevel optimization under a stochastic first-order oracle. We introduce MRT-FD, a single-loop first-order method that simultaneously tracks the upper-level variable, the lower-level solution, and the auxiliary response arising from implicit differentiation of the hyperobjective. MRT-FD performs one update of each variable per iteration and approximates the second-order derivative actions using order-$p$ finite differences. For any fixed finite smoothness order $p\ge1$ in the lower-level variable, MRT-FD finds an $\varepsilon$-stationary point using $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4-2/p})$ stochastic gradient queries. We also prove a matching $Ω(\varepsilon^{-4-2/p})$ oracle lower bound. The lower-bound construction starts from a hard nonconvex minimization chain with a stronger stochastic oracle, and lifts it to a bilevel problem through a sinusoidal coupling with a scalar lower-level variable. Consequently, the dependence on $\varepsilon$ is optimal for every fixed finite $p$, closing the upper--lower complexity gap in this stochastic first-order oracle setting.

</details>


### [205] [Randomized Matvec Lower Bounds for Simplex-Based Matrix Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02095)
*Wendao Wu,Cong Fang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了球-单纯形与单纯形-单纯形两类规范化矩阵博弈在随机化矩阵-向量查询模型下的下界，分别为 Ω(ε^{-2/3}/(log²(1/ε)loglog(1/ε))) 与 Ω(ε^{-2/3}/(log^{7/3}(1/ε)loglog(1/ε)))，与确定性上界在忽略对数因子意义下匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵博弈求解算法的查询复杂度已有确定性上界（Karmarkar、O'Carroll 与 Sidford），但其随机化查询下界尚不明确。本文旨在建立随机化矩阵-向量查询模型下的紧致下界，以刻画两类规范化矩阵博弈几何求解的内在困难度，并解释其上界是否最优。

Method: 采用对抗性硬实例构造与归约证明：在自适应双边查询后提取一个新的高斯核心，利用其最小奇异值的不确定性建立线性系统求解困难性；再通过两种归约将线性系统求解困难性传递到矩阵博弈，方法是将小的满鞍点间隙转化为小的残差，对单纯形-单纯形博弈额外引入对数级归一化损失。

Result: 对足够小的 ε，球-单纯形博弈的最坏情形查询复杂度为 Ω(ε^{-2/3}/(log²(1/ε)loglog(1/ε)))，单纯形-单纯形博弈为 Ω(ε^{-2/3}/(log^{7/3}(1/ε)loglog(1/ε)))。困难实例维度分别约为 ε^{-2/3} 与 ε^{-2/3}/log^{1/3}(1/ε)，且下界可推广到更大维度。这些下界与 Karmarkar、O'Carroll 和 Sidford 的确定性上界在忽略对数因子意义下匹配。

Conclusion: 随机化矩阵-向量查询下界与已知确定性上界在忽略对数因子意义下匹配，表明所研究的确定性算法在查询复杂度上已接近最优；同时建立了从线性系统求解困难性到矩阵博弈求解困难性的归约框架。

Abstract: We prove randomized matrix-vector query lower bounds for two normalized matrix-game geometries: a Euclidean unit ball against a probability simplex, with row norms at most one, and two probability simplices, with entries of absolute value at most one. Each query returns $(Ax,A^\top y)$ for arbitrary real vectors. The algorithm must return a feasible pair with full saddle-point gap at most $\varepsilon$, with probability at least $2/3$ on every admissible matrix. For sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, the worst-case query complexities are $Ω(\varepsilon^{-2/3}/(\log^2(1/\varepsilon)\log\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ for ball-simplex games and $Ω(\varepsilon^{-2/3}/(\log^{7/3}(1/\varepsilon)\log\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ for simplex-simplex games. The hard instances have dimensions of order $\varepsilon^{-2/3}$ and $\varepsilon^{-2/3}/\log^{1/3}(1/\varepsilon)$, respectively, and the bounds extend to larger dimensions. These lower bounds match the deterministic upper bounds of Karmarkar, O'Carroll, and Sidford up to logarithmic factors. The proof extracts a fresh Gaussian core after adaptive two-sided queries and uses uncertainty in its smallest singular value to establish linear-system solve hardness. Two reductions transfer this hardness to matrix games by converting a small full gap into a small residual, with an additional logarithmic normalization loss only for simplex-simplex games.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Self-Orthogonal $τ$-Tilting Modules and Tilting Modules III: Finitistic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00105)
*Xiaojin Zhang,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究τ-倾斜模与经典1-倾斜模的关系，证明C^op的有限小有限维数结合T的有限自扩张消没范围迫使T成为经典1-倾斜模；并建立自正交τ-倾斜猜想与有限维数猜想之间的联系，给出radical cube zero Artin代数上的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: τ-倾斜模是倾斜理论的重要推广，自正交τ-倾斜猜想是表示论中的核心问题之一。本文旨在揭示τ-倾斜模何时退化为经典1-倾斜模，并探讨该猜想与有限维数猜想之间的深层联系。

Method: 设A为Artin代数，T为τ-倾斜右A-模，令C=A/Ann_A(T)。通过分析C^op的有限小有限维数（little finitistic dimension）与T的自扩张消没范围的有限性条件，结合同调代数与倾斜理论工具进行论证。

Result: 证明了若C^op具有有限小有限维数且T的自扩张消没范围有限，则T必为经典1-倾斜模；若有限维数猜想对所有Artin代数成立，则自正交τ-倾斜猜想对所有Artin代数成立；自正交τ-倾斜猜想的反例将给出有限维数猜想的反例；作为应用，radical cube zero的Artin代数满足自正交τ-倾斜猜想。

Conclusion: 本文建立了τ-倾斜模、经典倾斜模与有限维数猜想之间的桥梁，表明自正交τ-倾斜猜想在一定意义上弱于有限维数猜想，并在radical cube zero情形下得到验证。

Abstract: Let $A$ be an Artin algebra and let $T$ be a $τ$-tilting right $A$-module. Put $C=A/{\rm Ann}_A(T)$. We prove that finite little finitistic dimension of $C^{\rm op}$, together with a finite range of self-extension vanishing for $T$, forces $T$ to be a classical $1$-tilting module. We also show that if the finitistic conjecture holds for all Artin algebras then the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for all Artin algebras. Moreover, a counterexample to the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture will give a counterexample to the finitistic dimension conjecture. As an application, the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for Artin algebras with radical cube zero.

</details>


### [207] [A characterization of triangulated structures induced by premutation triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00135)
*Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明条件(MT4)是Iitsuka预三角结构成为三角结构的充要条件，并证明其在同心孪生余挠对情形下与Nakaoka条件等价，同时构造反例否定Iitsuka的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Iitsuka引入premutation triple作为多种mutation构造的统一框架，并证明商范畴Z/[I]具有典范预三角结构，但该结构何时成为三角结构尚不清楚。本文旨在给出其精确刻画，并解决Iitsuka提出的相关问题。

Method: 在extriangulated范畴中研究premutation triple (S,Z,V)，通过分析兼容性条件(MT4)与三角结构的关系，并比较同心孪生余挠对中Nakaoka所用条件；最后通过具体构造给出反例。

Result: 证明(MT4)既是充分的也是必要的，即它精确刻画了Iitsuka典范预三角结构成为三角结构的条件；对同心孪生余挠对，Nakaoka的兼容性条件与(MT4)等价；构造出一个可约的triple，它不是mutation triple。

Conclusion: (MT4)是三角结构成立的充要条件；Nakaoka条件在同心孪生余挠对情形下与(MT4)一致；存在可约triple非mutation triple，从而否定了Iitsuka的问题。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{V})$ be a premutation triple in an extriangulated category. Set $\mathcal{I}=\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{Z}=\mathcal{Z}\cap\mathcal{V}$. Iitsuka introduced premutation triples as a unified framework for various mutation constructions and showed that the quotient category $\mathcal{Z}/[\mathcal{I}]$ admits a canonical pretriangulated structure. In this paper, we prove that the compatibility condition $(\mathrm{MT4})$ is not only sufficient but also necessary for this structure to be triangulated. Therefore, $(\mathrm{MT4})$ precisely characterizes when Iitsuka's canonical pretriangulated structure becomes triangulated. Furthermore, we prove that, for concentric twin cotorsion pairs, the compatibility condition used by Nakaoka in the construction of triangulated quotients is equivalent to $(\mathrm{MT4})$. Finally, we construct an explicit reducible triple which is not a mutation triple. This gives a negative answer to a question posed by Iitsuka.

</details>


### [208] [Explicit Transgression Primitives on Flag Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00300)
*Greg Weiler*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了相对超渡原语 α_j，给出 Borel 定理中特征形式消失的显式闭公式，并揭示其从曲率自由到扭结项链结构的转变，同时用 Koszul DGA 消解恢复旗流形上同调与 Poincaré 多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Borel 定理以非构造方式证明特征形式 p_j(F_t)=tr(F_t^j) 的消失，且从 Weil 代数 W(k) 投影得到的闭本原形式无法下降到 K/T。本文旨在构造可下降的相对超渡原语，给出消失性的显式实现。

Method: 在 Λ^{2j-1}m^∨)^T 上构造相对超渡原语 α_j，满足 d_m α_j = -p_j(F_t)；引入连接 θ_s = θ_t + s θ_m，使曲率分解为 F_s=(1-s)(F_t - s θ_m²)；通过非交换字和与 Euler Beta 积分展开，导出对所有 j≥1 的通用闭公式；并结合 A_2 根格上 T-不变上链与三角差 Ω_- - Ω_+ 的几何解释，以及极小 Koszul DGA 消解 K_•(p_2,…,p_k) 处理 coinvariant 代数。

Result: 原语呈现结构转变：α_2=-1/3 tr(θ_m³) 曲率自由，α_3 关于 F_t 线性，而在 j=4 时 [F_t,θ_m²]≠0 破坏循环迹对称性，产生不可约不变量 tr(θ_m∧F_t∧θ_m²∧F_t)。在 A_2 (sl_3(C)) 上 T-不变上链对应闭圈，d_m 作为边碎裂算子见证 c_2(E) 的正合性。Koszul DGA 消解恢复 Poincaré 多项式 P_{K/T}(t)=∏_{j=2}^k (1-t^{2j})/(1-t²) 与 Euler 特征 χ(K/T)=k!，且无矩阵秩歧义。

Conclusion: 相对超渡原语为 Borel 定理提供了构造性证明与显式公式，揭示了特征形式消失的层级结构；Koszul DGA 消解方法避免了特征理论在 k=4 时的重数障碍，直接计算旗流形上同调不变量。

Abstract: Borel's theorem presents the flag manifold cohomology as the coinvariant algebra $H^*(K/T; \mathbb{C}) \cong S(\mathfrak{h}^\vee)/I_W$, establishing the vanishing of characteristic forms $p_j(F_\mathfrak{t}) = \mathrm{tr}(F_\mathfrak{t}^j)$ non-constructively; projection from the Weil algebra $W(\mathfrak{k})$ yields closed primitives on $K$ failing to descend.
  We construct relative transgression primitives $α_j \in (Λ^{2j-1}\mathfrak{m}^\vee)^T \cong {}^KΩ^{2j-1}(K/T)$ satisfying $d_\mathfrak{m} α_j = -p_j(F_\mathfrak{t})$ on $K/T$. Via connections $θ_s = θ_\mathfrak{t} + sθ_\mathfrak{m}$, the curvature factors as $F_s = (1-s)(F_\mathfrak{t} - sθ_\mathfrak{m}^2)$; expanding via non-commutative word sums and Euler's Beta integral yields a universal closed formula for all $j \ge 1$.
  These primitives exhibit a structural transition: $α_2 = -\frac{1}{3}\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m}^3)$ is curvature-free, $α_3$ is linear in $F_\mathfrak{t}$, while at $j = 4$, $[F_\mathfrak{t}, θ_\mathfrak{m}^2] \neq 0$ shatters cyclic trace symmetry into independent necklaces, producing the irreducible invariant $\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m} \wedge F_\mathfrak{t} \wedge θ_\mathfrak{m}^2 \wedge F_\mathfrak{t})$.
  On the $A_2$ root lattice ($\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$), $T$-invariant cochains correspond to closed loops, $α_2 = Ω_- - Ω_+$ is the oriented difference of triangles, and $d_\mathfrak{m}$ acts as an edge-fracturing operator witnessing the exactness of $c_2(E)$. While character theory encounters a multiplicity barrier at $k=4$ in Specht decompositions, a minimal Koszul DGA resolution $K_\bullet(p_2, \dots, p_k)$ resolves the coinvariant algebra, recovering the Poincaré polynomial $P_{K/T}(t) = \prod_{j=2}^k \frac{1-t^{2j}}{1-t^2}$ and Euler characteristic $χ(K/T) = k!$ without matrix-rank ambiguities.

</details>


### [209] [Magnitude of module categories and special biserial algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00413)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Børve–Horiatakis–Kalck关于表示有限代数模范畴magnitude的猜想：magnitude ≥ 单模个数，且等号成立当且仅当代数特殊双列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Børve、Horiatakis和Kalck将表示有限代数模范畴的magnitude定义为不可分解模之间Hom维数矩阵之逆的元素之和，并证明它可由Auslander–Reiten箭图的顶点、箭头与网格交替计数得到Euler特征。他们猜想该magnitude至少等于单模数目，且等号仅在特殊双列代数时成立，本文旨在证明该猜想。

Method: 在代数闭域上证明该猜想。对于表示有向代数，建立模范畴、不含给定单合成因子的子范畴以及其理想商的magnitude之间的关系；利用Ringel和Vossieck的hammock理论，将该商范畴等价于有限偏序集表示范畴并计算其magnitude；一般情形通过将标准型分次化所得代数化归到表示有向情形。

Result: 证明了该猜想：表示有限代数的模范畴magnitude至少为单模个数，且等号恰好刻画特殊双列代数。同时对表示有向代数给出了模范畴、去除某单合成因子子范畴与理想商三者magnitude之间的关系，并计算了由hammock理论得到的有限偏序集表示范畴的magnitude。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Børve–Horiatakis–Kalck的猜想，并通过hammock理论与分次化技术将一般情形归约到表示有向代数，为模范畴magnitude与表示论结构（如特殊双列代数）之间的联系提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Børve, Horiatakis, and Kalck defined the magnitude of the module category of a representation-finite algebra as the sum of the entries of the inverse of the matrix of Hom dimensions between indecomposable modules. They showed it is an Euler characteristic, the alternating count of vertices, arrows, and meshes of the Auslander--Reiten quiver, and conjectured that it is at least the number of simple modules, with equality precisely for special biserial algebras. We prove this conjecture over an algebraically closed field. For a representation-directed algebra, we relate the magnitudes of the module category, the subcategory of modules without a given simple composition factor, and the ideal quotient by it. By the theory of hammocks of Ringel and Vossieck, this quotient is equivalent to a category of representations of a finite poset, whose magnitude we compute. The general case reduces to the representation-directed case through algebras obtained by grading a standard form.

</details>


### [210] [Big Finitistic Dimensions of Radical-Cube-Zero Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00433)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 构造了一个十维代数A，满足(rad A)^3=0、findim A=2、Findim A=∞、Findim(A^op)=0，从而反驳大finitistic维数猜想；并对相关模类、维数向量、Artin代数递归判据及二顶点情形进行了分类与刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大finitistic维数猜想长期未决，本文旨在通过构造反例来否定该猜想，并系统刻画此类反例代数的模论性质。

Method: 在任意域上构造十维代数A，使(rad A)^3=0且findim A=2、Findim A=∞、Findim(A^op)=0；采用显式逆syzygy构造产生各正有限投射维数的可数生成右模；通过识别投射覆盖核、用非零Ext检测分解长度；用可逆线性算子分类嵌入投射模根中且投射维数≤1的模；确定投射维数2的有限维模维数向量；对Artin代数给出递归充分判据；处理有限维初等radical-cube-zero代数的二顶点情形。

Result: 成功构造反例推翻大finitistic维数猜想；分类了嵌入投射模根中、投射维数≤1的模；确定了投射维数2模的所有维数向量并分类达到尖锐维数界者（含所有十四维模）；对两类特殊代数证明了大finitistic维数的有限性；通过精确表示嵌入证明投射维数2类的wild性，并在任意域上得到该类的超可分解纯内射模。

Conclusion: 该十维代数反例否定大finitistic维数猜想，并揭示了小finitistic维数与大finitistic维数可显著分离；所建立的分类、递归判据与表示嵌入结果深化了对投射维数受控模类的理解。

Abstract: We construct, over any field, a ten-dimensional algebra $A$ with $(\operatorname{rad}A)^3=0$ such that $\operatorname{findim}A=2$, $\operatorname{Findim}A=\infty$, and $\operatorname{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})=0$. An explicit inverse-syzygy construction gives countably generated right modules of every positive finite projective dimension, disproving the big finitistic dimension conjecture. At each step we identify the full projective-cover kernel, and nonzero Ext detects the exact resolution length. For this algebra, we classify the modules of projective dimension at most one that embed in radicals of projective modules, with no generation restriction, in terms of invertible linear operators. We determine all dimension vectors of finite-dimensional modules of projective dimension two and classify those attaining the sharp dimension bounds, including all fourteen-dimensional modules of projective dimension two. For arbitrary Artin algebras, we give recursive sufficient criteria that control finite projective resolutions through radical-annihilated ideals and suitable corners. For finite-dimensional elementary radical-cube-zero algebras, we prove finiteness of the big finitistic dimension in the two-vertex case with one loop at each vertex and obtain multiplication-map criteria for bounds of one or two. Finally, an exact representation embedding yields wildness of the projective-dimension-two class and, over every field, a superdecomposable pure-injective module in that class.

</details>


### [211] [Endomorphism algebras of Gorenstein-projective modules over string algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00937)
*Yu-Zhe Liu,Zheng Xin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文研究弦代数的Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对，给出极大好集合的刻画与有限相容图，并在G条件下构造tiled-surface模型并刻画表示有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦代数的Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对结构复杂，缺乏组合化刻画与统一的自同态代数模型，也缺少通过自同态代数判定表示有限性的判据。

Method: 将Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对等同于一类特殊的极大好集合，建立有限相容图；在G条件下利用带标号的tiled-surface模型描述End_A(T)，并比较表示有限性。

Result: 建立了支撑τ-倾斜对与极大好集合的对应及有限相容图；对任意非零基本Gorenstein-投射模T，在G条件下构造了End_A(T)的显式带标号tiled-surface模型；证明A表示有限当且仅当所有此类End_A(T)表示有限。

Conclusion: 该工作用组合与曲面模型统一刻画了弦代数上Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对及其自同态代数，并给出了表示有限性的等价判据。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a string algebra. We identify its Gorenstein-projective support $τ$-tilting pairs with a distinguished class of maximal good collections and give a finite compatibility graph for them. Under the G-condition, we construct an explicit labelled tiled-surface model for $\mathrm{End}_A(T)$ for every nonzero basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$. Under the same condition, we show that $A$ is representation-finite if and only if $\End_A(T)$ is representation-finite for every basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$.

</details>


### [212] [Homology and higher-order intermediate categories for proper abelian subcategories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01155)
*Zheng-Gang Wu,Xianhui Fu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在不依赖t-结构的前提下，于具有ℓ+1层扩张闭真阿贝尔子范畴的有限扩张窗口上建立自然的长正合同调序列，在负自扩张（1至ℓ+1次）消失的条件下证明该界是统一尖锐的，并在Krull-Schmidt情形下用相容序列分类保持纯同调层的中间子范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同调长正合序列通常依赖t-结构（或心）来构造。本文的动机在于：能否去掉t-结构这一前提，仅用扩张闭的真阿贝尔子范畴构成的有限扩张窗口，就得到自然的同调长正合序列，并刻画其成立的同调维数界，从而推广或弱化经典框架的假设。

Method: 作者在有限扩张窗口（含ℓ+1层扩张闭真阿贝尔子范畴）上直接构造同调长正合序列，而非借助t-结构；通过假设1至ℓ+1次的负自扩张消失来保证序列的连续性与自然性；进一步证明该ℓ+1层界是统一尖锐的（即不可改进）；最后在Krull-Schmidt（具局部自同态环）范畴中，借助相容序列对保持纯同调层的中间子范畴进行分类。

Result: 在给定消失条件下，成功建立了自然的同调长正合序列；证明所依赖的ℓ+1层界是最优且统一尖锐的；并在Krull-Schmidt情形下完成了对保持纯同调层的中间子范畴的分类（对应相容序列）。

Conclusion: 同调长正合序列的构造可以独立于t-结构，仅需扩张闭真阿贝尔子范畴的有限分层与适当的负自扩张消失条件；且所得维数界不可改进；这为分类中间子范畴提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Without needing a $t$-structure, we establish natural long exact homology sequences on finite extension windows with $\ell+1$ layers of extension-closed proper abelian subcategories, assuming vanishing of negative self-extensions in degrees $1,\ldots,\ell+1$, and prove uniform sharpness of this bound. In the Krull-Schmidt setting, we classify intermediate subcategories stable under taking pure homology layers by compatible sequences.

</details>


### [213] [Central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01159)
*Taeyeoup Kang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究局部域与数域上 Galois gerbe 的中心代数上同调，系数取连通约化群与代数中心子群；对具有来自 Kaletha 刚性 gerbe 比较态射的 gerbe 给出阿贝尔化上同调的纤维积公式，并在数域上通过笛卡儿方块由阿贝尔化与实局部化恢复完整上同调集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望系统理解 Galois gerbe 的中心代数上同调，特别是系数取一般代数中心子群（未必有限或连通）时的结构。已有工作多聚焦于特定 gerbe 或有限中心，缺乏适用于 Kaletha 刚性 gerbe 比较框架及 Kottwitz 局部与整体 gerbe 的统一描述。

Method: 通过引入 Kaletha 刚性 gerbe 到目标 gerbe 的比较态射，利用带的有限型条件建立比较存在性；借助代数基本群刻画有限中心商，并将上同调表示为“有限中心商的代数基本群”与“带中心子群的同态”的纤维积。在数域情形构造笛卡儿方块，联系阿贝尔化上同调与实局部化。

Result: 对容许比较态射的 gerbe，得到阿贝尔化上同调的纤维积公式；当带为有限型时比较态射存在。数域上无需比较假设，完整上同调集可由阿贝尔化与实局部化经笛卡儿方块恢复；在非阿基米德局部域与全虚数域上，阿贝尔化映射为双射。公式可特化到 Kottwitz 局部与整体 gerbe。

Conclusion: 文章统一刻画了 Galois gerbe 中心上同调的阿贝尔化结构，揭示其由有限中心商的代数基本群与带同态决定；数域情形下整体上同调由阿贝尔化与实局部化控制，并在非阿基米德及全虚数情形退化为双射，为 Kottwitz gerbe 等具体对象提供显式公式。

Abstract: We study central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes over local fields of characteristic zero and number fields, with coefficients in a connected reductive group and an algebraic central subgroup, not necessarily finite or connected. For gerbes admitting a comparison morphism from Kaletha's rigid gerbe, we obtain fiber product formulas for abelianized cohomology in terms of algebraic fundamental groups of finite central quotients and homomorphisms from the band to the central subgroup. Such comparisons exist whenever the band is of finite type. Over number fields, independently of the comparison hypothesis, the full cohomology set is recovered from its abelianization and its real localizations through a cartesian square. Over non-Archimedean local fields and totally imaginary number fields, abelianization is bijective. We also specialize the formulas to Kottwitz's local and global gerbes.

</details>


### [214] [Rotation fixed points of undecorated braid varieties and fusion rings of affine Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02034)
*Yuma Mizuno*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了braid variety上由词循环旋转诱导的Zamolodchikov变换（有限阶自等价）的不动点栈的粗模空间，同构于相应仿射Lie代数的fusion ring的谱，并推广到twisted Coxeter元素的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: braid variety与Coxeter元素、仿射Lie代数之间存在深刻的联系，而Zamolodchikov变换作为词循环旋转诱导的有限阶自等价，其不动点结构尚未被完全理解；作者希望建立该不动点栈与fusion ring之间的几何—代数对应。

Method: 设G为单连通半单代数群，考虑与Coxeter元素某次幂相联系的（未装饰）braid variety，研究其上由词循环旋转诱导的有限阶自等价（Zamolodchikov变换），并计算其不动点栈的粗模空间，进一步将构造推广到twisted Coxeter元素。

Result: Zamolodchikov变换的不动点栈的粗模空间同构于相应仿射Lie代数fusion ring的谱；该结果对twisted Coxeter元素亦成立，并且所有仿射Lie代数的fusion ring都可由此方式得到。

Conclusion: braid variety的Zamolodchikov变换不动点结构与仿射Lie代数fusion ring之间存在同构对应，且该对应具有一般性，可覆盖全部仿射Lie代数的fusion ring。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simply connected semisimple algebraic group. On the (undecorated) braid variety associated with a power of a Coxeter element, there is a finite-order autoequivalence, called the Zamolodchikov transformation, induced by cyclic rotation of words. In this paper, we show that the coarse moduli space of the fixed-point stack of this autoequivalence is isomorphic to the spectrum of the fusion ring of the corresponding affine Lie algebra. The above results also extend to twisted Coxeter elements, and the fusion rings of all affine Lie algebras are obtained in this way.

</details>


### [215] [Harmonic Norms on Quiver Representations over Non-Archimedean Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01694)
*Oren Ben-Bassat*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明任意完备非阿基米德域上有限箭图的每个多稳定表示都容许HKKP意义下的分裂调和范数，从而回答其预印本中的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Haiden–Katzarkov–Kontsevich–Pandit在《Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry》中提出的问题：多稳定表示是否总存在HKKP意义下的分裂调和范数；并摆脱此前对值群或箭图的限制。

Method: 利用Moret-Bailly闭像定理给出与失稳子对象表示的一致分离；对范数权重的有限估计得到稳定表示的线性矫顽性界，从而在全体范数的完备CAT(0)空间上得到扩展HKKP势的最小值；通过一阶变分计算将分裂极小元等同于HKKP调和范数；用严格支撑铰链把分裂达到性化归为带任意实半径标签的箭图半稳定约化；对提升分裂作小扰动使每个反向扩张非分裂，并用有限斜率—秩下降证明终止。

Result: 完备范数空间中的每个极小值都由一个分裂范数达到，且具有相同的箭头奇异剖面；该结论不依赖值群或箭图的限制；非分裂极小元在一、两种半径修改下说明分裂达到性；双参数Berkovich解析族区分了给定分裂范数的多稳定性、半稳定性与调和性。

Conclusion: 在任意完备非阿基米德域上，有限箭图的每个多稳定表示均存在分裂HKKP调和范数，完全回答了HKKP的问题，并给出了分裂达到性与调和性的精细刻画。

Abstract: Let $K$ be any complete non-Archimedean field, not necessarily spherically complete. We prove that every polystable representation of a finite quiver over $K$ admits a split harmonic norm in the sense of Haiden--Katzarkov--Kontsevich--Pandit (HKKP), answering a question from their preprint \emph{Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry}. Moret-Bailly's closed-image theorem gives a uniform separation from representations with destabilizing subobjects. A finite estimate on norm weights then yields a linear coercivity bound for stable representations and hence a minimum of the extended HKKP potential on the complete CAT(0) space of all norms. A direct first-variation computation identifies split minimizers with HKKP harmonic norms. Strict supporting hinges reduce split attainment to semistable reduction for quivers with arbitrary real radius labels. Small changes of the lifted splitting make each reversed extension nonsplit; a finite slope--rank descent proves termination. Consequently, every minimum in the completed norm space is attained by a split norm with the same arrow singular profiles, without restrictions on the value group or the quiver. Nonsplit minimizers illustrate split attainment in one and two radius modifications, and a two-parameter Berkovich analytic family separates polystability, semistability, and harmonicity of a chosen split norm.

</details>

### [216] [Scaling Collider Event Generation with Residual-Quantized Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00569)
*Dan Godi,Dmitrii Kobylianskii,Eilam Gross*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作将高能对撞机全事件数据通过残差量化离散化，训练粒子级自回归生成模型，实现基于探测器稳定粒子的条件生成，并研究其缩放规律与损失-物理保真度关系，为可扩展的全事件生成提供经验框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）下，全探测器模拟与重建预计将成为主要计算瓶颈，亟需快速、基于机器学习的代理模型。同时，大语言模型推动的离散生成建模（token化数据上的自回归Transformer）已成为生成任务的最先进方法，因此作者希望将这一范式扩展到对撞机全事件生成。

Method: 提出一种粒子级生成模型，在经残差量化（residual quantization）处理的全事件数据上进行训练，采用自回归Transformer的离散建模范式；模型支持以探测器稳定粒子为条件进行条件生成，并系统性研究数据集规模与模型规模的缩放行为、重复数据暴露（repeated data exposure）的影响。

Result: 模型家族具备从探测器稳定粒子进行条件生成的能力；揭示了跨数据集与模型规模的可扩展性规律，刻画了重复数据暴露带来的效应，并表明token级损失能够系统性地预测下游物理保真度。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于残差量化表示的可扩展对撞机全事件生成提供了实证框架，验证了离散生成范式在粒子物理模拟代理中的有效性。

Abstract: Full detector simulation and reconstruction of collider events are projected to become major bottlenecks at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, motivating the development of fast, ML-based surrogates. At the same time, LLMs have driven fast progress in generative discrete modeling: autoregressive transformers trained on tokenized data now represent the state of the art across a range of generative tasks. We extend the discrete modeling paradigm by introducing a particle-level generative model trained on residual-quantized full-event data. We demonstrate the ability of this model family to perform conditional generation from detector-stable particles; we study its scaling behavior across a range of dataset and model sizes, characterize the effects of repeated data exposure and demonstrate that token-level loss systematically predicts downstream physical fidelity. These results provide an empirical framework for scalable collider full-event generation based on residual-quantized representations.

</details>


### [217] [Search for dark matter produced in association with a low-multiplicity jet in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00995)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS利用13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据（138 fb⁻¹）首次在LHC采用低组分喷注特征搜寻暗物质，未观测到超出标准模型本底的显著信号，并对矢量与轴矢中介子简化模型给出95% CL截面上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗物质搜寻多依赖单喷注或大组分喷注等特征，在低组分喷注末态中信号可能被本底淹没。本文动机在于引入低组分喷注这一新特征以提升对该类末态的探测灵敏度，并首次在LHC开展此类搜寻。

Method: 使用CMS实验在2016–2018年采集的13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据，积分亮度138 fb⁻¹；选取大缺失横向动量伴随单个高能低组分喷注的事件；采用监督机器学习方法在该复杂末态中最大化对潜在信号的灵敏度；在含矢量与轴矢中介子的简化模型框架下，基于观测结果设置95% CL的暗物质产生截面上限。

Result: 未观测到事件数相对于标准模型本底预测的过剩。在简化模型下设置95% CL截面上限：当暗物质粒子质量约为150 GeV时，中介子质量上限被排除至4250 GeV；当暗物质粒子质量约为550 GeV时，中介子质量上限被排除至3500 GeV。

Conclusion: 该分析是LHC首个采用低组分喷注特征的暗物质搜寻，验证了机器学习在该末态中的有效性，并在矢量与轴矢中介子简化模型下给出目前较强的中介子质量排除界限。

Abstract: A search for dark matter particles is presented using events with large missing transverse momentum and a single energetic jet containing few constituents. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data recorded by the CMS experiment at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV. This corresponds to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, recorded during the 2016$-$2018 LHC data-taking period. This is the first search at the LHC to employ the low-multiplicity jet signature. Supervised machine learning is used to maximize sensitivity to potential signals in this challenging final state. No excess of events over the standard model background prediction is observed. The results are used to set upper limits on the dark matter production cross section at 95% confidence level (CL) within simplified models featuring vector and axial-vector mediators. For a dark matter particle mass of approximately 150 (550) GeV, mediator masses up to 4250 (3500) GeV are excluded at 95% CL.

</details>


### [218] [Full Event Interpretation at Belle II: Current Status and Recent Developments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01189)
*R. Tiwary,M. Marfoli,V. Vobbilisetti,K. Trabelsi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍Belle II实验分析框架中的全事件解释(FEI)算法:其分层重建强子与半轻子末态、基于梯度提升决策树的多级多变量训练、近期算法与工作流改进,以及效率标定与性能评估新技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FEI是Belle II分析框架的核心组件,用于高效重建排他性B介子衰变。对于涉及缺失能量的信号端衰变研究尤为重要:通过FEI重建其中一个B介子,可对另一信号端B介子的运动学施加强约束,从而提升本底抑制能力。因此有必要系统阐述其设计、实现与最新进展。

Method: FEI对强子与半轻子末态进行分层重建,采用多变量分类技术标记电子-正电子碰撞中产生的两个B介子之一。文中给出重建策略、候选事例筛选,以及基于梯度提升决策树的多阶段多变量训练流程。

Result: 近期改进包括:训练所用模拟数据的改进、中间粒子的重建、多变量输入的鲁棒性提升。此外还发展了FEI标定与性能评估新技术:精细化的效率标定流程、跨衰变通道与取数条件的性能检验,以及应用重加权技术诊断并缓解模拟与数据之间的差异。

Conclusion: 该贡献总结了FEI的设计、实现与最新发展,并简要讨论了旨在进一步提升重建性能与计算效率的进行中及未来工作。

Abstract: The Full Event Interpretation (FEI) algorithm is a central component of the Belle II analysis framework, designed for the efficient reconstruction of exclusive B-meson decays. The FEI performs a hierarchical reconstruction of hadronic and semileptonic final states, using multivariate classification techniques to tag one of the two B mesons produced in electron-positron collisions. The FEI is particularly important for studies of signal-side decays involving missing energy. By reconstructing one B meson with the FEI, the kinematics of the other, signal-side B meson are tightly constrained, leading to improved background rejection. In this contribution, we present the design, implementation, and recent developments of the FEI. We outline the reconstruction strategy, candidate selection, and multistage multivariate training based on gradient boosted decision trees. Recent improvements to the FEI workflow, including improvements in the simulated data used for training, reconstruction of intermediate particles, and improved robustness of the multivariate inputs, are discussed in detail. Beyond algorithmic updates, we also discuss newly developed techniques for the calibration and performance evaluation of the FEI. These include refined procedures to calibrate the efficiency, check the performance across decay channels and data-taking conditions, and application of reweighting techniques to diagnose and mitigate discrepancies between simulation and data. Finally, we briefly discuss ongoing and future developments aimed at further improving reconstruction performance and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [219] [Search for charged-lepton-flavour violating $eμtq$ interactions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01308)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用ATLAS探测器140 fb$^{-1}$的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，在顶夸克产生与衰变中寻找带电轻子味破坏，未观察到显著偏离标准模型的迹象，并在有效场论框架下给出Wilson系数与衰变分支比上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型在树图阶严格守恒轻子味，但中微子振荡证实轻子味守恒并非基本对称性。带电轻子味破坏（cLFV）在标准模型中的发生需通过极小的高阶过程，因而其可观测信号将是新物理的直接证据。顶夸克作为质量最大的粒子，其与重味夸克及轻子的耦合对新物理敏感，因此在顶夸克产生与衰变中搜索cLFV具有独特意义。

Method: 基于ATLAS探测器在质心能量13 TeV下记录的140 fb$^{-1}$质子-质子对撞数据，研究两种cLFV机制：一是单顶夸克（轻子衰变）与一对反号异味轻子的联合产生；二是顶夸克对产生，其中一个顶夸克衰变为反号异味轻子对与$u$或$c$夸克，另一个按标准模型衰变为轻子性$W$玻色子与$b$夸克。信号过程含三个带电轻子（电子或μ子）。通过统计分析与标准模型预期比较，并采用有效场论（EFT）框架对结果进行诠释。

Result: 未观察到与标准模型预期间的显著偏离。在95%置信水平下对六个Wilson系数给出上限，范围从张量$u$夸克耦合的$|C^{\mathrm{tensor}}_{e\mu tu}|/\Lambda^2 < 0.018$ TeV$^{-2}$到标量$c$夸克耦合的$|C^{\mathrm{scalar}}_{e\mu tc}|/\Lambda^2 < 0.32$ TeV$^{-2}$。将Wilson系数上限转换为cLFV顶夸克衰变分支比的95%置信水平上限，范围从标量$u$夸克耦合的$B(t \to e\mu u) < 6 \cdot 10^{-9}$到张量$c$夸克耦合的$B(t \to e\mu c) < 2.7 \cdot 10^{-7}$。

Conclusion: 在顶夸克产生与衰变中未发现cLFV信号，结果为相关有效场论算符及顶夸克cLFV衰变分支比设定了严格上限，进一步约束了新物理参数空间。

Abstract: A search for charged-lepton-flavour violation (cLFV) in top-quark production and decay is presented. The data analysed correspond to 140 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collisions recorded at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}= 13$ TeV by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider. The possible cLFV contribution is studied via two mechanisms. The first is via the production of a leptonically-decaying single-top quark in association with a pair of oppositely-charged different-flavour leptons. The second is the production of top-quark pairs, where one top quark decays into a pair of oppositely-charged different-flavour leptons and a $u$- or $c$-quark, while the other decays into a leptonically-decaying $W$ boson and a $b$-quark, according to the Standard Model. The signal processes contain three charged leptons, taken to be electrons or muons. No significant deviation from the Standard Model prediction is observed, and these results are interpreted within an Effective Field Theory framework. Upper limits are placed at 95% confidence level on six Wilson coefficients, ranging from $|C^{\mathrm{tensor}}_{eμtu}|/Λ^2 < 0.018$ TeV$^{-2}$ to $|C^{\mathrm{scalar}}_{eμtc}|/Λ^2 < 0.32$ TeV$^{-2}$ for the tensor $u$-quark and scalar $c$-quark couplings, respectively. These Wilson coefficient limits are translated into 95% confidence level upper limits on cLFV top-quark decay branching ratios, ranging from $B(t \to eμu) < 6 \cdot 10^{-9}$ to $B(t \to eμc) < 2.7 \cdot 10^{-7}$ for the scalar $u$-quark and tensor $c$-quark couplings, respectively.

</details>


### [220] [A-GHOST: High-rate streaming of trigger-level data to programmable GPU inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01761)
*I. Xiotidis,N. Clarke Hall,M. S. Larson,R. Gurunathan,C. Burdick,T. Du,N. Konstantinidis,K. Kordas,D. Leshchev,D. W. Miller,V. A. Petrovic,D. Sampsonidou,A. Thompson,T. Wengler*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: A-GHOST 提出了一种面向高能物理实验的高速率流式读出架构，将前端由决策平台转为数据聚合与流式源，把数据送至 GPU 后端运行复杂算法；本文基于 NVIDIA IGX Thor 构建概念验证后端，在 40–100 Gbps 下实现稳定推理，p95 延迟 0.118 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高能物理触发系统采用确定性前端与固定延迟处理，硬件触发受资源与延迟包络限制，难以部署更复杂的算法。作者希望突破这一限制，把触发决策从探测器附近的固定延迟逻辑转移到算力更充裕的 GPU 后端，从而支持生成式与时间感知等更复杂、更耗算力的神经网络工作负载。

Method: A-GHOST 将探测器近端的确定性前端与固定延迟处理从"决策平台"改造为"聚合与流式源"，把紧凑的触发级数据流式传输到支持 GPU 的后端。概念验证后端使用 NVIDIA IGX Thor 开发套件：软件限速发送器经 QSFP 接口与外部环回线缆送至第二个 QSFP 接口，用于在与 FPGA 源集成前研究网络到 GPU 的路径。利用 NVIDIA DAQIRI 将数据直接接收到 GPU 可访问内存，并用自定义 CUDA 内核把数据包净荷重组为持久、连续的 TensorRT 输入窗口，且不做数据类型转换（由模型处理）。数据采用高能物理派生的事件表示，含前十个量能器簇。

Result: 后端在 40 至 100 Gbps 范围内可扩展，同时维持恒定输入速率；推理延迟在 p95 分位为 0.118 ms。系统可连续运行数小时而不出现丢包或推理错误。生成式与时间感知神经网络展现出超越常规固定延迟 FPGA 触发系统所能部署的工作负载能力。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了 A-GHOST 作为未来 FPGA 到 GPU 流式读出系统基础的地位。

Abstract: A-GHOST (A Global Heterogeneous Online Scouting Trigger) is an R&D effort investigating high-rate streaming readout architectures for high-energy physics (HEP) experiments. The central idea is to convert the deterministic front-end and fixed-latency processing close to the detector from a decision-making platform to an aggregation and streaming source. Compact trigger-level data are streamed to a GPU-enabled backend, where substantially more complex algorithms can run beyond the resource and latency envelope of the hardware trigger. This paper presents a proof-of-concept backend using the NVIDIA IGX Thor development kit. A software rate-controlled transmitter sends data through a QSFP interface and external loopback cable to a second QSFP interface, allowing the network-to-GPU path to be studied before integration with FPGA sources. NVIDIA DAQIRI enables reception directly into GPU-accessible memory, while a custom CUDA kernel reassembles packet payloads into persistent, contiguous TensorRT input windows without data-type conversion, which is handled by the models. Using a HEP-derived event representation consisting of the ten leading calorimeter clusters, the backend scales from 40 to 100 Gbps while sustaining a constant input rate and an inference latency of 0.118 ms at the p95 interval. The system operates for multiple hours without drops or inference errors. Generative and temporally aware neural networks demonstrate workloads beyond those normally deployed in fixed-latency FPGA trigger systems. The result establishes A-GHOST as a basis for future FPGA-to-GPU streaming readout systems.

</details>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Closed-form evaluation of the cyclic four-electron atomic correlation integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00036)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 将四电子环状Slater积分（Bonham分类case V）用权重至多3的有限实数表达式给出：一般情形为18块显式和，仅含对数、二重对数与三重对数；等指数时坍缩为一个权重3的周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环状四电子Slater积分在量子化学与多体理论中具有基础地位，但一般指数的解析求值长期缺乏无根式、无广义多重对数、不依赖围道约定的有限闭式表达，因此需要给出完整可计算且数值稳定的显式公式。

Method: 对该case V积分进行解析求值：将所有对数宗量化为正值，并把非常数多重对数宗量限制在(-1,1)内，从而避免根式、广义多重对数、围道规定与未取极限；结果组织为18个块的显式和。等指数特殊化后化为涉及Li_3(1/4)、ζ(3)、π^2 log 2与log^3 2的权重3周期。并采用三种独立数值路线、经典公式的精确二进求值以及已发表标定值进行交叉验证，同时证明小/大指数极限律、二面体对称性与重排律。

Result: 一般指数下获得显式18块表达式，仅含对数、二重对数与三重对数；等指数时得 I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[Li_3(1/4)/2−7ζ(3)/24+π^2 log 2/12−2log^3 2/3]，为仅于2与3处的权重3周期。数值方面：三种独立方法在四组指数元组上至少32位小数一致；精确二进求值给出八个100位证书；三个已发表四电子标定值逐位复现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了四电子环状Slater积分的完整、实、权重至多3的闭式求值框架，附带极限律、对称性与重排律，并具高精度数值验证；但公式仅适用于四环拓扑，不处理两种带弦拓扑。

Abstract: For positive exponents $α_1,\ldots,α_4$ we evaluate the cyclic four-electron Slater integral, the topology recorded as case V in Bonham's 1965 classification, in a finite real expression of weight at most three. The general answer is an explicit sum of eighteen blocks involving only logarithms, dilogarithms and trilogarithms of rational functions of the exponents; all logarithm arguments are positive and all nonconstant polylogarithm arguments may be placed in $(-1,1)$, so no radical, generalized polylogarithm, contour prescription or unevaluated limit is required. At equal exponents the value collapses to $I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[\mathrm{Li}_3(1/4)/2-7ζ(3)/24+π^2\log 2/12-2\log^3 2/3]$, a weight-three period over $\mathbb{Q}$ ramified only at 2 and 3. Three independently implemented numerical routes agree at four exponent tuples to at least 32 decimal places, an exact-dyadic evaluation of the classical formula gives eight 100-place certificates, and three published four-electron calibration values are reproduced to every printed digit. We also prove the small- and large-exponent limit laws with closed-form coefficients, dihedral symmetry, and a strict rearrangement law for the three inequivalent cyclic placements of four exponents. The formula applies to the four-cycle and does not evaluate the two chorded topologies.

</details>


### [222] [Quantum Reverse Hypercontractivity for KMS-Symmetric Quantum Markov Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00242)
*Jinsong Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了在 p=1/2 处 p-对数 Sobolev 常数消失的本原 KMS-对称量子 Markov 半群，并证明任意此类半群在 1/2<p≤q<1 上满足量子反向超压缩性，同时给出退相位扰动和图上 KMS-对称半群的反向超压缩结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子 Markov 半群上的反向超压缩性在 p 接近 0 或 1/2 时的行为，判定是否存在正的均匀反向超压缩速率，并寻找反例与充分条件。

Method: 构造本原 KMS-对称量子 Markov 半群，使 p-对数 Sobolev 常数在 p=1/2 处为 0，并构造相关图上例子使其在 p→0+ 时趋于 -∞；结合 dephasing 扰动技术与图上 KMS-对称半群分析。

Result: 所构造的例子否定了 (0,1) 上正的均匀反向超压缩速率；证明本原 KMS-对称半群在 1/2<p≤q<1 上具有显式充分时间的量子反向超压缩性；对任意 δ>0，足够强的 dephasing 扰动在 [δ,1] 上满足均匀正的对数 Sobolev 不等式，从而在 δ<p≤q<1 上具有量子反向超压缩性；对 n≥3 的连通图，图上 KMS-对称半群在 δ<p≤q<1（0<δ<1）上满足反向超压缩性。

Conclusion: 反向超压缩速率在 p 趋向 0 或 1/2 时可能退化，不存在 (0,1) 上统一的正面结果；但通过限制参数区间、施加 dephasing 扰动或限定图结构，可获得显式且正的反向超压缩性。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant vanishes at $p=1/2$ and a correlated graph-based example for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant tends to $-\infty$ as $p\to 0^+$. These examples obstruct a positive uniform reverse-hypercontractivity rate on $(0,1)$. By contrast, we show that every primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroup satisfies quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $1/2< p\leq q < 1$ with explicit sufficient times. For each fixed $δ>0$, a sufficiently strong dephasing perturbation satisfies a uniform positive logarithmic Sobolev inequality on $[δ, 1]$ and hence quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $δ< p\leq q<1$ with fixed $0<δ<1/2$. Furthermore, we establish reverse hypercontractivity for graph-based KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for $δ< p\leq q< 1$ with fixed $0<δ<1$ for connected graphs when $n \geq 3$.

</details>


### [223] [Complete Factorial Asymptotics and Integrable Structure of Generalized Charlier Recurrence Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00396)
*Mahouton Norbert Hounkonnou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究广义Charlier多项式递推系数β_n与γ_n的渐近展开，证明偏差β_n−n和μ−γ_n超出一切代数阶、在μ^n/(n!)^2尺度出现；通过离散Painlevé II参数化与Bessel比较，得到精确连接公式c_n∼J_n(2√μ)及渐近式，并用Hankel行列式的扇区分解给出独立验证，揭示可积结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Charlier多项式对应离散权μ^k/(k!)^2，其递推系数的偏差渐近此前缺乏精确的阶与常数刻画。作者希望确定β_n−n与μ−γ_n的完整因子渐近，并理解其背后的可积结构。

Method: 采用参数化β_n−n=√μ c_n c_{n+1}、γ_n=μ(1−c_n^2)，将问题化为满足离散Painlevé II的正序列(c_n)。在c_n=0附近线性化得到离散Bessel方程，其递降解为J_n(2√μ)。结合Bessel比较与Hankel行列式的扇区分解证明c_n/J_n(2√μ)→L(μ)并识别L(μ)=1。另从Hankel行列式出发，将其视为离散对数气体，利用Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n−1)/2}T_n(μ)与扇区分解T_n(μ)=1+Σ S_q(μ)进行独立推导。

Result: 证明偏差超出所有代数阶且出现在μ^n/(n!)^2尺度；得到精确连接公式c_n∼J_n(2√μ)，进而β_n−n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!(n+1)!)、μ−γ_n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!)^2。并证明Hankel行列式扇区贡献S_q(μ)∼e^μ μ^q/(q!)^2，独立导出因子修正。

Conclusion: Bessel比较与扇区分解共同揭示了联系离散Painlevé动力学、Toda形变与Hankel—分拆表示的可积结构，给出了广义Charlier多项式递推系数偏差的精确因子渐近。

Abstract: We study the monic recurrence coefficients of the generalized Charlier polynomials associated with the discrete weight
  \[
  ρ_μ(k)
  =
  \frac{μ^k}{(k!)^2},
  \qquad
  k\in\mathbb N_0,
  \qquad
  μ>0.
  \]
  We establish complete factorial asymptotics for the deviations $β_n-n$ and $μ-γ_n$, proving that they are beyond all algebraic orders and appear at the scale $μ^{n}/(n!)^2$. The key step is a parametrization $β_n-n=\sqrtμ\,c_nc_{n+1}$, $γ_n=μ(1-c_n^2)$, which reduces the problem to a positive sequence $(c_n)$ satisfying a discrete Painlevé II equation.
  Linearization around $c_n=0$ yields a discrete Bessel equation whose recessive solution is $J_n(2\sqrtμ)$. We prove that the ratio $c_n/J_n(2\sqrtμ)$ converges to a finite limit $L(μ)$, which we identify exactly as $L(μ)=1$ by combining Bessel comparison with a global analysis based on the sector decomposition of the associated Hankel determinant. This yields the exact connection formula
  \[
  c_n \sim J_n(2\sqrtμ), \qquad n\to\infty,
  \]
  and consequently the precise asymptotics
  \[
  β_n-n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{n!(n+1)!}, \qquad
  μ-γ_n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{(n!)^2}.
  \]
  We further develop a complementary approach based on Hankel determinants interpreted as a discrete logarithmic gas. We show that the Hankel determinant factorizes as $Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n-1)/2}T_n(μ)$ and admits a sector decomposition $T_n(μ)=1+\sum_{q=1}^{n}S_q(μ)$ with sector contributions independent of $n$. We establish $S_q(μ)\sim e^μμ^q/(q!)^2$, providing an independent derivation of the factorial corrections. Together, the Bessel comparison and sector decomposition reveal a rich integrable structure linking discrete Painlevé dynamics, Toda deformations, and Hankel--partition representations.

</details>


### [224] [On spectrum of the harmonic Kronig-Penney model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00879)
*J. Gibbons,A. P. Veselov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究量子力学Kronig-Penney模型的调和修正（受dressing chain理论启发）的谱性质。在特殊情形下给出显式基态，并证明所有谱隙长度相等，其端点由相应Weber-Hermite函数的零点表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学Kronig-Penney模型的调和修正具有研究价值，其动机来自dressing chain理论，需要刻画该模型的谱结构（基态与谱隙）。

Method: 采用谱分析方法，结合dressing chain理论对量子力学Kronig-Penney模型作调和修正；利用Weber-Hermite函数刻画谱隙端点，并在特殊情形下求解基态。

Result: 在特殊情形下可显式给出相应的基态；所有谱隙长度相同，且谱隙端点可表示为相应Weber-Hermite函数的零点。

Conclusion: 该调和修正的Kronig-Penney模型具有规则谱结构——等长谱隙与显式基态，可用Weber-Hermite函数零点精确描述。

Abstract: We study the spectral properties of the harmonic modification of the quantum mechanical Kronig-Penney model motivated by the theory of the dressing chain. In particular, we show that in the special cases the corresponding ground states can be given explicitly and all spectral gaps have the same length with the ends expressed as zeros of the corresponding Weber-Hermite functions.

</details>


### [225] [The Buchholz Algebra from the Universal Resolvent Algebra--Particle Structure, Gibbs States, and Closed-Path Expansions without Fock Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01622)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在不使用Fock表示的前提下构造Buchholz代数：将其实现为规范固定预解代数之内禀粒子截断商的带界逆极限，并配备等距的粒子数度坐标；由此导出有限矩阵角上的迹、KMS态与迹范数乘积公式，以及为路径积分表示奠基的闭合路径展开；已知的Fock空间描述与一个真准局域子代数作为实现出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上Buchholz代数依赖Fock表示来定义，这限制了构造的普适性与代数内在性。作者希望给出一个不依赖Fock空间的、纯代数（C*-代数/算子代数）意义上的构造，从而使KMS态、迹与路径积分结构可在更一般的框架中讨论；同时把已知Fock描述降格为众多实现之一，以厘清概念依赖关系。

Method: 以规范固定的预解代数（gauge-fixed resolvent algebra）为出发点，构造其内禀的粒子截断商（intrinsic particle-cutoff quotients）；引入等距的粒子数度坐标（isometric particle-degree coordinates）；取这些商的带界逆极限（bounded inverse limit）得到Buchholz代数。在有限矩阵角（finite matrix corners）上定义典范迹，构造KMS态，证明迹范数乘积公式，并给出闭合路径展开（closed-path expansions）。最后验证Fock空间描述与准局域子代数均为该构造的实现。

Result: 成功在不使用Fock表示的情况下构造出Buchholz代数；有限矩阵角上存在典范迹与KMS态，并满足迹范数乘积公式；给出闭合路径展开，为路径积分表示奠定基础；已知Fock空间描述与一个真准局域子代数被实现为该代数的具体表示。

Conclusion: Buchholz代数可完全内禀地由规范固定预解代数的粒子截断商逆极限刻画，无需先验的Fock表示；所得结构统一了迹、KMS态与路径积分式展开，并把Fock描述与准局域子代数纳入其实现谱系，为后续路径积分表示与热力学态研究提供代数基础。

Abstract: We construct the Buchholz algebra without a Fock representation, as the bounded inverse limit of intrinsic particle-cutoff quotients of the gauge-fixed resolvent algebra, with isometric particle-degree coordinates. Its finite matrix corners carry canonical traces, KMS states, and trace-norm product formulas, together with closed-path expansions that lay the groundwork for path-integral representations. The known Fock-space description and a proper quasilocal subalgebra arise as realizations.

</details>


### [226] [Quantum hierarchies of the ILW and BO equations and the DR cycles on the moduli spaces of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01841)
*Alexandr Buryak,Abner Hernandez Rodriguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子ILW层级结构在特定极限下与Nazarov–Sklyanin量子BO层级结构之间的关系，并提出一个刻画该关系的猜想，同时给出多种验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作基于任意上同调场论与双分歧（DR）循环构造了一大类量子层级结构，其中量子ILW层级是一个特例。ILW方程具有KdV与Benjamin–Ono（BO）两个著名极限，而BO对应的量子层级已由Nazarov与Sklyanin构造。作者希望厘清量子ILW层级的极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin层级之间的联系。

Method: 基于上同调场论与稳定曲线模空间上DR循环的构造框架，考察量子ILW层级在BO极限下的行为，并与Nazarov–Sklyanin构造的量子层级进行比对，提出联系二者的猜想，并通过多种检验加以支持。

Result: 提出了描述量子ILW层级某一极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin层级之间关系的猜想，并给出了多项验证以支持该猜想。

Conclusion: 量子ILW层级的极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin量子BO层级之间存在预期的对应关系，该猜想得到多个独立检验的支持，为后续严格证明提供了方向。

Abstract: In an earlier paper of P. Rossi and the first author, a construction of a wide class of quantum hierarchies was presented, with the input data given by an arbitrary cohomological field theory. A crucial role in the construction is played by the double ramification (DR) cycles on the moduli spaces of stable curves. As a special case, one gets a quantization of the hierarchy of the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation. The ILW equation has two famous limits: the Korteweg--de Vries equation and the Benjamin--Ono (BO) equation, and for the latter one a quantum hierarchy was constructed by Nazarov and Sklyanin. In our paper, we propose a conjecture describing a relation between a limit of the quantum ILW hierarchy and the Nazarov--Sklyanin hierarchy and present various checks to support it.

</details>


### [227] [Quadratic symmetry algebras and Sturm algebraization of superintegrable systems with magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01869)
*Shams Ara,Md Fazlul Hoque,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了轴对称磁场中两个三维量子超可积系统，分别给出二次代数/变形振子、$sl(2)$ 与 $sl(3)$ 三种互补代数描述，并解释了二者共享 $sl(3)$ 结构的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨多可分离超可积系统在不同处理阶段（原始哈密顿量、抛物分离后、分离前的Sturm问题）会涌现出哪些不同的隐藏代数结构与精确/准精确可解性，并厘清两个原始分类上不同的磁哈密顿量为何具有共同代数结构。

Method: 对两个轴对称磁场中的三维超可积模型：利用积分构造带Casimir的二次代数，用有限维变形振子表示求代数谱，并用分离变量法验证；在圆抛物坐标下将分离方程化为具有限多项式扇区的六次双合流Heun方程，将其表述为以物理能量为参数、分离常数为对角的Sturm本征值问题；经规范变换实现$sl(2)$代数化并约化为有限三对角矩阵；进一步构造整个哈密顿量的分离前Sturm表示，以逆平方耦合为Sturm本征值，固定轴向积分、变号与规范旋转后，二变量Sturm哈密顿量及其非中心积分属于$U(sl(3))$。

Result: 获得三种互补代数描述：原哈密顿量的二次代数/变形振子表示、抛物分离后的$sl(2)$代数化、以及分离前完整Sturm问题的$sl(3)$代数化。两个磁哈密顿量虽在原始分类中不同，但固定轴向积分后都约化为同一奇异2:1振子正规形式，仅有效参数嵌入不同，从而解释了共同的$sl(3)$结构，同时并不意味原磁系统等价。

Conclusion: 多可分离超可积系统在不同阶段可展现不同的隐藏代数结构及精确或准精确可解性；共同$sl(3)$结构的出现源于固定轴向积分后的同一正规形式，而非原系统的等价性。

Abstract: We study two three-dimensional quantum superintegrable systems in nonvanishing axially symmetric magnetic fields. For each model the integrals generate a quadratic algebra with a Casimir, and finite-dimensional deformed-oscillator representations yield algebraic spectra. The same spectra are recovered by separation of variables. In circular parabolic coordinates the separated equations are sextic biconfluent-Heun equations with finite polynomial sectors. We formulate them as Sturm eigenvalue problems with the physical energy as a parameter and the separation constant diagonalized. After gauge transformation, each equation admits an $sl(2)$ algebraization and reduces to a finite tridiagonal matrix. We also construct a pre-separation Sturm representation of each complete Hamiltonian, taking the inverse-square coupling as the Sturm eigenvalue. The transformed integrals remain commuting and, after fixing the axial integral, changing variables and gauge rotating, the two-variable Sturm Hamiltonian and its non-central integrals belong to the universal enveloping algebra $U(sl(3))$. We thus obtain three complementary algebraic descriptions: the quadratic-algebra/deformed-oscillator representation of the original Hamiltonian, the $sl(2)$ algebraization after parabolic separation, and the $sl(3)$ algebraization of the complete Sturm problem before separation. Although the two magnetic Hamiltonians are distinct in the original classification, fixing the axial integral reduces them to the same singular 2:1 oscillator normal form with different effective parameter embeddings. This explains the common $sl(3)$ structure without implying equivalence of the original magnetic systems. These results show how distinct hidden algebraic structures and exact or quasi-exact solvability can emerge at different stages of a multiseparable superintegrable system.

</details>


### [228] [Superintegrability of a resonant ghostly tri-Hamiltonian model: polynomial first integrals and transcendental completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01901)
*Andreas Fring,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有不定动能的三维共振哈密顿系统的经典与量子超可积性，给出三种Poisson描述。多项式首次积分环除三个对合二次哈密顿量外，还含两个四次本原生成元；有理首次积分均为H1,H2,H3及I4^o的有理函数，故有理不变域功能秩为四。引入无分支超越积分后，在不变开集Δ>0上功能秩升至五，系统在多项式与有理类中最小超可积，在允许超越积分时于该不变域上最大超可积。Weyl量子化在三种Poisson实现中均给出精确高阶微分对称，且第五经典不变量的非局域量子对应存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有不定动能的三维共振哈密顿系统在经典与量子层面的超可积性，并考察同一流动的三种Poisson描述，以厘清多项式、有理及超越首次积分层次上的可积结构差异。

Method: 采用Poisson几何与首次积分环分析：识别三个对合二次哈密顿量H1,H2,H3及两个四次本原生成元I4^o、I4^e（奇、偶动量宇称）；利用单个八次关系确定多项式不变环的超越次数为四；求解完整首次积分方程刻画有理首次积分；引入无分支超越不变量提升功能秩；并通过Weyl量子化构造精确高阶微分对称及非局域量子对应。

Result: 多项式不变环由H1,H2,H3,I4^o,I4^e生成，受一个八次关系约束，超越次数为四；所有有理首次积分皆为H1,H2,H3,I4^o的有理函数，有理不变域功能秩为四；无分支超越不变量使不变开集Δ>0上的一般光滑功能秩升至五；Weyl量子化在三种Poisson实现中均给出精确高阶微分对称，第五经典不变量的非局域量子对应稠密定义。

Conclusion: 该系统在多项式与有理类中为最小超可积，而在允许超越积分时于不变域Δ>0上为最大超可积；经典超越完成具有具体的量子实现，体现了经典-量子超可积结构的对应。

Abstract: We study classical and quantum superintegrability of a resonant three-dimensional Hamiltonian with indefinite kinetic energy and three Poisson descriptions of the same flow. Besides three commuting quadratic Hamiltonians, the polynomial first-integral ring contains two primitive degree-four generators, $I_4^{\rm o}$ and $I_4^{\rm e}$, of odd and even momentum parity. A single octic relation leaves the polynomial invariant ring with transcendence degree four. Solving the complete first-integral equation shows that every rational first integral is a rational function of $H_1,H_2,H_3,I_4^{\rm o}$, so that the rational invariant field likewise has generic functional rank four. A branch-free transcendental invariant raises the generic smooth functional rank to five on the invariant open set $Δ>0$. The system is therefore minimally superintegrable in the polynomial and rational classes and maximally superintegrable on this invariant domain when transcendental integrals are admitted. Weyl quantisation yields exact higher-order differential symmetries in all three Poisson realisations. The branch-free fifth classical invariant also admits a densely defined nonlocal quantum counterpart, providing a concrete quantum realisation of the transcendental completion.

</details>
