# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Sectional category versus module sectional category for retractive maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00089)
*Paul-Eugene Parent*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 构造了一个具有同伦收缩的有理映射 p，满足 msecat(p)=2 < secat(p)=3，从而否证了 Carrasquel–Vera 关于收缩映射的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carrasquel–Vera 曾提出猜想：对于容许同伦收缩的映射，其截面范畴 secat 与单截面范畴 msecat 应满足某种关系（通常猜测二者相等或受某种约束）。本文旨在检验并推翻该猜想。

Method: 在有限有理型单连通有理空间之间构造映射 p，使其容许同伦收缩；目标是某个单连通十二维 CW 复形（含 11 个胞腔）的有理化，且该空间为有理双曲型；通过计算 secat 与 msecat 得到 msecat(p)=2、secat(p)=3。

Result: 所构造映射满足 msecat(p)=2 < secat(p)=3，即在收缩映射情形下二者不相等，直接给出 Carrasquel–Vera 猜想的反例。

Conclusion: Carrasquel–Vera 关于收缩映射的猜想不成立；即使映射容许同伦收缩，secat 与 msecat 仍可出现差异，且该反例发生在有理双曲的十二维有限 CW 复形有理化上。

Abstract: We construct a map between simply connected rational spaces of finite rational type which admits a homotopy retraction and satisfies $msecat(p)=2<secat(p)=3$. The target is the rationalization of a simply connected twelve-dimensional CW complex with eleven cells, and is rationally hyperbolic. This disproves Carrasquel--Vera's conjecture for retractive maps.

</details>


### [230] [A homotopical enhancement of Neisendorfer's algebraic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01350)
*Bruno Stonek*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为Neisendorfer关于有理单纯集、交换dg代数(cdga)、余交换dg余代数(cdgc)与dg李代数(dgl)同伦范畴等价，给出了一个∞-范畴层面的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neisendorfer的等价是在同伦范畴层面给出的经典结果，但在更高结构（∞-范畴）层面尚缺乏统一刻画。作者希望在保持连通性、幂零性、有限型等假设下，将这一系列等价提升到∞-范畴层次。

Method: 作者证明线性对偶是弱有限型连通cdgc与cdga之间的部分Quillen等价；并证明Quillen的Chevalley–Eilenberg伴随是cdgc与dgl之间的部分crossed derivable equivalence，后者是本文新引入的概念，用以精确刻画该伴随的行为。

Result: 线性对偶被确认为部分Quillen等价；Chevalley–Eilenberg伴随被确认为部分crossed derivable equivalence；由此得到Neisendorfer等价在∞-范畴层面的增强。

Conclusion: 该工作将Neisendorfer的有理同伦代数等价统一提升至∞-范畴框架，并通过部分Quillen等价与crossed derivable equivalence两个工具实现了这一提升。

Abstract: We give an $\infty$-categorical enhancement of Neisendorfer's equivalences between the homotopy categories of rational simplicial sets, commutative dg algebras (cdgas), cocommutative dg coalgebras (cdgcs) and dg Lie algebras (dgls), where these objects have respective hypotheses of connectedness, nilpotency and finite type. We do so by proving that linear duality is a partial Quillen equivalence between weak finite type connected cdgcs and cdgas, and that Quillen's Chevalley--Eilenberg adjunction is a partial crossed derivable equivalence between cdgcs and dgls, a concept we introduce in this paper to axiomatize precisely the behavior of this adjunction.

</details>


### [231] [Mumford--Morita--Miller classes in generalised cohomology theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01888)
*Oscar Randal-Williams*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文开启在任意复定向上同调理论中研究黎曼曲面模空间上Mumford–Morita–Miller类的计划，重点关注由此产生的新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典上Mumford–Morita–Miller（MMM）类通常在普通上同调中定义与研究；本文试图将其推广到任意复定向上同调理论，以揭示该推广所带来的新现象。

Method: 利用复定向上同调理论的框架，将黎曼曲面模空间上的MMM类从普通上同调推广到任意复定向上同调理论中，并对其展开初步研究。

Result: 给出了MMM类在任意复定向上同调理论中的定义方式，并指出这种推广会产生新的现象。

Conclusion: MMM类不仅可定义于普通上同调，也可定义于任意复定向上同调理论；对该方向的研究由此起步，新现象值得进一步探索。

Abstract: The Mumford--Morita--Miller classes on the moduli space of Riemann surfaces may be defined not only in ordinary cohomology but in any complex-oriented cohomology theory. We begin their investigation, emphasising the new phenomena that arise.

</details>


### [232] [Stable Complex Structures On Real Vector Bundles Over Connected Sums Of Quaternionic Projective Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02147)
*Souvik Mandal*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 通过分析复化映射 c: KO~(M)→K~(M) 的像，用 Pontryagin 类刻画了 M^{4n}_{ℓ,m} 上具有稳定复结构的实向量丛，并证明 M^{4n}_{ℓ,m} 稳定几乎复当且仅当 n=2 且 ℓ−m 为偶数，从而对一切 n≥3 不存在几乎复结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato 与 Suzuki 于 1974 年断言 M^{4n}_{ℓ,m} 在 3≤n≤10 时不存在几乎复结构，但遗留了 n=3 且 ℓ=3m+1 的可能例外。本文旨在对所有 n≥3 彻底解决该问题，并给出更强的稳定几乎复结构层面的结论。

Method: 研究四元数射影空间连通和 M^{4n}_{ℓ,m} 上复化映射 c: KO~(M^{4n}_{ℓ,m}) → K~(M^{4n}_{ℓ,m}) 的像；以此用 Pontryagin 类刻画允许稳定复结构的实向量丛。

Result: M^{4n}_{ℓ,m} 稳定几乎复当且仅当 n=2 且 ℓ−m 为偶数；因此对任意 n≥3，M^{4n}_{ℓ,m} 均不具几乎复结构，包括此前遗留的 n=3、ℓ=3m+1 情形。

Conclusion: 将 Sato–Suzuki 的部分结论推广至所有 n≥3，并以稳定几乎复结构的更强形式否定其存在性。

Abstract: For integers $n\ge 2$ and integers $\ell,m\ge 0$, not both zero, let $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}=\ell\,\mathbb{HP}^{n}\,\#\,m\,\overline{\mathbb{HP}^{n}}$ denote the connected sum of $\ell$ copies of the quaternionic projective space $\mathbb{HP}^{n}$ and $m$ copies of $\mathbb{HP}^{n}$ endowed with the opposite orientation. By analysing the image of the complexification map $c\,\colon\,\widetilde{KO}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})\to\widetilde{K}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})$, we characterise, in terms of Pontryagin classes, the real vector bundles over $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ admitting a stable complex structure, and deduce that $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ is stably almost complex if and only if $n=2$ and $\ell-m$ is even. Consequently no $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ with $n\ge 3$ admits an almost complex structure. Sato and Suzuki asserted the non-existence of almost complex structures on $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ in 1974 for $3\le n\le 10$, except possibly when $n=3$ and $\ell=3m+1$; we establish it for all $n\ge 3$, and in the stronger form of stable almost complex structures.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [A relaxation system for the low-Mach limit of kinetic equations: Stability and higher order asymptotic preserving scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00811)
*Giacomo Dimarco,Axel Klar,Theresa Köfler,Lorenzo Pareschi,Sudarshan Tiwari,Yizhou Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出低马赫数极限下动理学方程的双曲松弛系统，基于微宏分解与正交多项式投影导出闭环矩松弛系统，并构建高阶渐近保持数值框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低马赫数极限下动理学方程模拟的计算困难，需要兼顾跨物理区域一致性与高计算效率的模型及数值方法。

Method: 对缩放BGK模型进行微宏分解，将分布函数重构为宏观平衡部分与微观非平衡余项；将微观偏差投影到正交多项式上，导出闭环矩松弛系统（Grad 13矩形式，线性双曲部分+适配不可压极限的松弛项）；采用IMEX Runge-Kutta格式与有限差分WENO重构及中心差分构建高阶渐近保持（AP）框架。

Result: 证明了模型在不可压缩Navier-Stokes极限下的结构稳定性；数值格式在不同物理区域保持一致稳定性与相容性，并在尺度参数趋于零时自动退化为不可压缩热Navier-Stokes极限的一致高阶离散；一维与二维数值实验验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 该双曲松弛系统与高阶AP框架能显著降低计算成本，并在宽范围Knudsen数与马赫数下表现鲁棒。

Abstract: This work introduces a hyperbolic relaxation system designed for the simulation of kinetic equations in the low-Mach number limit. The methodology is built upon a micro-macro decomposition of the scaled BGK model, which reformulates the kinetic distribution function into a coupled system consisting of a macroscopic equilibrium part and a microscopic non-equilibrium remainder. By projecting the microscopic deviations onto a set of orthogonal polynomials, we derive a closed moment relaxation system. The resulting system is a version of Grad's 13 moment system with a linear hyperbolic part and a relaxation adapted to the incompressible limit. We prove the model's structural stability in the incompressible Navier-Stokes limit. Moreover, we develop a high order Asymptotic-Preserving (AP) numerical framework using Implicit-Explicit (IMEX) Runge Kutta schemes for temporal accuracy and finite difference WENO reconstructions as well as central difference approximations for high order spatial resolution. The proposed scheme ensures uniform stability and consistency across different physical regimes, automatically degenerating into a consistent high order discretization of the incompressible thermal Navier-Stokes limit as the scaling parameter vanishes. Numerical experiments in one and two dimensions corroborate the theoretical findings, demonstrating significant reductions in computational cost and robust performance across a wide range of Knudsen and Mach numbers.

</details>


### [234] [Matrix-Free Moment-Matching Method for Reduced-Order Modeling of Quadratic-Bilinear Descriptor Systems with Multiple Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00830)
*Zeyuan Gao,Cheol W. Lee,Oleg Zikanov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多输入二次-双线性描述系统的新型矩匹配降阶方法，支持多通道输入并采用无矩阵算法，适用于大规模CFD问题，二维流动实验验证了其精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模计算流体力学等问题中，多输入二次-双线性描述系统的全阶模型维度过高，现有降阶方法难以同时处理作用于微分方程和代数方程的多通道输入，且显式装配全阶矩阵与张量成本高昂。

Method: 提出矩匹配降阶方法，针对不同输入通道及输入组合分别构造投影空间，以应对多输入情形；采用无矩阵算法，在不显式装配或存储全阶系统矩阵与张量的条件下直接计算降阶矩阵与张量。

Result: 在二维流动问题上的数值实验表明，所得降阶模型在多路时变输入下能准确复现全阶模型的瞬态响应。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效处理多输入二次-双线性描述系统，兼具无矩阵计算与多通道投影的优势，尤其适合大规模CFD问题的降阶建模。

Abstract: This paper develops a new moment-matching order reduction method for multi-input quadratic-bilinear descriptor systems. The proposed approach accommodates multiple inputs acting on both the differential and algebraic equations and constructs separate projection spaces associated with different input channels and input combinations. The method uses a matrix-free algorithm, allowing the reduced-order matrices and tensors to be computed without explicitly assembling or storing the full-order system matrices and tensors. This feature makes the proposed method particularly suitable for large-scale computational fluid dynamics problems. Numerical experiments carried out for two-dimensional flow problems demonstrate that the resulting reduced-order models accurately reproduce the transient responses of the full-order models under multiple time-varying inputs.

</details>


### [235] [Uncertainty Quantification for Landweber Iteration with Randomized Normal-Operator Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00882)
*Anuj Abhishek,Sean Holman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究线性不适定反问题中Landweber迭代的不确定性量化，以Radon变换为例，结合广义信仰推断与正则化偏置界构造置信区间，并用随机SVD加速，实现低成本且可靠的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代方法广泛用于求解线性不适定反问题，其确定性收敛或半收敛已有大量研究，但由观测数据中随机噪声导致的重建结果不确定性量化问题关注甚少。作者希望为Landweber迭代重建提供统计意义上的不确定性度量。

Method: 以Radon变换为动机示例，从统计角度解释Landweber重建，分析测量数据不确定性如何经重建映射传播；该视角与广义信仰推断相关，通过反演观测数据与未知固定量之间的关系诱导参数不确定性。将随机不确定性与正则化偏置的理论界结合，构造重建解的置信区间。为降低大规模问题中前向算子重复操作的开销，采用随机奇异值分解获得Radon变换相关法向算子的低秩近似，并将该近似引入不确定性量化，同时把由此引入的额外随机化误差纳入分析。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方法在显著降低计算成本的同时，能够提供准确的重建和可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为线性不适定反问题的迭代重建建立了融合统计不确定性、正则化偏置与随机低秩近似误差的不确定性量化框架，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Iterative methods are extremely popular for solving linear ill-posed inverse problems. While deterministic convergence, or more precisely, semi-convergence of such methods is widely studied, the problem of quantifying uncertainty in the resulting reconstructions due to random noise in the observed data has received far less attention. In this work, we study uncertainty quantification for the Landweber iteration in linear inverse problems using the Radon transform as a motivating example. We interpret the Landweber reconstruction statistically by analyzing how uncertainty in the measured data is propagated through the reconstruction map. This perspective is closely related to generalized fiducial inference, where uncertainty about the parameter is induced by inverting the relation between the observed data and the unknown (fixed) quantity. We combine the resulting stochastic uncertainty with a theoretical bound on the regularization bias to construct confidence intervals for the reconstructed solution. The evaluation of the resulting uncertainty estimates require repeated operations with the forward operator that can become expensive in large-scale problems. To reduce this cost, we use randomized singular value decomposition to obtain a low-rank approximation of the normal operator associated with the Radon transform and incorporate this approximation in quantifying uncertainty. {In particular, this requires that the additional randomization error introduced due to the use of such randomized techniques be included in our approach for uncertainty quantification.} Numerical results show that the proposed method provides accurate reconstructions and reliable uncertainty estimates at substantially reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [236] [A Deferred Correction, Continuous Galerkin Method for Curvilinear Staggered-Grid Lagrangian Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00932)
*Steven Walton,Svetlana Tokareva,Nathaniel Morgan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于拉格朗日流体力学方程的连续伽辽金延迟修正（cG-DeC）方法，结合高阶连续有限元离散与显式DeC时间积分器，避免每步求解全局稀疏质量矩阵，并引入兼容该框架的超粘性模型以稳定激波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉格朗日流体力学中强激波的存在需要数值稳定机制，同时高阶方法通常因隐式质量矩阵求解而计算代价高昂，需在保证精度与稳定性的同时降低每步求解成本。

Method: 采用连续伽辽金空间离散与显式Deferred Correction时间积分相结合的cG-DeC格式，避免每步全局稀疏质量矩阵求逆；并设计一种与cG-DeC框架兼容的超粘性模型修正，在构造多调和算子的有限元近似时同样无需全局求解；此外给出DeC迭代的递归重表述。

Result: 分析了完全离散格式的动量与总能量守恒性；数值实验验证了cG-DeC格式的精度与鲁棒性，并与连续伽辽金Runge-Kutta（cG-RK）方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: cG-DeC方法在不求逆全局质量矩阵的前提下实现了高阶精度与激波稳定性，且其递归重表述为显式DeC处理双曲型偏微分方程时的收敛行为提供了新见解。

Abstract: We present a continuous Galerkin, Deferred Correction (cG-DeC) method for the equations of Lagrangian hydrodynamics. The proposed scheme combines a high-order continuous finite element discretization with an explicit Deferred Correction time integrator, yielding a formulation that avoids the inversion of a global sparse mass matrix at each update. To stabilize the method in the presence of strong shocks we introduce a modification of the hyperviscosity model that is compatible with the cG-DeC framework and likewise does not require a global solve to construct the finite element approximation to the polyharmonic operator. We further present an alternative reformulation of the DeC iteration which admits a simple recursive algorithmic structure and provides insight into previously observed convergence behavior of explicit DeC applied to hyperbolic partial differential equations. Conservation of momentum and total energy of the resulting fully discrete scheme is analyzed. A set of numerical experiments illustrates the accuracy and robustness of the cG-DeC scheme. Comparisons with a continuous Galerkin Runge-Kutta (cG-RK) method are provided.

</details>


### [237] [Positivity-preserving scalar auxiliary variable schemes for gradient flows via a quadratic reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01063)
*Qiong-Ao Huang,Zhi-Hao Liu,Ying-Wei Wang,Li-Na Yan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了SAV方法中离散标量辅助变量$r^{n+1}$可能失去正性的问题，提出了保持平方根形式、通过重构$(r^2)_t$方程确保唯一正根的改进格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAV方法中，$r(t)=\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_\mathcal{N}[\phi]+C}>0$在连续层面恒为正；但离散的向后欧拉与Crank--Nicolson格式无法保证计算得到的$r^{n+1}$仍为正，这与平方根假设相矛盾，可能影响长时间模拟的稳健性。该微妙却重要的问题此前关注甚少。

Method: 首先定量刻画该失效：推导关于时间步长的严格判据与充分条件，并构造实际参数下发生符号丢失的反例。不同于现有保持正性的变体修改$r$的定义，本文保留平方根形式，将离散演化从$r_t$重构为$(r^2)_t$，把标量方程转化为常数项严格为负的凸二次方程，从而总有唯一正根。对Crank--Nicolson格式，采用乘积形式离散$r^{n+1}r^n$以保持该二次结构。

Result: 所提格式计算代价与原SAV方法相同，并被证明无条件能量稳定。针对Cahn--Hilliard方程的数值实验验证了预测的正性、能量稳定性与收敛阶。

Conclusion: 通过保留平方根定义并重构离散标量方程，可在不增加计算量的前提下保证$r^{n+1}>0$与无条件能量稳定性，为SAV方法提供更稳健的长期模拟方案。

Abstract: The scalar auxiliary variable (SAV) method replaces the nonlinear part of the free energy by a positive scalar $r(t)=\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{_\mathcal{N}}[φ]+C}>0$, thereby yielding linear, unconditionally energy-stable schemes for gradient flows. At the discrete level, however, the standard backward Euler and Crank--Nicolson discretizations provide no guarantee that the computed $r^{n+1}$ remains positive, an inconsistency with the continuous definition that contradicts the square-root ansatz and may compromise long-time robustness. Although the SAV method has been widely applied, this subtle but consequential issue has received little attention. We first characterize this failure quantitatively by deriving a sharp criterion and a sufficient condition on the time step size, and construct an explicit counterexample showing that sign loss occurs for parameters of practical relevance. Rather than modifying the definition of $r$ as in existing positivity-preserving variants, we retain the square-root form and reformulate the discrete evolution from $r_t$ to $(r^{2})_t$, which converts the scalar equation into a convex quadratic with a strictly negative constant term, always yielding a unique positive root. For the Crank--Nicolson scheme, the product-form discretization $r^{n+1}r^{n}$ preserves this quadratic structure, while conventional alternatives do not. The resulting schemes incur the same computational cost as the original SAV method and are proved unconditionally energy-stable. Numerical experiments for the Cahn--Hilliard equation confirm the predicted positivity, energy stability, and convergence rates.

</details>


### [238] [A higher order lattice-based method for high-dimensional numerical integration using periodization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01113)
*Felix Bartel,Alexander D. Gilbert,Michael Griebel,Frances Y. Kuo,Ian H. Sloan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种针对高维单位立方体上非周期函数的数值积分新方法，采用带（非等）权重的变换格点求积规则并可选子采样，收敛率接近 |J|^{-(α-1/2)} 和 |J|^{-α}(1.110721)^s，且在有利条件下常数与维数 s 无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非周期函数积分是数值计算中的难点，传统格点规则主要针对周期函数，直接应用于非周期被积函数时收敛性受影响。本文旨在构建一种能处理非周期被积函数、同时保持高维下良好收敛率并控制维数灾难的方法。

Method: 方法包含三个核心步骤：(i) 将被积函数周期化；(ii) 用核插值在格点上逼近光滑周期函数；(iii) 精确积分核插值与非光滑密度的乘积，并对步骤(ii)和(iii)提供子采样替代方案。最终形成带（非等）权重的变换格点求积规则，适用于混合导数（直至 α 阶）关于乘积 Chebyshev 密度平方可积的被积函数。

Result: 以 |J| 表示函数求值次数，方法达到接近 |J|^{-(α-1/2)} 与 |J|^{-α}(1.110721)^s 的收敛率，其中隐含常数在有利条件下与维数 s 无关；两种结果可插值以在收敛率与维数增长之间进行权衡。数值实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 该加权变换格点求积方法为高维非周期函数积分提供了有效途径，兼顾收敛率与维数依赖性，并可通过插值灵活权衡二者，子采样选项增强了实用性。

Abstract: We present a novel numerical integration method for non-periodic functions over the high-dimensional unit cube $[0,1]^s$ by a specially crafted (unequally-)weighted quadrature rule using transformed lattice points, with the additional option of subsampling. The method is designed for integrands whose mixed derivatives up to order $α$ are square-integrable with respect to a product Chebyshev density. The ingredients are (i) periodizing the integrand, (ii) approximating the smooth periodic function by a kernel interpolant at lattice points, (iii) integrating exactly the product of the kernel interpolant and the nonsmooth density, together with subsampling alternatives for (ii) and~(iii). With $|J|$ corresponding to the number of function evaluations, we achieve the convergence rate close to the order $|J|^{-(α-1/2)}$ and $|J|^{-α}\,($1.110721$)^s$, where the implied constants are independent of $s$ under favorable conditions. We can interpolate these two results to trade between the convergence rate and the growth in $s$. We provide numerical experiments to demonstrate our theory.

</details>


### [239] [Sign-Switched Antithetic Coupling for Signed Gradient-Particle Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01120)
*Stephen Abkin,Prabir Daripa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出符号切换配对(sign-switched pairing)的随机游走配对采样法，以降低一维对流-扩散方程梯度随机游走模拟的方差，并在热方程与Burgers方程上验证其显著优于独立采样、反射配对与同号配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度随机游走法用带符号粒子表示解的梯度，通过累加粒子质量恢复解，但存在采样误差。作者希望在不改变求解器概率分布（即不引入额外偏差）的前提下，通过配对采样降低方差，从而减少达到给定均方误差所需的模拟次数。

Method: 成对运行模拟，并按粒子位置顺序进行匹配。反射配对（标准对偶选择）使匹配粒子位移相反；符号切换配对则让同号粒子位移相反、异号粒子位移相同。每次模拟保持求解器原有概率分布，故成对平均的期望与偏差与单次模拟一致。作者对固定匹配方式证明：在最后扩散步，符号切换配对使每对匹配粒子的方差贡献最小；并推导其相对反射配对所消除方差的确切公式。对热方程，两个结论推广到每一步，并数值测量累积方差削减。Burgers测试中，样本量由试运行确定并在新运行上校验。

Result: Burgers测试中，符号切换配对用4次模拟即达到均方误差目标，而独立采样需65次、反射配对需34次。在8192粒子下，其方差比同号配对（仅匹配同号粒子）低约26%；在2048粒子下，它以约少40%的时间达到第二个目标。该增益在更多剖面、更晚时刻、其他黏度及三次通量下依然保持。

Conclusion: 符号切换配对是一种低成本改进重复场估计的方法，尤其适用于异号粒子相遇的场景，可显著降低梯度随机游走模拟的方差与计算成本。

Abstract: Gradient random-walk methods represent the spatial derivative of a solution by signed particles and recover the solution by summing their masses. We reduce the sampling error of such simulations for one-dimensional convection-diffusion equations by running them in pairs with particles matched in order of position. Reflected pairing, the standard antithetic choice, gives matched particles opposite displacements. In sign-switched pairing, matched particles of equal sign receive opposite displacements and those of opposite sign the same displacement. Each simulation keeps the probability distribution of the solver, so the pair average has the expectation and bias of one simulation. For a fixed matching, we prove that sign-switched pairing minimizes the variance contribution of each matched pair at the last diffusion step. We also derive an exact formula for the variance it removes relative to reflected pairing. For the heat equation both results extend to every step, and we measure the accumulated variance reduction numerically. In Burgers tests, with sample sizes chosen from pilot runs and checked on fresh runs, sign-switched pairing meets a mean-squared-error target with 4 simulations, compared with 65 for independent sampling and 34 for reflected pairing. Against within-sign pairing, which matches only particles of equal sign, it has about 26% lower variance with 8192 particles. With 2048 particles it meets a second target in about 40% less time. The gain persists for further profiles, later times, other viscosities, and a cubic flux, supporting sign-switched pairing as an inexpensive way to improve repeated field estimates where particles of opposite sign meet.

</details>


### [240] [Dirichlet--Neumann waveform relaxation for heterogeneous heat equations: fully discrete $l^2$ analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01475)
*Philipp Birken,Niklas Kotarsky*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对跨低维界面耦合、模拟共轭传热的两个线性热方程，采用Dirichlet--Neumann波形松弛的分区求解框架，利用指数加权Fourier技术在完全离散情形下推导了有限时间$T$内的$l^2$误差估计，揭示了线性与超线性收敛行为，并分析其与CFL数、网格比$\Delta x_1/\Delta x_2$的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轭传热问题中不同空间域上的子问题需要独立求解（分区方法），为发展更高效的分区算法，需要可靠的误差估计；已有结果多为时间离散情形，缺乏完全离散且能刻画线性/超线性收敛的误差估计。

Method: 对两个不同空间域上通过低维界面耦合的线性热方程，采用Dirichlet--Neumann波形松弛实现分区求解；使用指数加权Fourier技术，在完全离散情形下推导有限时间$T$内的$l^2$误差估计。

Result: 完全离散误差估计与已有的时间离散估计接近；当子求解器CFL数较大时，估计关于$\Delta x_1$、$\Delta x_2$独立；当CFL数较小时，收敛行为依赖网格比$\Delta x_1/\Delta x_2$；估计同时刻画线性与超线性行为。

Conclusion: 数值实验表明，该完全离散误差估计在广泛的网格尺寸$\Delta x_1,\Delta x_2$、时间步长$\Delta t$与终止时间$T$范围内均是准确的，为分区Dirichlet--Neumann波形松弛方法的效率分析提供了可靠依据。

Abstract: We consider two coupled linear heat equations on different spatial domains that interact through a lower dimensional interface. This models conjugate heat transfer. The problem is solved using Dirichlet--Neumann waveform relaxation. This allows the subproblems to be solved using separate codes, a so called partitioned approach. Our overall goal is to develop more efficient partitioned methods, and to this end, we want reliable error estimates.
  Here, we use an exponentially weighted Fourier technique to derive new error estimates in $l^2$ for finite time $T$ in the fully discrete setting. These describe both linear and superlinear behavior. We show that the fully discrete estimate is close to a previously obtained time discrete estimate and independent of $Δx_1$ and $Δx_2$ when the CFL number in the subsolvers is large. We also show that the convergence behaviour depends on the ratio $Δx_1/Δx_2$ when the CFL number is small.
  Our numerical experiments show that the fully discrete estimate is accurate across a wide range of grid sizes $Δx_1, Δx_2$, time step sizes $Δt$ and $T$.

</details>


### [241] [Finite element exterior calculus for spectra and pseudospectra of advection-diffusion of differential forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01501)
*Daniele Boffi,Kaibo Hu,Yizhou Liang,Umberto Zerbinati*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于有限元外微积分（FEEC）的结构保持有限元方法，用于研究微分形式的对流-扩散算子的谱与伪谱，以重新审视数值发电机研究中的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值发电机作用的研究长期活跃，但仍面临诸多挑战与开放问题；同时，结构保持方法与有限元外微积分（FEEC）的发展，为重新审视数值发电机研究、探索计算中的既有开放问题提供了契机。

Method: 基于有限元de Rham复形，构建结构保持的有限元格式，用于计算微分形式的对流-扩散算子的谱与伪谱。

Result: 提出了针对发电机问题的FEEC方法，并给出了基于有限元de Rham复形的格式及其分析。

Conclusion: 结构保持有限元方法可有效应用于发电机问题的谱与伪谱计算，为数值发电机研究提供新的计算框架。

Abstract: Numerical investigations of dynamo action have remained active in fluid mechanics over the past decades, presenting numerous challenges and open problems. Meanwhile, the development of structure-preserving methods and finite element exterior calculus (FEEC) inspires a revisit of numerical dynamo studies and an exploration of existing open questions in computation. In this paper, we present a FEEC approach for dynamo problems. In particular, we investigate structure-preserving finite element schemes for computing the spectra and pseudospectra of advection-diffusion operators of differential forms. The schemes and their analysis are based on finite element de Rham complexes.

</details>


### [242] [An Unfitted Hybrid High-Order Method for the Elastodynamics Problem with Imperfect Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01504)
*Peiqi Huang,Erik Burman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文设计并分析了一种用于弹性波方程的非拟合杂交高阶（HHO）方法，处理由线性滑移型不完美界面分隔的双组分介质。方法在切割单元中加倍未知量、采用单元聚合处理小切割，并在局部对称应变重构中通过正则化界面刚度纳入界面条件，同时构造界面稳定项。半离散问题被证明具有矫顽性与连续性，得到能量误差 \(O(h^{k+1})\) 与 \(L^2\) 误差 \(O(h^{k+2})\)，常数与柔度参数及界面切割方式无关。结合 Newmark 或 SDIRK 时间格式，数值实验验证了预测收敛率及对柔度的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性波在不完美界面（线性滑移型）介质中的传播问题，界面条件为牵引连续、位移跳变通过柔度张量正比于牵引。传统拟合网格方法需生成与界面贴合的网格，代价高且难以处理界面移动或复杂几何。因此需要一种非拟合（unfitted）方法，既能避免网格拟合，又能稳健处理界面切割网格、小切割以及大范围柔度参数。

Method: 采用非拟合 HHO 方法：网格不与界面拟合，在切割单元中未知量加倍，小切割通过单元聚合处理，界面不附加未知量。核心为两个要素——(1) 每个切割子单元内的局部对称应变重构，仿照 Hansbo-Hansbo 思路，通过正则化界面刚度 \(\bS_h=(h_T\delta^{-1}\bI+\bK)^{-1}\) 纳入界面条件；(2) 由同一矩阵构造的界面稳定项。半离散层面证明双线性形式的矫顽性与连续性，并推导误差估计。时间离散采用 Newmark 格式（精确守恒离散能量）或最高四阶的单对角隐式 Runge-Kutta（SDIRK）格式。

Result: 证明离散双线性形式矫顽且连续；获得能量误差估计 \(O(h^{k+1})\) 与 \(L^2\) 误差估计 \(O(h^{k+2})\)，常数独立于柔度参数及界面切割网格的方式。二维数值实验验证了 \(k\in\{1,2,3\}\) 的预测收敛率、对柔度跨十六个数量级的鲁棒性，并展示了弹性波跨越未解析滑移界面的传播。

Conclusion: 该非拟合 HHO 方法为双组分介质中带线性滑移界面的弹性波问题提供了稳健、高阶且对界面切割与柔度参数均不敏感的计算框架，可用于界面不被网格解析的情形。

Abstract: We design and analyse an unfitted hybrid high-order (HHO) method for the elastic wave equation in a medium made of two components separated by an imperfect interface of linear slip type, across which the traction is continuous and the displacement jump is proportional to the traction through a compliancy tensor $\bK=α\bI+(β-α)\bn\otimes\bn$. The mesh is not fitted to the interface: the discrete unknowns are doubled in the cut cells, the small cuts are cured by a cell agglomeration procedure, and no unknown is attached to the interface. The two specific ingredients of the method are a local symmetric strain reconstruction in each cut subcell, which incorporates the interface condition through the regularised interface stiffness $\bS_h=(h_Tδ^{-1}\bI+\bK)^{-1}$ in the spirit of Hansbo and Hansbo {\em{A finite element method for the simulation of strong and weak discontinuities in solid mechanics.}} {Comput. Methods Appl. Mech. Engrg.}, 193, 2004, and an interface stabilisation built from the same matrix. For the space semi-discrete problem we prove that the discrete bilinear form is coercive and continuous, and we derive an energy-error estimate of order $h^{k+1}$ and an $L^2$-error estimate of order $h^{k+2}$, with constants independent of the compliancy parameters and of how the interface cuts the mesh. The scheme is combined either with the Newmark scheme, which conserves a discrete energy exactly, or with singly diagonally implicit Runge--Kutta schemes of order up to four. Numerical experiments in two dimensions confirm the predicted convergence rates for $k\in\{1,2,3\}$, the robustness with respect to the compliancy over sixteen orders of magnitude, and illustrate the propagation of elastic waves across an unresolved slipping interface.

</details>


### [243] [Deterministic and stochastic particle methods for the Fokker-Planck equation in S--formulation with application to point set registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01700)
*Klaas Willems,Angelo Iollo,Giovanni Russo,Tommaso Taddei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于Fokker-Planck方程的粒子方法，用于有界区域上的点集配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点集配准在有界区域（含障碍物）及高维场景下存在困难，需要能处理边界条件、粒子云强压缩/膨胀，并具备良好可扩展性的方法。

Method: 方法一：基于S-形式的移动最小二乘离散化，移动网格点（粒子）由含目标分布的漂移项对流，扩散在动态演化的粒子云上求解；通过新型幽灵点方法施加反射边界条件以处理障碍物。方法二：求解相关Langevin随机微分方程的蒙特卡洛求解器，利用演化粒子密度对数梯度的局部近似，从个体随机轨迹中提取宏观渗透路径。

Result: 通过大量数值实验展示了两种方法的主要特性与性能；蒙特卡洛方法因内在并行性表现出优异的可扩展性，并适合高维配准问题。

Conclusion: 两种粒子方法均能有效实现有界域点集配准，其中蒙特卡洛方法在高维和大规模场景下更具优势。

Abstract: We present two particle methods for point set registration in bounded domains based on the Fokker-Planck equation. The first method relies on a moving least squares discretization of the S--formulation, in which moving grid points (particles) are advected by the drift with the target distribution, diffusion is resolved on a dynamically evolving particle cloud. This setting naturally leads to strong compression and expansion of the particle cloud. Obstacles are handled by enforcing reflective boundary conditions through a novel ghost point method. The second method is a Monte Carlo solver for the associated Langevin stochastic differential equation. It relies on a local approximation of the logarithmic gradient of the evolving particle density to extract macroscopic osmotic paths from individual stochastic trajectories. Owing to its inherent parallelism, this method exhibits excellent scalability and is well suited for high-dimensional registration problems. We illustrate the main features and performance of both approaches through extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [244] [Toeplitz Infinite GMRES for Parameterized Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01740)
*Weiguo Gao,Feiyang Jiang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出 Toeplitz infinite GMRES,用于求解大量参数值下的大规模稀疏解析参数化系统,利用伴随 Krylov 序列的块上三角 Toeplitz 结构构造 Arnoldi 过程,无需存储全部 Arnoldi 向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解 A(s)x(s)=z 在大量参数 s 处的解析参数化系统,传统 GMRES 需逐个参数重复存储与计算,开销大;需要利用参数化问题的结构降低算术与存储成本。

Method: 利用伴随 Krylov 序列的块上三角 Toeplitz 结构构造 Arnoldi 过程并恢复解近似;推导增量递推,每 p 步 Arnoldi 仅需 O(np^2+p^3) 算术与 O(np+p^2) 存储(不计分解建立,假设系数作用与三角求解为线性成本);引入动态 generator 刷新策略抑制基本递推中的相消;对具固定分离表示的矩阵函数,基于紧致收缩幂零矩阵发展隐式 rebase 方法。

Result: 两种变体在精确算术下均保持 Arnoldi 过程,rebase 方法需精确矩阵函数作用;给出平方可和 Taylor 系数的残差下界,解释归一化单位圆外的缩放障碍;理论分析与数值实验验证方法的计算效率。

Conclusion: Toeplitz infinite GMRES 通过 Toeplitz 结构显著降低参数化系统求解的算术与存储复杂度,generator 刷新与隐式 rebase 提升数值稳定性与适用性,数值实验验证其高效性。

Abstract: We develop Toeplitz infinite GMRES for solving large sparse analytic parameterized systems $A(s)x(s)=z$ at many parameter values. The method exploits a block upper triangular Toeplitz structure in the companion Krylov sequence to construct the Arnoldi process and recover solution approximations without storing full Arnoldi vectors. We derive incremental recurrences requiring $\mathcal{O}(np^2+p^3)$ arithmetic and $\mathcal{O}(np+p^2)$ storage for $p$ Arnoldi steps, excluding factorization setup and assuming linear-cost coefficient actions and triangular solves. A dynamic generator-refreshing strategy addresses cancellation in the basic recurrence. For matrix functions admitting a fixed separated representation, we further develop an implicitly rebased method based on a compact, contractive nilpotent matrix. Both variants preserve the Arnoldi process in exact arithmetic, with exact matrix-function actions required for the rebased method. A residual lower bound for square-summable Taylor coefficients explains a scaling obstruction outside the normalized unit disk. Theoretical analysis and numerical experiments illustrate the computational efficiency of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [245] [Adaptive Multiresolution Diffusion Operators: A Variational Theory on Evolving Multiresolution Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01809)
*Christian Tantardini,Stig Rune Jensen,Roberto Di Remigio Eikås,Joakim Henrik Beck*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应多分辨率表示上的状态依赖扩散变分框架，其中扩散算子由自适应状态自身生成，并以此构建AMDI用于正则化反问题与去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自适应小波方法中，算子是预先给定的，仅在演化的逼近空间上进行表示；而这里希望细化与粗化过程同时改变表示、交互图与算子本身，从而在自适应层级因离散拓扑变化而演化时，需要一个时间离散变分原理而非固定空间上的微分演化来描述耦合动力学。

Method: 状态由容许的多分辨率树、其活动逼近空间与基、以及相应的系数表示构成，由此确定对称非负的交互形式与相关联的半正定内禀扩散算子；采用时间离散变分原理建模耦合动力学。针对正则化反问题，构造Adaptive Multiresolution Diffusion Imaging (AMDI)，在状态依赖能量中融合数据保真、内禀扩散、系数稀疏性与树的复杂度。数值实验验证算子恒等式、考察细化交换子衰减、确认离散能量耗散，并使用自适应Haar与高阶多小波进行计算。

Result: 建立了离散更新的存在性、离散能量不等式、系数空间零模以及冻结自适应状态下的收缩性。数值实验验证了组装算子的恒等式，考察了细化交换子的衰减，并确认了离散能量耗散；自适应Haar与高阶多小波计算展示了在异质数据上的分辨率局部化。在去噪中，AMDI以不到10%的完整活动表示保持了高结构重建质量，并在留出的噪声实现上表现稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架将表示、交互图与算子耦合为一个自适应状态，通过时间离散变分原理给出可分析的性质，并成功应用于正则化反问题与去噪，表明状态依赖的内禀扩散在低表示预算下能有效保留结构信息。

Abstract: We develop a variational framework for state-dependent diffusion on adaptive multiresolution representations in which the diffusion operator is generated by the adaptive state itself. The state consists of an admissible multiresolution tree, its active approximation space and basis, and the corresponding coefficient representation. It determines a symmetric nonnegative interaction form and an associated positive semidefinite intrinsic diffusion operator. In contrast to classical adaptive wavelet methods, where a prescribed operator is represented on an evolving approximation space, refinement and coarsening here modify simultaneously the representation, interaction graph, and operator. Because the adaptive hierarchy evolves through discrete topological changes, the coupled dynamics are formulated through a time-discrete variational principle rather than a differential evolution on a fixed space. We establish existence of the discrete updates, a discrete energy inequality, the coefficient-space null mode, and contractivity for frozen adaptive states. For regularized inverse problems, the construction yields Adaptive Multiresolution Diffusion Imaging (AMDI), combining data fidelity, intrinsic diffusion, coefficient sparsity, and tree complexity in a state-dependent energy. Numerical experiments verify the assembled operator identities, examine refinement-commutator decay, and confirm discrete energy dissipation. Adaptive Haar and higher-order multiwavelet calculations demonstrate localization of resolution on heterogeneous data. In denoising, AMDI retains high structural reconstruction quality with less than 10\% of the full active representation, with stable behavior across held-out noise realizations.

</details>


### [246] [Approximation and computation of the geodesic Sinkhorn distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02007)
*Hugo Lavenant,Jonas Luckhardt,Bernhard Schmitzer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究熵正则最优传输导出的Sinkhorn散度所定义的黎曼度量 $\mathsf{d}_S$ 的逼近与计算，证明空间离散下的 Gromov–Hausdorff 收敛与时间离散下的 $\Gamma$-收敛，并给出数值实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作在概率分布空间上由熵正则最优传输（Sinkhorn 散度 $S_\varepsilon$）引入了黎曼度量 $\mathsf{d}_S$，但缺乏在实际计算中逼近与求解该度量的方法，本文旨在填补这一计算空白。

Method: 空间上对底空间采用越来越细的 Eulerian 离散化，证明度量 $\mathsf{d}_S$ 的 Gromov–Hausdorff 收敛及测地线收敛；时间上证明链式离散泛函 $N\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}S_\varepsilon(\mu_k,\mu_{k+1})$ 到定义 $\mathsf{d}_S$ 的能量泛函的 $\Gamma$-收敛。据此推导并实现数值格式。

Result: 建立了度量 $\mathsf{d}_S$ 在空间离散（Gromov–Hausdorff 意义）与时间离散（$\Gamma$-收敛意义）下的一致性，并实现了计算 $\mathsf{d}_S$ 近似值的数值算法。

Conclusion: $\mathsf{d}_S$ 可由有限离散化在空间与时间两个层面一致逼近，从而为其实际计算提供了理论保证与可实现的数值方案。

Abstract: In [H. Lavenant, J. Luckhardt, G. Mordant, B. Schmitzer, L. Tamanini, The Riemannian geometry of Sinkhorn divergences. Ann. Inst. H. Poincaré Anal. Non Linéaire 43 (2026)] we introduced a Riemannian metric $\mathsf{d}_S$ on the space of probability distributions obtained from entropic optimal transport, specifically from the Sinkhorn divergence $S_\varepsilon$. In the present work we discuss how to approximate and compute $\mathsf{d}_S$. Spatially, we prove Gromov--Hausdorff convergence of the metric and convergence of geodesics for increasingly fine Eulerian discretization of the base space. Temporally, we show $Γ$-convergence of the chain discretization $N \sum_{k=0}^{N-1} S_\varepsilon(μ_k, μ_{k+1})$ to the energy functional defining $\mathsf{d}_S$. We deduce and implement numerical schemes to compute approximations of $\mathsf{d}_S$.

</details>


### [247] [Entropy dissipative high order schemes for a hyperbolic model of two-layer thin film flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02059)
*Rahul Barthwal,Philipp Öffner,Chetan Singh,Jan Zawallich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为描述含完全可溶溶质颗粒的两层不互溶薄膜流的双曲守恒律系统，构造了高阶熵耗散格式，比较了精确与简化近似两类熵守恒通量，并在有限差分与DG谱元两种框架下验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两层薄膜流耦合溶质输运的方程组缺乏同时保证高阶精度与熵稳定性的数值方法；且实际常用的简化熵守恒通量缺乏结构，其与精确通量在效率与鲁棒性上的差异尚不明确。

Method: 基于Tadmor熵守恒通量思想，分别构造精确形式与常用简化近似形式两种熵守恒通量，并将其嵌入熵耗散有限差分格式与熵耗散间断Galerkin谱元法（DGSEM）中。

Result: 通过两种空间离散的数值实验，考察了所提方法的性能与鲁棒性，并表明数值通量的选择对求解器效率有显著影响。

Conclusion: 精确熵守恒通量尽管推导更复杂，但结构更清晰、实现成本更低；简化近似虽构造简单且应用广泛，却结构化程度低、实现计算代价更高，通量选择是影响求解效率的关键因素。

Abstract: In this article, we develop high-order, entropy-dissipative schemes for a hyperbolic system of conservation laws describing the first-order dynamics of two-layer thin-film flows of immiscible fluids with perfectly soluble solute particles. The key aspect is to construct entropy-conservative fluxes in the sense of Tadmor. Here, we consider and compare two different construction processes: an exact formulation and a commonly used simplified approximation in the flux evaluation. While the approximate approach is simpler at the level of construction and is frequently employed in practice (also in other contexts), it is less structured, leading to increased computational cost in the implementation compared to the exact formulation, which has been much more complicated to derive. We employ these fluxes within both an entropy-dissipative finite-difference framework and an entropy-dissipative discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method. Through numerical experiments using two distinct spatial discretizations, we investigate the performance and robustness of the proposed methods and demonstrate that the choice of numerical flux has a noticeable impact on the efficiency of the resulting solvers.

</details>


### [248] [Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Free-Boundary Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02084)
*Tan Phuong Dong Le*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的物理信息框架求解自由边界问题，将障碍约束、PDE不等式、互补条件与边界条件编码为残差型损失函数，在线性椭圆障碍问题、非线性p-Laplacian障碍问题及一相Stefan问题上验证，KAN相比PINN与ResNet基线具有更低相对L2/L∞误差，并能准确刻画接触区域与移动界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由边界问题涉及不等式约束、互补条件与移动界面，传统数值方法（如有限元/有限差分）在网格生成、界面追踪与高维推广上存在困难；而现有物理信息神经网络（PINN）在求解此类非光滑、带约束的PDE时精度与稳定性有限。作者希望探索KAN这一新型网络表示是否可作为更有效的替代方案。

Method: 在物理信息框架下使用KAN近似解函数，将障碍约束、PDE不等式、互补条件及边界条件统一转化为残差型损失项进行无网格训练。选取三类代表性自由边界问题：线性椭圆障碍问题、非线性p-Laplacian障碍问题以及时间相关的一相Stefan问题。将KAN求解器与PINN及残差网络（ResNet）基线在相同设置下进行对比。

Result: 数值实验表明，KAN在相对L2误差与L∞误差指标上均低于PINN与ResNet基线，同时能够较准确地分辨接触区域（contact region）以及移动界面（moving interface）。

Conclusion: KAN表示为求解自由边界PDE提供了一种有效的替代方案，验证了其在带不等式约束和移动界面问题上的潜力。

Abstract: We study free-boundary problems within a physics-informed framework using Kolmogorov-Arnold network (KAN) approximations. The proposed approach incorporates obstacle constraints, partial differential equation (PDE) inequalities, complementarity conditions, and boundary conditions through residual-based loss functions. We consider a linear elliptic obstacle problem, a nonlinear $p$-Laplacian obstacle problem, and a time-dependent one-phase Stefan problem. The proposed KAN solver is compared with physics-informed neural network (PINN) and residual-network baselines. Numerical experiments show that KANs achieve low relative $L^2$ and $L^\infty$ errors while accurately resolving contact regions and moving interfaces. The results indicate that KAN representations provide an effective alternative for solving free-boundary PDEs.

</details>
